Санскритский словарь Махешвары Сури. Электронная транскрипция издания 1911 года. В исходной копии на странице 17 отсутствует стих 179; этот пробел не восполнялся. Текст представлен в латинской транслитерации IAST. Источник: sanskrit-kosha/kosha. Оцифровка: Dr. Dhaval Patel и Shree Ramkrishna Knowledge Foundation (SRKKF). Лицензия GNU GPL v3.0. Новая сверка со сканами не выполнялась.

# Viśvaprakāśa

## Preface

## Preface / 1 \[unit 1\]

śrīḥ \|

viśvaprakāśaḥ

vidvadvaraśrīmaheśvaraviracitaḥ

namaḥ samyaksambuddhāya \|\|

stuvīmahi mahāmohakleśāntakabhiṣagvaram \|

traidhātukanidānajñaṃ sarvajñaṃ duḥkhahānaye \|\| 1 \|\|

## Preface / 2 \[unit 2\]

kalāvilāsānmakarandabindu-

mudrāṃ vinidre hṛdayāravinde \|

yā kalpayantī ramate kavīnāṃ

devīṃ namasyāmi sarasvatīṃ tām \|\| 2 \|\|

## Preface / 3 \[unit 3\]

kavīndrakumudānandakandodgamasudhākaram \|

vācaspatimatisparddhiśemuṣīcandrikojjvalam \|\| 3 \|\|

## Preface / 4 \[unit 4\]

kṣubhyatkṣīrābdhikallolamālollāsiyaśaḥśriyam \|

guruṃ vande jagadvandyaṃ guṇaratnaikarohaṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

## Preface / 5 \[unit 5\]

śrīsāhasāṅkanṛpateranavadyavaidya-

vidyātaraṅgapadamadvayameva bibhrat \|

yaścandracārucarito haricandranāmā

svavyākhyayā carakatantramalañcakāra \|\| 5 \|\|

## Preface / 6 \[unit 6\]

āsīdasīmavasudhādhipavandanīye

tasyānvaye sakalavaidyakalāvataṃsaḥ \|

śakrasya dasra iva gādhipurādhipasya

śrīkṛṣṇa ityamalakīrttilatāvitānaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## Preface / 7 \[unit 7\]

saṅkalpasambhavadanalpavikalpajalpa-

kalpānalākulitavādisahasrasindhuḥ \|

tarkatrayatrinayanastanayo yadīyo

dāmodaraḥ samabhavadbhiṣajāṃ vareṇyaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## Preface / 8 \[unit 8\]

tasyābhavat sūnarudāravāco

vācaspatiśrīlalanāvilāsī \|

sadvaidyavidyānalinīdineśaḥ

śrīmahlaṇaḥ satkumudākarenduḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## Preface / 9 \[unit 9\]

yadbhrātṛjaḥ sakalavaidyakatattvaratna-

ratnākaraśriyamavāpya ca keśavo'bhūt \|

kīrtterniketanamanindyapadapramāṇa-

vākyaprapañcaracanācaturānanaśrīḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## Preface / 10 \[unit 10\]

kṛṣṇasya cā'jani sutaḥ smitapuṇḍarīka-

ṣaṇḍātapatraparabhāgayaśaḥparāgaḥ \|

śrībrahma ityavikalātmamukhāravinda-

sollāsalāsitarasārdrasarasvatīkaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## Preface / 11 \[unit 11\]

tasyātmajaḥ sarasakairavakāntakīrtiḥ

śrīmānmaheśvara iti prathitaḥ kavīndraḥ \|

niḥśeṣavāṅmayamahārṇavapāradṛśvā

śabdāgamāmburuhaṣaṇḍaravirbabhūva \|\| 11 \|\|

## Preface / 12 \[unit 12\]

yaḥ sāhasāṅkacaritādimahāprabandha-

nirmmāṇanaipuṇaguṇāgatagauravaśrīḥ \|

yo vaidyakatrayasarojasarojabandhu-

rbandhuḥ satāṃ sukavikairavakānanenduḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## Preface / 13 \[unit 13\]

iyaṃ kṛtistasya maheśvarasya

vaidagdhyasindhoḥ puruṣottamānām \|

dedīpyatāṃ hṛtkamaleṣu nitya-

mākalpamākalpitakaustubhaśrīḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## Preface / 14 \[unit 14\]

labdhaiḥ kathañcidabhijātasuvarṇakāra-

līlena koṣavararatnadhiśabdaratnaiḥ \|

viśvaprakāśa iti kāñcanabandhaśobhāṃ

bibhranmayā'tra ghaṭito mukhakhaṇḍa eṣaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## Preface / 15 \[unit 15\]

kṣoṇīśvarodīritaśabdakoṣa-

ratnākarāloḍanalālasānām \|

sevyaḥ kathaṃ naiṣa suvarṇaśailo

viśvaprakāśo vibudhādhipānām \|\| 15 \|\|

## Preface / 16 \[unit 16\]

bhogīndrakātyāyanasāhasāṅka-

vācaspativyāḍipuraḥsarāṇām \|

saviśvarūpāmalamaṅgalānāṃ

śubhāṅgagopālitabhāgurāṇām \|\| 16 \|\|

## Preface / 17 \[unit 17\]

koṣāvakāśāt prakaṭaprabhāva-

sambhāvitā'narghyaguṇaḥ sa eṣaḥ \|

sampādayanneṣyati vāñchitārthān

kathaṃ na cintāmaṇitāṃ kavīnām \|\| 17 \|\|

## Preface / 18 \[unit 18\]

ā mitraśailacaramācalamekalādri-

kailāsabhūbhivalayādyadihā'sti kiñcit \|

ekatra sambhṛtamagocaraśabdaratna-

mālokyatāṃ tadakhilaṃ sudhiyaḥ kavīndrāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## Preface / 19 \[unit 19\]

yadyasti vāṅmayamahārṇavaṇmathanecchā

prāptuṃ padaṃ phaṇipateryadi kautukaṃ vaḥ \|\|

viśvaprakāśamaniśaṃ tadimaṃ niṣevya

sambhāvyatāṃ paramaśābdikaśekharaśrīḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## Preface / 20 \[unit 20\]

satāṃ pustakasambhārabhāramokṣaḥ kṛto mayā \|

nāmānuśāsanamidaṃ sampūrṇaṃ tanvatā'dbhutam \|\| 20 \|\|

## Preface / 21 \[unit 21\]

ekadvitricatuḥpañcaṣaḍvarṇānukramojjvalaiḥ \|

kāntādivargairnānārthasaṅgraho'yaṃ vitanyate \|\| 21 \|\|

## Preface / 22 \[unit 22\]

nānārthaḥ prathamānto'tra sarvatrādau pradarśitaḥ \|

saptamyanteṣu śabdeṣu vartamānaḥ suniścitaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## Preface / 23 \[unit 23\]

dṛṣṭāntena saha kvā'pi saptamyādhāra eva ca \|

spaṣṭāya liṅgabhedāya kvā'pyatra punaruktatā \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kaikakam / 24 \[unit 24\]

ko brahmātmānilārkeṣu śamane sarvanāmni ca \|

pāvake ca mayūre ca sukhaśīrṣajaleṣu kam \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 25 \[unit 25\]

akaṃ pāpe ca duḥkhe ca śako rājanyadeśayoḥ \|

vakastu vakapuṣpe syāt kaṣṭe śrīde ca rakṣasi \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 26 \[unit 26\]

śuko vyāsasute kīre rāvaṇasya ca mantriṇi \|

granthiparṇe śirīṣe ca śukaṃ syācchoṇake kvacit \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 27 \[unit 27\]

dvikaḥ kāke ca koke ca sṛko bāṇānilotpale \|

arko'rkaparṇe sphaṭike ravau tāmre divaspatau \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 28 \[unit 28\]

karkaḥ karke rāśibhede śuklāśve darpaṇe ghaṭe \|

tarkaḥ kāṅkṣāvitarkohakarmabhedeṣu kīrtyate \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 29 \[unit 29\]

śloko yaśasi padye ca lokastu bhuvane jane \|

stoko'lpe cātake tokamapatyaputrayorapi \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 30 \[unit 30\]

kokaścakre vṛke jyeṣṭhyāṃ kharjjūrīdrumabhekayoḥ \|\|

roko dīptau vile rokaṃ naukākrayaṇabhedayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 31 \[unit 31\]

beko vidagdhe chekaśca viśvastamṛgapakṣiṇoḥ \|

reko vireke śaṅkāyāṃ rekaḥ syādavame'pi ca \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 32 \[unit 32\]

kākaḥ syādvāyase vṛkṣaprabhede pīṭhasarpiṇi \|

śirobakṣāline mānaviśeṣadīpabhedayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 33 \[unit 33\]

kākā syāt kākanāsāyāṃ kākolīkākajaṅghayoḥ \|

rakte kāyāṃ malapvāñca kākamācyāñca kīrttitā \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 34 \[unit 34\]

kākaṃ strīratarabandhe syāt kākānāmapi saṃhatau \|

pākaḥ śiśau jarāniṣṭhāpacanakledaneṣu ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 35 \[unit 35\]

śāko dvīpāntare śaktau nṛpadrumaviśeṣayoḥ \|

śākaṃ haritake cāpi nākaḥ svargāntarikṣayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 36 \[unit 36\]

mūko'pyavāṅmatau dīne śūko'nukrośaśṛṅgayoḥ \|

bhūkaśchidre ca kāle ca bheko maṇḍūkameghayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 37 \[unit 37\]

ekantu kevalaṃ śreṣṭha itarasmiṃśca vācyavat \|

śaukaṃ śukasamūhe ca strīṇāñca karaṇāntare \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 38 \[unit 38\]

aṅkaḥ sthāne'ntike mantau rūpakotsaṅgalakṣmasu \|

nāṭikādiparicchede citrayuddhe ca bhūṣaṇe \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 39 \[unit 39\]

kaṅkaśchadmadvije khyāto lohapṛṣṭhakṛtāntayoḥ \|

vaṅkaḥ paryyāṇabhāge syānnadīpātre ca bhaṅgure \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 40 \[unit 40\]

ṭaṅkaḥ kapitthabhede syānmānāntarakhanitrayoḥ \|

kope'sikośe jaṅghāyāṃ ṭaṅkaṇe grāvadāraṇe \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 41 \[unit 41\]

raṅkastu kṛpaṇe malle paṅkaḥ karddamapāpayoḥ \|

nyaṅkurmṛge ṛṣau nākurvāmalūre girau munau \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 42 \[unit 42\]

śaṅkuḥ kīle śive'stre ca saṃkhyāyādaprabhedayoḥ \|

kalkaḥ pāpāśaye pāpe dambhe viṭkiṭṭayorapi \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 43 \[unit 43\]

valkantu valkale khaṇḍe śalkaṃ śakalavalkayoḥ \|

śulkaṃ ghaṭṭādideye syājjāmāturbandhake'pi ca \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 44 \[unit 44\]

niṣkamaṣṭādhikasvarṇaśate dīnārakarṣayoḥ \|

vakṣo\[ṣko\]'laṅkaraṇe hemapātre hemapale'pi ca \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 45 \[unit 45\]

muṣko'ṇḍakośe saṅghāte muṣko mokṣe ca pādape \|

kiṣkurvitastau haste ca prakoṣṭhe kutsite'pi ca \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 46 \[unit 46\]

trikākūpasya nemau syāt trikaṃ pṛṣṭhādhare traye \|

laṅkā rakṣaḥpurīśākhāśākinīkulaṭāsu ca \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 47 \[unit 47\]

śaṅkā trāse vitarke ca rākā tu saridantare \|

kacchūnavarajakanyāpūrṇendupūrṇimāsu ca \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 48 \[unit 48\]

kanakaṃ campake svarṇe kiṃśuke nāgakeśare \|

dhuttūre kāñcanāre ca kālīye'pi kvacinmatam \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 49 \[unit 49\]

karakastu karaṅke syāddāḍime ca kamaṇḍalau \|

pakṣibhede kare cāpi karakā ca ghanopale \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 50 \[unit 50\]

narako niraye daitye sarakaḥ sīdhubhājane \|

acchinnādhvagapaṅktau ca sīdhupāne ca sīdhuni \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 51 \[unit 51\]

khanakaścittatattvajñe sandhicaure ca mūṣike \|

janakaḥ pitṛrājarṣyoryutakaṃ saṃśaye yuge \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 52 \[unit 52\]

yautake calanāgre ca strīvastrāñcalayuktayoḥ \|

kṛṣakaḥ kaṣarke phāle rajako dhāvake śuke \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 53 \[unit 53\]

madhuko vandibhede syājjyeṣṭhyāhve vihagāntare \|

madhukaṃ madhuparṇyāṃ syādanuko nipuṇālpayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 54 \[unit 54\]

vasukaḥ śivamallyāṃ syādarkkaparṇe ca romake \|

śiśukaḥ śiśumāre syādbālakolūpinorapi \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 55 \[unit 55\]

kramukaḥ paṭṭikālodhre bhadre mustakapūgayoḥ \|

phale kārpāsikāyāśca kramuko brahmadāruṇi \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 56 \[unit 56\]

bahukaḥ karkkaṭe'rke ca dātyūhe jalakhātake \|

dhanikaḥ sādhughanyākadhaveṣu dhanikā striyām \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 57 \[unit 57\]

priyakaḥ pītaśāle syānnīpe citramṛge'lini \|

kuṅkume ca priyaṅgau ca pṛthukaścipiṭe śiśau \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 58 \[unit 58\]

yamakaṃ yamaje śabdālaṅkāre saṃyame'pi ca \|

maśakaḥ kathyate kṣudrarogajantuviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 59 \[unit 59\]

kulakantu paṭole syācchlokasambandhagucchake \|

kulakaḥ syāt kulaśreṣṭhe valmīke kākatinduke \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 60 \[unit 60\]

tilakaṃ citrake prāhurlalāme tilakālake \|

rogavidrumabhedeṣu lomni sauvarcale'pi ca \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 61 \[unit 61\]

kuliko nāgabhede syād drubhede kulasattame \|

kuśikaḥ syānmunau tailaśeṣe sarjjākṣavṛkṣayoḥ \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 62 \[unit 62\]

caṣakantu surāpātre madhumadyaprabhedayoḥ \|

caṭakaḥ kalaviṅke syāccaṭakā tatstryapatyayoḥ \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 63 \[unit 63\]

kaṭakaṃ valaye sānau rājadhānīnitambayoḥ \|

sāmudralavaṇe dantamaṇḍale dantināmapi \|\| 63 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 64 \[unit 64\]

kaṭukā kaṭurohiṇyāṃ vyoṣe'pi kaṭukaṃ smṛtam \|

culukaḥ prasutau bhāṇḍabhede culukavanmataḥ \|\| 64 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 65 \[unit 65\]

pulakaḥ kṛmibhede syādgucchārkkamaṇidoṣayoḥ \|

gajānnapiṇḍe romāñce haritāle śilāntare \|\| 65 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 66 \[unit 66\]

rucakaṃ maṅgaladravye grīvābharaṇadantayoḥ \|

utkaṭe bījapūre ca sauvarcalaviḍa\[ha\]ṅgayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 67 \[unit 67\]

rocanāyāñca rucakamaśvābharaṇamālyayoḥ \|\|

kṣurakaḥ kokilākṣe syādgokṣure tilakadrume \|\| 67 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 68 \[unit 68\]

kāmukaḥ kamane'śokapādape cātimuktake \|

pātukaḥ pātayālau syāt prapātajalahastinoḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 69 \[unit 69\]

ānakaḥ paṭahe bheryyāṃ mṛdaṅge svanadambude \|

āḍhakaṃ mānabhede syāt tuvaryyāmāḍhakī matā \|\| 69 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 70 \[unit 70\]

kārakaḥ karttari proktaḥ karmmādāvapi kārakam \|

cārakaḥ pālake'śvādeḥ syāt sañcārakavandhayoḥ \|\| 70 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 71 \[unit 71\]

tārakaḥ karṇadhāre syāddaitye ca dṛśi tārakam \|

kanīnikāyā nakṣatre tārakaṃ tāraketi ca \|\| 71 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 72 \[unit 72\]

vārako'śvagatāvaśvaviśeṣe ca niṣedhake \|

jāhako ghoṅkhamārjjārakhaṭvākāruṇḍikāsu ca \|\| 72 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 73 \[unit 73\]

pāvako'gnau sadācāre vahnimanthe ca citrake \|

bhallātake viḍaṅge ca sāyakaḥ śarakhaḍgayoḥ \|\| 73 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 74 \[unit 74\]

nāyako netari śreṣṭhe hāramadhyamaṇāvapi \|

prāṇakaḥ sattvajātīye jīvakadrumavelayoḥ \|\| 74 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 75 \[unit 75\]

jālakaṃ korake proktaṃ kulāye jālinīphale \|

ānāye dambhabhede ca vastrabhede ca jālikā \|\| 75 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 76 \[unit 76\]

bālako'gre śiśau keśe vājivāraṇavāladhau \|

aṅgulīyakahīverapārihāryyeṣu vālakam \|\| 76 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 77 \[unit 77\]

vārddhakaṃ vṛddhasaṅghāte vṛddhatve vṛddhakarmyaṇi \|

māmakaṃ mamatāyukte mātule māmakaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 78 \[unit 78\]

yājako yājini khyāto yājako rājakuñjare \|

grāhako ghātavihage vyālānāñca grahītari \|\| 78 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 79 \[unit 79\]

dārako bhedake'patye rātrakaṃ pañcarā\[tra\]ke \|

yena veśyāgṛhe nīto vatsarastatra rātrakaḥ \|\| 79 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 80 \[unit 80\]

lāsako'lasake lāsyakāriṇyapi mayūrake \|

śārkakaḥ śarkarāpiṇḍe dugdhaphene ca śārkakaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 81 \[unit 81\]

hārakaḥ kitave caure gadyavijñānabhedayoḥ \|

sthāsakākhyā ca cārccikye jalāderapi budbude \|\| 81 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 82 \[unit 82\]

drāvakastu śilābhede vidagdhe moṣake'pi ca \|

bhrāmako jambuke dhūrte sūryāvartāśmabhedayoḥ \|\| 82 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 83 \[unit 83\]

piṇyākaḥ sihlake hiṅgau tilacūrṇe'pi kuṅkume \|

pināko harakodaṇḍe pāṃśuvarṣatriśūlayoḥ \|\| 83 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 84 \[unit 84\]

kāvṛkaḥ kṛkavākau syāpī\[tpī\]tamustakakokayoḥ \|

kṣārakaḥ pakṣimatsyādipiṭake jālake'pi ca \|\| 84 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 85 \[unit 85\]

pāṭakaḥ syānmahākiṣkau kaṭakāntaravādyayoḥ \|

akṣādicālane mūladravyopacayavedhasoḥ \|\| 85 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 86 \[unit 86\]

{atrādarśe ekāṅkasyānirdeśāt‌ truṭiḥ saṃbhāvyate \| }

varākaḥ saṅgare śocye parāko vratakhaḍgayoḥ \|

pulākastucchadhānye syāt saṅkṣepe bhaktasikthake \|\| 86 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 87 \[unit 87\]

vipākaḥ kīrttyate svādau pariṇāme ca durgatau \|

vipākaḥ pacane svede'pyasatkarmmaphale'pi ca \|\| 87 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 88 \[unit 88\]

lampāko lampaṭe deśe śimphākaḥ śaphamūrkhayoḥ \|

sampākastārkike dhṛṣṭe sampākaścaturaṅgule \|\| 88 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 89 \[unit 89\]

mocakaḥ kadalīśigrunirmmocakavirāgiṣu \|

pecako gajalāṅgūlamūlaparyyantaghūkayoḥ \|\| 89 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 90 \[unit 90\]

mecako barhicandre syānmecakaḥ śyāmale'pi ca \|

timire kṛṣṇavarṇe tu mecakaṃ vācyavadbhavet \|\| 90 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 91 \[unit 91\]

sūcakaḥ sīvanadravye bodhake vṛṣadaṃśake \|

piśune śuni kāke ca kecakaḥ sektṛmeghayoḥ \|\| 91 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 92 \[unit 92\]

locako māṃsapiṇḍe syādakṣṇi tāre ca kaṅgule \|

lalāṭābharaṇe strīṇāṃ kadalīnīlavastrayoḥ \|\| 92 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 93 \[unit 93\]

nirbuddhau karṇapūre ca maurvyāṃ bhūślathacarmmaṇi \|

modako harṣake jñeyaḥ khādyabhede ca modakam \|\| 93 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 94 \[unit 94\]

kholakaḥ pākavalmīkapūgakoṣaśirastrake \|

golako vidhavāputre jārācca maṇike guḍe \|\| 94 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 95 \[unit 95\]

kūpako guṇavṛkṣe syāttailapātre kukundare \|

udapāne citāyāñca kūpikā'mbhogatopale \|\| 95 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 96 \[unit 96\]

kūlakaḥ kṛmiśaile ca pratīrastūpayorapi \|

rūpakaṃ nāṭake dhūrte kāvyālaṅkaraṇe viduḥ \|\| 96 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 97 \[unit 97\]

cūtakaḥ kūpake'pyāmre sūtakaṃ rasajanmanoḥ \|

lūnakaḥ syāt paśau bhinne śūcakaḥ prāvaṭe rase \|\| 97 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 98 \[unit 98\]

kīcako daityabhede syācchuṣkavaṃśe drumāntare \|

kīṭakaḥ kṛmibhede syānniṣṭhure kīṭako'nyavat \|\| 98 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 99 \[unit 99\]

jīvakaḥ pītaśāle syāt kṣapaṇe vṛddhajīvini \|

sevini prāṇake'pyāhituṇḍike pādapāntare \|\| 99 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 100 \[unit 100\]

ājīve jīvikāmāhurjīvantyāmapi kutracit \|

dīpakaṃ vāgalaṅkāre dīpako dīptikārake \|\| 100 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 101 \[unit 101\]

cīrako dhikkriyālerave pataṅgyāṃ\[?\] cīrikā matā \|

sevakastu praseve syādanujīvini cānyavat \|\| 101 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 102 \[unit 102\]

aśoko vañcule māne drumaniḥśokayormataḥ \|

aśokā kaṭurohiṇyāmaśokaṃ pādape smṛtam \|\| 102 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 103 \[unit 103\]

āloko darśane dyote āloko vandibhāṣaṇe \|

peṭakaṃ pustakādīnāṃ mañjūṣāṇāṃ kadambake \|\| 103 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 104 \[unit 104\]

herako buddhabhede syānmahākālagaṇe'pi ca \|\|

dhenukaṃ karaṇe strīṇāṃ dhenūnāmapi saṃhatau \|\| 104 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 105 \[unit 105\]

vivekaḥ syājjaladroṇyāṃ vicāre'pi rahasyapi \|

gairikaṃ kanake dhātau prasekaḥ secane cyutau \|\| 105 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 106 \[unit 106\]

vaijike śigrutaile ca hetau sadyo'ṅkure'pi ca \|

korakaṃ kuḍmale vidyāt kakkolakamṛṇālayoḥ \|\| 106 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 107 \[unit 107\]

kautukañcābhilāṣe syādutsave narmmaharṣayoḥ \|

paramparāsamāyātamaṅgale ca kutūhale \|\| 107 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 108 \[unit 108\]

vivare sūtragītādibhogakābde ca smṛtam \|

kauśiko nakule vyālagrāhe gugguluśakrayoḥ \|\| 108 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 109 \[unit 109\]

viśvāmitre ca kośāgryokṛkayorapi kauśikaḥ \|

kauśikī caṇḍikāyāñca nadībhede ca kauśikī \|\| 109 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 110 \[unit 110\]

pratīko'vayave proktaḥ pratikūlavilomayoḥ \|

bhūtīkamapi bhūnimbadīpyakarpūrakattṛṇe\[?\] \|\| 110 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 111 \[unit 111\]

abhīkaḥ kāmuke krūre kavau ca bhayavarjite \|

valmīko vāmalūre syānmunirogaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 111 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 112 \[unit 112\]

anīkantu priye proktaṃ vyalīko nāgare smṛtaḥ \|

vyalīkamapriyākāryye vailakṣyeṣvapi pīḍane \|\| 112 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 113 \[unit 113\]

nālīkaṃ śarakhaṇḍe'bje nālīkaḥ śaraśalyayoḥ \|

alīkamapriye proktamalīkamanṛte'pi ca \|\| 113 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 114 \[unit 114\]

anūkantu kule śīle'pyanūko gatajanmani \|

ulūkaḥ kuravinde syāt pecake jambhabhedini \|\| 114 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 115 \[unit 115\]

śambūko gajakumbhānte daityabhede ca ghoṅgake \|

bandhūko bandhujīve syād bandhūkaḥ pītaśālake \|\| 115 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 116 \[unit 116\]

maṇḍūkaṃ bandhubhede'pi dardure śoṇake viduḥ \|

maṇḍūkaparṇyāṃ maṇḍūkī varṇakaścāraṇe smṛtaḥ \|\| 116 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 117 \[unit 117\]

vilepane candane ca varṇakaṃ syādathārbhakaḥ \|

ḍimbhe mūrkhe kṛśe cūṇe vartako'śvakhure khage \|\| 117 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 118 \[unit 118\]

puṣpakaṃ prītikānetrarogayo ratnakaṅkaṇe \|

kuberasya vimāne ca kāśīśe ca rasāñjane \|\| 118 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 119 \[unit 119\]

lohe kāṃsye mṛdaṅgāraśakaṭyāmapi puṣpakam \|

pūrṇakaḥ svarṇacūḍe syānnāsācchinnyāntu pūrṇikā \|\| 119 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 120 \[unit 120\]

darśakaḥ syāt pratīhāre darśayitṛpravīṇayoḥ \|

padmakaṃ padmakāṣṭebhavindujālakayorapi \|\| 120 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 121 \[unit 121\]

dīpyakañcājamode syādyavānībarhicūḍayoḥ \|

sasyakaḥ syānmaṇau khaḍge nārikelasya cāntare \|\| 121 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 122 \[unit 122\]

raktako mlānabandhūkaraktavastrānurāgiṣu \|

citrakaḥ syāttaruvyāghrabhedayorbheṣajāntare \|\| 122 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 123 \[unit 123\]

vṛścikastu druṇe\[?\] rāśau śūkakīṭe tathauṣadhe \|

aṃśukaṃ sūkṣmavastre syādvastramātrottarīyayoḥ \|\| 123 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 124 \[unit 124\]

antikaṃ nikaṭe cullyāmantikā śītalauṣadhe \|

tathā jyeṣṭhabhaginyāñca nāṭyoktau kīrttyate'ntikā \|\| 124 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 125 \[unit 125\]

svastiko maṅgaladravye catuṣkagṛhabhedayoḥ \|

piṣṭakasya vikāre ca svastiko rajatālike \|\| 125 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 126 \[unit 126\]

kañcuko vārabāṇe syānnirmmoke kavace'pi \[ca\] \|

varddhāpakagṛhītāṅgasthitavastre ca colake \|\| 126 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 127 \[unit 127\]

auṣadhau kañcukī cātha vañcakaḥ khaladhūrttayoḥ \|

jambūke gṛhababhrau ca guhyakaśchekanighnayoḥ \|\| 127 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 128 \[unit 128\]

bandhaka syādvinimaye pum̐ścalyāṃ syācca bandhakī \|

gāndhikaṃ lekhake prāhuḥ sugandhivyavahāriṇi \|\| 128 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 129 \[unit 129\]

granthikaṃ pippalīmūle granthikaṃ gugguludrume \|\|

mādreye ca karīre ca daivajñe ca prakīrttitam \|\| 129 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 130 \[unit 130\]

bāhliko deśabhede'śve bāhnikaṃ dhīrahiṅgunoḥ \|

anayorapi vāhīko yājñiko yājake kuśe \|\| 130 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 131 \[unit 131\]

vārṣikaṃ trāyamāṇe syādvarṣākālabhave'nyavat \|

khaṅgiko mahiṣīkṣīre phenasaunikayorapi \|\| 131 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 132 \[unit 132\]

āhnikaṃ dinanirvartye bhojane nityakarmaṇi \|

guṇḍako maline dhūlau kaloktisnehapātrayoḥ \|\| 132 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 133 \[unit 133\]

gaṇḍakaḥ khaḍgini khyātaḥ saṅkhyāvidyāprabhedayoḥ \|

avacchede'ntarāye ca gaṇḍakī saridantare \|\| 133 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 134 \[unit 134\]

taṇḍakaḥ khañjane phene samāsaprāyavāci ca \|

gṛhadārau taruskandhamāyābahulayorapi \|\| 134 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 135 \[unit 135\]

kaṇṭakaḥ kṣudraśatrau ca karmmasthānakadoṣayoḥ \|

romāñce ca drumāṅge ca kaṇṭako makare'pi ca \|\| 135 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 136 \[unit 136\]

takṣakaḥ phaṇibhede ca varddhakidrumabhedayoḥ \|

pakṣakaḥ pārśvamātre ca pakṣadvāre ca pakṣakaḥ \|\| 136 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 137 \[unit 137\]

śaṅkhakaṃ valaye kambau śiroroge ca śaṅkhakaḥ \|

nandako hariravaḍge syāddharṣake kulapālake \|\| 137 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 138 \[unit 138\]

śītakaḥ śītakāle ca susthite dīrghasūtriṇi \|

uṣṇakastu nidāghe syāddāture kṣiprakāriṇi \|\| 138 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 139 \[unit 139\]

ducchako gandhakuṭyāṃ syādvihārābhyavakāśayoḥ \|

dundukaḥ śoṇake krūre kṣullakaḥ svalpanīcayoḥ \|\| 139 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 140 \[unit 140\]

piṣṭako netraroge syāddhānyādicamase'pi ca \|

bhasmakaṃ rogabhede syādviḍa\[ha\]ṅgakaladhautayoḥ \|\| 140 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 141 \[unit 141\]

tumvakaḥ syād bahugurudhūrttāyaskāntakāmuke \|

jambukaḥ śvāpade nīce pratīcīdikpatāvapi \|\| 141 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 142 \[unit 142\]

kārmukaṃ vaṃśadhanuṣoḥ karmmaśakte tu vācyavat \|

nirmmoko mokṣake vyomni sannāhe sarpakañcuke \|\| 142 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 143 \[unit 143\]

ruḍukko vādyabhede syāddātyūhe madamattake \|

turuṣkaḥ sihla\[?\]ke mlecchajātau śrīvāsake kvacit \|\| 143 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 144 \[unit 144\]

ikṣvākuḥ kaṭutumbyāñca sūryyavaṃśanṛpe'pi ca \|

kuṇṭhākuśca durādharṣe duḥśīle ca bileśaye \|\| 144 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 145 \[unit 145\]

kuṣākurmarkaṭe bhānau parottāpini pāvake \|

pṛdākurvṛścike vyāghre citrake ca sarīsṛpe \|\| 145 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 146 \[unit 146\]

sṛdākuranile vajre dāvāgnau pratisūryyake \|

goraṅkuḥ pakṣijātau ca bhavennagnakabandinoḥ \|\| 146 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 147 \[unit 147\]

triśaṅkuḥ śalabhe rājaviśeṣe vṛṣadaṃśake \|

palyaṅko mañcaparyyaṅkavṛṣīparyyastikāsu ca \|\| 147 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 148 \[unit 148\]

karaṅko mastake śasyanārikelaphalāsthini \|

kalaṅko'ṅke'pavāde ca kālāyasamale'pi ca \|\| 148 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 149 \[unit 149\]

bhālāṅkaḥ karapatre syācchākabhede ca rohite \|

mahālakṣaṇasampannapuruṣe kacchape hare \|\| 149 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 150 \[unit 150\]

vṛṣāṅkaḥ śaṅkare sādhau bhallātakamahokṣayoḥ \|

mṛgāṅko mārute candre stabakaḥ śubhragucchayoḥ \|\| 150 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 151 \[unit 151\]

ātaṅko rogasantāpaśaṅkāsu murajadhvanau \|

pālaṅkaḥ śallakīśākabhedayoḥ prājipakṣiṇi \|\| 151 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 152 \[unit 152\]

alarko dhavalārke syādyogonmādini kukkure \|

udarka eṣyatkālīye phale madanakaṇṭake \|\| 152 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 153 \[unit 153\]

samparkaḥ sarate pṛktau vitarkaḥ saṃśayohayoḥ \|

ambikā pārvatīpāṇḍujananyorapi mātari \|\| 153 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 154 \[unit 154\]

andhikā kaitave miśre sarṣapyāmapi kīrtyate \|\|

amlikā tintriḍīkāmlodgāracāṅgerikāsu ca \|\| 154 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 155 \[unit 155\]

yūthikā'mlānake puṣpaviśeṣe'pi ca yūthikā \|

rājikāpi ca kedāre rājasarṣaparekhayoḥ \|\| 155 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 156 \[unit 156\]

rasikāpi rasālāyāṃ kāñcīrasanayorapi \|

ruṇḍikā dvārapiṇḍyāñca dūtikāyāṃ raṇakṣitau \|\| 156 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 157 \[unit 157\]

bhūmikā racanāyāṃ syānmūrtyantaraparigrahe \|

karṇikā karihastāgre karamadhyāṅgulāvapi \|\| 157 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 0158 \[unit 158\]

kramukādicchaṭāṃśe ca karṇikā karṇabhūṣaṇe \|

bījakośe sarojasya kuṭṭanyāmapi kutracit \|\| 0158 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 159 \[unit 159\]

karṇikā kathyate'tyantaṃ sūkṣmavastvagnimanthayoḥ \|

gaṇikā yūthikāveśyātarkārīkariṇīṣu ca \|\| 159 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 160 \[unit 160\]

gaṇako'pi ca daivajñe kārikānaṭayoṣiti \|

kṛtau vivaraṇaśloke yātanāyāñca kārikā \|\| 160 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 161 \[unit 161\]

nāpitādikaśilpe'pi jālikā jālinīphale \|

bhaṭānāmaśmaracitāṅgarakṣiṇyāśca jālikā \|\| 161 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 162 \[unit 162\]

girisāre jalaukāyāmapi syādvidhavāstriyām \|

jālikaḥ punaruddiṣṭo grāmakūṭe ca dhīvare \|\| 162 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 163 \[unit 163\]

nīlikā nīlinīkṣudrarogaśephālikāsvapi \|

kālikā yoginībhede kārṣṇye gauryyāṃ ghanāvalau \|\| 163 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 164 \[unit 164\]

kramadeye vastumūlye bhūsarīnavameghayoḥ \|

paṭolaśākhāromālīmāṃsīkākīṣu kālikā \|\| 164 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 165 \[unit 165\]

tathā vṛścikapatre'pi mālikā saridantare \|

graiveye puṣpamālāyāṃ pakṣimalle ca mālikā \|\| 165 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 166 \[unit 166\]

bāālikā vālukābālāpiccholākarṇabhūṣaṇe \|

tūlikā kathitā lekhyakūrccikātūlaśayyayoḥ \|\| 166 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 167 \[unit 167\]

cūlikā nāṭakasyā'ṅge karṇamūle ca dantinām \|

mallikā tṛṇaśūnye'pi mīnamṛtpātrabhedayoḥ \|\| 167 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 168 \[unit 168\]

malliko haṃsabhede syājjhillikā jhilikāpi ca \|

vilepane male jhillyāmātapasya ruci smṛtā \|\| 168 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 169 \[unit 169\]

vālukā sikatāsu syādvālukantvailavāluke \|

nagnikā tu kumāryyāṃ syādatha kṣapaṇava\[ba\]ndinoḥ \|\| 169 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 170 \[unit 170\]

nagnako dhenukādhenau strīkareṇvoḥ prasūtayoḥ \|

reṇukāpi hareṇau syājjāmadagnistriyāmapi \|\| 170 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 171 \[unit 171\]

jatukā jinapatrāyāṃ jatukaṃ hiṅgulākṣayoḥ \|

kūrcikā sūcikāyāñca tūlikāyāñca kuḍmale \|\| 171 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 172 \[unit 172\]

kavāṭakuṭṭake kṣīravikṛtāvapi kūrcikā \|

mātṛkā dhātrikāmātrormātrikaṃ karaṇe'mbare \|\| 172 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 173 \[unit 173\]

patākā vaijayantyāñca saubhāgye'ṅke dhvaje'pi ca \|

syāt putrikā puttalikā duhitroryāvatūlike \|\| 173 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 174 \[unit 174\]

putrakaḥ śarabhe dhūrte śailavṛkṣaprabhedayoḥ \|

uṣṭrikā mṛttikābhāṇḍabhede'pi karabhastriyām \|\| 174 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 175 \[unit 175\]

ḍimbikā jalanimbe syānmoṇake kāmukastriyām \|

kāmikā nīlikāyāñca syamīko nākuvṛkṣayoḥ \|\| 175 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 176 \[unit 176\]

dūṣikā tūlikāyāñca dūṣikā netrayormale \|

ūrmmikā cāṅgulīye syādvastrabhaṅgataraṅgayoḥ \|\| 176 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 177 \[unit 177\]

tathodbāāhulake'pi syādūrmmikā madhupadhvanau \|

syācchalākāpi madanaśārikāśalyayoḥ śalā \|\| 177 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 178 \[unit 178\]

chatrapañjarakāṣṭhyāñca śallakī paśuvṛkṣayoḥ \|

narttakī\[ \]lāsikāyāñca kareṇvāmapi narttakī \|\| 178 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 180 \[unit 179\]

narttakaḥ kelake poṭagalacāraṇayornaṭe \|\|

culukī śiśumāre'pi kuṇḍībhede kulāntake \|\| 180 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / fragment 180

catuṣkī maśake haryyāṃ catuṣkī yaṣṭikāntare \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 181 \[unit 181\]

alimakaḥ pike bheke madhuke padmakeśare \|\| 181 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 182 \[unit 182\]

bhavedalipako bhṛṅge kokile rathahiṇḍake \|

smṛtaḥ kuravakaḥ śoṇāmlānajhiṇṭīprabhedayoḥ \|\| 182 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 183 \[unit 183\]

bhavenmaruvakaḥ puṣpaviśeṣe madanadrume \|

balāhako girau meghe daityanāgaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 183 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 184 \[unit 184\]

bhramarako jale bhṛṅge girije cūrṇakuntale \|

varāṭakaḥ sthūlarajjau padmabīje kaparddake \|\| 184 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 185 \[unit 185\]

śṛṅgāṭakaṃ bhavedvārikaṇṭake ca catuṣpathe \|

gokaṇṭako gokṣurake sthapuṭe ca gavāṃ khure \|\| 185 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 186 \[unit 186\]

ākalpakastamomohagranthīṣūtkalikāmudoḥ \|

ākṣepako'nilavyādhau vyādhe nindākare'pi ca \|\| 186 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 187 \[unit 187\]

pracalākaḥ śarāghāte dandaśūke bhujaṅgame \|

vṛndārakaḥ sure śreṣṭhe manojñe punaranyavat \|\| 187 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 188 \[unit 188\]

niyāmakaḥ karṇadhāre potavāhe niyantari \|

vināyakastu herambe tārkṣye vijñe jine gurau \|\| 188 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 189 \[unit 189\]

niścārakaḥ purīṣasya kṣaye svaire samīraṇe \|

bhaṭṭārakaḥ sure rājñi kathitaśca tapodhane \|\| 189 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 190 \[unit 190\]

dāserakaḥ syāt karabhe dāsīputre ca dhīvare \|

aṅgārakaṃ kuje vidyādulmukāṃśe kuruṇṭake \|\| 190 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 191 \[unit 191\]

aṅgārikekṣukāṇḍe syāt korake kiṃśukasya ca \|

dalāḍhakastu kunde syāt karikarṇaśirīṣayoḥ \|\| 191 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 192 \[unit 192\]

svayañjātatile phene khātake nāgakeśare \|

vātyāyāṃ praśnikāyāñca gairike ca mahattare \|\| 192 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 193 \[unit 193\]

bhavet pūrṇānakaṃ pūrṇapātre paṭahapātrayoḥ \|

prakīrṇakaṃ granthabhede cāmare vistare haye \|\| 193 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 194 \[unit 194\]

smṛtaḥ puṣkalako gandhamṛge kṣapaṇakīlayoḥ \|

kośātakaḥ kace jyautsnipaṭolīghoṣakeṣu tu \|\| 194 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 195 \[unit 195\]

kośātakī vāṇijake vāḍavāgnau vaṇijyapi \|

nirgranthakaḥ syāt kṣapaṇe niṣphale'pyaparicchade \|\| 195 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 196 \[unit 196\]

syādaśmantakamuddhāne mālukācchadane'pi ca \|

kaṭillakastu parṇāse varṣābhūkāravellayoḥ \|\| 196 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 197 \[unit 197\]

kapardako varāṭe syājjaṭājūṭe'pi dhūrjjaṭeḥ \|

gomedakaṃ pītamaṇau kākole patrake'pi ca \|\| 197 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 198 \[unit 198\]

maṇḍodakaścitrarāge bhavedātaparṇe'pi ca \|

śatānīko munerbhede vṛddhe cātakharālikaḥ \|\| 198 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 199 \[unit 199\]

grāmaṇīrbhāṇḍabhārāvopadhāneṣu prayujyate \|

lekhaniko lekhahāre yaḥ svahastaṃ vilekhayet \|\| 199 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 200 \[unit 200\]

lekheṣu parahastena tatrāpyeṣa pradarśitaḥ \|

bhavenmaṇḍalakaṃ vi\[bi\]mbe kuṣṭhabhede ca darpaṇe \|\| 200 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 201 \[unit 201\]

pippalakaṃ stanavṛnte mataṃ sīvanasūtrake \|

piṇḍītakaḥ syāttagare madanadrau phaṇijyake \|\| 201 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 202 \[unit 202\]

trivarṇakaṃ gokṣurake triphalāyāṃ kaṭutrike \|

śālāvṛkaḥ śṛgāle'pi sārameye balīmukhe \|\| 202 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 203 \[unit 203\]

kauleyakaḥ kulaśreṣṭhe syādindramahakāmuke \|

supratīkaḥ śobhanāṅge bhavedīśānadiggaje \|\| 203 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 204 \[unit 204\]

jaivātṛkaḥ kṛśe candre bhaiṣajyāyuṣmatorapi \|

ratarddhikaṃ syāddivase sukhasnāne'ṣṭamaṅgale \|\| 204 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 205 \[unit 205\]

vaināśikaḥ syāt kṣaṇike paratantorṇanābhayoḥ \|

vaidehako vāṇijake śūdrādvaiśyāsute'pi ca \|\| 205 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 206 \[unit 206\]

vitunnakantu dhanyāke tathā jhāṇṭāmalauṣadhau \|

gokuṇikaḥ kekare syāt paṅkasthe gavyupekṣake \|\| 206 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 207 \[unit 207\]

bhāryāṭiko bhavedbhāryā\[ṃ\] nirjite hariṇāntare \|

kāpaṭiko'nyamarmajñe chātrasyākādaśāsini \|\| 207 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 208 \[unit 208\]

kaukkuṭiko dāmbhike syādadūrapreritekṣaṇe \|

lālāṭikaḥ prabhorbhāvadarśinyāśleṣaṇāntare \|\| 208 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 209 \[unit 209\]

kāryākṣame'pyākalitaḥ śilāṭakaḥ śilāṭṭayoḥ \|

karkoṭakaḥ syānmālūrakādraveyaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 209 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 210 \[unit 210\]

varaṇṭakastu mātaṅgavaidyāṃ yauvanakaṇṭake \|

saṃvarttule'pyākhanikaścaure kroḍe ca mūṣake \|\| 210 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 211 \[unit 211\]

vaitāliko bodhakare kheṭitāle'pyudīritaḥ \|

tiktaśākastu khadire varuṇe patrasundare \|\| 211 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 212 \[unit 212\]

mayūrako'pyapāmārge tucchake tu mayūrakam \|

viśeṣakaḥ syāttilake viśeṣāvāhake'pi ca \|\| 212 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 213 \[unit 213\]

vidūṣakaścāṭupaṭau paranindākare'pi ca \|

bhavet saikatikaṃ mātṛyātrāmaṅgalasūtrayoḥ \|\| 213 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 214 \[unit 214\]

tathā sanyastasandehajīvakṣepaṇakeṣvapi \|

eḍamūkaḥ smṛto\[ \]dhīraiḥ śaṭhe vākśrutivarjjite \|\| 214 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 215 \[unit 215\]

varttarūko nadībhede kākanīle jalāvaṭe \|

kākarūka ulūke syānniḥsve strījitadambhayoḥ \|\| 215 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 216 \[unit 216\]

digambare bhīruke ca dandaśūko'hirakṣasoḥ \|

muṣṭeruko vadānye'pi miṣṭāśinyatithidviṣi \|\| 216 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 217 \[unit 217\]

kṛkavākurmayūre'pi saraṭe caraṇāyudhe \|

rādharaṅkastu nāsīre śīkare ca ghanopale \|\| 217 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 218 \[unit 218\]

kalaviṅkaḥ smṛto grāmacaṭake'pi kuliṅgake \|

saugandhikantu kahlāre padmarāge ca kattṛṇe \|\| 218 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 219 \[unit 219\]

gandhopale gāndhike'pi saugandhika iti dhvaniḥ \|

somavakla\[lka\]stu dhavalakhadire khaṭphale'pi ca \|\| 219 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 220 \[unit 220\]

bhayānakaḥ smṛto vyāghre saiṃhikeye'pi bhīṣaṇe \|

puṇḍarīkaṃ sitāmbhoje sitacchatre ca bheṣaje \|\| 220 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 221 \[unit 221\]

kośakārāntare vyāghre puṇḍarīko'gnidiggaje \|

jarjjarīkaṃ bahucchidre jarjjarīko jarāture \|\| 221 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 222 \[unit 222\]

carccarīko mahākāle keśavinyāsaśākayoḥ \|

tarttarīkaṃ vahitre syāttarttarīkaśca pārage \|\| 222 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 223 \[unit 223\]

pharpharīkaśca peṭe syāt pharpharīkantu mārddave \|

bhavedutkalikotkaṇṭhāhelāsalilavīciṣu \|\| 223 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 224 \[unit 224\]

śṛgālikā śivāyāṃ syāt trāsādapi palāyane \|

caṇḍālikā kinnarāyāmumāyāmoṣadhībhidi \|\| 224 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 225 \[unit 225\]

pāñcālikā bhavedvastraputrikāgītabhedayoḥ \|

kanīnikā tārake'kṣṇaḥ syāt kaniṣṭhāṅgulāvapi \|\| 225 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 226 \[unit 226\]

bhaved guṇanikā nṛtye śūnyāṅke pāṭhaniścitau \|

santānikā'pi kṣīrādisāre markkaṭajālake \|\| 226 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 227 \[unit 227\]

sandaṃśikā'pi sucuṭīlohayantraviśeṣayoḥ \|

saṅghāṭikā tu yugme syāt kuṭṭanīghrāṇayorapi \|\| 227 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 228 \[unit 228\]

jīvantikā'pi jīvākhyaśākavandīguḍūciṣu \|

vidyād bṛhantikā'bhikhyāmūrubastraprabhedayoḥ \|\| 228 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 229 \[unit 229\]

vakerukā balākābhidvātavellitaśākhayoḥ \|

smṛtā ghargharikā kṣudraghaṇṭikābhraṣṭadhānyayoḥ \|\| 229 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / fragment 230

vādyaprabhedavāditre daṇḍayorāpagāntare \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 230 \[unit 231\]

syādā'cchuritakaṃ hāsanakhāghātaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 230 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 231 \[unit 232\]

pratisūryyakamicchanti kṛkalāsopasūryyayoḥ \|

aneḍamūkamicchanti vākyaśrutivivarjjite \|\| 231 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 232 \[unit 233\]

rājapaṭasya kācasya guḍake kācavarttikaḥ \|

gojāgarikamityāhurmaṅgale kaṇṭakārake \|\| 232 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 233 \[unit 234\]

syātkṛmikaṇṭakañcitrāviḍa\[ha\]ṅgodumbareṣu ca \|

suvasantaka ityeṣa vāsantyāṃ madanotsave \|\| 233 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 234 \[unit 235\]

kakṣāvekṣakamicchanti śuddhāntodyānapālayoḥ \|

dvārapāle kavau ṣiṅge raṅgājīvinyapi kvacit \|\| 234 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 235 \[unit 236\]

nāgacārika uddiṣṭo rājakuñjarahastipe \|

gaṇastharāje garuḍe citramekhalake kvacit \|\| 235 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 236 \[unit 237\]

syādvrīhirājikaḥ kāmalikāyāṃ cīnadhānyake \|

śītacampakaśabdo'yaṃ syādātarpaṇadīpayoḥ \|\| 236 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 237 \[unit 238\]

andhau jalakaraṅkaḥ syānnālikeraphale'mbude \|

śaṅkhe jalalatāyāñca kathyate jalavāpikā \|\| 237 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 238 \[unit 239\]

kākacīrādiśapharamatsyayoḥ kaṭakhādakaḥ \|

khādake kācakalaśe balipuṣṭe ca jambuke \|\| 238 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 239 \[unit 240\]

syāccilanīlikā kaṇṭhībhede khadyotavidrutoḥ \|

syānnavaphalikā navye navajātarajaḥstriyām \|\| 239 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 240 \[unit 241\]

syācchataparvikādūrvāvacayorupakārikā \|

viṣṭabhede gṛhe rājñāmupakartryāmapīṣyate \|\| 240 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 241 \[unit 242\]

syādvyavahārikā\[ \]lokayātrā sammārjjanīṅgude \|

syāddhemapuṣpikā yūthyāṃ campake hemapuṣpakaḥ \|\| 241 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam / 242 \[unit 243\]

lūtāmarkkaṭakaḥ putryāṃ navamālyāṃ plavaṅgame \|

sindūratilako nāge sindūratilakā striyām \|\| 242 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam / 243 \[unit 244\]

varṇaviloḍakaḥ śloke chāyāhāriṇi kumbhile \|

mātulaputrako dhūrttaphale syānmātulātmaje \|\| 243 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam / 244 \[unit 245\]

grāmamadgurikā śṛṅgyāṃ grāmayuddhe ca kīrttyate \|

syānmadanaśalākā'pi śāryyā kāmodayoṣadhau \|\| 244 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam / fragment 246

iti kāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khaikakam / 1 \[unit 247\]

khamindriye sukhe svarge śūnye bindau vihāyasi \|

pure saṃvedane kṣetre kulāhalaphale kvacit \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 2 \[unit 248\]

nakhaḥ kararuhe śastre gandhadravye nakhaṃ nakhī \|

mukhaṃ niḥsaraṇe vaktre prārambhopāyayorapi \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 3 \[unit 249\]

sukhaṃ śarmmaṇi nāke ca sukhā puryāṃ pracetasaḥ \|

śaṅkho nidhyantare kambulalāṭāsthinakhīṣu ca \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 4 \[unit 250\]

rekhā syādalpake chadminyāmbhogollekhayorapi \|

lekho lekhye sure lekhā lipirājikayormatā \|

mukhaḥ samyagmanojñe'pi sāmnaḥ ṣaṭpraṇayeṣvapi \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 5 \[unit 251\]

śikhā śiphāyāṃ cūḍāyāṃ jvālāyāmagramātrake \|

lāṅgalyāścāpi śākhāyāṃ cūḍāyāñca śikhaṇḍinaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 6 \[unit 252\]

sakhā mitre sahāye ca śākhā\[ \]pakṣāntare bhuje \|

śākhā vedavibhāge ca pādapāṅge'ntike'pi ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 7 \[unit 253\]

preṅkhā paryaṭane nṛtye bhavedaśvagatāvapi \|\|

khaṅkhā gativiśeṣe'pi śūkaśimbyāśca narttane \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 8 \[unit 254\]

triśikhaṃ śekhare vidyā strī\[tstrī\]lekhā\[?\]rākṣasāntare \|

sumukhaṃ tārkṣyatanaye phaṇibhede ca paṇḍite \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 9 \[unit 255\]

durmukho nāgarāje'pi mukhare vānarāśvayoḥ \|

gomukhaṃ kuṭilāgāre vādyabhāṇḍe ca lepake \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 10 \[unit 256\]

pramukhaḥ prathame śreṣṭhe viśikhastomare śare \|

rathyāṃ khanitryāṃ viśikhā nalikāyāmapi kacit \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 11 \[unit 257\]

viśākhastārake skande viśākharṣau kaṭillake \|

mayūkhastviṭkarajvāle vaiśākho rādhamanthayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 12 \[unit 258\]

agnimukho dvije deve bhallāte citrake kvacit \|

pañcanakho gaje kūrme śilīmukho'libāṇayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 13 \[unit 259\]

bhavedvyāghranakhaḥ kandagandhadravyaviśeṣayoḥ \|

mahāśaṅkho narāsthni syānnidhisaṅkhyāprabhedayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 14 \[unit 260\]

indulekhā'mṛtāsomalatāśaśikalāsu ca \|

śaśilekhā kalābhāge bākucīvṛttabhedayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 15 \[unit 261\]

agniśikhā lāṅgalikyāṃ kuṅkume'gniśikhaṃ matam \|

baddhaśikhā jaṭāyāṃ syād bāle baddhaśikho mataḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khapañcakam / 16 \[unit 262\]

syānmalinamukhaḥ prete golāṅgūle khale'nale \|

atha śītamayūkho'pi śaśāṅkaghanasārayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khapañcakam / 17 \[unit 263\]

sarvatomukhamākhyātamantarikṣe ca pāthasi \|

vidhikṣetrajñarudreṣu kathitaḥ sarvatomukhaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / khāntavargaḥ / khapañcakam / fragment 264

iti khāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gaikakam / 1 \[unit 265\]

gauḥ svarge vṛṣabhe raśmau vajre candramasi smṛtaḥ \|

arjjune netradigbāṇabhūvāgvāriṣu gaurmatā \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 2 \[unit 266\]

agaḥ śaile drume bhānau nagavatpavanāsane \|

khagaḥ sūrye grahe deve mārgaṇe ca vihaṅgame \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 3 \[unit 267\]

bhagamaiśvarthyamāhātmyajñānavairāgyayoniṣu \|

yaśovīryyaprayatnecchāśrīdharmmaravimuktiṣu \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 4 \[unit 268\]

yugaṃ hastacatuṣke'pi rathasīrāṅgayoryugaḥ \|

yugaṃ kṛtādau yugale vṛddhināmauṣadhe'pi ca \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 5 \[unit 269\]

mṛgaḥ paśau kuraṅge ca karinakṣatrabhedayoḥ \|

yācñāyāṃ mṛgayāyāñca mṛgī syādvanitāntare \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 6 \[unit 270\]

bhṛguḥ śukre prapāte ca jamadagnau pinākini \|

bhāge rūpārddhake prokto bhāgadheyaikadeśayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 7 \[unit 271\]

nāgantu sīsake raṅge strībandhe karaṇāntare \|

nāgaḥ pannagamātaṅgakrūracāriṣu toyade \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 8 \[unit 272\]

nāgakeśarapunnāganāgadantakamustake \|

dehānilaprabhede'tha śreṣṭhe syāduttarasthitaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 9 \[unit 273\]

rāgo'nuraktau mātsaryye kleśādau lohitādiṣu \|

gāndhārādau nṛpe rāgastyāgo varjjanadānayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 10 \[unit 274\]

bhogaḥ sukhe dhane cāheḥ śarīraphaṇayormataḥ \|

pālane'bhyavahāre ca nirveśe paṇyayoṣitām \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 11 \[unit 275\]

yogo'pūrvārthasamprāptau saṅgatidhyānayuktiṣu \|

vapusthairyye prayoge ca viṣkambhādiṣu bheṣaje \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 12 \[unit 276\]

viśrabdhaghātini dravyopāyasaṃnahaneṣvapi \|

kārmmaṇe'pi ca yogaḥ syādrogaḥ kuṣṭhauṣadhe gade \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 13 \[unit 277\]

vego jave pravāhe ca mahākālaphale'pi ca \|

pūgastu kramuke vṛnde caṅgaḥ śobhanadakṣayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 14 \[unit 278\]

aṅgaṃ gātrāntikopāyapratīkeṣvapradhānake \|

aṅgā deśaviśeṣe syuraṅga samvodhane'vyayam \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 15 \[unit 279\]

iṅgaḥ syādadbhute jñāne jaṅgameṅgitayorapi \|

ṭaṅgaḥ kṛpāṇabhede syājjaṅghāyāñca khanitrake \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 16 \[unit 280\]

tuṅgaḥ punnāganagayostuṅgaḥ syādunnate'nyavat \|

varvarāniśayostuṅgī taṅgaṃ sīsakaraṅgayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 17 \[unit 281\]

vaṅgaḥ kārpāsavārttākadeśabhedeṣu bhāṣitaḥ \|

laṅgaḥ saṅge ca ṣiṅge ca vyaṅgo hīnāṅgabhekayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 18 \[unit 282\]

bhaṅgastaraṅge rugbhede bhede jayaviparyaye \|

bhaṅgā śaṇākhyasasye'pi bhṛṅgo dhūmyāṭaṣiṅgayo \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 19 \[unit 283\]

madhuvrate'pi bhṛṅgantu keśarājaguḍatvacoḥ \|

raṅgo raṇe khale rāge nṛtye raṅgaṃ trapunyapi \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 20 \[unit 284\]

liḍgaṃ cihne'numāne'pi sāṅkhyoktaprakṛtāvapi \|

śivamūrtiviśeṣe'pi mehane ca pracakṣate \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 21 \[unit 285\]

śṛṅgaṃ prabhutve śikhare cihne krīḍāmbuyantrake \|

viṣāṇotkarṣayoścāya śaṅgaḥ syātkūrccaśīrṣake \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 22 \[unit 286\]

śṛṅgī viṣāyāmṛṣabhe svarṇamīnaviśeṣayoḥ \|

gāṅgastu gaṅgāsambhūte bhīṣme śaktidhare'pi ca \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 23 \[unit 287\]

piṅgaḥ piśaṅge piṅgī tu śamyāṃ piṅgantu bālake \|

rāmaṭe nāḍikāyāñca piṅgā gorocanomayoḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 24 \[unit 288\]

khaḍgo gaṇḍakaśṛṇaḥgāsibuddhabhedeṣu gaṇḍake \|

durgaḥ syād durgame durgā gaurīnīlakayorapi \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 25 \[unit 289\]

sargastu niścayādhyāyamohotsāhātmasṛṣṭiṣu \|

mārgo mṛgamade māsaprabhede'nveṣaṇādhvanoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 26 \[unit 290\]

śārṅgaṃ kārmmukamātre'pi viṣṇorapi śarāsane \|

valgu chāge manojñe ca valgubhāṣitamanyavat \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 27 \[unit 291\]

phalguḥ prokto male sāre niḥsāre phalgu vācyavat \|

śuṅgī vaṭāmrātakayoḥ parkaṭyāmapi viśrutā \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 28 \[unit 292\]

pannagaḥ padmakāṣṭhe syāt pannago'pi bhujaṅgame \|

plavago vānare bheke sārathau coṣṇadīdhiteḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 29 \[unit 293\]

jihmago mandage sarpe vihagaścāgraje khage \|

sarvagaṃ salile khyātaṃ sarvagaḥ śaṅkare vibhau \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 30 \[unit 294\]

ābhogo vāruṇacchatre pūrṇatāyatnayorapi \|

āyogo vyāpṛtau rodhagandhamālyopahārayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 31 \[unit 295\]

ayogo vidhure kūṭe viśleṣe kaṭhinodyame \|

āśugo mārute bāṇe'pyudvegaṃ kramukīphale \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 32 \[unit 296\]

udvego'pyudvāhulakodvejanākodgame'pi ca \|

sambhogaḥ surate bhoge sambhogo jinaśāsane \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 33 \[unit 297\]

parāgaḥ puṣparajasi dhūlīsnānīyayorapi \|

giriprabhede vikhyātāvuparāga ca candane \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 34 \[unit 298\]

prayāgastīrthabhede syādyajñe śatamakhāśvayoḥ \|

prayogaḥ kārmaṇe proktaḥ prayuktau ca nidarśane \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 35 \[unit 299\]

vātigaḥ kathito bhaṇṭyāṃ dhātuvādini vātigaḥ \|

taḍāgastu jalādhāraviśeṣe kaṭake'pi ca \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 36 \[unit 300\]

punnāgaḥ puruṣaśreṣṭhe pāṇḍunāge sitotpale \|

jātīphale ca punnāgo nisargaḥ sargaśīlayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 37 \[unit 301\]

visargastyāgavaddāne visargo malanirgame \|

visarjjanīye'pyayanaprabhede'pi vibhāvasoḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 38 \[unit 302\]

utsargo varjjane tyāge sāmānyanyāyadānayoḥ \|

trivargo dharmakāmārthe triphalāyāṃ kaṭutrike \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 39 \[unit 303\]

vṛddhisthānakṣaye sattvarajastamasi ceṣyate \|

tātaguḥ kṣudratāte syājjanayitrīhite'pi ca \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 40 \[unit 304\]

anaṅgo madane'naṅgamākāśamanasoḥ smṛtam \|

mṛdaṅgaḥ paṭahe ghoṣe bhujaṅgaḥ ṣiṅgasarpayoḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 41 \[unit 305\]

pataṅgaḥ śalabhe śāliprabhede vihage ravau \|

pataṅgaṃ pārade kvāpi naraṅgantu varaṇḍake \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 42 \[unit 306\]

śephe naraṅgamapyāhu niṣaṅgaḥ saṅgatūṇayoḥ \|

viḍaṅgaṃ kṛmighne khyātaṃ viḍaṅgo nāgare'nyavat \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 43 \[unit 307\]

kaliṅgaḥ pūtikaraje dhūmyāṭe nīvṛdantare \|

kaliṅgaṃ kauṭajaphale kaliṅgā yoṣiti smṛtā \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 44 \[unit 308\]

apāṅgamaṅgahīne syānnetrānte tilake'pi ca \|

varāṅgaṃ yonimātaṅgamastakeṣu guḍatvaci \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 45 \[unit 309\]

patrāṅgaṃ padmake bhūrjje patrāṅgaṃ raktacandane \|

rathāṅgaścakravāke'pi rathāṅgañcakra iṣyate \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 46 \[unit 310\]

raktāṅgaṃ vidrume bhaume vidyāt kampilladhīrayoḥ \|

raktāṅgaścāpi jīvantyāṃ mātaṅgaḥ śvapace gaje \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 47 \[unit 311\]

kāliṅgo bhūmikarkārau dantāvalabhujaṅgayoḥ \|

kāliṅgī rājakarkaṭyāṃ dhāraṅgaḥ khaḍgatīrthayoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 48 \[unit 312\]

hemāṅgo druhiṇe tārkṣye nāraṅgaṃ pippalīrase \|

nāgaraṅge viṭe jantau yamaje'pi prakīrttitam \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 49 \[unit 313\]

sāraṅgaścātake bhṛṅge kuraṅge ca mataṅgaje \|\|

pakṣabhede ca sāraṅgaḥ sāraṅgaḥ śabale'nyavat \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 50 \[unit 314\]

priyaṅguḥ phalinīkaṅgupippalīrājikāsu ca \|

nīlāṅguḥ kṛmibhede syādbhramarālīmusārayoḥ \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 51 \[unit 315\]

turaṅgī cāśvagandhāyāṃ turaṅgaścittavājinoḥ \|

cakrāṅgī kaṭurohiṇyāṃ cakrāṅgo mānasaukasi \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 52 \[unit 316\]

īhāmṛgo vṛke janto prabhede rūpakasya ca \|

mallanāgo'bhramātaṅge vātsyāyanamunāvapi \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 53 \[unit 317\]

uparāgaḥ parīvāde rāhugrastārkkacandrayoḥ \|

durnaye grahakallole'pavargastyāgamokṣayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 54 \[unit 318\]

kriyāvasānasāphalye'pyapavargaḥ prayujyate \|

upasargaḥ smṛto rogabhedopaplavayorapi \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 319\]

samāyogastu saṃyoge samavāye prayojane \|

samprayogaṃ rate vidyādanvite kārmmaṇe'pi ca \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 320\]

dīrghādhvagaḥ śaṅkhalake lekhahāre tu bhedyavat \|

abhiṣaṅgastu śapathe syādākrośe parābhave \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 57 \[unit 321\]

chatrabhaṅgo'pi vaidhavye svātantryanṛpanāśayoḥ \|

kaṭabhaṅgastu sasyānāṃ hastacchede nṛpātyaye \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gapañcakam / 58 \[unit 322\]

kathāprasaṅgo vātūle viṣavaidye ca vācyavat \|

nāḍītaraṅgaḥ kākole hiṇḍake ratahiṇḍake \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / gāntavargaḥ / gapañcakam / fragment 323

iti gāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghaikakam / fragment 324

gho gharghare ca ghaṇṭāyāṃ kāñcikāghātayorapi \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 1 \[unit 325\]

aghaṃ tu vyasane proktamaghaṃ pātakaduḥkhayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 2 \[unit 326\]

arghaḥ pūjāvidhau mūlye'pyaṅgipādadrumūlayoḥ \|

ogho vṛnde payovege drutanṛtyopadeśayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 3 \[unit 327\]

oghaḥ paramparāyāñca meghaḥ syānmustake ghane \|

laghurmanojñaniḥsārāguruṣu prathito laghu \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 4 \[unit 328\]

kṛṣṇāguruṇi śīghre ca spṛkkāyāñca smṛtā laghuḥ \|

udghaḥ syātpāvake hastapuṭake dehajānile \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 5 \[unit 329\]

moghā'pi pāṭalāyāṃ syānmogho niṣphalahīnayoḥ \|

maghā māghī ca nakṣatre bheṣaje ca yathākramam \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / fragment 330

ślāghā matā praśaṃsāyāṃ paricaryyābhilāṣayoḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 6 \[unit 331\]

anagho nirmmalāpāpamanojñeṣvabhidheyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 7 \[unit 332\]

palighaḥ kācakalaśaghaṭaprākāragopure \|

parigho yogabhede syānmudgare'rgalaghātayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 8 \[unit 333\]

pratighaḥ pratighāte ca roṣe ca pratigho mataḥ \|

kācighaḥ kāñcane prokto'nnamaṇḍe mūṣake'pi ca \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 9 \[unit 334\]

mahārghastu mahāmūlye tathā lāvakapakṣiṇi \|

nidāgho grīṣmakāle syāduṣṇasvedāmbunorapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 10 \[unit 335\]

ullāghastu śucau hṛṣṭe dakṣanīrogayorapi \|

sarvaugho guruvegārthasarvasaṃhananārthayoḥ \|

amoghaḥ saphale'moghā viḍaṅgābhayayoḥ smṛtā \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / fragment 336

iti ghāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 1 \[unit 337\]

kacaḥ keśe guroḥ putre bandhe śuṣkavraṇe kacaḥ \|

kacā kariṇyāṃ kācastu manau śikye mṛdantare \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 2 \[unit 338\]

netraroge'pi nīcastu vāmane pāmare smṛtaḥ \|

mocaḥ śobhāñjane prokto mocā śālmalirambhayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 3 \[unit 339\]

picustūle ca karṣe ca kuṣṭharoge'surāntare \|

bhairavasya prabhede'pi picuḥ kvā'pi prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 4 \[unit 340\]

kūrcco vikatthane madhye bhuvo śmaśruṇi kaitave \|

krauñcaḥ khaganagadvīpaprabhedeṣu prayujyate \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 5 \[unit 341\]

cañco nalādinirmāṇe cañcā tu tṛṇapūruṣe \|

cañcuḥ pañcāṅgule troṭyāṃ gonāḍīke'pi paṭhyate \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 6 \[unit 342\]

śuciḥ śuddhe'nupahate śṛṅgārāṣāḍhayoḥ site \|

grīṣme hutavahe'pi syādupadhāśuddhamantriṇi \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 7 \[unit 343\]

rucirmayūkhe śobhāyāmabhiṣvaṅgābhilāṣayoḥ \|

vīciḥ svalpe taraṅge syādavakāśe sukhe'pi ca \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 8 \[unit 344\]

sūcī karādyabhinaye nārīṇāṃ karaṇāntare \|

sūcī ca sīvanadravye śacī'ndrāṇyāṃ śatāvarau \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 9 \[unit 345\]

kāñcī syānmekhalādāmni guñjāyāṃ nīvṛdantare \|

vacā syādugragandhāyāṃ sārikāyāṃ śuke vacaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 10 \[unit 346\]

arcā pūjāpratimayoścarccā sthāsakacintayoḥ \|

carccikāyāñca rukśobhākiraṇecchāsu ceṣyate \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 11 \[unit 347\]

tvak carmavalkayostvāce nyaṅ nīce nimnakārṇyayoḥ \|

vāg vacane sarasvatyāṃ bhāṣāgīrbhāratī yathā \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / catrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 12 \[unit 348\]

kavaco garddabhāṇḍe syāt sannāhe paṭahe'pi ca \|

vikacaḥ kṣapaṇe ketugrahe ca sphuṭite'nyavat \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 13 \[unit 349\]

krakacaḥ karapatre syādgranthilākhyadrume'pi ca \|

saṅkocamapi kāśmīre saṅkoco mīnabandhayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 14 \[unit 350\]

nārāco lohabāṇe syānnārāco jalahastini \|

nārācyeṣaṇikāyāñca samyak sammatahṛdyayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 15 \[unit 351\]

prapañcaḥ sañcaye prokto vistare ca pratāraṇe \|

marīciḥ kṛpaṇe dīptāvṛṣibhede'pi dṛśyate \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 16 \[unit 352\]

mārīco rakṣaso bhede kakkole yājake dvije \|

mārīcī devatābhede namuciḥ smaradaityayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 17 \[unit 353\]

kanīciḥ puṣpitalatāguñjayoḥ śakaṭe'pi ca \|

avīcirnarakānūrmyorvipañcī kelivīṇayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 18 \[unit 354\]

prāgudakpratyagityete digdeśe kālato'vyayam \|

prācyodīcyapratīcyānāṃ vācakaṃ syādanavyayam \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / cacatuṣkam / 19 \[unit 355\]

malimluco'nile caure jalasūcirjalaukasi \|

śṛṅgāṭe śiśumāre ca kaṅke śeṭijhaṣe'pi ca \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / capañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / capañcakam / 20 \[unit 356\]

ratanārīca ityeṣa kathito ratavallabhe \|

kukkuraṃ ratanārīcaḥ sītkāre varayoṣitām \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / cāntavargaḥ / capañcakam / fragment 357

iti cāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 1 \[unit 358\]

acchaḥ sphaṭikabhallūkanirmaleṣvacchamavyayam \|

ābhimukhye'tha kacchaḥ syādanūpe tunnakadrume \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 2 \[unit 359\]

naukānte paridhānasya paścādañcalapallave \|\|

kacchā tu cīrikāyāñca vārāhyāñca nigadyate \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 3 \[unit 360\]

syād gucchaḥ stabake stambe hārabhedakalāpayoḥ \|

pucchaḥ pāścātyabhāge syāllāṅgūle pucchamiṣyate \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 4 \[unit 361\]

mlecchaḥ pāpatare jātibhede syādapabhāṣiṇi \|

picchā tu śālmalīveṣṭe maṇḍe cāśvapadāmaye \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / fragment 362

paṅktau pūgacchaṭākośamocānijayineṣu ca \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chacatuṣkam / 5 \[unit 363\]

picchapucche mahākacchaḥ samudre ca pracetasi \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / chāntavargaḥ / chacatuṣkam / fragment 364

iti chāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jaikakam / 1 \[unit 365\]

jo janyo jātamātre ca mṛtyuñjaye janārddane \|

jūrākāśe sarasvatyāṃ piśalyā javane'pi ca \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 2 \[unit 366\]

kañjaḥ keśe viriñcau ca kañjaḥ pīyūṣapadmayoḥ \|

ajo harau hare kāme vidhau chāge raghoḥ sute \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 3 \[unit 367\]

vrajo goṣṭhādhvavṛndeṣu nijamātmīyanityayoḥ

dhvajaṃ cihne patākāyāṃ dhvajaḥ śauṇḍikaśephayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 4 \[unit 368\]

khaḍvāṅge ca svajaṃ rakte svajaḥ prasvedaputrayoḥ \|

dvijo vipre'ṇḍaje dante bhārgīreṇukayordvijā \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 5 \[unit 369\]

bījaṃ retasi tattve ca hetāvaṅkurakāraṇe \|

ādhāne'pi ca sajjaḥ syāt sannaddhe sambhūte'pi ca \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 6 \[unit 370\]

vājaṃ ghṛte'pi yajñānne vājo niḥsvanapakṣayoḥ \|

vājaṃ jale munau vājo vyājaḥ śāṭhyāpadeśayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 7 \[unit 371\]

bhujaḥ pāṇau ca bāhau ca kujaḥ syānnarakārayoḥ \|\|

abjo dhanvantarau candre nicule śaṅkhapadmayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 8 \[unit 372\]

abjaṃ syādatha kubjo'pi nyubjaḥ syāt pādapāntare \|

nyubjastu karṇaroge syānnyubjaḥ proktaḥ kuśasruci \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 9 \[unit 373\]

adhomukhe ca kubje ca nyubjo vācyavadiṣyate \|

ājiḥ samāvanau yuddhe rājiḥ syāt paṅktirephayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 10 \[unit 374\]

lājāḥ syurbhṛṣṭabhānyeṣu lājaḥ syādārdrataṇḍule \|

uśīre lājamuddiṣṭaṃ prajāsantānalokayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 11 \[unit 375\]

guñjā'pi paṭahe proktā kākaciñcyāṃ kaladhvanau \|

gañjā khānau surāgehe syādbhāṇḍāgārarīḍhayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 12 \[unit 376\]

gañjaḥ kuñjo nikuñje'pi hanau dante ca dantinām \|

sañjaḥ prajāpatau rudre lañjaḥ syāt paṭṭakacchayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 13 \[unit 377\]

bharjjā bale mahotsāhe khajā manthaprahastayoḥ \|

piñjā tūle haridrāyāṃ piñjastu vyākule'nyavat \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 14 \[unit 378\]

piñjo bale caye piñjaṃ rajjurveṇyāṃ guṇe smṛtā \|

kharjjūḥ kaṇḍvāñca kīṭe ca kharjūrīpādape'pi ca \|

marjjūḥ syādrajake śuddhau sarjūrvaṇijavidyuti \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 15 \[unit 379\]

karajastu karañje syānnakhe vyāghranakhe'pi \|

kuṭajo vṛkṣabhede syādagastyadroṇayorapi \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 16 \[unit 380\]

sahajastu nisarge syāt sahotthe punaranyavat \|

nīrajaṃ kamale kuṣṭhe jalajaṃ śaṅkhapadmayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 17 \[unit 381\]

valajaṃ gokṣure kṣetre sasyasaṅgarayorapi \|

valajā varayoṣāyāṃ vasumatyāmapi smṛtā \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 18 \[unit 382\]

kārujaḥ śilpināñcitre kalabhe nāgakeśare \|

svayañjātatile phene valmīke gairike'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 19 \[unit 383\]

bāhujaḥ kṣatriye kīre svayañjātatile'pi ca \|

vanajo mustake padme vanajaṃ vanajo gaje \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 20 \[unit 384\]

vanajā mudgaparṇyāñca vaṇig vāṇijyajīvini \|

vaṇik karaṇabhede ca vaṇijyāyāmapīṣyate \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 21 \[unit 385\]

sāmajastu gaje proktaḥ sāmotthe punaranyavat \|

bhūmijo narake'ṅgāre sītāyāmapi bhūmijā \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 22 \[unit 386\]

girijañcābhrake khyātaṃ śilājatusugandhayoḥ \|

lohe'pi girijā gaurīmātuluṅgyo\[?\]rudīritā \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 23 \[unit 387\]

kambojo hastibhede syācchaṅkhadeśaviśeṣayoḥ \|

aṅgajaṃ rudhire'naṅgaputrakeśamade'ṅgajaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 24 \[unit 388\]

kāmbojasturage somavalke punnāgapādape \|

kāmbojī māsaparṇyāñca hiṅguparṇyāmapi kvacit \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 25 \[unit 389\]

aṇḍajaḥ kṛkalāse syāt khage mīne bhujaṅgame \|

kastūryāmaṇḍajā proktā parañjastailayantrake \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 26 \[unit 390\]

phene ca karavāle ca parañjaḥ kṣurikāphale \|

himajā'pi śacīgauryormaināke himajaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 27 \[unit 391\]

jaghanyajo'nuje śūdre bhāradvājaḥ smṛto munau \|

droṇe ca vanakārpāsyāṃ bhāradvājī ca kathyate \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 28 \[unit 392\]

bhāradvājo guroḥ putre vyāghrākākhyavihaṅgame \|

kāśmīrajākhyā'tiviṣā kuṣṭhakuṅkumapauṣkare \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 29 \[unit 393\]

kṣīrābdhijaṃ tu sāmudralavaṇe mauktike kvacit \|

kṣīrābdhijā śriyāṃ proktā candre kṣīrābdhijo bhavet \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 30 \[unit 394\]

dvijarājaḥ śaśadhare suparṇe'nantabhogini \|

dharmarājo yame buddhe dharmarājo yudhiṣṭhire \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 31 \[unit 395\]

rājarājaḥ kubere'pi sārvabhaume sudhākare \|

bhṛṅgarājastu madhupe mārkave vihagāntare \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 32 \[unit 396\]

graharājo ravau candre'pyahibhukkekitārkṣyayoḥ \|

yakṣarāṭ tryamvakasarave mallānāṃ raṅgacatvare \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / japañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / japañcakam / 33 \[unit 397\]

munibheṣajamāgastye harītakyāñca laṅghane \|

vṛṣabhadhvajaśabdo'sau śaṅkare cārhadantare \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jāntavargaḥ / japañcakam / fragment 398

iti jāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jhāntavargaḥ / jhadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jhāntavargaḥ / jhadvikam / 1 \[unit 399\]

jhañjñā dhvaniviśeṣe'pi jhañjhā'ṇujalavarṣaṇe \|

jhañjñā vāte tāravāyau naṣṭe'pi parikīrttitā \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / jhāntavargaḥ / jhadvikam / fragment 400

iti jhāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ñāntavargaḥ / ñaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ñāntavargaḥ / ñaikakam / fragment 401

jño viriñce budhe saumye

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvikam / 1 \[unit 402\]

cājñastu jaḍamūrkhayoḥ \|

prajñā buddhau paṇḍite'pi prajño vācyavadiṣyate \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvikam / 2 \[unit 403\]

saṃjñā nāmani gāyatryāṃ cetanāyāṃ ravistriyām \|

arthasūcanikāyāñca hastādyairapi kathyate \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ñāntavargaḥ / ñatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ñāntavargaḥ / ñatrikam / 3 \[unit 404\]

sarvajñaḥ śaṅkare buddhe kṛtajñaḥ kukkure'pi ca \|

daivajño gaṇake'pi syāt kṣetrajñaścheka ātmani \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ñāntavargaḥ / ñatrikam / fragment 405

iti ñāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 1 \[unit 406\]

kaṭaḥ śroṇau kriyākāre kiliñje'tiśaye śave \|

samaye gajagaṇḍe ca pippalyāśca kaṭī matā \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 2 \[unit 407\]

khaṭo'ndhakūpakaphayoḥ prahārāntaraṭaṅkayoḥ \|

bhaṭaḥ pāmarabhede ca vīre ca pragame bhaṭiḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 3 \[unit 408\]

dhaṭaḥ samādhibhedebhaśiraḥkūṭakuṭeṣu ca \|

ghaṭā ghaṭanagoṣṭhībhaghaṭanāsu ca dṛśyate \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 4 \[unit 409\]

kuṭaḥ koṭe ghaṭe gehe śilākuṭṭakapūruṣe \|

syācchuṭī kumbhadāsyāñca surāyāñcitramustake \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 5 \[unit 410\]

paṭaḥ priyālavṛkṣe ca sucele ca puraskṛtau \|

paṭaḥ pramādavacanadoṣayorapyudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 6 \[unit 411\]

vaṭo gole gule bhakṣye vaṭo sāmyavarāṭayoḥ \|

vaṭaḥ kapardde nyagrodhe śumbaraktikayorvaṭī \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 7 \[unit 412\]

viṭo'drau lavaṇe ṣiṅge mūṣake khadire'pi ca \|

sphuṭo vyakte praphulle ca vyāpte ca kalite'nyavat \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 8 \[unit 413\]

nirbhinnakarkkaṭīsasye pādasphoṭe sphuṭirmatā \|

kūṭaṃ yantre'nṛte rāśau niścale lohamudgare \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 9 \[unit 414\]

māyādriśṛṅgayostucche sīrāvayavadambhayoḥ \|

puradvāre ca śaṃsanti lāṭo deśeṃ'śuke'pi ca \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 10 \[unit 415\]

jhāṭo nikuñje kāntāre vraṇasammārjjane'pi ca \|

vaṭo vṛtau ca mārge'pi vāṭī tu gṛhaniṣkuṭe \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 11 \[unit 416\]

caṭuścāṭau picaṇḍe ca vratināmāsanāntare \|

kaṭuḥ sugandhe tīkṣṇe syādakārye makṣare rase \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 12 \[unit 417\]

kaṭuḥ priyaṅgukaṭukā rājikāsvatha dūṣaṇe \|

kaṭu proktaṃ paṭustīkṣṇe nīroge cature'nyavat \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 13 \[unit 418\]

paṭuḥ paṭolapatre'pi chatrālavaṇayoḥ paṭuḥ \|

kheṭaḥ kaphe grāmabhede nindite vasunandake \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 14 \[unit 419\]

aṭṭaṃ bhakte ca śuṣke ca kṣaume'tyarthe gṛhāntare \|

paṭṭaḥ peṣaṇapāṣāṇe vraṇādīnāñca vandhane \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 15 \[unit 420\]

catuṣpathe ca rājādiśāsanāntarapīṭhayoḥ \|

paṭṭī lalāṭabhūṣāyāṃ paṭṭī lākṣāprasādhane \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 16 \[unit 421\]

kruṣṭañca rodane rāve kaṣṭaṃ gahanakṛcchayoḥ \|

iṣṭamāśaṃsite pūjyatame priyatame'nyavat \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 17 \[unit 422\]

iṣṭaṃ saṃskārayoge ca yāge ca kratukarmaṇi \|

riṣṭaṃ kṣemāśubhābhāvakṛpāṇeṣu pracakṣyate \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 18 \[unit 423\]

mliṣṭamavyaktavāci syānmliṣṭaṃ mlāne'nyavanmatam \|

diṣṭaṃ daive ca kāle syāddiṣṭaḥ sṛṣṭaṃ tu nirmite \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 19 \[unit 424\]

yuktaniścitayoḥ prājye hṛṣṭo hṛṣitavanmataḥ \|

romāñcite prahasite jātadarpe ca vismite \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 20 \[unit 425\]

vyuṣṭaṃ dine prabhāte ca phale paryuṣite'pi ca \|

tvaṣṭā śilpini devānāṃ tathā varddhakisūryayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 21 \[unit 426\]

iṣṭiḥ syādabhilāṣe'pi saṅgrahaślokayāgayoḥ \|

viṣṭiḥ karmakare bhadre preṣaṇe vetane'pi ca \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 22 \[unit 427\]

diṣṭiḥ parīmāṇamudordṛṣṭirvidarśanākṣiṣu \|

riṣṭiḥ khaḍge samṛddhau ca kṛṣṭiḥ syāt karṣaṇe budhe \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 23 \[unit 428\]

sṛṣṭiḥ svabhāve nirmāṇe puṣṭirvṛddhau ca poṣaṇe \|

yaṣṭirhāralatāśastrabhedayordhvajadaṇḍake \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 24 \[unit 429\]

bhāṇḍyāñca madhuyaṣṭyāñca muṣṭirbaddhakare pale \|

vyuṣṭiḥ syād gharṣaṇe viṣṇukrāntāsparddhākiriṣvapi \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 25 \[unit 430\]

jaṭālagnakace mūle plakṣamākṣikayorjaṭī \|\|

saṭā jaṭākeśarayoḥ phaṭā ca phaṇadambhayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 26 \[unit 431\]

ghoṇṭā syād badare pūge ghaṭīkhaṇḍe ca vāsasaḥ \|

parīkṣārthatulāyāñca ghaṭaḥ sūribhiriṣyate \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 27 \[unit 432\]

paṭiḥ paṭaviśeṣe'pi dvāśule kuṣṭikādrume \|

syāt koṭiraśrau cāpāgre saṅkhyābhedaprakarṣayoḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 28 \[unit 433\]

syāt truṭiḥ saṃśaye leśe sūkṣmailākālamānayoḥ \|

troṭiḥ syāt kaṭphale cañcvāṃ khagamīnaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 29 \[unit 434\]

khāṭiḥ kiṇe śavarathe khāṭirekagrahe smṛtā \|

naṭī haṭṭavilāsinyāṃ śoṇake narttake naṭaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 30 \[unit 435\]

avaṭaḥ syāt khile garte kūpe kuhakajīvini \|

vikaṭaḥ sundare prokto viśālavikarālayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 31 \[unit 436\]

karaṭo gajagaṇḍe syāt kusumbhe nindajīvini \|

ekādaśāhasārddhe'pi durdurūḍhe'pi vāyase \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 32 \[unit 437\]

karaṭo vādyabhede'pi nirddaṭo nirddaye smṛtaḥ \|

parāpavādasaṃrakte nirddaṭo niṣprayojane \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 33 \[unit 438\]

cipiṭaḥ khādyabhede'pi cipiṭo vistṛte'nyavat \|

jakuṭo yamale khyāto vārttākakusume śuni \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 34 \[unit 439\]

niṣkuṭastu gṛhodyāne kedārakakapāṭayoḥ \|

harmmuṭaḥ kacchape sūrye'pyutkaṭastīvramattayoḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 35 \[unit 440\]

carpaṭaḥ sphāravipulacapeṭe parpaṭe'pi ca \|

parpaṭaḥ piṣṭavikṛtau parpaṭo bheṣajāntare \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 36 \[unit 441\]

piccaṭo netraroge syāt piccaṭaṃ trapusīsayoḥ \|
