sampāṭaṃ śikyabhede'pi dhautāñjanyāmapi smṛtam \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 37 \[unit 442\]

karddaṭaḥ karahāṭe syāt paṅkapaṅkārayorapi \|\|

kṛpīṭamudare\[ \]nīre kārpaṭo jatukāryiṇoḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 38 \[unit 443\]

kukkuṭastāmracūḍe syāt kukkubhe'gnikaṇe'pi ca \|

niṣādaśūdrayoḥ putre tālamadhye tu kukkuṭam \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 39 \[unit 444\]

raso nasyaprabhede syāduccaṭā dambhacarccayoḥ \|

kekaṭaḥ śroṇake viṣṇāva'rgaṭo'ntargale gale \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 40 \[unit 445\]

karkaṭaḥ karaṇe strīṇāṃ rāśibhede kulīrayoḥ \|

śālmalīphalavālukyoḥ karkkaṭī karkaṭaḥ khage \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 41 \[unit 446\]

kīkaṭasturage niḥsve deśabhede mitampace \|

mocāṭaḥ kṛṣṇajīre syādrambhāsthni malayodbhave \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 42 \[unit 447\]

cakrāṭo viṣavaidye'pi dhūrttadīnārayorapi \|

varṇāṭo gāyane citrakāre strīkṛtajīvini \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 43 \[unit 448\]

bhāvāṭo bhāvake sādhuniveśe kāmuke'pi ca \|

śailāṭo devale siṃhe śuklakācakirātayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 44 \[unit 449\]

dvohāṭaḥ kayito gāthāprabhede mṛgalubdhake \|

vaiḍālavṛttike'pi syāddhārāṭaścātakāśvayoḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 45 \[unit 450\]

moraṭastu bhavedikṣumūlāṅkoṭaprasūnayoḥ \|

saptarātrāt pare kṣīre mūrvikāyāntu moraṭā \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 46 \[unit 451\]

vekaṭaḥ syādvaikaṭike tathā sañjātayauvane \|

veraṭo miśrite nīce veraṭaṃ badarīphale \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 47 \[unit 452\]

trikūṭaṃ sindhulavaṇe trikūṭaśca suvelake \|

bhākūṭaḥ kathyate mīnabhede śailāntare'pi ca \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 48 \[unit 453\]

ariṣṭaḥ phenile nimbe laśune kākakaṅkayoḥ \|

ariṣṭaṃ sūtikāgāre takre cihne śubhāśubhe \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 49 \[unit 454\]

saṃsṛṣṭaṃ tadgate vidyāt saṃśuddhe vasanādinā \|

avaṭuḥ kathito ghāṭā kūpagartteṣu sūribhiḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 50 \[unit 455\]

parīṣṭiḥ paricaryāyāṃ prākrāmye'pi gaveṣaṇe \|

varaṭā haṃsayoṣāyāṃ gandholyāṃ varaṭā'pi ca \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 51 \[unit 456\]

tripuṭā mallikāyāñca sūkṣmailātrivṛtorapi \|

satīlake ca tīre ca tripuṭaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 52 \[unit 457\]

barvaṭī vrīhibhede syādraghaṭīpaṇyayorapi \|

parkkaṭī nūtanaphale pūgādeḥ plakṣapādape \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 53 \[unit 458\]

markaṭī vānarīśūkaśimbyoḥ syāt karajāntare \|

markkaṭastūrṇanābhe'pi kapau strīkaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 54 \[unit 459\]

kuruṇṭī dāruputryāñca kuruṇṭo jhiṇṭikāntare \|

ciraṇṭī tu suvāsinyāṃ syāddvitīyavayaḥstriyām \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 460\]

śrutikaṭaḥ prāñcalohe prāyaścittabhujaṅgayoḥ \|

ucciṅgaṭo mīnabhede tathā kopanapūruṣe \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 461\]

kuṭannaṭastu śoṇāke kaivarttau mustake'pi ca \|

syāt khañjarīṭaḥ phalakāsidhārāvratacāriṇoḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 57 \[unit 462\]

kuṇḍakīṭastu cārvākavacanābhijñapūruṣe \|

jārajabrāhmaṇīputradāsīkāmukayorapi \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 58 \[unit 463\]

nārakīṭo'śmakūṭe syāt svadattāśmavihantari \|

kāryakūṭastu kṛpaṇonmattānarthakareṣvapi \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 59 \[unit 464\]

kāmakūṭastu veśyāyāḥ priyavibhramayormataḥ \|

cakravāṭaḥ kriyārohe paryante ca śikhātarau \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 60 \[unit 465\]

varkarāṭaḥ kaṭākṣe syāt taruṇādityarociṣi \|

strīṇāṃ payodharotsaṅgakāntadattanakhe'pi ca \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 61 \[unit 466\]

karahāṭo'bjakande syāt puṣpavṛkṣaprabhedayoḥ \|

parapuṣṭaḥ parabhṛte parapuṣṭā''paṇastriyām \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 62 \[unit 467\]

pratikṛṣṭaṃ mataṃ guhye dvirāvṛttyā ca karṣite \|

pratikṛṣṭaḥ preṣite syāt pratyākhyāte ca vācyavat \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 63 \[unit 468\]

śipiviṣṭastu khalatau śive duścarmaṇi smṛtaḥ \|

catuḥṣaṣṭirbahvṛce'pi catuḥṣaṣṭikalāsvapi \|\| 63 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / fragment 469

syādgāḍhamuṣṭiḥ kṛpaṇe kṛpāṇādiṣu ceṣyate \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcakam / 64 \[unit 470\]

atha syāddaśanocchiṣṭo niśvāsādharacumbayoḥ \|\| 64 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcakam / fragment 471

iti ṭāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 1 \[unit 472\]

kaṭhaḥ svara ṛcāmbhede syāttadadhyetṛvedinoḥ \|

munāvapi haṭho vāriparṇyāṃ syāt prasabhe'pi ca \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 2 \[unit 473\]

śaṭho madhyasthapuruṣe śaṭho dhuttūradhūrttayoḥ \|

śaṭho'lase ca mūrkhe ca kuṇṭho karmaṇyamūrkhayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 3 \[unit 474\]

pāṭhaḥ syāt paṭhane viddhakarṇyāṃ pāṭhā'pi paṭhyate \|

kaṇṭho gale saṃvidhāne dhvanau madanapādape \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 4 \[unit 475\]

vaṇṭhaḥ syādakṛtodvāhe kharve kuntāyudhe'pi ca \|

pṛṣṭhaṃ caramagātre syāddehasyāvayavāntare \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 5 \[unit 476\]

koṣṭhaḥ kuśūle cātmīye kukṣerantargṛhasya ca \|

kuṣṭho roge sugandhe ca goṣṭhaṃ gosthānake'pi ca \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 6 \[unit 477\]

goṣṭhī sabhāyāṃ saṃlāpe duṣṭhuḥ syād durbale'dhame \|

praṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhāgrayoruktaḥ praṣṭhā cāṇḍālikauṣadhau \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 7 \[unit 478\]

jyeṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhe'tivṛddhe ca jyeṣṭho māsāntare'pi ca \|

gṛhagodharkṣayorjyeṣṭhā śreṣṭhā vaiśravaṇe vare \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 8 \[unit 479\]

kāṣṭhā dāruharidrāyāṃ kālamānaprakarṣayoḥ \|

syāddiśi sthānamātre ca kāṣṭhamākhyātamindhane \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 9 \[unit 480\]

niṣṭhā niṣpattināśāntayācñānirvahaṇeṣu ca \|

ṣaṣṭhī ṣaṇṇāñca pūraṇyāṃ kātyāyanyāmapīṣyate \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 10 \[unit 481\]

kamaṭhaḥ kacchape bhāṇḍabhede ca kamaṭhaṃ matam \|

jaraṭhaḥ karkaśe pāṇḍau jaraṭhaḥ kaṭhine'nyavat \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 11 \[unit 482\]

narmmaṭhaścūcuke ṣiṅge vaikuṇṭhaḥ kṛṣṇaśakrayoḥ \|

śrīkaṇṭhaḥ khaṇḍaparaśau śrīkaṇṭhaḥ kurujāṅgale \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 12 \[unit 483\]

variṣṭhaḥ syādarutare pravare tittirāvapi \|

variṣṭhaṃ marice tāmre sādiṣṭho'tidṛḍhāryyayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 13 \[unit 484\]

ambaṣṭho deśabhede'pi viprādvaiśyāsute'pi ca \|

ambaṣṭhā cāmlaloṇyāṃ syāt pāṭhāyūtikayorapi \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 14 \[unit 485\]

kaniṣṭho'lpe'nuje yūni kaniṣṭhā durbalāṅgulau \|

laghiṣṭho'tyalpake bhele'pyapaṣṭhuḥ kālabālayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 15 \[unit 486\]

prakoṣṭho vistṛtakare bhūpakakṣāntare'pi ca \|

kūrparādadhare cāpi pratiṣṭhā sthānamātrake \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 16 \[unit 487\]

gaurave yāganiṣpatticaturakṣarapadyayoḥ \|

makuṣṭho vrīhibhede syānmakuṣṭho manthare'nyavat \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 17 \[unit 488\]

dantaśaṭhaḥ syājjambīre kapitthe karmmaraṅgake \|

nāgaraṅge'pi cāṅgeryyā smṛtā dantaśaṭhā budhaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 18 \[unit 489\]

sūtrakaṇṭho dvije proktaḥ khañjarīṭakapotayoḥ \|

hārikaṇṭhaḥ parabhṛte hārānvitagale'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 19 \[unit 490\]

kālakaṇṭhastu dātyūhe kalaviṅke ca khañjane \|

sitāpā\[?\]ṅge hare pīte sārake nīlakaṇṭhavat \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 20 \[unit 491\]

kalakaṇṭhaḥ kaladhvāne haṃse pārāvate pike \|

devakāṣṭhantu saraladevadārumahīruhoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 21 \[unit 492\]

kālapṛṣṭhaṃ bhavet karṇacāpe kodaṇḍamātrake \|

kālapṛṣṭhastu sāraṅgaviśeṣe kaṅkapakṣiṇi \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / fragment 493

iti ṭhāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 1 \[unit 494\]

guḍaḥ syādgolake haste sannāhekṣuvikārayoḥ \|

guḍā snuhīguḍikayorgaḍo mīnāntarāyayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 2 \[unit 495\]

jaḍo mūrkhe himagraste śūkaśimbyāṃ jaḍā matā \|

gaḍuḥ puṣṭaguḍe kubje ṣaḍaḥ peyāntare bhidi \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 3 \[unit 496\]

vyāḍo hiṃsrapaśau sarpe tāḍastāḍanaghoṣayoḥ \|

muṣṭimeyatṛṇādau ca tāḍī tālīdaladrume \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 4 \[unit 497\]

nīḍaṃ sthāne kulāye ca sakārapūrvako'ntike \|

kroḍaḥ sūkarapātaṅgyo kroḍaṃ krauḍe ca vakṣasi \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 5 \[unit 498\]

coḍo deśaviśeṣe syāccoḍaḥ prāvaraṇāntare \|

kṣveḍaḥ karṇāmaye dhvāne garale kuṭile'pi ca \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 6 \[unit 499\]

lohitārkaphale kṣveḍaṃ ghoṣapuṣpe durāsade \|

yodhānāṃ siṃhanāde tu kṣveḍā syādvaṃśaśalyake \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 7 \[unit 500\]

aṇḍaṃ khagādikośe syānmuṣke vīrye'pi ca kvacit \|

syāt khaṇḍaḥ śakale cekṣuvikāramaṇidoṣayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 8 \[unit 501\]

gaṇḍaḥ kapole piṭake yogabhede ca gaṇḍake \|

gaṇḍaḥ pravīre cihne syādaśvabhūpaṇabudrude \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 9 \[unit 502\]

caṇḍo daityāntare tīvre caṇḍo dāse yamasya ca \|

caṇḍā dhanaharīśaṅkhapuṣpyoścaṇḍo'tikopane \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 10 \[unit 503\]

caṇḍī kātyāyanīhiṃsrākopanastrīṣu sammatā \|

ṣaṇḍaṃ padmādisaṅghāte khaṇḍaḥ syād gopatāvapi \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 11 \[unit 504\]

kuṇḍaṃ devajalādhāre piṭhare'tha kamaṇḍalau \|

kuṇḍī kuṇḍaḥ smṛto jārāt pativatnīsute'pi ca \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 12 \[unit 505\]

daṇḍī yame mānabhede laguḍe damasainyayoḥ \|

vyūhabhede prakāṇḍe'śve koṇamanthānayorapi \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 13 \[unit 506\]

abhimāne grahe daṇḍaścaṇḍāṃśoḥ pāripārśvike \|

gauḍaḥ pāmarajātau ca vṛddhanābhau ca vācyavat \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 14 \[unit 507\]

piṇḍaṃ sāndre balaṃ\[le\] vole gṛhāṃśe jīvanāyasoḥ \|

piṇḍo dehe javāpuṣpe nivāpe sihlake'pi ca \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 15 \[unit 508\]

piṇḍī syāttagare'lāvurarurndarībhedayorapi \|

paṇḍaḥ ṣaṇḍe dhiyāṃ paṇḍā pāṇḍuḥ kuntīpatau site \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 16 \[unit 509\]

maṇḍaṃ mastuni śāke ca maṇḍaḥ syāt sārapicchayoḥ \|

eraṇḍe bhūṣaṇe cātha maṇḍā dhātryāmudīritā \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 17 \[unit 510\]

muṇḍo daityāntare rāhau mūrddhamuṇḍitayorapi \|

śauṇḍo matte'pi vikhyātaḥ śauṇḍā pippalicaryayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 18 \[unit 511\]

kāṇḍaṃ nāle taruskandhe bāṇe'vasaranīrayoḥ \|

kutsite rahasi stambe kāṇḍaṃ garve'pyudāhṛtam \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 19 \[unit 512\]

bhāṇḍaṃ bhūṣaṇamātre'pi bhāṇḍaṃ mūlavaṇigdhane \|

nadīpātre turaṅgāṇāṃ bhūṣaṇe bhājane'pi ca \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 20 \[unit 513\]

iḍā vāci gavi kṣoṇāviḍā ca budhayoṣiti \|

krīḍā keliprakāre syāt khelāvajñānayorapi \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 21 \[unit 514\]

cūḍā śikhāyāṃ valabhau cūḍā vāhuvibhūṣaṇe \|

pīḍā kṛpāśiromālāpamarddasaraladruṣu \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 22 \[unit 515\]

nāḍī nāle śirāgaṇḍadūrvayoḥ syādguṇāntare \|\|

nāḍī ṣaṭkṣaṇakāle'pi caryyāyāṃ kuhanasya ca \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 23 \[unit 516\]

raṇḍā mūṣikaparṇyāñca raṇḍā vidhavayoṣiti \|

vaṇḍā'pi pāṃsulāyāṃ syādvaṇḍo hastādivarjjite \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 24 \[unit 517\]

śuṇḍā'pi jalahastinyāṃ madirākarihastayoḥ \|

nalinyāṃ vārayoṣāyāṃ śuṇḍastu madanirjhare \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 25 \[unit 518\]

gāruḍaṃ syānmarakate viṣaśāstre'pi gāruḍam \|

drāviḍaḥ syājjānapade vedhamukhyakaśaṅkhayo\[ḥ\] \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 26 \[unit 519\]

pracaṇḍo durvahe śvetakaravīre pratāpini \|

picaṇḍamudare vidyāt paśoravayave'pi ca \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 27 \[unit 520\]

taraṇḍo baḍiśīsūtrabaddhavastuni melake \|

varaṇḍo'pyantarāvedau sandohamukharogayoḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 28 \[unit 521\]

pūtyaṇḍo gandhakīṭe'pi tathā gandhamṛge smṛtaḥ \|

mārttaṇḍastapane kroḍe śikhaṇḍo barhacūḍayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 29 \[unit 522\]

prakāṇḍo viṭape śaste mūlaskandhāntare taroḥ \|

kodaṇḍo bhrūlatāyāṃ syāddeśabhede ca kārmuke \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 30 \[unit 523\]

saraṇḍaḥ saraṭe dhūrte saraṇḍo bhūṣaṇāntare \|

māruṇḍo'ṇḍe bhujaṅgīnāṃ mārge gomayamaṇḍale \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 31 \[unit 524\]

karaṇḍo madhukośāsikāraṇḍavadalāḍhake \|

kūṣmāṇḍo gaṇabhede syād bhrūṇe kararkkāruke'pi ca \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 32 \[unit 525\]

kūṣmāṇḍo cauṣadhīgaurvyorbheruṇḍo bhīmadarśane \|

bheruṇḍo devatābhede pakṣiṇo bhidi ceṣyate \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 33 \[unit 526\]

vāruṇḍaḥ sekapātre syānmale dṛkkarṇayorapi \|

gaṇistharāje vāruṇḍī dvārapiṇḍyāmudīritā \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 34 \[unit 527\]

tintiḍī bhūruhe kāladāme daityāntare'pi ca \|

tintiḍastintiḍī tu syācciñcāvṛkṣāmlayorapi \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 35 \[unit 528\]

vicaṇḍā sārikāvarttikhaḍgadhenuṣu kathyate \|

vitaṇḍā vādabhede syāt kacchīśāke śilāhvaye \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 36 \[unit 529\]

vitaṇḍā karavīryāñca nirguṇḍī sinduvārake \|

nīlaśephālikāyāñca karahāṭe'pi kutracit \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam / 37 \[unit 530\]

bhavedvātathuḍo vātyā\[?\] vāmavyorvātaśoṇite \|

picchilasphoṭikāyāñca devatāḍastu ghoṣake \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam / 38 \[unit 531\]

saiṃhikeyagrahe cāpi devatāḍo hutāśane \|

cakravāḍo'dribhede syāccakravāḍantu maṇḍale \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam / 39 \[unit 532\]

jalaruṇḍo jalāvartte payoreṇau bhujaṅgame \|

apogaṇḍastu śiśuke vikalāṅge'tibhīruke \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam / fragment 533

iti ḍāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhādvakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhādvakam / 1 \[unit 534\]

dṛḍhaḥ sthūle mṛśe śakte pragāḍhe'pi dṛḍho mataḥ \|

bāḍhaṃ dṛḍhe pratijñāyāṃ mūḍhastandritabālayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhādvakam / 2 \[unit 535\]

gūḍhaṃ rahasi guhye ca saṃvṛte punaranyavat \|

vyūḍhaḥ saṃhatavinyastapṛthuleṣu ca bhedyavat \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhādvakam / 3 \[unit 536\]

ṣaṇḍho varṣadhare klībe gopatau bandhyapūruṣe \|

voḍhā syādbhārite sūte soḍhā marṣaṇaśaktayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhādvakam / 4 \[unit 537\]

rāḍhā sūkṣme ca śobhāyāṃ dāḍhā daṃṣṭrābhilāṣayoḥ \|

māḍhiḥ patrādibhaṅgau syānmāḍhirdainyaprakāśane \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 5 \[unit 538\]

āṣāḍho vratināṃ daṇḍe māse malayaparvate \|\|

vārūḍhaḥ śambale'pi syādvastrāñcalakapāṭayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 6 \[unit 539\]

pañjare pāvake vātha prarūḍho ja\[ṭha\]re smṛtaḥ \|

baddhamūle virūḍhastu sañjāte'ṅkurite'nyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 7 \[unit 540\]

samūḍhaḥ puñjite bhugne sadyojāte'nupaplute \|

udūḍha ūḍhe sthūle syādupoḍho nikaṭoḍhayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 8 \[unit 541\]

nigūḍho garhite gupte pramīḍho mūtrite ghane \|

sarūḍho'ṅkurite prauḍhe pragāḍho dṛḍhapṛcchayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / fragment 542

adhyūḍhā kṛtasāpatnyāṃ nāryāmadhyūḍha īśvare \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacatuṣkam / 9 \[unit 543\]

adhyārūḍhaṃ samārūḍhe'pyadhike cā'pya'bhedyavat \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacatuṣkam / fragment 544

iti ḍhāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 1 \[unit 545\]

kaṇo'tisūkṣme dhānyāṃśe kṛṣṇājīrakaṇādiṣu \|

paṇo varāṭamāne syānmūlye kārṣāpaṇe glahe \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 2 \[unit 546\]

krayyaśākaṭikadyūtavyavahāre bhṛtau dhane \|

gaṇaḥ samūhe prathame saṅkhyāsainyaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 3 \[unit 547\]

ghrāṇaṃ ghrāte ca nāsāyāṃ kāṇaḥ kākaikacakṣuṣoḥ \|

bāṇaḥ syāt kevale kāṇḍe kāṇḍāvayavadaityayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 4 \[unit 548\]

bāṇā jhiṇṭyāṃ matā dhīraiḥ śaṇī māsacatuṣṭaye \|

kaṣaṇe karapatre ca śāṇī prāvaraṇāntare \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 5 \[unit 549\]

trāṇā tu trāyamānāyāṃ rakṣite rakṣaṇe'pi ca \|

prāṇo hṛnmārute bāle kāvyajīve'nile vale \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 6 \[unit 550\]

vācyavat pūrite prāṇaṃ prāṇāścā'suṣu kīrttitāḥ \|

druṇaṃ cāpe ca khaṅaḍge ca druṇo vṛścikabhṛṅgayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 7 \[unit 551\]

druṇī kacchapikāyāñca jaladroṇyāmudāhṛtā \|

droṇaḥ kṛpīpatau kṛṣṇakā\[?\]ke syādāḍhake'pi ca \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 8 \[unit 552\]

āḍhavāpacatuṣke ca droṇī syānnīvṛdantare \|\| {ādarśe, jābhujāpacatuṣka iti pāṭhaḥ}

droṇī kāṣṭhāmbuvāhinyāṃ gavāṅghāsabhuji sthitau \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 9 \[unit 553\]

moṇaḥ śuṣkaphale nakramakṣikāhikaraṇḍayoḥ \|

koṇo vādyaprabhede syāt koṇo'stre laguḍe'rkaje \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 10 \[unit 554\]

vīṇā vādyādinopāye'pyekadeśe gṛhasya ca \|

śoṇaḥ śyoṇākanadayoḥ śoṇaḥ kokanadacchade \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 11 \[unit 555\]

lohitāśve kṛśānau ca bhrūṇaḥ strīgarbhaḍimbhayoḥ \|

tīkṣṇaṃ sāmudralavaṇe viṣalohāgnimuṣkake \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 12 \[unit 556\]

tīkṣṇaḥ sāre ca tigmātmatyāginorvācyavadbhavet \|

karṇaḥ pṛthājyeṣṭhasute suvarṇālau śrutāvapi \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 13 \[unit 557\]

jarṇaścandre ca vṛkṣe ca varṇaḥ kāñcanayajñayoḥ \|

varṇo dvijādau śuklādau stutau rūpayaśo'kṣare \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 14 \[unit 558\]

vilepane kathāyāñca varṇaḥ syād guṇabhedayoḥ \|

parṇaṃ syāt kiṃśuke patre śīrṇaṃ tanuviśīrṇayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 15 \[unit 559\]

pūrṇaḥ śabde samagre ca pūrite cāpi vācyavat \|

cūrṇaṃ kṣode kṣārabhede cūrṇā nivāsayuktiṣu \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 16 \[unit 560\]

kīrṇaṃ channe parikṣipte hiṃsite kīrṇamiṣyate \|

jīrṇaṃ pakve purāṇe ca dīrṇaṃ dāritabhītayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 17 \[unit 561\]

uṣṇaḥ syādātape grīṣme dakṣe kṛṣṇastu keśave \|

vyāpte'rjjune kokile ca kṛṣṇaṃ maricalohayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 18 \[unit 562\]

kṛṣṇā tu draupadīnīlīhārahūrāsu kathyate \|

vācyavanmecake kṛṣṇe jiṣṇuḥ śakre dhanañjaye \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 19 \[unit 563\]

jitvare cātha viṣṇuḥ syāt kṛṣṇe'rke vasudaivate \|

geṣṇurnaṭe gāyane ca deṣṇurdātūri durdame \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 20 \[unit 564\]

vṛṣṇistu yādave meṣe dṛṣṇiḥ pākhaṇḍicaṇḍayoḥ \|

pārṣṇiḥ pāścātyabhāge syāt pādamūlonmadastriyoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 21 \[unit 565\]

senāpṛṣṭhe ca kumbhyāśañca tṛṣṇā lipsāpipāsayoḥ \|

ūrṇā meṣādilomni syādantarāvarttake bhruvoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 22 \[unit 566\]

maṇirgole mehanāgre ratne cājāgalastane \|

aṇirāṇivadakṣāgrakīle syādastisīmayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 23 \[unit 567\]

kuṇistunnakavṛkṣe'pi kuṇiḥ syāt kukare'nyavat \|

jhūṇiḥ kramukabhede'pi tuṣṭadaivaśrutāvapi \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 24 \[unit 568\]

śreṇiḥ paṅktau sekapātre śreṇiḥ syāt kārusaṃhatau \|

vāṇiḥ syādūtibhāratyorvāṇirmūlye balāhake \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 25 \[unit 569\]

veṇī kacasya bandhe syānnadīnāṃ melake'pi ca \|

devatāḍe ca tūṇī tu nīlyā tūṇau niṣaṅgake \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 26 \[unit 570\]

ghṛṇā juguṇākṛpayoḥ phāṇirguḍakarambayoḥ \|

śrāṇā yavāgvāṃ pakve tu śrāṇaṃ sthūṇā'yasaḥ smṛtā \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 27 \[unit 571\]

pratimāyāṃ gṛhastambhe vīṇā vallakividyutoḥ \|

veṇurbhūpāntare vaṃśe sthāṇuḥ kīle sthire hare \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / fragment 572

reṇuḥ parpaṭake dhūlyāmalpe'ṇurvrīhisūkṣyayoḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 28 \[unit 573\]

aruṇo vyaktarāge syāt sandhyārāge'rkkasārathau \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 29 \[unit 574\]

niḥśabde kapile kuṣṭhe dravye vācyavadiṣyate \|

aruṇā'tiviśāśyāmāmañjiṣṭhātrivṛtāsu ca \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 30 \[unit 575\]

karuṇastu rase vṛkṣe kṛpāyāṃ karuṇā matā \|

varuṇaḥ sammato nīre svarloke parameṣṭhini \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 31 \[unit 576\]

varuṇastarubhede'psu pratīcīpatisūryayoḥ \|

varaṇastiktaśāke'pi prākāāre varaṇaṃ vṛtau \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 32 \[unit 577\]

taruṇaḥ syānnave yūni kubjapuṣporubūkayoḥ \|

caraṇaṃ bahvṛcādau syānmūle gotre pade'pi ca \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 33 \[unit 578\]

caraṇaṃ bhramaṇe'ttau ca caraṇa rāsabhadhvanau \|

lavaṇo rasarakṣo'bdhibhedeṣu lavaṇānvite \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 34 \[unit 579\]

karaṇaṃ sādhanakṣetrakācakāyasthakarmmasu \|

gītāṅgahārasaṃveśakriyābhedendriyeṣu ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 35 \[unit 580\]

bālavādau ca karaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ śūdrāviśoḥ sute \|

jaraṇaṃ jīrakājājihiṅgusauvarcaleṣvapi \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 36 \[unit 581\]

dharaṇaṃ mānabhede'pi dhāraṇe dharaṇībhuvi \|

ramaṇaṃ syāt paṭolasya mūle'pi ramaṇaḥ priye \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 37 \[unit 582\]

śaraṇaṃ vadharakṣitroḥ śaraṇaṃ rakṣaṇe gṛhe \|

kattṛṇaṃ tṛṇabhede'pi vāriparṇyāñca kattṛṇam \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 38 \[unit 583\]

yantraṇaṃ syānniyamane bandhane rakṣaṇe'pi ca \|

praghaṇastāmrakumbhe syādalinde lohamudgare \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 39 \[unit 584\]

drughaṇo mudgare'pi syād drughaṇā ca paraśvadhe \|

pravaṇaḥ kramanimnorvyāmāyatte ca catuṣpathe \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 40 \[unit 585\]

praguṇe'pi kṣaṇe'pi syādīraṇaṃ śūnya ūṣare \|

grahaṇaṃ tūpalabdhau syādādare grahaṇaṃ kare \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 41 \[unit 586\]

grahoparāgasvīkāravandiṣu grahaṇaṃ matam \|

īkṣaṇaṃ darśane dṛṣṭau prokṣaṇaṃ secane vadhe \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 42 \[unit 587\]

lakṣaṇaṃ nāmni cihne ca saumitre cā'pi lakṣaṇaḥ \|

lakṣmaṇaṃ lāñchane nāmni rāmabhrātari lakṣmaṇaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 43 \[unit 588\]

lakṣmaṇauṣīdhasārasyoḥ saśrīke cābhidheyavat \|

dakṣiṇaḥ saralāvāmaparacchandānuvarttiṣu \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 44 \[unit 589\]

vācyavaddakṣiṇāvācī yajñadānapratiṣṭhayoḥ \|

draviṇaṃ kāñcane vitte draviṇañca parākrame \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 45 \[unit 590\]

masṛṇaḥ karkaśe snigdhe kṣumāyāṃ masṛṇā bhavet \|

ūṣaṇaṃ marice khyātaṃ kaṇāyāmūṣaṇā bhavet \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 46 \[unit 591\]

dhiṣaṇastridaśācāryye dhiṣaṇā dhiyi sammatā \|

dharṣaṇaṃ syāt paribhave rate'satyāṃ tu dharṣaṇī \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 47 \[unit 592\]

bhīṣaṇo dāruṇe rudre bhīṣaṇaṃ sallakīrase \|

haraṇaṃ yautukadravye bhuje'pi haraṇaṃ hṛtau \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 48 \[unit 593\]

hiraṇaṃ retasi svarṇe varāṭe ca hiraṇyavat \|

hariṇaḥ syāt kuraṅge'pi hariṇaḥ pāṇḍure'nyavat \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 49 \[unit 594\]

hariṇī haritāyāñca cārustrīvṛttabhedayoḥ \|

suvarṇapratibhāyāñca harṣaṇo'kṣirugantare \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 50 \[unit 595\]

harṣake yogabhede ca śrāddhadeve'pi kvacit \|

śravaṇaṃ syād vṛkṣabhede śravaṇaṃ śrutikarṇayoḥ \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 51 \[unit 596\]

śrāvaṇo māsapāṣaṇḍe dadhyālyāṃ śrāvaṇī matā \|

śramaṇo yatibhede'pi śramaṇo nindyajīvini \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 52 \[unit 597\]

sudarśanāmuṇḍatikāmāṃsīṣu śramaṇāṃ viduḥ \|

kalyāṇamakṣaye svarṇe kalyāṇaṃ maṅgale'pi ca \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 53 \[unit 598\]

kṛpāṇo'sauḥ kṛpāṇī tu churikāyāṃ ca kartarau \|

siṃhāṇaṃ kācapātre ca lohanāsikayormale \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 54 \[unit 599\]

purāṇaṃ vācyavat pratne purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇe \|

pramāṇaṃ nityamaryādāsatyavādipramātṛṣu \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 55 \[unit 600\]

ekatāyāmiyattāyāṃ hetuśāstreṣu ca smṛtam \|

niryāṇaṃ vānarāpāṅgadeśe mokṣe ca nirgame \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 56 \[unit 601\]

nirmāṇaṃ nirmitau sāre nirmāṇañca samañjase \|

nirvāṇamastaṅgamane nirvṛtau gajamajjane \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 57 \[unit 602\]

apavarge ca kurvāṇe bhṛtyakārakayorapi \|

daurvāṇaṃ naṣṭaparṇe syād dūrvāyāḥ svarase'pi ca \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 58 \[unit 603\]

vāraṇaṃ pratiṣedhe syādvāraṇastu mataṅgaje \|

kāraṇaṃ karaṇe hetubandhayorapi kāraṇam \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 59 \[unit 604\]

kāraṇā yātanāyāñca kṛpaṇaḥ kutsite kṛmau \|

kārmmaṇaṃ mantratantrādiyojane karmakārake \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 60 \[unit 605\]

mārgaṇaṃ prārthane'nveṣe mārgaṇo yācake sare \|

matkuṇo nirviṣāṇebhe niḥśmaśrupuruṣe'pi ca \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 61 \[unit 606\]

uddaśe nārikele ca dhararmmaṇo'hidrubhedayoḥ \|

koṅkaṇo deśabhede syācchastrabhede tu koṅkaṇam \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 62 \[unit 607\]

kaṅkaṇaṃ śekhare hastasūtramaṇḍanayorapi \|

pūraṇaḥ pūrake piṣṭabhede pūraṇamiṣyate \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 63 \[unit 608\]

paṭārambhakasūtre ca śālmalī pūraṇī matā \|

suṣeṇaḥ karamardde'pi viṣṇusugrīvavaidyayoḥ \|\| 63 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 64 \[unit 609\]

roṣaṇaḥ pārade hemagharṣaṇopalayostathā \|

roṣaṇe'pi viṣāṇantu kroḍadviradantayoḥ \|\| 64 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 65 \[unit 610\]

paśoḥ śṛṅge viṣāṇā tu meṣaśaṅgyāṃ prakīrttitā \|

kareṇurgajahastinyoḥ karṇikāre'pi ca kvacit \|\| 65 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 66 \[unit 611\]

hareṇustu satīle syādreṇukākulayoṣitoḥ \|

suvarṇaṃ svarṇamātre syāt suvarṇaṃ karṣabistayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 67 \[unit 612\]

suvarṇaśca suvarṇālukṛṣṇāgurumakhāntare \|

suparṇastāmracūḍe syādgaruḍe kṛtamālake \|\| 67 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 68 \[unit 613\]

suparṇā kamalinyāñca suparṇā tārkṣyamātari \|\|

śrīparṇamagnimanthe'bje śrīparṇī śālmalau haṭhe \|\| 68 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 69 \[unit 614\]

durvarṇañca kuvarṇe syādvāstuke rajate'pi ca \|

patrorṇaṃ dhautakauṣeye patrorṇaḥ śoṇake'pi ca \|\| 69 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 70 \[unit 615\]

saṅkīrṇaṃ nicite proktamaśuddhe cāpi bhedyavat \|

gokarṇo'śvatare sarpe sāraṅge pramathāntare \|\| 70 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 71 \[unit 616\]

aṅguṣṭhānāmikonmāne gokarṇī mūrvikauṣadhau \|

araṇirvahnimanthe'pi smṛto nirmmanthyadāruṇi \|\| 71 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 72 \[unit 617\]

taraṇistaraṇe bhānau kumāryoṣadhinaukayoḥ \|

vipaṇiḥ paṇyavīthyāñca bhavedāpaṇapaṇyayoḥ \|\| 72 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 73 \[unit 618\]

praveṇirveṇikuthayoḥ saraṇiḥ paṅktivartmanoḥ \|

niḥśreṇistvadhirohiṇyāṃ kharjjūrīpādape'pi ca \|\| 73 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 74 \[unit 619\]

bharaṇī śoṇake ṛkṣe bharaṇaṃ vetane bhṛtau \|

bhramaṇī kāruṇḍikāyāṃ krīḍādyāyāmadhīśituḥ \|\| 74 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 75 \[unit 620\]

grāmaṇīrbhogike patyau pradhāne nāpite'pi ca \|

grāmaṇī nīlikāyāñca grāmeyīpaṇyayoṣitoḥ \|\| 75 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 76 \[unit 621\]

dhāraṇī nāḍikāyāṃ syādbuddhoktamantrabhidyapi \|

indrāṇī karaṇe strīṇāṃ paulomīsindhuvārayoḥ \|\| 76 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 77 \[unit 622\]

eṣaṇī vraṇamārgānusāriṇyāñca tulābhidi \|

kṣepaṇī jālabhede syānnaukādaṇḍe ca kathyate \|\| 77 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 78 \[unit 623\]

vāruṇī gaṇḍadūrvāyāṃ pratīcīsurayorapi \|

sāraṇī svalpasarite prasāraṇyāñca sāraṇī \|\| 78 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 79 \[unit 624\]

sāraṇaḥ syādatīsāre sacive rāvaṇasya ca \|

kākiṇī paṇaturyāṃśe mānadaṇḍe ca kākiṇī \|\| 79 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 80 \[unit 625\]

kṛṣṇalaikavarāṭyoḥ syādudamānāṃśake'pi ca \|

brāhmaṇī pañjikāspṛkkādvijapatnīṣu viśrutā \|\| 80 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 81 \[unit 626\]

vipre tu brāhmaṇo vede brāhmaṇaṃ dvijasaṃhatau \|

pakṣiṇī pūrṇimāyāṃ syāt pakṣiṇī śākinībhidi \|\| 81 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 82 \[unit 627\]

āgāmivarttamānāharyuktāyāṃ niśi pakṣiṇī \|

rohiṇī kaṭurohiṇyāṃ rohiṇī lohitāgavoḥ \|\| 82 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / fragment 628

kaṇṭharogāntarebhe ca somavalke ca rohiṇī \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 83 \[unit 629\]

ātarpaṇañca sauhitye vidyādālimpane'pi ca \|\| 83 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 84 \[unit 630\]

ātharvaṇo'pyatharvajñe brāhmaṇe'pi purodhasi \|

ārohaṇaṃ syāt sopāne samārohe prarohaṇe \|\| 84 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 85 \[unit 631\]

raktareṇustu sindūre palāśakalikodgame \|

utkṣepaṇantu vyajane dhānyamarddanavastuni \|\| 85 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 86 \[unit 632\]

uddharaṇaṃ samuddhāravāntānnonmūlaneṣvapi \|

nigaraṇaṃ bhojane'pi gale nigaraṇo mataḥ \|\| 86 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 87 \[unit 633\]

niḥśaraṇaṃ syānmaraṇopāyaniryāṇamuktiṣu \|

saṃsaraṇaṃ raṇārambhe puranirgamagopure \|\| 87 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 88 \[unit 634\]

ghaṇṭāpathe ca sasāre'pyasambādhacamūgatau \|

nistaraṇaṃ syādupāye nistāre nirgame'pi ca \|\| 88 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 89 \[unit 635\]

vidāraṇaṃ raṇe bhede viḍambe ca vidāraṇam \|

nirūpaṇaṃ syādāloke vicāre ca nidarśane \|\| 89 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 90 \[unit 636\]

nārāyaṇo'cyute'bhīrugauryyornārāyaṇī viduḥ \|

parāyaṇamabhīṣṭe syāt tatparāśrayayorapi \|\| 90 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 91 \[unit 637\]

pārāyaṇaṃ samāsaṅge kārtsnye pāragatāvapi \|

kārṣāpaṇaḥ kārṣike syāt paṇaṣoḍaśake'pi ca \|\| 91 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 92 \[unit 638\]

kāmaguṇaḥ smṛto rāge viṣayābhogayorapi \|

parīraṇaḥ syāt kamaṭhe daṇḍe ca paṭṭaśāṭake \|\| 92 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 93 \[unit 639\]

samīraṇaḥ syāt pavane pathike ca phaṇijjhake \|

parvarīṇastu parṇasya śirāyāṃ dyūtakambale \|\| 93 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 94 \[unit 640\]

parṇavṛntarase'pi syāt parvarīṇañca parvaṇi \|

taṇḍurīṇaḥ kīṭamātre varvare taṇḍulodake \|\| 94 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 95 \[unit 641\]

mīnāmrīṇo dardurāmre mīnāmrīṇaśca khañjane \|

pravāraṇaṃ niṣedhe syāt kāmyadāne pravāraṇam \|\| 95 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 96 \[unit 642\]

juhurāṇo'nale'dhvaryau rerihāṇo hare'mbare \|

devamaṇiḥ śive'śvasya kaṇṭhāvartte ca kaustubhe \|\| 96 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 97 \[unit 643\]

cūḍāmaṇiḥ śiroratne kākaciñcāphale'pi ca \|

paravāṇiḥ smṛto dharmmādhyakṣavatsarayorapi \|\| 97 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 98 \[unit 644\]

śaravāṇiḥ śaramukhe ṣāṇḍakaṣe śarajīvini \|

lambakarṇaḥ smṛtaḥ kolapādape chagale'pi ca \|\| 98 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 99 \[unit 645\]

rāgacūrṇaḥ smare dantadhāvane raktavāluke \|

hastikarṇo'rubūke syāt palāśagaṇabhedayoḥ \|\| 99 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 100 \[unit 646\]

cīrṇaparṇastu nimbe syāt kharjjūrībhūruhe'pi ca \|

tailaparṇī malayaje śrīvāse siṃhake'pi ca \|\| 100 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 101 \[unit 647\]

pīluparṇī tu bimbāyāṃ mūrvāyāmoṣadhībhidi \|

dākṣāyaṇī tvaparṇāyāṃ rohiṇyāṃ tārakāsu ca \|\| 101 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 102 \[unit 648\]

puṣkariṇī sarojinyāṃ hastinyāñca jalāśaye \|

śikhariṇī syādromālyāṃ rasālāvṛttabhedayoḥ \|\| 102 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 103 \[unit 649\]

strīratne mallikāyāñca proktā śikhariṇī budhaiḥ \|

aṅgāriṇī hasantyāñca bhāskaratyaktadiśyapi \|\| 103 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / fragment 650

bhavedvaitaraṇī nadyāṃ pretānāṃ yātumātari \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 104 \[unit 651\]

avagrahaṇamityāhuḥ pratirodhe'pyanādare \|\| 104 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 105 \[unit 652\]

pravidāraṇamākhyātaṃ saṅgare ca vidāraṇe \|

avatāraṇaṃ bhūtādigrahe vastrāñcale'rccane \|\| 105 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 106 \[unit 653\]

romaharṣaṇamityetad romāñce'pi bibhītake \|

paribhāṣaṇamālāpe niyame paribhāṣaṇam \|\| 106 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 107 \[unit 654\]

nindopālambhavacane paribhāṣaṇamiṣyate \|

mattavāraṇamicchanti dānaklinnakaṭadvipe \|\| 107 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 108 \[unit 655\]

mahāprāsādavīthīnāṃ varaṇḍe cāpyapāśraye \|

vātarāyaṇamunmatte niṣprayojanapūruṣe \|\| 108 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 109 \[unit 656\]

kāṇḍe ca karapatre ca kūṭe ca śarasaṅkrame \|

maṇdūkaparṇo'raluke śoṇake ca kapītane \|\| 109 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / fragment 657

maṇḍūkaparṇī mañjiṣṭhābrāhmīgojihvakāsu ca \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaṣaṭkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaṣaṭkam / fragment 658

dohadalakṣaṇaṃ garbhe vayaḥsandhau ca dṛśyate \|

yauvanalakṣaṇā'bhikhyā\[ \]lāvaṇye ca payodhare \|\| 110

iti ṇāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 1 \[unit 659\]

tataṃ vitatavadvyāpte vistṛte vācyavanmatam \|

tataṃ vīṇādivādye ca pavamāne tataḥ punaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 2 \[unit 660\]

itaṃ gate syādvijñāte rataṃ surataguhyayoḥ \|

itamuñchaśile toye dīpte satye'bhipūjite \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 3 \[unit 661\]

kṛtaṃ yuge'pi paryāpte vihite hiṃsite phale \|

ghṛtamājye pradīpte ca salile ca ghṛtaṃ matam \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 4 \[unit 662\]

mṛtaṃ syādyācite mṛtyau mṛtyumatyabhidheyavat \|

gatantu yātrādhigate mataṃ pūjitasandhite \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 5 \[unit 663\]

dhutaṃ tyakte vibhūte ca dhūtaṃ kampitabhartsite \|

pūtaṃ pavitre raṭite pūtañca bahulīkṛte \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 6 \[unit 664\]

śrutaṃ śāstrāvadhṛtayoḥ potamotaprasūtayoḥ \|

nataṃ syādvācyavannamre natastagarapādape \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 7 \[unit 665\]

drutaṃ śīghre vilīne ca vidrāve cābhidheyavat \|

yutaṃ hastacatuṣke syādyutaṃ yukte yutaṃ pṛthak \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 8 \[unit 666\]

sthitaṃ bhavedgatyabhāve supratijñe suniścite \|

sitaḥ samāpte dhavale nibaddhajñātayorapi \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 9 \[unit 667\]

śitaṃ śātañca niśite kṛśe śāntaśca śarmaṇi \|

hitaṃ dhṛte gate pathye sutaḥ pārthivaputrayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 10 \[unit 668\]

plutaṃ turaṅgamagatau plutamāhustrimātrake \|

protantu gumphite vastre preto bhūtāntare mṛte \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 11 \[unit 669\]

sūtastu sārathau tākṣṇi prasūte prerite'pi ca \|

kṣatriyād brāhmaṇīje'pi sūtaḥ pāradabandinoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 12 \[unit 670\]

śvetaṃ rūpye'nyavacchukle śveto dvīpādribhedayoḥ \|

śvetā varāṭikā kāṣṭhapāṭalīśaṅkhinīṣu ca \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 13 \[unit 671\]

sat praśaste vidyamāne satyābhihitasādhuṣu \|

satī patīvratāgauryoretaḥ karbūra āgate \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 14 \[unit 672\]

tāto'nukampye janake jātaṃ jātyoghajanmasu \|

pātaḥ syāt patane trāte ghātaḥ kāṇḍaprahārayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 15 \[unit 673\]

potaḥ śiśau vahitre ca late śmaśruṇi corite \|

gītantu śabdite gāne prītaṃ hṛṣitanarmaṇoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 16 \[unit 674\]

vītañcāsārahastya'śve vītamaṅkuśakarmaṇi \|

bhītaṃ bhaye bhayayute vācyavat parikīrttitam \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 17 \[unit 675\]

bhūtaṃ kṣmādau piśācādau nyāyye satyopamānayoḥ \|\|

bhūto devalake prāptavṛttayorabhidheyavat \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 18 \[unit 676\]

keturdyutau patākāyāṃ grahotpātārilakṣyasu \|

seturālau ca varuṇe kraturyajñe munerbhidi \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 19 \[unit 677\]

dhātuḥ syādaśmavikṛtau viṣayeṣvindriyeṣu ca \|

bhūvādirasaraktādiśleṣmādivasudhādiṣu \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 20 \[unit 678\]

varttate dhātuśabdo'yaṃ viśeṣādasthni gairike \|

ṛtuḥ strīkusume māsi vasantādiṣu dhārayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 21 \[unit 679\]

gātuḥ puṃskokile bhṛṅge gandharve roṣaṇe'pi ca \|

mantuḥ syādaparādhe'pi manuṣye'pi prajāpatau \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 22 \[unit 680\]

dhūrttūḥ syāt khaṇḍalavaṇe khaladhuttūrayorapi \|

pūttastu pūrite channe pūrtaṃ khātādikarmaṇi \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 23 \[unit 681\]

mūrttaṃ mūrttimita\[?\] proktaṃ mūrcchāle kaṭhine'pi ca \|

garttastrigarttabhede syādavaṭe ca kukundare \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 24 \[unit 682\]

āptaḥ sabhye ca labdhe ca prāptaṃ vitte samañjase \|

vyāptaṃ khyāte samākrānte guptaṃ rakṣitagūḍhayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 25 \[unit 683\]

liptaṃ viṣāpte gupte ca vācyavat syādvilepite \|

dīptaṃ nirbhāsite dagdhe vadanti jvalite'pi ca \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 26 \[unit 684\]

astaṃ kṣipte gate proktamastaḥ paścimabhūdhare \|

grastaṃ grāsīkṛte'pi syālluptavarṇapadodite \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 27 \[unit 685\]

pustantu pustake lekhyakarmavijñānayorapi \|

śastaṃ kṣeme praśaste ca vyaktaṃ vyāpte ca saṅkule \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 28 \[unit 686\]

hasto nakṣatrabhede syāt karebhakarayorapi \|

kacātparaḥ kalāpārthe saprakoṣṭhe tatāṅgulau \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 29 \[unit 687\]

vittaṃ khyāte dhane labdhe vittaṃ jñāte vicārite \|

kṛttantu ceṣṭite chinne dattaṃ viśrāṇite'rcite \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 30 \[unit 688\]

vṛtto'tīte dṛḍhe khyāte varttule'pi vṛte mṛte \|

vācyavadvarttate vṛttaṃ caritracchandasorapi \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 31 \[unit 689\]

antaḥ prānte'ntike nāśe svarūpe'timanohare \|

vṛntaṃ prasavabandhe syādghaṭadhārākucāgrayoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 32 \[unit 690\]

danto nikuñje daśane dantaḥ sānuni kathyate \|

dantī syādoṣadhībhede kāntacetasi gahvare \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 33 \[unit 691\]

kuntaḥ prāpte caṇḍabhāve kṣudrajantau gavedhuke \|

kuntī pāṇḍupriyāyāñca śallakyāṃ gugguludrume \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 34 \[unit 692\]

kānto'śmani gṛhe cārau kāntānuphalinīstriyoḥ \|

śānto rasaviśeṣe syādatibhuktakadāntayoḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 35 \[unit 693\]

uktamekākṣaraṃ chandasyuktaṃ syādbhāṣite'pi ca \|

śukto'mle paruṣe pūte vyaktaṃ sphuṭamanīṣiṇoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 36 \[unit 694\]

tikto rase sugandhe ca tiktaṃ parpaṭake smṛtam \|

tiktā tu kaṭurohiṇyāṃ riktaṃ kānanaśūnyayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 37 \[unit 695\]

raktaṃ syāt kuṅkume tāmre pracīnāmalake'sṛji \|

anurāgiṇi nīlyādirañjite lohite'nyavat \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 38 \[unit 696\]

yantā hastipake sūte vaptā pitari pāvake \|

tretā yuge'gnitritaye bharttā svāmini dhārake \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 39 \[unit 697\]

śāstā ca śāsake buddhe vaktā vāgmini paṇḍite \|

muktā tu mauktike muktaḥ prāptamukte ca mocane \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 40 \[unit 698\]

kṣattā śūdrāt kṣatriyāje pratīhāre ca sārathau \|

bhujiṣyatanaye kṣattā niyukte ca prajāsṛji \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 41 \[unit 699\]

dhātā hiraṇyagarbhe syāt pālake tvabhidheyavat \|

mātā kātyāprasūgoṣu brahmaṇyādyāstu mātaraḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 42 \[unit 700\]

latā jyotiṣmatī spṛkkā śākhāvallīpriyaṅguṣu \|\|

latā\[ \]kastūrikāyāñca mādhavīdūrvayorapi \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 43 \[unit 701\]

lūtā pipīlikāyāṃ syādūrṇanābhe gadāntare \|

pītā smṛtā haridrāyāṃ pītaṃ syādgaurapītayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 44 \[unit 702\]

sītā rāmakalatre syāt tathā lāṅgalapaddhatau \|

nabhonadyāñca śītastu vānīrabahuvārayoḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 45 \[unit 703\]

śītaṃ himaguṇe proktaṃ vācyavacchītale'lase \|

citā cchanne cullikāyāṃ citā saṃhaticintayoḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 46 \[unit 704\]

vārttā vātiṅgaṇe vṛttau vārttā kṛṣyādyudantayoḥ \|

vṛttimannīrajorvārttā vārttamārogyaphalgunoḥ \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 47 \[unit 705\]

gatirmārge daśāyāñca jñāne yātrābhyupāyayoḥ \|

nāḍīvraṇasaraṇyāñca matirhrīcchāsmṛtiṣvapi \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 48 \[unit 706\]

citiḥ samūhe cintāyāṃ ditiḥ syāt khaṇḍane ditau \|

patiḥ prabhau gatau mūle dṛtiścarmmapuṭe jhaṣe \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 49 \[unit 707\]

yatirnikāre yatini pāṭhacchedakrame yatiḥ \|

śitiḥ kṛṣṇe śitirbhūrjje bhṛtirbharaṇamūlyayoḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 50 \[unit 708\]

kṣitirnivāse medinyāṃ sthānamātre'pi ca kṣitiḥ \|

dhṛtiryogāntare dhairye dhāraṇādhvaratuṣṭiṣu \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 51 \[unit 709\]

śrutiḥ śrotre tathā''mnāye vārttāyāṃ śrotrakarmaṇi \|

smṛtiricchādhiyordharmasaṃhitāyāmapi smṛtiḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 52 \[unit 710\]

ratiḥ smarapriyāyāñca rāge ca ramaṇe ratiḥ \|

ṛtirgatau jugupsāyāṃ sparddhāmaṅgalayorapi \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 53 \[unit 711\]

vṛttiḥ syādvaraṇe vāṭe mṛtirgaganamārgayoḥ \|

mitirmāne'pyavacchede kṛtiḥ karaṇahiṃsayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 54 \[unit 712\]

ītiḥ pravāse ḍimbe syādativṛṣṭyādiṣaṭsu ca \|

vītirgatau ca dīptau ca dhāvane prajane'śane \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 55 \[unit 713\]

gītiśchandasi gāne ca nītistu prāpaṇe naye \|

rītiḥ prasāre syande ca lohakiṭṭārakūṭayoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 56 \[unit 714\]

pītiḥ pāne turaṅge ca bhītiḥ sādhvasakampayoḥ \|

prītiryogāntare premṇi smarapatnīpramodayoḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 57 \[unit 715\]

bhūtirmātaṅgaśṛṅgāre jātau bhasmani sampadi \|

syūtiḥ sīvanasantatyorūtiḥ smṛtau ca rakṣaṇe \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 58 \[unit 716\]

hetiḥ syādāyudhe jvāle hetistaraṇitejasi \|

vṛttirvivaraṇe jīvye kauśikyādiṣu ceṣyate \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 59 \[unit 717\]

kṛttiścarmatvaco bhūrjje kṛttikāyāñca kīrttitā \|

pattiḥ padātau gamane vīrasainyaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 60 \[unit 718\]

vittirjñāne vicāre ca lābhasambhavayorapi \|

bhittiḥ kuḍye pradeśe ca saṃvibhāgāvakāśayoḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 61 \[unit 719\]

āptiḥ saṃvaraṇaprāptyoḥ prāptirlābhe mahodaye \|

guptiḥ kārāgṛhe proktā bhūgarte rakṣaṇe yame \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 62 \[unit 720\]

suptiḥ sparśajñatāyāñca svāpe viśrambhaghātini \|

śāntiḥ śame'pi kalyāṇe kāntiḥ śobhābhilāṣayoḥ \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 63 \[unit 721\]

jātiśchandasi sāmānye mālatyāṃ gotrajanmanoḥ \|

jātirjātīphale dhātryāṃ cullīkampillayorapi \|\| 63 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 64 \[unit 722\]

jñātiḥ sagotre pitari vyāptirvyāpanalambhayoḥ \|

sātirdāne'vasāne ca prāptiḥ pūrttipradeśayoḥ \|\| 64 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 65 \[unit 723\]

varttirdīpadaśādīpagātrānulepanīṣu ca \|

varttirbheṣajanirmmāṇanayanāñjanalekhayoḥ \|\| 65 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 66 \[unit 724\]

arttiḥ pīḍādhanuṣkoṭyormūrttiḥ kāṭhinyakāyayoḥ \|

kīrttiḥ prasādayaśasorvistāre karddame'pi ca \|\| 66 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 67 \[unit 725\]

bhrāntirmithyāmatau khyātā bhramaṇe cā'navasthitau \|

nṛtiḥ kṛmau narttane ca sannaḥ sariti bartmani \|\| 67 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 68 \[unit 726\]

bhaktirvibhāge sevāyāṃ paṅktirgauravapākayoḥ \|

muktiḥ syānmocane mokṣe yuktirnyāye ca yojane \|\| 68 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 69 \[unit 727\]

paṅktirdaśākṣaracchandodaśasaṅkhadhāvalīṣvapi \|

śaktirvale prabhāvādau śaktiḥ praharaṇāntare \|\| 69 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 70 \[unit 728\]

śuktiḥ kapālaśakale nakhyaśvāvarttayorapi \|

muktāsphoṭe ca durnāmā\[?\] śaṅkhaśaṅkhanakheṣu ca \|\| 70 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 71 \[unit 729\]

amṛtaṃ yajñaśeṣe syāt pīyūṣe salile ghṛte \|

ayācite ca mokṣe ca dhanvantarisuparvaṇoḥ \|\| 71 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 72 \[unit 730\]

syādamṛto'mṛtāpathyāguḍūcyāmalakīṣu ca \|

anṛtaṃ kṛṣāvasatye'pyajitaḥ kamalāpatau \|\| 72 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 73 \[unit 731\]

anirjjite cācyutastu sthire syādgaruḍadhvaje \|

ardditaṃ yācite jñeyaṃ vātavyādhau ca hiṃsite \|\| 73 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 74 \[unit 732\]

akṣatañcāpi lājeṣu tṛtīyaprakṛtāvapi \|

ahiṃsite'pi cāpātastadātve patane'pi ca \|\| 74 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 75 \[unit 733\]

āghrātaṃ śiṅghite krānte'pyākhyātaṃ bhāṣite tiṅi \|

ādhmātaṃ pavanavyādhau dagdhaśabditayorapi \|\| 75 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 76 \[unit 734\]

āplutaḥ snātake snāte'pyāhṛtaḥ sādare'rccite \|

ācitaḥ śakaṭonmeye palānāmayutadvaye \|\| 76 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 77 \[unit 735\]

channe'pi saṅgṛhīte syādāhatantu mṛṣārthake \|

āhataṃ guṇite'pi syāt tāḍite nāmite'pi ca \|\| 77 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 78 \[unit 736\]

syāt purātanavastre ca nūtanavastre tvāhatam \|

ānartto deśabhede syānnṛtyasthāne jane raṇe \|\| 78 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 79 \[unit 737\]

āvartta āvarttane syāccintane cāmbhasāmbhrame \|\|

saṃvarttaḥ pralaye prokto bibhītakatarāvapi \|\| 79 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 80 \[unit 738\]

trigarttaḥ syājjanapade trigartto gaṇitāntare \|

bhaved ghargharikāyāntu trigarttā kāmukastriyām \|\| 80 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 81 \[unit 739\]

vivarttaḥ samudāye syādapavarttananṛtyayoḥ \|

uttaptaṃ śuṣkamāṃse'pi santapte ca pariplave \|\| 81 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 82 \[unit 740\]

ucitaṃ proktamabhyaste mite jñāte samañjase \|

udvṛttaṃ syāduttulite parimuktojjhite'pi ca \|\| 82 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 83 \[unit 741\]

utthitaṃ vṛddhayukte ca prodyatotpannayorapi \|

uṣitaṃ vyuṣite dagdhe muṣitaṃ khaṇḍite hṛte \|\| 83 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 84 \[unit 742\]

uddhātaḥ kathyate pādaskhalane samupakrame \|

pavanābhyāsayogasya kumbhakāditraye'pi ca \|\| 84 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 85 \[unit 743\]

uttuṅge mudgare'pi syāduditaṃ prokta udgate \|

syāducchritantu sañjāte samunnaddhapravṛddhayoḥ \|\| 85 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 86 \[unit 744\]

rajataṃ viśade dantidantayostārahārayoḥ \|

surataṃ syānnidhuvane devatve suratā matā \|\| 86 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 87 \[unit 745\]

prahataṃ vijite kṣuṇṇe saṃhataṃ saṃhate dṛḍhe \|

rasitantu suvarṇādikhacite stanite rute \|\| 87 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 88 \[unit 746\]

palitaṃ śailaje tāpe pāṇḍukeśe ca kardame \|

lalitaṃ hārabhede syādīpsite lalite'pi ca \|\| 88 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 89 \[unit 747\]

skhalitaṃ calite bhraṃśe kalitaṃ viditāptayoḥ \|

jvalitaṃ bhāsvare dagdhe tvaritaṃ prajave hute \|\| 89 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 90 \[unit 748\]

stimito'cañcalaklinne haṃsitaṃ kṣiptadagdhayoḥ \|

suhito'tihite tṛpte mūrcchitaṃ socchraye dṛḍhe \|\| 90 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 91 \[unit 749\]

hṛṣitaṃ vismite prīte praṇate hṛṣṭaromaṇi \|

garjjito mattamātaṅge garjitaṃ jaladadhvanau \|\| 91 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 92 \[unit 750\]

varddhitaṃ prasute cchinne pūrite cāpi varddhitam \|

sthāpitaṃ niścite nyaste ceṅgitaṃ gaticeṣṭayoḥ \|\| 92 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 93 \[unit 751\]

veṣṭitaṃ rasake ruddhe strīṇāñca karaṇāntare \|

bhāvitaṃ vāsite prāpte viditaṃ viditārthayoḥ \|\| 93 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 94 \[unit 752\]

vellitaṃ kuṭile proktaṃ vācyavadvidhṛte plute \|

grathitaṃ gumphitākrāntahiṃsiteṣu samīritam \|\| 94 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 95 \[unit 753\]

prathitaṃ nirjjalodaśvityāloḍitanighṛṣṭayoḥ \|

prārthitaṃ yācite śatrusaṃruddhe'bhihite'pi ca \|\| 95 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 96 \[unit 754\]

jṛmbhitaṃ karaṇe strīṇāmīhāsphuṭitayorapi \|

kranditaṃ khyātamāhvāne kranditaṃ rudite'pi ca \|\| 96 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 97 \[unit 755\]

prokṣitaṃ nihate kṣipte vāpitaṃ muṇḍitaṃ matam \|

bījākṛte daṃśitastu duṣṭacarmmitayorapi \|\| 97 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 98 \[unit 756\]

kṣāritaḥ srāvite sāre cābhiśaste ca saṃśitaḥ \|

sukṛtañca śubhe puṇye suvidhāne ca vācyavat \|\| 98 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 99 \[unit 757\]

nikṛtaṃ vipralabdhaṃ syānnīce viprakṛte'pi ca \|

vikṛtau saṃskṛte'pi syād bībhatse rogite'pi ca \|\| 99 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 100 \[unit 758\]

saṃskṛtaḥ kṛtrime śaste bhūṣite bhedyavanmataḥ \|

saṃskṛtaṃ lakṣaṇopetā prabhūtaṃ prājya udgate \|\| 100 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 101 \[unit 759\]

prasṛtaḥ saprasāre syādvinīte vigate'pi ca \|

arddhāñjalau ca prasṛtaṃ jaṅghāyāṃ prasṛtā matā \|\| 101 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 102 \[unit 760\]

sūnṛtaṃ maṅgale satyapriyavāci ca sūnṛtam \|

prasūtaṃ vācyavajjāte prasūtaṃ kusume'pi ca \|\| 102 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 103 \[unit 761\]

visṛtaṃ jñātasaṃhṛṣṭapratīteṣvabhidheyavat \|

nirmuktastyaktasaṅge syānmuktakañcukabhogini \|\| 103 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 104 \[unit 762\]

avyaktaḥ śaṅkare viṣṇāvavyaktaṃ mahadādike \|\|

ātmanyapi syādavyaktamasphuṭe cā'bhidheyavat \|\| 104 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 105 \[unit 763\]

viviktaṃ syādasampṛkte vivekānvitapūtayoḥ \|

vācyavaddarśito dhīrairviviktaṃ rahasi smṛtam \|\| 105 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 106 \[unit 764\]

rohitaṃ rudhire dhīra ṛjuśakraśarāsane \|

rohito mānamṛgayorbhede rohitakadrute \|\| 106 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 107 \[unit 765\]

lohitaṃ kaṅkume rakte gośīrṣe ca kucandane \|

lohitaḥ syānnade bhaume varṇabhede tu vācyavat \|\| 107 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 108 \[unit 766\]

kuṭitaṃ syāddhastapuṭe pāṭitasūtayorapi \|

pīḍitaṃ bādhite strīṇāṃ karaṇe cāpi yantrite \|\| 108 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 109 \[unit 767\]

piṇḍitaṃ gaṇite sāndre paṇḍitaḥ sallake kavau \|

hārītaḥ pakṣibhede syānmunibhede'pi kaitave \|\| 109 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 110 \[unit 768\]

praṇītaḥ saṃskṛte vahnau vihite ca praveśite \|

nikṣipte copasampanne praṇīto bhedyavanmataḥ \|\| 110 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 111 \[unit 769\]

pratītaḥ sādare jñāte prakhyātaḥ khyātahṛṣṭayoḥ \|

vācyavat syāt pramītantu prokṣite'pi mṛte'pi ca \|\| 111 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 112 \[unit 770\]

vinītamupanīte syādapanīte jitendriye \|

vāṇije suvahe'śve ca nibhṛte cābhidheyavat \|\| 112 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 113 \[unit 771\]

pṛṣato hariṇe vindau pṛṣataścāśvarohite \|

bhavet pṛṣadiva śvete binduyuktaṃ tu vācyavat \|\| 113 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 114 \[unit 772\]

bharato vādyabhede'pi dauṣyantau sañcare naṭe \|

rāmānuje ca bharatastantuvāye'pi ca kacit \|\| 114 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 115 \[unit 773\]

bhārataṃ granthabhede syājjambūdvīpe'pi bhāratam \|

bhāratī tu sarasvatyāṃ pakṣiṇīvṛttibhedayoḥ \|\| 115 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 116 \[unit 774\]

durjjātaṃ vyasane proktamasamyagjātavastuni \|

pareto bhūtabhede syāt pareto vācyavanmṛte \|\| 116 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 117 \[unit 775\]

kapotaḥ pārāvate syāt kavakākhyavihaṅgame \|

kāpotamañjane'pi syāt kapotānāñca saṃhatau \|\| 117 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 118 \[unit 776\]

vyāyataṃ vyāpṛte dīrghe dṛḍhe cātiśaye'nyavat \|

vyāghāto yogabhede syādanvarāyaprahārayoḥ \|\| 118 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 119 \[unit 777\]

saṅghātaḥ saṃhatau ghāte saṅghāto narakāntare \|

raivatastu suvarṇālau śailabhede'pi śūlini \|\| 119 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 120 \[unit 778\]

syāt kirātastu bhūnimbe mlecche cālpatanāvapi \|

mandākinyāṃ kirātī syāt kuṭṭinyāmapi dṛśyate \|\| 120 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 121 \[unit 779\]

āsphītaścārkaparṇe syādāsphītaḥ kovidārake \|

āsphītā girikarṇyāṃ syādvanamallyāñca viśrutā \|\| 121 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 122 \[unit 780\]

udāttaḥ svarabhede syāt kāvyālaṅkārahṛdyayoḥ \|

udātto dātṛmahatornimittaṃ hetulakṣmaṇoḥ \|\| 122 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 123 \[unit 781\]

unmatta unmādavati dhuttūramucukundayoḥ \|

parvataḥ śailadevarṣau prapāto nirjhare bhṛgau \|\| 123 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 124 \[unit 782\]

paryāptantu yatheṣṭe syāt tṛptau śakte nivāraṇe \|

jīmūtaḥ syād dhṛtikare śakre'drau ghoṣake ghane \|\| 124 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 125 \[unit 783\]

jīvāturjīvite bhakte jīvāturjīvanauṣadhe \|

edhatuḥ puruṣe vahnau vāhetuḥ pathike vṛṣe \|\| 125 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 126 \[unit 784\]

vihasto vihvale paṇḍe paryastaḥ patite hate \|

āyastaḥ stimite kṣipte kleśite kupite hate \|\| 126 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 127 \[unit 785\]

nirastaḥ kleśiteṣau syānniṣṭhyūte tvaritodite \|

santyakte ca pratihate viśvasto nibhṛte smṛtaḥ \|\| 127 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 128 \[unit 786\]

viśvāsayoge viśvasto viśvastā vidhavāstriyām \|

nivāto dṛḍhasannāhe vātānte cāśraye'pi ca \|\| 128 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 129 \[unit 787\]

anantaḥ śeṣaviṣṇvoḥ syādanantaḥ suravartmani \|

anantaścāpyanavadhau vācyavat samudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 129 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 130 \[unit 788\]

anantā śārivādūrvāviśalyālāṅgalīṣu ca \|

anantā haimavatyāñca guḍūcyāñca nirūpyate \|\| 130 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 131 \[unit 789\]

śakuntaḥ pakṣibhede syādbhāsapakṣivihaṅgayoḥ \|

dṛṣṭāntaḥ śāstra ubhayodāharaṇe prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 131 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 132 \[unit 790\]

kṛtānto yamasiddhāntadaivākuśalakarmmasu \|

niśāntaḥ kathitaḥ śānte niśāntaṃ bhavanoṣasoḥ \|\| 132 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 133 \[unit 791\]

vṛttānto'vasare bhāvakārtsnyavārttāviśeṣayoḥ \|

vṛttāntaḥ prakriyāyāñca kvacidekāntavācakaḥ \|\| 133 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 134 \[unit 792\]

udvānto nirmadagaje samudgīrṇe ca vācyavat \|

śuddhānto'ntaḥpure rājño rahaḥkakṣāntare'pi ca \|\| 134 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 135 \[unit 793\]

asvantamaśubhe cullyāṃ maraṇe'navadhāvapi \|

kṣetre'pi kathyate'svantamudantaḥ sādhuvārttayoḥ \|\| 135 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 136 \[unit 794\]

jayantaḥ śaṅkare'pi syājjayantaḥ pākaśāsanau \|

jayantī pādape gauryāmindraputrīpatākayoḥ \|\| 136 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 137 \[unit 795\]

godanto haritāle syāddaṃśite harite'pi ca \|

bhāsantaḥ sundarākāre bhāsanto bhāsapakṣiṇi \|\| 137 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 138 \[unit 796\]

mahadrājye viśāle ca mahatī vallakībhidi \|

maruddeve samīre syādarhan pūjye jine'pi ca \|\| 138 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 139 \[unit 797\]

jagadākhyā smṛtā vāte viṣṭape jaṅgame'pi ca \|\| ,

jagatī bhuvane kṣmāyāṃ chandobhede jane'pi ca \|\| 139 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 140 \[unit 798\]

pitsan\[t‌\] pipatiṣannetau\[?\] patanecchuvihaṅgayoḥ \|

harit kakubhi varṇe ca tṛṇavājiviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 140 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 141 \[unit 799\]

harite'pi ca dūrvāyāṃ haridvarṇayute'nyavat \|

vidyut saudāminīsandhyorniṣprabhe tvabhidheyavat \|\| 141 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 142 \[unit 800\]

garmut suvarṇalatayo rohit sūryalatābhidoḥ \|

bhūbhṛnnarendre śaile ca patan pātukapakṣiṇoḥ \|\| 142 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 143 \[unit 801\]

kurvan karmakare bhṛtye syādarvan kukṣite'nyavat \|

arvatī kumbhadāsyāñca vāmyāñca parikīrttitā \|\| 143 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 144 \[unit 802\]

śrīmāṃstinnakavṛkṣe syānmanojñe dhanake'pi ca \|

dhīmān vācaspatau dhīre bhāsvān bhāsvarasūryayoḥ \|\| 144 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 145 \[unit 803\]

suvratā puṅkhasandohyasurabhyāṃ śobhanavrate \|

vācyavadvivṛtā kṣudraroge syādvivṛtaṃ sphuṭe \|\| 145 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 146 \[unit 804\]

vanitā janitātyarthānurāgāyāñca yoṣiti \|

vanitaṃ yācite'pi syādvanitaṃ śocite'pi ca \|\| 146 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 147 \[unit 805\]

jāmātā vallabhe sūryāvartte'pi duhituḥ patau \|

vinetā deśike rājñi vidhātā vedhasi smare \|\| 147 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 148 \[unit 806\]

vinatā piṭakābhede vinatā tārkṣyamātari \|

vinataḥ praṇate bhugne śikṣite'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 148 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 149 \[unit 807\]

pañcatā pañcabhāve syāt pañcatā maraṇe'pi ca \|

vijātākhyā prasūtāyāṃ janite vikṛte'pi ca \|\| 149 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 150 \[unit 808\]

sikatā vālukāyāṃ syuḥ sikatā'pyāmayāntare \|

sikatānvitadeśe ca śarkarāyāñca kīrttitā \|\| 150 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 151 \[unit 809\]

vāsitā kariṇīnāryorvāsitaṃ surabhīkṛte \|

jñānamātre khagārāve vāsitaṃ vastraveṣṭite \|\| 151 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 152 \[unit 810\]

piśitā māṃsikāyāṃ syādāmiṣe piśitaṃ matam \|

tṛṇatā'pi tṛṇatve syāttṛṇatā kārmuke'pi ca \|\| 152 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 153 \[unit 811\]

dvijātiraṇḍaje vipre śrīpatiḥ kṣmāpatau harau \|

gopatiḥ śaṅkare ṣaṇḍe pārthive'pi vikarttane \|\| 153 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 154 \[unit 812\]

sthapatiḥ śilpibhede'pi sthapatiḥ kañcukinyapi \|

jīveṣṭiyājake sūte'pyamatiḥ kālacandrayoḥ \|\| 154 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 155 \[unit 813\]

aṅkati syādagnihotre jvalanāmbujayoniṣu \|

ramatirnāyake svarge'pyahatistyāgarāgayoḥ \|\| 155 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 156 \[unit 814\]

nirṛtiḥ syādalakṣmyāñca dikpāle nirupadrave \|

agastirvaṅgasene syādagadastiḥ kumbhasambhave \|\| 156 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 157 \[unit 815\]

gabhastiḥ kiraṇe sūrye svāhāyāñca vilokyate \|

saṅgatiḥ saṅgame jñāne sannatiḥ praṇatau dhvanau \|\| 157 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 158 \[unit 816\]

āyatiḥ saṅgame dairghye prabhāvāgāmikālayoḥ \|

āpattiḥ syād bale snehe vaśitve vāsare'pi ca \|\| 158 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 159 \[unit 817\]

maryādāyāṃ tathā''pattirvipattiryātanāpadoḥ \|

niyatirniyame daive paddhatiḥ paṅktivartmanoḥ \|\| 159 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 160 \[unit 818\]

santatiḥ paṅktivistāragotreṣu kavibhiḥ smṛtā \|

paramparābhave'pi syāt santatiḥ putrakanyayoḥ \|\| 160 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 161 \[unit 819\]

sammatiḥ syādanujñāne'pyabhilāṣe'pi sammatiḥ \|

samitiḥ saṅgare sāmye sabhāyāmapi saṅgame \|\| 161 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 162 \[unit 820\]

aditirvasudhāyāṃ syādaditirdevamātari \|

vasatiḥ syādavasthāne yāminyāñca niketane \|\| 162 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 163 \[unit 821\]

pakṣatiḥ pakṣamūle syāt pakṣatiḥ pratipattithau \|

unnatistārkṣyadāreṣu samṛddhāvudaye'pi ca \|\| 163 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 164 \[unit 822\]

prasūtistanayotpattau tathā duhitari smṛtā \|

vratatirvistṛtau vallyāṃ vahatiḥ sacive gavi \|\| 164 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 165 \[unit 823\]

durgatirnarake naiḥsve vikṛtirḍimbarogayoḥ \|

nikṛtirbhartsane kṣepe vadanti śaṭhaśāṭyayoḥ \|\| 165 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 166 \[unit 824\]

ākṛtiḥ kathitārūpasāmānyavapuṣorapi \|

prakṛtiḥ sahaje yonāvamātye paramātmani \|\| 166 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 167 \[unit 825\]

nirvṛttiḥ svasthatāyāṃ syādastaṅgamanasaukhyayoḥ \|

sunītiḥ śobhananaye sunītirdhruvamātari \|\| 167 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 168 \[unit 826\]

pravṛttiḥ kathitā vṛttau pravāhodantayorapi \|

vicchittiraṅgarāge syāddhāvavicchedayorapi \|\| 168 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 169 \[unit 827\]

saṃvittiḥ pratipattau syādavivāde janasya ca \|

āpattiḥ prāpaṇe doṣe cāsattiḥ saṅghalābhayoḥ \|\| 169 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 170 \[unit 828\]

paryāptiḥ syāt prakāme'pi prāptau ca parirakṣaṇe \|

samāptiścāvasāne syāt samāptiśca samarthane \|\| 170 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 171 \[unit 829\]

bṛhatī kṣudravārttākyāṃ kaṇṭakāryāñca vāci ca \|

vāridhānyāṃ mahatyāñca chandovasanabhedayoḥ \|\| 171 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 172 \[unit 830\]

mālatī jātiyuvatījyotsnāsu saridantare \|

kākamācyāṃ viśālāyāṃ vibhāvaryāmapīṣyate \|\| 172 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 173 \[unit 831\]

bhavatī bāṇabhede syādanyavad vatsadartha\[?\]yoḥ \|

revatī halipatnyāṃ syāt tārābhede'pi mātṛṣu \|\| 173 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 174 \[unit 832\]

pārvatī śallakīdurgājropadījīvinīṣu ca \|

sravantī ca dravantī ca saridoṣadhibhedayoḥ \|\| 174 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 175 \[unit 833\]

jīvantī jīvinīśamyorguḍūcīvṛndayorapi \|

hasantyaṅgāradhānyāñca mallikāśākinībhidoḥ \|\| 175 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 176 \[unit 834\]

vāsantī mādhavīyūthyoḥ pāṭalāyāṃ kramelake \|

vāsantī kokile'pi syādvāsanto'vihite viṭe \|\| 176 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 177 \[unit 835\]

bhavedavasitaṃ jñāte gatau ruddhāvasānayoḥ \|

avadātaṃ site pīte viśuddhe pravare'pi ca \|\| 177 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 178 \[unit 836\]

avagītantu nirvāde muhurdṛṣṭe vigarhite \|

antargataṃ vismṛte syānmadhyaprāpte ca kathyate \|\| 178 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 179 \[unit 837\]

aṅgāritaṃ palāśīyakalikodgamadagdhayoḥ \|

apāvṛtaḥ svatantre syāt pihite cā'pyapāvṛtaḥ \|\| 179 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 180 \[unit 838\]

atyāhitaṃ mahābhītau jīvanopekṣakarmaṇi \|

upāhito'nalotpāta āhite cāpyupāhitaḥ \|\| 180 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 181 \[unit 839\]

upākṛto'dhvarahatapaśūpadrutayormataḥ \|

udāsthitaḥ pratīhāre'dhyakṣe ca praṇidhau care \|\| 181 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 182 \[unit 840\]

abhinīto bhavennyāyye saṃskṛtāmarṣiṇorapi \|

abhijātaḥ smṛto nyāyye kulīnaprāptarūpayoḥ \|\| 182 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 183 \[unit 841\]

pārijātaḥ suratarau mandāre pāribhadrake \|

bhavetparigataṃ lābhe prayāte veṣṭite'pi ca \|\| 183 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 184 \[unit 842\]

khyātaṃ praṇihitaṃ labdhe vinyaste ca samāhite \|

bhavet pratihito'dviṣṭe pratiskhalitaruddhayoḥ \|\| 184 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 185 \[unit 843\]

ullikhitaṃ samutkīrṇe vācyavacca tanūkṛte \|

vidyādupacitaṃ digdhe samṛddhe ca samāhite \|\| 185 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 186 \[unit 844\]

samuddhataḥ samudgīrṇe'pyavinīte samuddhataḥ \|

kuharitaṃ pikālāpe raṭite ratanisvane \|\| 186 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 187 \[unit 845\]

bhavet pallavitaṃ lākṣārakte saprasave tate \|

ujjṛmbhitantu ceṣṭāyāmutphulle cābhidheyavat \|\| 187 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 188 \[unit 846\]

udgrāhitamupanyaste baddhagrāhitayorapi \|

nistuṣitaṃ nistvaci syādvarjjite ca laghūkṛte \|\| 188 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 189 \[unit 847\]

airāvato'bhramātaṅganāraṅgalikucāhiṣu \|

airāvataṃ mahendrasya ṛjudīrghaśarāsane \|\| 189 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 190 \[unit 848\]

airāvatī syāttaḍiti saridbhede'pi lakṣyate \|

kaladhautaṃ rūpyahemnoḥ kaladhautaṃ kaladhvanau \|\| 190 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 191 \[unit 849\]

divābhītaḥ kumbhile syādulūke kumudākare \|\|

pāśupato vakapuṣpe paśupatyadhidaivate \|\| 191 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 192 \[unit 850\]

niṣkāsito nirgamite'pyūhite dhikkṛte'pi ca \|

vyatīpāto mahotpāte yogabhedāpayānayoḥ \|\| 192 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 193 \[unit 851\]

samāghāto vadhe yuddheparighāto'straghātayoḥ \|

vinipāto'vapāte syāddaivādivyasane'pi ca \|\| 193 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 194 \[unit 852\]

samāhitaḥ pratijñāte samādhisthe yatātmani \|

vācyavannihite siddhe samādhāne samāhitam \|\| 194 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 195 \[unit 853\]

nandyāvarttaḥ smṛto veśmaprabhede tagaradrume \|

parivartto vinimaye kūrmarāje palāyane \|\| 195 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 196 \[unit 854\]

abhiyuktaḥ parai ruddhe tathā syādatitatpare \|

atimuktastu vāsantyāṃ tiniśe niṣkale'pi ca \|\| 196 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 197 \[unit 855\]

uparaktaḥ saihikeye tadgrahe vyasanāture \|

sūryyabhakto bandhujīve tathā bhāskarapūjake \|\| 197 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 198 \[unit 856\]

nadīkāntaḥ samudre syāt sinduvāre'pi hijjale \|

nadīkāntā smṛtā jambūkākajaṅghālatāsu ca \|\| 198 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 199 \[unit 857\]

nāgadanto dviparade gṛhānnitadāruṇi \|

nāgadantī tu kumbhāyāṃ śrīhastinyāmapīṣyate \|\| 199 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 200 \[unit 858\]

puṣpadantastu diṅnāge nāgavidyādharāntare \|

candrakānto'śmabhede syāccandrakāntaśca kairave \|\| 200 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 201 \[unit 859\]

vaijayanto gṛhe śakraprāsādadhvajayorapi \|

vaijayantī patākāyāṃ jayantī\[ \]vahnimanthayoḥ \|\| 201 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 202 \[unit 860\]

puraskṛtaṃ skīkṛtābhiśastayo'rabhidheyavat \|

agre kṛtābhyarccitayoḥ sikte cāpi puraskṛtam \|\| 202 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 203 \[unit 861\]

avadhvastaṃ parityakte nindite'pyavacūrṇite \|

dhūmaketuḥ smṛto vahnāvutpātagrahabhedayoḥ \|\| 203 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 204 \[unit 862\]

citraguptaḥ kṛtānte syāllekhake cāsya sammataḥ \|

pañcaguptastu cārvākadarśane kamaṭhe'pi ca \|\| 204 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 205 \[unit 863\]

adhikṣiptaḥ praṇihite tathā nirbhartsite'pi ca \|

pratikṣiptaṃ pratihate preṣite ca nirākṛte \|\| 205 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 206 \[unit 864\]

āyuṣmān yogabhede syādāyuṣmāṃścirajīvini \|

vivasvān vibudhe bhānau bhagavān buddhapūjyayoḥ \|\| 206 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 207 \[unit 865\]

dvīpavān sindhunadayordvīpavatyāpagābhuvoḥ \|

garutmān vihage tārkṣye saṅkhyāvān paṇḍite mite \|\| 207 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 208 \[unit 866\]

aṃśumān bhāskare śālaparṇyāmaṃśumatī matā \|

arthapatiḥ kuvere syādīśvare'rthapatistathā \|\| 208 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 209 \[unit 867\]

gṛhapatirgṛhasthe syādatyādhāne ca satriṇi \|

senāpatiḥ kārttikeye'pyanīkādhikṛte'pi ca \|\| 209 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 210 \[unit 868\]

lakṣmīpatirvāsudeve lavaṅgadrumapūgayoḥ \|

prajāpatirvidhau bhūpe himārātiḥ khage'nale \|\| 210 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 211 \[unit 869\]

vanaspatirvṛkṣabhātre vinā puṣpaṃ phaladrume \|

sadāgatiḥ syāt pavane nirvāṇe ca sadīśvare \|\| 211 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 212 \[unit 870\]

divākīrttistu cāṇḍāle nāpitolūkayorapi \|

śatadhṛtirdhātarī'ndre sudhāsūtirmakhe vidhau \|\| 212 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 213 \[unit 871\]

bhaveda'pacitiḥ pūjākṣayahāniṣu niṣkṛtau \|

anumatiḥ syādūnendupūrṇimā'nujñayorapi \|\| 213 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 214 \[unit 872\]

pratikṛtiḥ pratīkāre pratimāyāśca pūjane \|

nirākṛtiḥ pratikṣepe'pyasvādhyāye'pyanākṛtau \|\| 214 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 215 \[unit 873\]

pratipattiḥ padaprāptau pravṛttau gaurave'pi ca \|

prāgalbhye ca prabodhe ca pratipattiḥ prayujyate \|\| 215 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 216 \[unit 874\]

upasattiḥ saṅgame ca sevāyāṃ pratipādane \|\|

abhiśastiḥ smṛtā lokāpavāde prārthane'pi ca \|\| 216 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 217 \[unit 875\]

pārāvatī gopagītilavalīlatayormatā \|

pārāvataḥ kalarave śaile markaṭatinduke \|\| 217 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 218 \[unit 876\]

bhogavatī bhujaṅgānāṃ nagarīsaritorapi \|

gandhavatī surābhūmyoḥ purīyojanagandhayoḥ \|\| 218 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 219 \[unit 877\]

haimavatya'bhayāsvarṇakṣīryoḥ śvetavacomayoḥ \|

carmaṇvatī nadībhede kadalīpādape'pi ca \|\| 219 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 220 \[unit 878\]

kumudvatī kumudinyāṃ kuśapatnyāṃ kumudvatī \|

śubhradantī sudantyāṃ syāt puṣpadantebhayoṣiti \|\| 220 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 221 \[unit 879\]

sarasvatī saridbhede govāgdevatayorgiri \|

strīratne cāpagāyāñca sarasvān sindhuke'mbudhau \|\| 221 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 222 \[unit 880\]

rāgamātā'pi kuṭṭanyāṃ smṛtā bhūmiruhāmaye \|

prabhūmitā kleśitāyāṃ sūryagantavyadiśya'pi \|\| 222 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 223 \[unit 881\]

pravrajitā tapasvinyāṃ muṇḍīryāmāṃsikauṣadhau \|

ṛṣyaproktā śatāvaryyāṃ śūkaśimbyāṃ balābhidi \|\| 223 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 224 \[unit 882\]

kṛṣṇavṛntā pāṭalāyāṃ māṣaparṇyāñca bhāvitā \|

samudrāntā tu kārpāsīspṛkkādurālabhāsu ca \|\| 224 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 225 \[unit 883\]

syāda'valokito buddhe prakṣipte tvavilokitam \|

upadhūpita āsannamaraṇe paridhūpite \|\| 225 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 226 \[unit 884\]

syāda'parājito viṣṇau śrīkaṇḍe cā'parājitā \|

śvetājayantīdurgāsva'nirjjite tva'bhidheyavat \|\| 226 \|\|
