## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 227 \[unit 885\]

gaṇādhipatirvikhyātaḥ śaṅkare'pi gajānane \|

yādasāmpatirambhodhau pratīcīdikpatāvapi \|\| 227 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 228 \[unit 886\]

syāt pṛthivīpatirbhūpe ṛṣabhākhyauṣadhe'pi ca \|\|

mūrddhābhiṣikto bhūpāle pradhāne kṣatriye'pi ca \|\| 228 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 229 \[unit 887\]

vasantadūtaścūte syāt pikapañcamarāgayoḥ \|

vasantadūtī pāṭalyāṃ pratīcyāmatimuktake \|\| 229 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / taṣaṭkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / taṣaṭkam / fragment 888

arddhapārāvataścitrakaṇṭhe tittirapakṣiṇi \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tasaptakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tasaptakam / 230 \[unit 889\]

samudranavanītākhyā pīyūṣāmṛtaraciṣoḥ \|\| 230 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / tāntavargaḥ / tasaptakam / fragment 890

iti tāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 1 \[unit 891\]

rathaḥ syāt syandane kāye caraṇe vetase'pi ca \|

kuthaḥ praveṇīkuśayoḥ pītho'rke pīthamambhasi \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 2 \[unit 892\]

kothastu śaṭhite netrarugbhede manthane'pi ca \|

protho'dhvage'śvaghoṇāyāṃ kaṭīstrīgarbhayorapi \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 3 \[unit 893\]

kvāthaḥ syād dravaniṣpāke duḥkhavyasanayorapi \|

sikthaṃ nīlyāṃ madhūcchiṣṭe siktha odanasambhave \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 4 \[unit 894\]

pṛthurnṛpe kṛṣṇajīre vāpyāṃ pṛthu mahatyapi \|

duḥsthaḥ syād durgate mūrkhe prasthaḥ śikharamānayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 5 \[unit 895\]

tuttho'gnāvañjane tutthā nīlīsūkṣmailayorapi \|

manthaḥ sāktavamanthānanetrāmayadivākare \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 6 \[unit 896\]

grantho dhane syāt sandarbhe dvātriṃśadvarṇanirmmitau \|

granthiḥ parvaṇi kauṭilye granthiparṇe gadāntare \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 7 \[unit 897\]

arthaḥ prakāre viṣaye vittakāraṇavastuṣu \|

abhidheye'pi śabdānāṃ nivṛttau ca prayojane \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 8 \[unit 898\]

tīrthaṃ śāstrādhvarakṣetropāyopādhyāyamantriṣu \|\|

avatārarṣijuṣṭāmbhaḥstrīrajaḥsu ca viśrutam \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 9 \[unit 899\]

sārtho vaṇiksamūhe syāda'pi saṅghātamātrake \|

pārthastu kakubhe jiṣṇau gāthāvāgbhedavṛttayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 10 \[unit 900\]

kanthā mṛnmayabhittau syāt kanthā prāvaraṇāntare \|

āsthā cā'lambanāsthānayatnāpekṣāsu kathyate \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 11 \[unit 901\]

saṃsthā sthitau care nāśe vyaktisādṛśyayorapi \|

vīthī gṛhāṅge paṅktau ca nāṭyarūpakavartmanoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 12 \[unit 902\]

yūthī puṣpaprabhede syānmāgadhyāñca kuraṇṭake \|

yūthaṃ tiryaksamūhe syādvṛndamātre ca bhāṣitam \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 13 \[unit 903\]

manmathaḥ kāmacintāyāṃ kapitthe kusumāyudhe \|

unmāthaḥ kūṭayantre syānmāraṇe ghātake'pi ca \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 14 \[unit 904\]

pramatho'pi gaṇe proktaḥ pathyāyāṃ pramathā bhave \|

niśītho'pyarddharātre syānniśītho rātrimātrake \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 15 \[unit 905\]

viditho yogikṛtinorudrathaḥ śunikukkuṭe \|

śamathaḥ sacive śāntau damatho damadaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 16 \[unit 906\]

varūtho rathaguptau syādvarūthaṃ carmaveśmanoḥ \|

atithiḥ kuśaputre'pi kope prāghuṇake'pica \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 17 \[unit 907\]

vamathurvamane'pi syād gajasya karaśīkare \|

kṣavathuḥ kathitaḥ kāśe hikkāyāmapi dṛśyate \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 18 \[unit 908\]

samartho'pi hite śakte sambaddhārthe'pi sammataḥ \|

siddhārthaḥ śākyasiṃhe syāt siddhārthaḥ sitasarṣape \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 19 \[unit 909\]

kāyastho'pi nṛjāteḥ syāt prabhede paramātmani \|

kāyasthā tu harītakyāmāmalakyāñca darśitā \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 20 \[unit 910\]

vayaḥstho vācyavadyūni vayaḥsthā śālmalīdrume \|

brāhmīguḍūcīkākolīsūkṣmailāmalakīṣu ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 21 \[unit 911\]

aśvattho garddabhāṇḍe syāt pippale pūrṇimātithau \|

upastho meṇḍhra utsaṅge bhage pāyau ca kathyate \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 22 \[unit 912\]

nirgrantho nagnake'pi syānniḥsvabāliśayorapi \|

gogranthistu karīṣe syād goṣṭhe gojihvikauṣadhe \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 23 \[unit 913\]

avyathā tu harītakyāṃ pannage nirvyathe'pi ca \|

ṣaṅgranthā'pi vacāsiṃhyoḥ ṣaḍgrantho'pi prakīrtyate \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / 24 \[unit 914\]

citrarathaḥ syādgandharve ravau vidyādharāntare \|

catuṣpathaścaturmārgasaṅgame'pi dvije'pi ca \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / 25 \[unit 915\]

vānaprastho madhūke syāt kiṃśuke cāśramāntare \|

daśamīsthaṃ naṣṭabīje sthavire'pi pracakṣyate \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / 26 \[unit 916\]

anīkastho raṇakhale gajaśikṣāvicakṣaṇe \|

rājarakṣiṣu cihne ba vīramarddanake'pi ca \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / 27 \[unit 917\]

bhaveditikathā naṣṭadharme'pārthe vacasya'pi \|

aśraddheye'pyudarathiḥ samudre ca viyanmaṇau \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / fragment 918

iti thāntavargaḥ

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 1 \[unit 919\]

gado bhrātari viṣṇoḥ syādāmaye'pyāyudhe gadā \|

nadaḥ samudre ninade nadī tu sariti smṛtā \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 2 \[unit 920\]

mado retasi kastūryāṃ garve harṣebhadānayoḥ \|

madye'pi mada ākhyāto madī kṛṣakavastuni \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 3 \[unit 921\]

padaṃ śabde ca vākyaṃ ca vyavasāyāpadeśayoḥ \|

pādapaccihnayoḥ sthānatrāṇayoraṅkavastunoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 4 \[unit 922\]

chadaḥ palāśe garuti granthiparṇitamālayoḥ \|

rado vilekhane dante bhadraṃ kalyāṇaśarmmaṇoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 5 \[unit 923\]

bhedo dvaidhe viśeṣe syādupajāpe vidāraṇe \|

svedastu svedane gharmme śādaḥ karddamaśasyayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 6 \[unit 924\]

pādo bradhnee turīyāṃśe śailapratyantaparvate \|

caraṇe ca mayūkhe ca kṣodo rajasi peṣaṇe \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 7 \[unit 925\]

sūdastu sūpavat sūpakāre ca vyañjane'pi ca \|

svādurmanojñe mṛṣṭe ca mṛduḥ syāt komale'khare \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 8 \[unit 926\]

abdaḥ saṃvatsare meghe giribhede ca mustake \|

vindurdantacchade'pi syāttathā veditṛvimuṣoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 9 \[unit 927\]

kundaścakrabhramau mādhye nidhibhede muradviṣi \|

kandastu śaraṇe śasyamūle jaladhare'pi ca \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 10 \[unit 928\]

mandaḥ khale mandarate mūrkhe svaire'lparāgiṇoḥ \|

abhāgye'pi ca mātaṅge gajajātiprabhedayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 11 \[unit 929\]

chando vaśe'pyabhiprāye hṛdākhyācittabukkayoḥ \|

vediraṅgulamudrāyāṃ budhe'laṅkṛtabhūtale \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 12 \[unit 930\]

anduḥ syād ṣandhanadravye prabhede bhūṣaṇasya ca \|

nandirānandane dyūte pratīhāre harasya ca \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 13 \[unit 931\]

vidā jñāne ca buddhau ca dhīdā kanyāmanīṣayoḥ \|

nindā'pavāde kutsāyāṃ nandā maṇikasampadoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 14 \[unit 932\]

dhanado dātari śrīde jalado mustake'mbude \|

naladaṃ syātpuṣparase māṃsikośīrayorapi \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 15 \[unit 933\]

pramadaḥ sammade matte kāminyāṃ pramadā matā \|

viśadaḥ pāṇḍure vyakte jīvado vaidyavidviṣoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 16 \[unit 934\]

aṅgadaḥ kapibhede syāt keyūre'ṅgadamiṣyate \|

aṅgadā yāmyadigdantihastinyāṃ samudāhṛtā \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 17 \[unit 935\]

varado'pi prasanne syādvaradaḥ śāntacetasi \|

varadā'pi ca kanyāyāmāmodo gandhaharṣayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 18 \[unit 936\]

narmadaḥ kelisacive narmadā saridantare \|

kṣaṇado gaṇake rātrau kṣaṇadā kṣaṇadañjale \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 19 \[unit 937\]

kapa. khaṇḍaparaśorjaṭājūṭe varāṭake \|

āspadantu pade kṛtye niṣādaḥ śvapace svare \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 20 \[unit 938\]

prasādo'nugrahe kāvyaguṇasvāthyaprasaktiṣu \|

prasādaḥ kathyate devanaradevanivāsayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 21 \[unit 939\]

śāradaḥ pītamudge syācchālīne pratibhe nave \|

varṣe'tha jalapippalyāṃ saptaparṇe ca śāradi \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 22 \[unit 940\]

dārado viṣabhede syāddhiṅgule pārade'pi ca \|

toyadantu ghṛte proktaṃ toyado mustakābdayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 23 \[unit 941\]

nirvādaḥ syāt parīvādapariniṣṭhitavādayoḥ \|

arbudo māṃsakīle syāddaśakoṭiṣu cā'rbudaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 24 \[unit 942\]

śaile'rbudo'tha sambhedaḥ sphuṭane sindhusaṅgame \|

kusīdaṃ jīvane vṛddhyā kusīdaśca kusīdike \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 25 \[unit 943\]

praṇādastāraśabde syāt praṇādaḥ śravaṇāmaye \|

pramadaḥ pramade daitye dāyāde jñātiputrayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 26 \[unit 944\]

menādaḥ kekimārjjāracchāgaleṣu samīritaḥ \|

govindo vāsudeve syādgavādhyakṣe bṛhaspatau \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 27 \[unit 945\]

ākrando dāruṇaraṇe mitre trātari rodane \|

mākandaḥ sahakāre syānmākandyāmalakīphale \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 28 \[unit 946\]

arddhenduścandraśakale galahastanakhāṅkayoḥ \|\|

arddhenduḥ syādatiprauḍhe strīguhyāṅguliyojane \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 29 \[unit 947\]

karṇendurutkṣiptikāyāṃ karṇapāśyāmapīritā \|

syāt kaumudaḥ kārttikike candrikāyāñca kaumudī \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 30 \[unit 948\]

kumudaṃ kairave raktapaṅkaje kumudaḥ kapau \|

daityāntare ca diṅgāganāgayorapi kīrttitaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 31 \[unit 949\]

kumudā kumbhigāmbhāryoḥ syāt kumut kṛpaṇe'nyavat \|

kumude'pi kumut proktaṃ kravyāṃ māṃsāśirakṣasoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 32 \[unit 950\]

kakudvat kakudaṃ śreṣṭhe vṛṣāṅke rājalakṣmaṇi \|

mukundaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣe ratnabhede ca pārade \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 33 \[unit 951\]

darat prapāte'dribhave śarat saṃvatsare ṛtau \|

dṛṣat peṣaṇapaṭṭe syād dṛśatpāṣāṇamātrake \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 34 \[unit 952\]

sampadbhūtau guṇotkarṣe hārabhede'pi dṛśyate \|

bhāsat prabhāsvare māse tarat kāraṇḍave plave \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 35 \[unit 953\]

saṃvijjñāne pratijñāyāṃ saṅketācāranāmasu \|

sambhāṣaṇe toṣaṇe'pi kriyākāre ca saṅgare \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 36 \[unit 954\]

kāmadā dhenukāyāṃ syādvācyavat kāmadogdhari \|

sunandā rocanānāryormaryādādhāraṇe sthitā \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 37 \[unit 955\]

ekapadaṃ syāttatkāle padavyāmekapadya'pi \|

catuṣpadaṃ syāt svīkāre gavāśvādipaśuṣva'pi \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 38 \[unit 956\]

bhavedviṣṇupadābhikhyā kṣīrode gagane'mbuje \|

proktā viṣṇupadī gaṅgāsaṃkrāntidvārakāsu ca \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 39 \[unit 957\]

bhavejjanapado deśe jane jānapado'pi ca \|

priyaṃvadaḥ khecare syāt priyavāciṣu vācyavat \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 40 \[unit 958\]

apavādastu nindāyāmājñāviśrambhayorapi \|\|

parivādaḥ kalaṅke syādvīṇāvādanavastuni \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 41 \[unit 959\]

samaryādaḥ samīpe syānmaryādāsahite'pi ca \|

puṭabhedo nadīvaktre patanātodyayorapi \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 42 \[unit 960\]

aṣṭāpadaṃ syāt kanake śārīṇāṃ phalake'pi ca \|

aṣṭāpadaśca śarabhe candramalyāñca markkaṭe \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 43 \[unit 961\]

meghanādastu varuṇe rāvaṇasya sute'pi ca \|

mahānādaḥ kuñjare syādvarṣukābje śayānake \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 44 \[unit 962\]

bhavet kokanadaṃ raktakairave raktapaṅkaje \|

viśārado budhe dhṛṣṭe'bhimarddo raṇamanthayoḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 45 \[unit 963\]

pīṭhamarddo'tidhṛṣṭe syānnāṭyoktau nāyakapriye \|

mucukundo drubhede syānmunidaityaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 46 \[unit 964\]

kuruvindastu mustāyāṃ kulmāṣavrīhibhedayoḥ \|

hiṅgule padmarāge syānmukure ca samīritaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 47 \[unit 965\]

abhiṣyando'tivṛddhau syādāśrāve'kṣigade'pi ca \|

kaṭukandaṃ viduḥ śigrau śṛṅgaverarasonayoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 48 \[unit 966\]

tamonuda'grīndvarkeṣu pratipattithisaṃvidoḥ \|

śatahradā bhavedvajre saudāminyāñca kīrttitā \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dapañcakam / 49 \[unit 967\]

sahasrapādaḥ kāraṇḍe mārtaṇḍe yajñapūruṣe \|

bhaṣedupaniṣaddharmpe vedānte'pi rahasya'pi \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dāntavargaḥ / dapañcakam / fragment 968

iti dāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 1 \[unit 969\]

rādho vaiśākhamāse syādrādhā gopyāñca vidyuti \|

viṣṇukrāntāmalakyośca citravedhe ca dhanvinām \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 2 \[unit 970\]

gādhaḥ sthāne ca lipsāyāṃ vyādho mṛgayuduṣṭayoḥ \|

medhaḥ kraturdhiyāṃ medhā medhaḥ syāt khaladāruṇe \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 3 \[unit 971\]

budhaḥ kavau rauhiṇeye middhamālasyanidrayoḥ \|

tathā cittābhisaṃkṣepe naddhamudvṛttabaddhayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 4 \[unit 972\]

siddho vyāsādiṣu khyātaḥ siddhāḥ syurdaivayoniṣu \|

nitye prasiddhe niṣpanne'pīddhamātaptadīstayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 5 \[unit 973\]

ṛddhaṃ tvatisamṛddhe syāt samyagvāte tu vācyavat \|

vṛddhantu śailaje sūrijīrṇarūḍheṣu bhedyavat \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 6 \[unit 974\]

viddhaṃ syādvedite kṣipte sādṛśye'pi nigadyate \|

buddho jine ca dhīre ca saumye ca budhite'pi ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 7 \[unit 975\]

arddhaḥ khaṇḍe samāṃśe'rddhaṃ lubdho mṛgayukāṅkhiṇoḥ \|

andhaṃ syāttimire dṛṣṭihīne'ndhaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 8 \[unit 976\]

gandho gandhaka āmode leśe sambandhagarvayoḥ \|

skandhaḥ prakāṇḍe sainyāṃśe bāhumūlasamūhayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 9 \[unit 977\]

samīkanṛpayoścā'pi bandhastvādhau ca vandhane \|

dagdhaṃ pluṣṭe'nyavaddagdhā sthitārkadiśi darśitā \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 10 \[unit 978\]

digdhaṃ viṣāktavāṇe syāddigdhaṃ liptārthake'nyavat \|

dugdhaṃ prapūraṇe kṣīre mugdhaḥ sundaramūḍhayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 11 \[unit 979\]

madhu kṣaudre jale kṣīre madye puṣparase madhuḥ \|

daitye caitre vasante ca jīvāśāke madhudrume \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 12 \[unit 980\]

vidhuḥ śaśāṅke karpūre hṛṣīkeśe'pi rākṣase \|

sādhurvārdhuṣike cārau sajjane cābhidheyavat \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 13 \[unit 981\]

sindhuḥ samudre nadyāñca nade deśebhadānayoḥ

bandhurvandhūkapuṣpe syādbandhurbhrātari bāndhave \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 14 \[unit 982\]

ādhirmānasapīḍāyāṃ pratyāśāyāśca\[ñca\] bandhake \|\|

vyasane cāpadhiṣṭhāne vyādhirāmayavākyayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 15 \[unit 983\]

bodhiḥ samādhibhede syādbodhiḥ pippalapādape \|

sandhirbhede suruṅgāyāṃ bhage saṅghaṭane'pi ca \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 16 \[unit 984\]

abdhiḥ samudre sarasi siddhirniṣpattiyogayoḥ \|

vṛddhirānandane ṛddhau varddhane'pi kalāntare \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 17 \[unit 985\]

dogdhā gopālake vatse kavau vārttopajīvini \|

vidhā gajānne ṛddhau ca prakāre vetane vidhau \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 18 \[unit 986\]

godhā tale nihākāyāṃ bādhā duḥkhaniṣedhayoḥ \|

sudhā'mṛte snuhīmūrvālepagāṅgeṣṭikāsu ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 19 \[unit 987\]

sandhā sthitau pratijñāyāṃ śraddhā śrāddhatvakāṅkṣayoḥ \|

sparddhā saṃgharṣaṇe sāmye sparddhā kramasamunnatau \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 20 \[unit 988\]

vadhūḥ snuṣānavoḍhāstrībhāryāspṛkkāṅganāsu ca \|

śaṭyāñca śārivāyāṃ syāt sṛdhūrbuddhau gude'pi ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 21 \[unit 989\]

vivadho vīvadhaścāpi paryāhāre'dhvabhārayoḥ \|

vibudhaḥ paṇḍite deva sambādhaḥ saṅkaṭe bhage \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 22 \[unit 990\]

niṣadhaḥ kaṭhine deśe tadrāje ca nagāntare \|

saṃrodho rodhane kṣepe nirodho rodhanāśayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 23 \[unit 991\]

utsedhaścocchraye kāye durvidho durgate khale \|

viśrabdho''nudbhaṭe'tyarthe śātaviśvastayorapi \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 24 \[unit 992\]

nyagrodhī vyāmavaṭayornyagrodhaśca śamītarau \|

nyagrodhe vṛṣaparṇyāñca mohanākhyauṣadhāvapi \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 25 \[unit 993\]

agādho vivare sthāne prasiddho'laṅkṛte'pi ca \|

vikhyāte'pi ca sannaddhe varmitavyūḍhayorapi \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 26 \[unit 994\]

ānaddhaṃ baddhamātre syādānaddhaṃ murujādike \|

āviddho vācyavajñeyaḥ kuṭile ca parāhate \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 27 \[unit 995\]

kabandhastūdare rakṣoviśeṣe'pi vidhuntude \|

ābandho dṛḍhabandhe syāt premālaṅkārayorapi \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 28 \[unit 996\]

sugandhiḥ syādiṣṭagandhe sugandhi harivāluke \|

upādhirdharmacintāyāṃ kaitave'pi viśeṣaṇe \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 29 \[unit 997\]

kuṭumvavyāpṛte'pi syādupadhirvyādhicakrayoḥ \|

paridhiryajñiyataroḥ śākhāyāmupasūryake \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 30 \[unit 998\]

avadhistvavadhāne syāt sīmni kāle bile'pi ca \|

samādhirniyame dhyāne nīvāke ca samarthane \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 31 \[unit 999\]

sannidhiḥ sannidhāne'pi bhavedindrayagocare \|

saṃsiddhiḥ sahaje siddhau madogrāyāmapi smṛtā \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 32 \[unit 1000\]

praṇidhiścare yācñāyāṃ vīrudviṭapaśākhayoḥ \|

māgadhī pippalīyūthyormāgadho magadhodbhave \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / fragment 1001

vaiśyajakṣatriyāputre śuklajīrakabandinoḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 33 \[unit 1002\]

avarodhastirodhāne śuddhānte rājaveśmani \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 34 \[unit 1003\]

mahauṣadhaṃ śṛṅgave\[be\]re viṣāyāṃ laśune'pi ca \|

parivyādho vetase syāt parivyādho drumotpale \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 35 \[unit 1004\]

avaṣṭabdhamadūre syādākrānte ca vilambite \|

samunnaddhaḥ samudbhūte paṇḍitampanyapṛṣṭayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 36 \[unit 1005\]

aniruddha uṣānāthe care cānargale'nyavat \|

anubandhaḥ prakṛtyāderdoṣotpāde vinaśvare \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 37 \[unit 1006\]

mukhyānuyāyini śiśau pravṛttasya nivarttane \|

anubandhī ca hikkāyāṃ tṛṣṇāyāmapi kathyate \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 38 \[unit 1007\]

kālaskandhastamāle syāttinduke jīvakadrume \|

āśāvandhaḥ samāśvāse tayā markaṭajālake \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 39 \[unit 1008\]

brahmabandhuradhikṣepe nirdeśe ca dvijanmanām \|

ugragandhājamodāyāṃ vacāyāṃ chikkakauṣadhau \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 40 \[unit 1009\]

tīkṣṇagandhā vacāyāñca śigrurājikayorapi \|

ikṣugandhā kokilākṣe kroṣṭryāṃ kāśe ca gokṣure \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / fragment 1010

tṛṇagodhā citrakole kṛkalāse ca lakṣyate \|

upalabdhirmatau prāptau jñāne cā'pi prayujyate \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcakam / 41 \[unit 1011\]

yojanagandhā kastūryyāṃ sītāyāṃ vyāsamātari \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcakam / fragment 1012

iti dhāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 1 \[unit 1013\]

inaḥ sūrye nṛpe patyau dhanaṃ godhanavittayoḥ \|

jinaḥ syādativṛddhe ca buddhe cārhati jitvare \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 2 \[unit 1014\]

jano loke maharllokāt paraloke ca pāmare \|

janī sīmantinīvadhvorutpattau ca janirmatā \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 3 \[unit 1015\]

ghanaṃ syāt kāṃsyatālādivādyamadhyamanṛtyayoḥ \|

ghanaṃ sāndre dṛḍhe dārjye vistāre lohamudgare \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 4 \[unit 1016\]

meghamustakayoścā'pi vanaṃ kānananīrayoḥ \|

pravāse nilaye cā'pi vanaṃ prasravaṇe'pi ca \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 5 \[unit 1017\]

vānaṃ śuṣkaphale śuṣke kaṭe sīvanakarmaṇi \|

jalasaṃsravavātormisuraṅgāgatisaurabhe \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 6 \[unit 1018\]

dānaṃ gajamade tyāge pālanacchedaśuddhiṣu \|

mānaṃ prayāṇe prasthādau mānaścittonnatau grahe \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 7 \[unit 1019\]

yānaṃ gatau vāhane'pi pānaṃ pītau ca rakṣaṇe \|\|

styānaṃ snigdhe pratidhvāne ghanatvālasyayorapi \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 8 \[unit 1020\]

sthānaṃ sthitau ca sādṛśye sanniveśāvakāśayoḥ \|

yuktārthe karaṇārthe ca sthāne syādavyayaṃ punaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 9 \[unit 1021\]

sūnaṃ vācyavadūneṣu sūnaṃ prasavapuṣpayoḥ \|

sūnā putryāṃ vadhasthānagalaśuṇḍikayorapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 10 \[unit 1022\]

ūnaṃ hīne raṇodyāne smṛtaḥ kiraṇasūryayoḥ \|

mīno rāśyantare matsye sīno'jagaramūrkhayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 11 \[unit 1023\]

cīno mṛgāntare tantuvrīhideśāṃśukāntare \|

hīnaṃ garhyonayornyūnamanayorapi kīrttitam \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 12 \[unit 1024\]

kṛtsnaṃ kaṣṭe samaste ca rasnastakṣakacaṇḍayoḥ \|

vasnaṃ syādvetane mūlye vasanadravyayorapi \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 13 \[unit 1025\]

svapnaḥ suptasya vijñāne svapnaḥ svāpe ca darśane \|

budhnaḥ śiphāyāṃ rudre ca ratnaṃ śreṣṭhe maṇāvapi \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 14 \[unit 1026\]

nagno'pi ca kṣapaṇake vivastre cā'bhidheyavat \|

lagnaṃ rāśyudaye lagnaṃ saktalajjitayorapi \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 15 \[unit 1027\]

channaṃ rahaśchāditayoḥ syādannaṃ bhuktabhaktayoḥ \|

bhinnamanyārthavacane saṅgate dārite sphuṭe \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 16 \[unit 1028\]

vinnaṃ jñāte sthitau labdhe chinnaṃ bhinne tu vācyavat \|

chinnā'mṛtāyāṃ puṃścalyāmunnaḥ klinne ca sūrate \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 17 \[unit 1029\]

cihnaṃ dhvaje patākāyāṃ putraṃ vitte parākrame \|

dhanuḥsaṃjñā piyāladrau rāśibhede śarāsane \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 18 \[unit 1030\]

tanuḥ kāye kṛśe cā'lpe virale'pi ca vācyavat \|

hanuḥ praharaṇe mṛtyau kapolāvayave gade \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 19 \[unit 1031\]

hanurhaṭṭavilāsinyāṃ sūnuḥ putre'nuje ravau \|

sānuḥ śṛṅge budhe mārge vātyāyāṃ pallave nave \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 20 \[unit 1032\]

dānurdātari vijñāte bhānuḥ kiraṇasūryayoḥ \|

dhenaḥ samudre nadyāntu dhenī syonaḥ site khage \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 21 \[unit 1033\]

muniryamīṅgudībuddhapiyālāgasti\[?\] kiṃśuke \|

jyānirhānau sravantyāñca yoniḥ syādākare bhage \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 22 \[unit 1034\]

jyotsnī paṭolikāyāṃ syājjyotsnāyuktavibhāvarau \|

jyautsnī sacandranakṣatrarātrau candrātape'pi ca \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 23 \[unit 1035\]

rāsnā smṛtā bhujaṅgākṣyāmelāparṇyāñca kathyate \|

mṛtsnā tu varikāyāṃ syānmṛtsāyāmapi dṛśyate \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 24 \[unit 1036\]

gīnā mūṣikayoṣāyāṃ durgate kātare'nyavat \|

dhānābhṛṣṭayave proktā dhānyāke'bhinavodbhidi \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 25 \[unit 1037\]

avanaṃ rakṣaṇe prītau bhavanaṃ bhāvaveśmanoḥ \|

bhuvanaṃ viṣṭape mahyāṃ salile'pi viyatya'pi \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 26 \[unit 1038\]

savanaṃ yajñiyasnāne somanirddalane'pi ca \|

ayanaṃ pathi bhānoḥ syādudagdakṣiṇato gatau \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 27 \[unit 1039\]

jayanaṃ syātturaṅgādisannāhe vijaye'pi ca \|

śayanaṃ surate nidrāśayyayorapi bhāṣitam \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 28 \[unit 1040\]

śayanaṃ śāntivadhayoḥ śamanaḥ śrāddhadaivate \|

daśanaḥ kavace dante daśanaḥ śikhare'pi ca \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 29 \[unit 1041\]

jaghanaśca striyāḥ śroṇipurobhāge kaṭīrake \|

tapanastaraṇau tāpe bhallāte narakāntare \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 30 \[unit 1042\]

rasanaṃ svādane dhvāne rasanā kāñcijihvayoḥ \|

rasanañcā'pi rāsnāyāṃ vasanaṃ chādaneṃ'śuke \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 31 \[unit 1043\]

hasanañca smṛtaṃ hāse'ṅgāradhānyā hasanyapi \|

śvasano mārute śvāse śvasano madanadrume \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 32 \[unit 1044\]

kadanaṃ marddane pāpe sadanaṃ mandire jale \|

madanaḥ smaradhuttūravasantadrumasikthake \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 33 \[unit 1045\]

dahanaścitrake vahnau bhallāte duṣṭaveṣṭite \|

gahanaṃ gahane duḥkhe vipine kalile'pi ca \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 34 \[unit 1046\]

stananaṃ dhvanimātre syāt kunthane meghagarjite \|

nidhanaṃ syāt kule nāśe pradhanaṃ dāraṇe raṇe \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 35 \[unit 1047\]

pavanākhyā kulāle syāt pākasthāne'pi mārute \|

niṣpāve cātha\[ \]vapanaṃ bījādhāne'pi muṇḍane \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 36 \[unit 1048\]

damanaḥ kusume vīre vamanaṃ charddane'rddane \|

syādāhṛtau ca yamanaṃ duḥkhe coparatau yame \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 37 \[unit 1049\]

kamunaḥ kāmuke kāme'bhirūpe'śokapādape \|

dhamano'pyanale bhastrādhmāpakakrūrayoḥ smṛtā \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 38 \[unit 1050\]

dhamanī tu haridrāyāṃ kandharāśirayorapi \|

malanaḥ paṭavāse syānmarddane malanaṃ matam \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 39 \[unit 1051\]

calanaṃ kampanaṃ kampe kampre calanakampanau \|

calanī vastraghargharyyāṃ vāribhede'pi ca kvacit \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 40 \[unit 1052\]

secanaṃ rakṣaṇe seke naukāsecanabhājane \|

sevanaṃ sīvanopāstyoḥ saṃvyānaṃ chādaneṃ'śuke \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 41 \[unit 1053\]

samānaṃ satsamaikeṣu samāno nābhimārute \|

śobhano grahayoge syācchobhanaṃ sundare'nyavat \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 42 \[unit 1054\]

śāsanaṃ rājadattorvyāṃ lekhājñāśāstraśāstriṣu \|

vāsanaṃ vasane vīrāsane jñāne ca dhūpane \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 43 \[unit 1055\]

āsanaṃ dviradaskandhe pīṭhe yātrānivarttane \|

asanī paṇyavīthyāṃ syādāsano jīvakadrume \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 44 \[unit 1056\]

sparśano mārute sparśadhānayoḥ sparśanaṃ matam \|

darśanaṃ nayanasvapnabuddhidharmopalabdhiṣu \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 45 \[unit 1057\]

śāstradarpaṇayoścā'pi daṃśanaṃ daṃśavarmaṇoḥ \|

piśunaṃ kuṅkume krūre sūcake cā'bhidheyavat \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 46 \[unit 1058\]

piśunaḥ kapivaktre syāt spṛkkāyāṃ piśunā matā \|

śakunañca śubhāśaṃsānimitte śakunaḥ khage \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 47 \[unit 1059\]

mithuno rāśibhede syānmithunaṃ dampatīyuge \|

talunaṃ pavane yūni striyāñca talunī viduḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 48 \[unit 1060\]

talinaṃ virale stoke svacche'pi talinaṃ matam \|

malinaṃ dūṣite kṛṣṇe ṛtumatyāṃ malinya'pi \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 49 \[unit 1061\]

kaṭhinaṃ niṣṭure sthālyāṃ śarkarāyāṃ guḍasya ca \|

kaṭhinā kāṭikāyāntu kaṭhinīti prayujyate \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 50 \[unit 1062\]

pītanaṃ haritālaṃ syāt kuṅkume pītadāruṇi \|

āmrātake pītanaśca maithunaṃ saṅgate rate \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 51 \[unit 1063\]

ketanaṃ lāñchane kārṣe gehe copanimantraṇe \|

charddano'lambuṣe nimbe chardanaṃ vamane tare \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 52 \[unit 1064\]

chedanaṃ karttane bhede chadanaṃ pakṣapatrayoḥ \|

rodanaṃ vyathane totre rodanaṃ rodhane'śruṇi \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 53 \[unit 1065\]

apānantu gude proktamapānastasya mārute \|

santānaṃ santatau gotre syādapatye suradrume \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 54 \[unit 1066\]

vitāno yajñavistārolloceṣu kratukarmaṇi \|

vṛttabhedāvasarayorvitānaṃ tucchamandayoḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 55 \[unit 1067\]

uttānaṃ syādagambhīre tathā suptonmukhe'nyavat \|

ādanaṃ grahaṇe vidyādalaṅkāre ca vājinām \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 56 \[unit 1068\]

nidānaṃ kāraṇe roganirṇaye vatsadāmani \|

udyānaṃ vanabhede syānniḥsṛte'pi prayojane \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 57 \[unit 1069\]

amlāno jhiṇṭikābhede vācyavannirmale smṛtaḥ \|

vyutthānaṃ svairakaraṇe virodhācaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 58 \[unit 1070\]

utthānamudyame tantre pauruṣe pustake raṇe \|

aṅganodgamaharṣeṣu malavege'pi kīrttitam \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 59 \[unit 1071\]

saṃsthānamāhṛtau mṛtyau sanniveśe catuṣpathe \|

dhāvanaṃ pragame śuddhau pṛśniparṇyāntu dhāvanī \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 60 \[unit 1072\]

pāvanantu jale kṛcchre pāvakādhmāśayorviduḥ \|

pāvanaḥ sihlake vahnau prāyaścitte ca pāvanam \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 61 \[unit 1073\]

vācyavat pāvayitari harītakyāntu pāvanī \|

vāmano koṭavaikuṇṭhakharvadakṣipādiggaje \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 62 \[unit 1074\]

sthāpanaṃ syāt puṃsavane samādhau ropaṇe'pi ca \|

yāpana varttane kālakṣepe nirasane'pi ca \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 63 \[unit 1075\]

yuñjānaḥ sārathau vipre bu\[?\]dhānaḥ paṇḍite gurau \|

dṛśānaṃ jyotiṣi proktaṃ dṛśānaśca virocane \|\| 63 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 64 \[unit 1076\]

javano deśabhede syādvegavegādhikāśvayoḥ \|

javanyoṣadhibhede syādvācyavadvegini smṛtaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 65 \[unit 1077\]

jīvanaṃ varttane nīre putrajīve tu jīvanaḥ \|

jīvanī jīvanā ceti jīvantīmedayoḥ kramāt \|\| 65 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 66 \[unit 1078\]

devanaṃ vyavahāre syājjigīṣākrīḍayorapi \|

akṣeṣu devanaḥ prokto vācyavad vāgmiviprayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 67 \[unit 1079\]

temanaṃ vyañjane klede temane cullikāntare \|

sāvano yajñakarmmānte yajamānapracetasoḥ \|\| 67 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 68 \[unit 1080\]

vijñānaṃ karmaṇe jñāne prajñānaṃ lāñcchane dhiyi \|

hāyano'rcciṣi varṣe ca vrīhibhede ca hāyanaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 69 \[unit 1081\]

phālgunastu tapasye syāt pūrṇimāyāntu phālgunī \|

phālgunaḥ kathitaḥ pārthe nadīje kakubhe'pi ca \|\| 69 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 70 \[unit 1082\]

veṣṭanaṃ karṇaśaṣkulyāmuṣṇīṣe parivāraṇe \|

gostano hārabhede syād drākṣāyāṃ gostanīti ca \|\| 70 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 71 \[unit 1083\]

kalpanaṃ karttane kḷptau sajjanāyāntu kalpanā \|

karttanaṃ chedane prāhurnārīṇāṃ tūlasevane \|\| 71 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 72 \[unit 1084\]

laṅghanantūpavāse syāt kramaṇe plavane'pi ca \|

maṇḍanaṃ syādalaṅkāre'laṅkariṣṇuni vācyavat \|\| 72 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 73 \[unit 1085\]

bhaṇḍanaṃ kavace yuddhe khalīkāre ca varttate \|

muṇḍanaṃ vapane trāṇe hiṇḍinaṃ lekhane rate \|\| 73 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 74 \[unit 1086\]

aṅganaṃ prāṅgane yāne kāminyāmaṅganā matā \|

gandhanaṃ sūcanotsāhahiṃsaneṣu prakāśane \|\| 74 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 75 \[unit 1087\]

nandanaṃ vāsavodyāne nandano harṣake sutai\[te\] \|

syandanantu sṛtau nīre syandanastiniśe rathe \|\| 75 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 76 \[unit 1088\]

krandanaṃ rodane hāne candanaṃ malayodbhave \|

candanaḥ kapibhede syānnadībhede tu rudanī \|\| 76 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 77 \[unit 1089\]

varddhanaṃ chedane vṛddhau vāridhānyāntu varddhanī \|

vyañjanaṃ temane cihne śmaśruṇyavayave'pi ca \|\| 77 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 78 \[unit 1090\]

rañjano rāgarajane rañjanaṃ raktacandane \|

guñjārocanikānīlīmañjiṣṭhāsu ca rañjanī \|\| 78 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 79 \[unit 1091\]

añjanaṃ kajjale cāṅke sauvīre ca rasāñjane \|

añjano diggaje jeṣṭhyāmañjanā'pyanilastriyām \|\| 79 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 80 \[unit 1092\]

añjanī lepanāyāṃ syād gṛñjanastu rasonake \|

viṣadigdhapaśormāṃse guñjanañca prakīrttitam \|\| 80 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 81 \[unit 1093\]

lekhanaṃ chadane bhūrjje lipinyāse ca lekhanam \|

yojanaśca catuṣkrośyāṃ yoge ca paramātmani \|\| 81 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 82 \[unit 1094\]

garjanaṃ nisvane kope varjanaṃ tyāgahiṃsayoḥ \|

mārjjanaṃ kathitaṃ mārṣṭau mārjjano lodhraśākhini \|\| 82 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 83 \[unit 1095\]

sajjano vācyavat sādhau sajjanaṃ ghaṭṭagulmake \|

sajjanā kalpanāyāntu bhājanaṃ yogyapātrayoḥ \|\| 83 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 84 \[unit 1096\]

arjjunaḥ kakubhe pārthe kārttavīryyamayūrayoḥ \|

māturekasute ca syādarjjuno dhavale'nyavat \|\| 84 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 85 \[unit 1097\]

arjjunantu tṛṇe netragade cā'pyārjjunī gavi \|

uṣāyāṃ karatoyāyāṃ kuṭṭanyāmapi ca kvacit \|\| 85 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 86 \[unit 1098\]

vṛjinaṃ kalmaṣe keśe vṛjinaḥ kuṭile'nyavat \|

lāñchanaṃ lakṣaṇe nāmni bandhanaṃ vadhabandhayoḥ \|\| 86 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 87 \[unit 1099\]

jagagnuragnau jantau ca mehanaṃ mūtraśiśnayoḥ \|

prasūnaṃ vācyavajjāte prasūnaṃ phalapuṣpayoḥ \|\| 87 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 88 \[unit 1100\]

kānīnaḥ kanyakājātatanaye vyāsakarṇayoḥ \|

kānanaṃ vipine gehe parameṣṭhimukhe'pi ca \|\| 88 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 89 \[unit 1101\]

kāñcanaḥ kāñcanāre syāccampake nāgakeśare \|

udumbare ca punnāge haridrāyāñca kāñcanī \|\| 89 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 90 \[unit 1102\]

kāñcanaṃ hemni kiñjalke punnāge kācabhājane \|

golake cātha śakuniḥ saubale karaṇe khage \|\| 90 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 91 \[unit 1103\]

rocano raktakahlāre rocanaḥ kūṭaśālmalau \|

maṅgalye'pi ca gopitte rocanā rucirastriyām \|\| 91 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 92 \[unit 1104\]

vyasanantvaśubhe saktau pānastrīmṛgayādiṣu \|

daivāniṣṭaphale pāpe vipattau niṣphalodyame \|\| 92 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 93 \[unit 1105\]

udāno'pyudarāvartte marutsarpaviśeṣayoḥ \|

uddhānamudgate cullyāṃ vilagno madhyalagnayoḥ \|\| 93 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 94 \[unit 1106\]

sandhānaṃ syādabhiṣave tathā saṅghaṭane'pi ca \|\|

rādhanaṃ rādhane prāptau rādhanaṃ toṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 94 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 95 \[unit 1107\]

sādhanaṃ nirvṛtau meṇaṇḍhre sainye siddhau gatau vadhe \|

upāye mṛtasaṃskāre dāpane'nugame dhane \|\| 95 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 96 \[unit 1108\]

pradhānaḥ syānmahāmātye prakṛtau paramātmani \|

prajñāyāñca pradhānaṃ syādekatve'nusadottame \|\| 96 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 97 \[unit 1109\]

grāmīno grāmasambhūte grāmīnaḥ śunivāyase \|

pāṭhīno mīnabhede syāt pāṭhake gugguludrume \|\| 97 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 98 \[unit 1110\]

kaulīnaḥ syāt kulīnatve guhye janye kukarmmaṇi \|

kauśeyake vihaṅgādipaśūnāṃ saṅgare'pi ca \|\| 98 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 99 \[unit 1111\]

kaupīnaṃ syādakārye ca guhyacīrapradeśayoḥ \|

viklinno jarayā jīrṇe śīrṇe cārdre ca vācyavat \|\| 99 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 100 \[unit 1112\]

āpannaḥ savipattau syāt prāpte vācyavadīritaḥ \|

vipannaḥ kathito naṣṭe vipannaśca bhujaṅgame \|\| 100 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 101 \[unit 1113\]

vicchinnañca samālabdhe vipanne kuṭile'nyavat \|

sampannaṃ sādhite proktaṃ sampattisahite'nyavat \|\| 101 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 102 \[unit 1114\]

tamoghnaḥ sūryavahnīndubuddhaśaṅkaraviṣṇuṣu \|

viṣaghnākhyā śirīṣe syāttrivṛtā'mṛtayorapi \|\| 102 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 103 \[unit 1115\]

śikhī vahnau balīvardde śare ketugrahe drume \|

kekikukkuṭayoścā'tha śikhī cūḍānvite'nyavat \|\| 103 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 104 \[unit 1116\]

śākhī syāt pādape vede turuṣkākhyajane'pi ca \|

khaḍgī khaḍgāyudhe gaṇḍe śṛṅgī nāgadrumādriṣu \|\| 104 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 105 \[unit 1117\]

cakrī kulālavaikuṇṭhe kokāhigrāmajāliṣu \|

dhanvī dhanurdhare pārthe vidagdhe kakubhadrume \|\| 105 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 106 \[unit 1118\]

kṛtī syāt paṇḍite yogye pāśī pāśadhare'rapatau \|

nandī harapratīhāre garddabhāṇḍe vaṭadra\[dru\]me \|\| 106 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 107 \[unit 1119\]

nandinī tu nanandāyāṃ dhenvāṃ surasarityapi \|

rohī rohitake'śvatthavaṭapādapayorapi \|\| 107 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 108 \[unit 1120\]

halī bale kṛṣīke syādalī vṛścikabhṛṅgayoḥ \|

varṇī syāllekhake citrakare'pi brahmacāriṇi \|\| 108 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 109 \[unit 1121\]

śilpī syādvācyavat kārau nāyikāyāñca śilpinī \|

kāmī syāt kāmuke cakravāke pārāvate'pi ca \|\| 109 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 110 \[unit 1122\]

gomī tu pāśake pūrau vāgmī vācaspatau paṭau \|

carmmī phalakapāṇau syādbhūrjje bhṛṅgaraṭāvapi \|\| 110 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 111 \[unit 1123\]

arthī syādyācake yajñe sevake ca vivādini \|

svāmī prabhau viśākhe ca rāgī rakte ca kāmuke \|\| 111 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 112 \[unit 1124\]

dhvajī śaile rathe vipre bhujaṅgamaturaṅgayoḥ \|

bhogī bhujaṅgame rājñi grāmiṇyāṃ nāpite'pi ca \|\| 112 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 113 \[unit 1125\]

vihāya mahiṣīñcānyarājayoṣiti bhoginī \|

dyuvā tvabhinave bhānau pretvā maruti sārake \|\| 113 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 114 \[unit 1126\]

rājā prabhau nṛpe candre yakṣe kṣatriyaśakrayoḥ \|

adhvā vartmani saṃsthāne sāśravaskandhakālayoḥ \|\| 114 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 115 \[unit 1127\]

dhanvā jaṅgaladeśe syāddhanvacāpe sthale'pi ca \|

ātmā dehamanobrahmasvabhāvadhṛtibuddhiṣu \|\| 115 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 116 \[unit 1128\]

prayatne cā'pyatha grāvā prastare jalade girau \|

brahmā\[ \]viriñcadvijayorṛtvikcandrārkayogayoḥ \|\| 116 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 117 \[unit 1129\]

brahma proktaṃ tapo'dhyātyavedajñāneṣu sūribhiḥ \|

ardhā ca kukṣite vāhe vṛṣā karṇamahendrayoḥ \|\| 117 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 118 \[unit 1130\]

yuvā syāttaruṇe śreṣṭhe nisargabalaśālini \|

parva syādutsave granthau prastāve lakṣaṇāntare \|\| 118 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 119 \[unit 1131\]

darśapratipadoḥ sandhau viṣuvatprabhṛtiṣvapi \|

bharmma svarṇe bhṛtau bhāre carmma syādvalkalatvacoḥ \|\| 119 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 120 \[unit 1132\]

karmma vyāpye kriyāyāñca vartmanetracchadādhvanoḥ \|

varṣma dehe pramāṇātisundarākārayorapi \|\| 120 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 121 \[unit 1133\]

sadma syānmandire nīre chadma sādhyāpadeśayoḥ \|

dhāma dehe gṛhe raśmau sthāne janmaprabhāvayoḥ \|\| 121 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 122 \[unit 1134\]

prema narmmaṇi ca snehe vyoma vārivihāyasoḥ \|

pakṣma sūtrādisūkṣmāṃśe kiñjalke netralomani \|\| 122 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 123 \[unit 1135\]

lakṣmi\[?\] cihne pradhāne syādaśaniḥ pavividyutoḥ \|

aratniḥ kaphaṇau haste saprakoṣṭhatatāṅgulau \|\| 123 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 124 \[unit 1136\]

senānīḥ kārttikeye syāttathā senāpatāva'pi \|

hlā\[?\]dinī vajrataḍitoḥ kāminī bhīruvandayoḥ \|\| 124 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 125 \[unit 1137\]

vāhinī syāttaraṅgiṇyāṃ senāsainyaprabhedayoḥ \|

varttanī tarkupiṇḍe syādvarttanī maline pathi \|\| 125 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 126 \[unit 1138\]

bodhanī bodhapippalyorbodhanaṃ gandhabodhane \|

vāṇinī narttakīmattavidagdhavanitāsu ca \|\| 126 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 127 \[unit 1139\]

rajanī nīlikālākṣāharidrāyāminīṣu ca \|

nalinī padminīpadmavyomasindhusarovare \|\| 127 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 128 \[unit 1140\]

nalikāyāñca nalinī nalinaṃ kamale jale \|

mālinī vṛttabhede syānmālākārastriyāmapi \|\| 128 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 129 \[unit 1141\]

campānagaryyāṃ gauryyāñca mandākinyāñca mālinī \|

śaṅkhinī śvetapunnāge caurapuṣpyāṃ vadhūbhidi \|\| 129 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 130 \[unit 1142\]

padminī strīviśeṣe syāt sarasyāmambuje'pi ca \|

hastinī gajayoṣāyāṃ nārībhede ca hastinī \|\| 130 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 131 \[unit 1143\]

sandhinī tu vṛṣākrāntākāladugdhagavoḥ smṛtā \|

bhaṭṭinī dvijabhāryyāyāṃ nāṭyoktau rājayoṣiti \|\| 131 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 132 \[unit 1144\]

recanī trivṛtādantī guḍarocanikāsu ca \|

arśoghnī tālamūlyāṃ syādarśoghnaḥ śūraṇe'pi ca \|\| 132 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 133 \[unit 1145\]

śataghnī śastrabhede syād vṛścikālyāṃ karañjake \|

asiknī syādavṛddhāntaḥpurapreṣyastriyāṃ dhunau \|\| 133 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 134 \[unit 1146\]

sūcanā\[?\]vyadhane dṛṣṭau gandhane'bhinaye'pi ca \|

bhāvanā lepane dhyāne vedanā jñānaduḥkhayoḥ \|\| 134 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 135 \[unit 1147\]

kuhanā dambhacaryyāyāmīrṣyālau kuhanaḥ punaḥ \|

pūtanā rākṣasībhede harītakyāñca pūtanā \|\| 135 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 136 \[unit 1148\]

pṛtanā'nīkanīsenā camūsainyāntare'pi ca \|

cetanā saṃvidi proktā vācyavat prāṇini smṛtā \|\| 136 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 137 \[unit 1149\]

prasannā madurāyāñca prasādasahite'nyavat \|

lalanā nāḍikābhede jihvāyāṃ varayoṣiti \|

śleṣmaghnī kathitā mallyāṃ kampillakaphaṇijjayoḥ \|\| 137 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 138 \[unit 1150\]

mahādhanaṃ mahālaye suvastre silhake'pi ca \|

tapodhanastāpase syānmuṇḍīryyāñca tapodhanā \|\| 138 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 139 \[unit 1151\]

ārādhanaṃ toṣaṇe syāt prāptau ca pacane'pi ca \|

upadhānaṃ viśeṣe ca geḍuke praṇaye'pi ca \|\| 139 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 140 \[unit 1152\]

praṇidhānaṃ prayatne syāt praveśe ca samāhitau \|

āyodhanaṃ vadhe yuddhe'valagno madhyalagnayoḥ \|\| 140 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 141 \[unit 1153\]

abhijanaḥ kule khyātau janmabhūmyāṃ kuladhvaje \|

bhavet puṇyajano yakṣe rākṣase sajjane'pi ca \|\| 141 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 142 \[unit 1154\]

visarjanaṃ parityāge dāne sampreṣaṇe'pi ca \|

pṛthagjanaḥ smṛto nīce mūrkhe cā'tha prayojane \|\| 142 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 143 \[unit 1155\]

kāryyahetvornidhuvanaṃ surate kampane'pi ca \|

anūcāno vinīte syāt sāṅgavedavicakṣaṇe \|\| 143 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 144 \[unit 1156\]

apācīnamavāgarthe viparyaste ca vācyavat \|

pravacanaṃ smṛtaṃ vede prakṛṣṭavacane'pi ca \|\| 144 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 145 \[unit 1157\]

vairocanastu sugate balidaityārkaputrayoḥ \|

virocano'rke dahane candre prahlādanandane \|\| 145 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 146 \[unit 1158\]

ātañcanaṃ pratīvāpajavanāpyāyaneṣu ca \|

abhipanno'parādhye'bhidrute graste vipadgate \|\| 146 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 147 \[unit 1159\]

pratipannaḥ smṛto dhīrairvijñāte'ṅgīkṛte'pi ca \|

samāpannaṃ samāse syāt prāpte kliṣṭe vadhe'pi ca \|\| 147 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 148 \[unit 1160\]

bhavet prajananaṃ yonau janmani pragame'pi ca \|

ākalanaṃ vibandhe syādākāṅkṣāparisaṅkhyayoḥ \|\| 148 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 149 \[unit 1161\]

sammūrcchanamabhivyāptau sammūrcchāmohayorapi \|

niryātanaṃ vairaśuddhau dāne nyāsārpaṇe matau \|\| 149 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 150 \[unit 1162\]

kapītanaṃ garddabhāṇḍaśirīṣāmrātakeṣu ca \|

aśvatthe ca samutthānamudyame roganirṇaye \|\| 150 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 151 \[unit 1163\]

ācchādanaṃ sampidhāne smṛtaṃ vastre pravāraṇe \|

rājādanaṃ kṣīrikāyāṃ priyāle kiṃśuke'pi ca \|\| 151 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 152 \[unit 1164\]

krauñcādanastu pippalyāṃ ciñcoṭakamṛṇālayoḥ \|

utsādanaṃ samullekhodvāhanodvarttaneṣu ca \|\| 152 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 153 \[unit 1165\]

vṛkṣādanaścaladale madhukṣudrakuṭhārayoḥ \|

vṛkṣādanī smṛtā vandāvidārīgandhayorapi \|\| 153 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 154 \[unit 1166\]

sadādāno'bhramātaṅge herambe gandhahastini \|

avadānamitivṛtte karmakhaṇḍanayorapi \|\| 154 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 155 \[unit 1167\]

kucandanañca patrāṅge drubhede raktacandane \|

āskandanaṃ tiraskāre raṇe saṃśoṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 155 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 156 \[unit 1168\]

saṃvananaṃ samāloce vaśīkāre prakīrttitam \|

viheṭhanantu hiṃsāyāṃ marddane ca viḍambane \|\| 156 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 157 \[unit 1169\]

jalāṭano lohapṛṣṭhe jalaukāyāṃ jalāṭanī \|

prasphoṭanaṃ bhavet sūrye tāḍane ca prakāśane \|\| 157 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 158 \[unit 1170\]

utpatanaṃ samutpattau tathārddhagamane'pi ca \|

udvartanaṃ sotpatane'pyapāvṛttau vilepane \|\| 158 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 159 \[unit 1171\]

samādānaṃ samīcīnagrahaṇe nityakarmaṇi \|

saṃyamanī vrate puryyāṃ saṃyamanī yamasya ca \|\| 159 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 160 \[unit 1172\]

niśamanaṃ stutau dṛṣṭau dṛṣṭyāloce niśāmanam \|

pratimānaṃ praticchāyāgajadantāntarālayoḥ \|\| 160 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 161 \[unit 1173\]

abhimānaḥ smṛto jñāne varge praṇayahiṃsayoḥ \|

adhiṣṭhānaṃ purecakre prabhāve'dhyāsane'pi ca \|\| 161 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 162 \[unit 1174\]

varddhamānaḥ puṣṭibhede śarāvairaṇḍaviṣṇuṣu \|

māludhānaścitrasarpe māludhānī latāntare \|\| 162 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 163 \[unit 1175\]

pratiyatnastu saṃskāralipsopagrahaṇeṣu ca \|

bhavedudayano vatsarāje'gastyamunāva'pi \|\| 163 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 164 \[unit 1176\]

vihananaṃ vighāte syāt piñjane tūladhūnane \|

saṃvāhanaṃ vāhane'pi bhārāderaṅgamarddane \|\| 164 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 165 \[unit 1177\]

udvāhanaṃ diśītye syādrajjāvudvāhinī matā \|

mahāseno viśākhe syānmahāsainyapatāvapi \|\| 165 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 166 \[unit 1178\]

rasāyanaṃ viḍaṅge syādrājavyādhijitauṣadhe \|

rasāyanaḥ pakṣirāje viṣe'pi ca rasāyanam \|\| 166 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 167 \[unit 1179\]

kātyāyano vararucau gauryāṃ kātyāyanī matā \|

kāṣāyavastravidhavā'rddhajaratyāmapīṣyate \|\| 167 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 168 \[unit 1180\]

sudarśano hareścakre'marāvatyāṃ sudarśanā \|

ājñāyāmoṣadhībhede marujambvāṃ sudarśanā \|\| 168 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 169 \[unit 1181\]

nirbhartsanaṃ khalīkāre'laktake ca samāpanam \|

paricchede samāptau ca samādhāne pramāpaṇe \|\| 169 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 170 \[unit 1182\]

anvāsanaṃ snehavastāvupāstāvanuśocane \|

upāsanaṃ śarābhyāse śuśrūṣāyāñca hiṃsane \|\| 170 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 171 \[unit 1183\]

nirasanaṃ nirāse syādvadhe niṣṭhīvane'pi ca \|

nirvāsanaṃ hiṃsane ca nagarāderbahiṣkṛtau \|\| 171 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 172 \[unit 1184\]

āveśanaṃ śilpigṛhe bhūtāveśapraveśayoḥ \|

sārasanaṃ mekhalāyāmurastre ca tanutriṇām \|\| 172 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 173 \[unit 1185\]

prahasanaṃ bhavedbhaṅge prahāsākṣepayorapi \|

vārāsanaṃ vāḥsadane śūlmapadvārapālayoḥ \|\| 173 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 174 \[unit 1186\]

ghanāghano ghātukamattadantino-

nirantare vāsavavarṣukābdayoḥ \|

sanātanaḥ śāśvatakaṃsavairiṇoḥ

śive pitṝṇāmatithau suniścale \|\| 174 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 175 \[unit 1187\]

suyāmuno vatsarāje prasāde'bhyantare'cyute \|

vismāpanākhyā kuhake gandharvanagare smare \|\| 175 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 176 \[unit 1188\]

sukarmā grahayoge syāt sukarmā devaśilpini \|

durnāmākhyātigudajadīrghakośikayorapi \|\| 176 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 177 \[unit 1189\]

dvijanmā daśane vipre bhūtātmā pudgale vidhau \|

āryamā sūryabhaktāyāṃ sūrye ca pitṛdaivate \|\| 177 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 178 \[unit 1190\]

sudāmā bhūdhare meghe viśvagjñā'nalacandrayoḥ \|

sudhanvā prauḍhadhānuṣke sudhanvā viśvakarmaṇi \|\| 178 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 179 \[unit 1191\]

vanaśvā gandhamārjjāre bandhakavyāghrayorapi \|

pariśvā kratukarmajñe yājñike paricārake \|\| 179 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 180 \[unit 1192\]

atharvā brāhmaṇe vede tva'tharva parikīrtitam \|

suparvā tridaśe vaṃśe śare dhūme'pi parvaṇi \|\| 180 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 181 \[unit 1193\]

lalāma ca lalāmañca lāñchanadhvajavājiṣu \|

śṛṅge pradhāne bhūṣāyāṃ ramye bāladhipuṇḍrayoḥ \|\| 181 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 182 \[unit 1194\]

prabhāve ca kalāpī tu plakṣabarhiṇayorapi \|

pratyarthī kathitaḥ śatrau pratyarthī prativādini \|\| 182 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 183 \[unit 1195\]

keśarī turage siṃhe punnāge nāgakeśare \|

śikharī pādape śaile tathā'pāmārgakoṭṭayoḥ \|\| 183 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 184 \[unit 1196\]

śṛṅgārī tu suveśe syāt kramuke ca mataṅgaje \|

vilāsī bhogini vyāle palāśī vṛkṣarakṣasoḥ \|\| 184 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 185 \[unit 1197\]

śikhaṇḍī tu mayūre syādbāṇakṣatriyabhedayoḥ \|

kalāpe cā'tha guñjāyāṃ yūthikāyāṃ śikhaṇḍinī \|\| 185 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 186 \[unit 1198\]

viṣayi tvindriye khyātaṃ vācyavadviṣayānvite \|

vyavāyī kāmape dravyaviśeṣe ca samīritaḥ \|\| 186 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 187 \[unit 1199\]

tapasvī tāpase cānukampake ca tapasvinī \|

māṃsikā kaṭurohiṇyostarasvī vegiśūrayoḥ \|\| 187 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 188 \[unit 1200\]

lāṅgalī balabhadre syānnārikele ca lāṅgalī \|

kuṇḍalī varuṇe kekibhoginośca sakuṇḍale \|\| 188 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 189 \[unit 1201\]

colakī tu karīre syānnāraṅge kiṣkuparvaṇi \|

phalakī syādallisākhyamatsye phalakapāṇike \|\| 189 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 190 \[unit 1202\]

kañcukī bhujage ṣiṅge malle jāṅgalikadrume \|

sāmayonistu sāmotthe sāmayonirgaje vidhau \|\| 190 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 191 \[unit 1203\]

kumbhayoniragastye syādarjjunasya gurāvapi \|

ātmayonirvidhau kāme citrabhānuḥ khage'nale \|\| 191 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 192 \[unit 1204\]

mahāmuniragastye syāddhanyākāgastyayorapi \|

kaladhvaniḥ kokile syāt pārāvatamayūrayoḥ \|\| 192 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 193 \[unit 1205\]

gadayitnuḥ smṛtaḥ kāme jalpake kāmuke'pi ca \|

madayitnurbhavet sīdhau madye syānmadayitnu ca \|\| 193 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 194 \[unit 1206\]

harṣayitnu sute hemni ghoṣayitnurdvije pike \|

stanayitnuḥ payovāhe taddhvanau mṛtyurogayoḥ \|\| 194 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 195 \[unit 1207\]

viṣvaksenā phalinyāṃ syādviśvakseno janārddane \|

devasenendravāhinyāṃ senāyāñca divaukasām \|\| 195 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 196 \[unit 1208\]

nāgāṅganā nāgayaṣṭikarimudgarikākhyayoḥ \|

śleṣmaghanā mallikāyāṃ ketakyāmapi kathyate \|\| 196 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 197 \[unit 1209\]

prasādhinī kaṅkatikā siddhau veśe prasādhanam \|

sāmidhenī ṛci proktā sāmadhenī samidhya'pi \|\| 197 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 198 \[unit 1210\]

sarojinī syāt kāsāre padminīpadmayostathā \|

vilepanī syātturalā suveśāṅganayorapi \|\| 198 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 199 \[unit 1211\]

mātulānī kalāpe sthādbhaṅgāyāṃ mātulastriyām \|

payasvinī tu godhenvāṃ vibhāvaryāṃ payasvinī \|\| 199 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 200 \[unit 1212\]

gavādanīndravāruṇyā gavāṃ ghoṣādanāśraye \|

saudāminya'psarobhede taḍittadbhedayorapi \|\| 200 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 201 \[unit 1213\]

pītacandanamityetat kālīyakahandriyoḥ \|

varacandanamākhyātaṃ kālīye devadāruṇi \|\| 201 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 202 \[unit 1214\]

haricandanamākhyātaṃ gośīrṣe surapādape \|

jyotsnāyāṃ kuṅkume cā'tisarjjanaṃ vadhadānayoḥ \|\| 202 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 203 \[unit 1215\]

madhusūdanasaṃjñā ca bhramare vanamālini \|

syānmṛtyuvañcanaḥ śambhau śrīphaladroṇakākayoḥ \|\| 203 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 204 \[unit 1216\]

syādapavarjjanaṃ mokṣe parityāge vihāyite \|

apasarjjanamāsnāte parivarjjanadānayoḥ \|\| 204 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 205 \[unit 1217\]

mahārajanamuddiṣṭaṃ śātakumbhakusumbhayoḥ \|

syāt pratipādanaṃ dāne pratipattau ca bodhane \|\| 205 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 206 \[unit 1218\]

gandhamādanamityāhurgandhake vānarāntare \|

adribhede ca bhṛṅge ca surāyāṃ gandhamādanī \|\| 206 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 207 \[unit 1219\]

syādanuvāsanaṃ snehakarmmadhūpanayorapi \|

śvetavāhana ityākhyā sudhādhāmni dhanañjaye \|\| 207 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 208 \[unit 1220\]

harivāhana ityuktaḥ śacīpativivasvatoḥ \|

abhiniṣṭhānaśabdo'pi visarjjanīye'kṣare mataḥ \|\| 208 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 209 \[unit 1221\]

dhūmaketanamicchanti hutāśagrahabhedayoḥ \|

syādupasparśanaṃ sparśe snānācamanayorapi \|\| 209 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 210 \[unit 1222\]

śivakīrttananāmā'pi bhṛṅgarīṭe'suradviṣi \|

śālaṅkāyanaśabdaḥ syādṛṣibhede ca nandini \|\| 210 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 211 \[unit 1223\]

syātpadmalācchanābhikhyā vidhau lokeśvarārkayoḥ \|

dhanade ca sarasvatyāṃ tārālakṣmyorapi smṛtā \|\| 211 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 212 \[unit 1224\]

syāt ṣaṣṭhahāyano dhānyaviśeṣe'pi mataṅgaje \|

jānīyādupasampannaṃ nihite ca susaṃskṛte \|\| 212 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 213 \[unit 1225\]

viśvakarmā devaśilpimunibhedoṣṇaraśmiṣu \|

kṛṣṇavartmā hutāśe syād durācāre vidhuntude \|\| 213 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 214 \[unit 1226\]

agrajanmā dvije jyeṣṭhabhrātari brahmaṇi smṛtaḥ \|

śvetadhāmā kalānāthe ghanasārābdhibhedayoḥ \|\| 214 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 215 \[unit 1227\]

tiktaparvā tu yaṣṭyāhvaguḍūcīhilamociṣu \|

viṣkuparvā bhavedveṇāvikṣau poṭagale'pi ca \|\| 215 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 216 \[unit 1228\]

vṛṣaparvā hare daitye śṛṅgāruṇi kaseruṇi \|

vyomacārī khage deve cirajīvī dvikākayoḥ \|\| 216 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 217 \[unit 1229\]

kārandhame kāṃsyakāre dhātuvādarate'pi ca \|

vanamālī tu govinde vārāhyāṃ vanamālinī \|\| 217 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 218 \[unit 1230\]

pracalākī bhujaṅge syāccitramekhalake'pi ca \|

samprayogī kalākelau kāmuke suprayojake \|\| 218 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 219 \[unit 1231\]

antevāsī bhavecchiṣye cāṇḍāle prāntage'pi ca \|

vighnakārī smṛto ghoradarśane'pi vighātini \|\| 219 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 220 \[unit 1232\]

kāmacārī tu kamane svacchandakalaviṅkayoḥ \|

hṛṣṭaśṛṅgī bhaveddaṃśe nārīṣaṇḍe vṛkodare \|\| 220 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 221 \[unit 1233\]

śakulādanya'bhikhyā tu māṃsīpiculikāhvayoḥ \|

kaṭukājalapippalyoḥ kathyate śakulādinī \|\| 221 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 222 \[unit 1234\]

syādvaravarṇinī lākṣāharidrārocanāsu \|

strīratne ca phalinyāñca dṛśyate varavarṇinī \|\| 222 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṭkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṭkam / 223 \[unit 1235\]

antāvasāyī śvapace munibhede ca nāpite \|

jāyānujīvī tu naṭe durgatāśvinayorbake \|\| 223 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṭkam / 224 \[unit 1236\]

sahasravedhī syādamlavetase rāmaṭe'pi ca \|

kalānunādī rolambe kalaviṅke kapiñjale \|\| 224 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṭkam / fragment 1237

iti nāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 1 \[unit 1238\]

rūpaṃ svabhāve saundaryye nāmake paśuśabdayoḥ \|

granthāvṛttau nāṭakādāvākāraślokayorapi \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 2 \[unit 1239\]

repaḥ smānnindite krūre ropo ropaṇavāṇayoḥ \|

sūpo vyañjanabhede syāt sūpakāre ca kīrttitaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 3 \[unit 1240\]

chupaḥ kṣupasparśanayoścupaḥ pavanayuddhayoḥ \|

kūpaḥ kūpakagartābhyumṛnmānaguṇavṛkṣake \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 4 \[unit 1241\]

tāpo'stitāpe davathau tāpī tu saridantare \|

śāpaḥ śapatha ākrośe trapusīsakaraṅgayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 5 \[unit 1242\]

svāpaḥ sparśājñatānidrāśayanāśanamātrake \|

nīpo dhūlikadambe syānnīlāśoke ca dhanvini \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 6 \[unit 1243\]

gopo grāmaughagoṣṭhādhikṛtayorvallave nṛpe \|

gopī gopālamahilā śārivārattikāsu ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 7 \[unit 1244\]

kṣepo vilambe nindāyāṃ helāpe raṇalaṅghane \|

garve'pi lepastu sudhābhojanālepaneṣu ca \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 8 \[unit 1245\]

talpantu śayanīye syāttalpamaṭṭakalatrayoḥ \|

kalpaḥ syāt pralaye nyāye śāstre brāhmadine vidhau \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 9 \[unit 1246\]

darpo'haṅkārakasūryorbāṣpo netrajaloṣmaṇoḥ \|

puṣpaṃ vikāśe kusume strīṇāñca rajasi smṛtam \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 10 \[unit 1247\]

śaṣpaṃ syāt pratibhāhānau śaṣpaṃ bālatṛṇe'pi ca \|

śaṣpaḥ stave kriyāyogye śaṣpaḥ krodhe balātkṛtau \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 11 \[unit 1248\]

kṛpā dayāyāṃ vyāsarṣau kṛpo bhāratapūruṣe \|

trapā lajjākulaṭayorvapā vivaramedasoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 12 \[unit 1249\]

kṛtapaḥ syāt kuśe vādye tapane chāgakambale \|

kṛtapo bhāgineye syādaṣṭamāṃśe dinasya ca \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 13 \[unit 1250\]

kuṭapo mānabhede syāt kuṭapo niṣkuṭe munau \|

viṭapaḥ pallave śṛṅge vistāre stambaśākhayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 14 \[unit 1251\]

ulapastṛṇabhede syād gulminyāmulapaḥ smṛtaḥ \|

uḍupastu plave candre prapātaḥ svedatejasoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 15 \[unit 1252\]

raktapo rakṣasi prokto jalaukāyāntu raktapā \|

jihvāpaḥ śuni mārjjāre vyāghracitrakayorapi \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 16 \[unit 1253\]

kāśyapaḥ syānmunau mānabhede bhūmau tu kāśyapī \|

pādapaḥ pādapīṭhe'drau pādukāyāntu pādapā \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 17 \[unit 1254\]

anūpaṃ mahiṣe vidyājjalaprāye tu vācyavat \|

āvāpo bhāṇḍapavane parikṣepālavālayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 18 \[unit 1255\]

ākṣepo bhartsanākṛṣṭikāvyālaṅkṛtiṣu smṛtaḥ \|

ekoktyā kaśipurbhaktyā chādane ca dvayoḥ pṛthaka \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 19 \[unit 1256\]

ākalpaḥ kalpane veśe vikalpo bhrāntipakṣayoḥ \|

kacchapī vallakībhede dulau kṣudragadāntare \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 20 \[unit 1257\]

mallabandhaviśepe'pi kacchapaḥ kuṇapī punaḥ \|

viṭśārikāyā kuṇapaḥ pūtigandhau śave'pi ca \|

kalāpo bhūṣaṇe barhe tūṇīre saṃhate'pi ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 21 \[unit 1258\]

prāptarūpo budhe ramye'pyabhirūpaḥ surūpavat \|

bahurūpaḥ śive viṣṇau dhūnake saraṭe smare \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 22 \[unit 1259\]

vṛkadhūpastu sarale dravakṛttrimadhūpayoḥ \|

parivāpastu paryāptau jalasthāne paricchade \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 23 \[unit 1260\]

upatāpastvarāyāṃ syāduttāpagadayorapi \|

avalepastu garve syāllepane dūṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 24 \[unit 1261\]

vipralāpo virodhāktāvapārthavacane'pi ca \|

vṛṣākapiḥ śive kṛṣṇe jvalane ca vṛṣākapiḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 25 \[unit 1262\]

parikampo bhave kampe'palāpaḥ premṇyapahnave \|

bījapuṣpaṃ maruvake tathā damanake'pi ca \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 26 \[unit 1263\]

hemapuṣpaṃ javāpuṣpe campakāśokayormatam \|

nāgapuṣpastu punnāge nāgakeśaracampake \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 27 \[unit 1264\]

piṇḍapuṣpamaśoke ca javāyāñca kuśeśaye \|

meghapuṣpantu piṇḍābhre nādeyajalayorapi \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / fragment 1265

jalakūpī kūpagartte puṣkariṇyāñca kathyate \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / papañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / papañcakam / 28 \[unit 1266\]

bhaveccāmarapuṣpastu cūte ketakahāsayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / pāntavargaḥ / papañcakam / fragment 1267

iti pāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / phāntavargaḥ / phadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / phāntavargaḥ / phadvikam / 1 \[unit 1268\]

repho ravarṇe samproktaḥ kutsite vācyavat punaḥ \|

śaphaṃ mūle tarūṇāṃ syādgavādīnāṃ khure'pi ca \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / phāntavargaḥ / phadvikam / 2 \[unit 1269\]

guphaḥ syādgumphane bāhoralaṅkāre ca kīrtyate \|

śiphā jaṭāyāṃ sariti māṃsikāyāñca mātari \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / phāntavargaḥ / phadvikam / fragment 1270

iti phāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / badvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 1 \[unit 1271\]

pūrvākhyā pūrvajeṣu syādagre prāci ca vācyavat \|

nimbaḥ syāt picumardde ca tiktake ca cirāyate \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 2 \[unit 1272\]

bimbaṃ phale bimbikāyāṃ pratibimbe ca maṇḍale \|

ḍimbaḥ syādviplavaplīhnoreraṇḍe pupphuse bhaye \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 3 \[unit 1273\]

stambo gulme tṛṇādīnāmaprakāṇḍadrume'pi ca \|

śambaḥ syānmuṣalāgrasthe lohamaṇḍalake pavau \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 4 \[unit 1274\]

śubhānvite ca kharbaḥ syād hrasve saṅkhyāntare'pi ca \|

kambuḥ śambūkagajayorvīvānalakaśaṅkhayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 5 \[unit 1275\]

jambūḥ sumerusariti dvīpa maviśeṣayoḥ \|

kambiraṃśe ca vaṃśasya khajākāyāmapīṣyate \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 6 \[unit 1276\]

darbī bhavet khajākāyāṃ phaṇāyāmuragasya ca \|

dārbī dāruharidrāyāṃ devadāruharidrayoḥ \|

cārbī tu śobhanāvṛddhyorlambā śrītiktatumbayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / batrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 7 \[unit 1277\]

kadambamāhuḥ siddhārthe nīpe'pi nikurambake \|

kādambaḥ kalahaṃseṣvordvijihbo bhujage khale \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 8 \[unit 1278\]

gajāhbā karipippalyāṃ gajāhvaṃ hastināpure \|

nitambo rodhasi skandhe śikhare'pi kaṭīrake \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 9 \[unit 1279\]

herambo vighnarāje syāt kāsare śauryagarvite \|

kalambaḥ śāyake nīpe nā'liśābakalabyapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 10 \[unit 1280\]

pralambo daityabhede syād bālāṅkura\_śākhayoḥ \|

pralambo hārabhede syāttrapuṣe'pi payodhare \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 11 \[unit 1281\]

gandharvo mṛgabhede syat puṃskokilaturaṅgayoḥ \|

antarābhavasattve ca gāyane khecare'pi ca \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 12 \[unit 1282\]

goḍumbaḥ śīrṇavṛnte syādgavādanyāḥ phale'pi ca \|

bhūjambūrapi godhūme vikaṅkataphale'pi ca \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / bacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / bacatuṣkam / 13 \[unit 1283\]

lalajjihvo mato hiṃsre kramelakaśunorapi \|

śataparbā ca dūrvāyāṃ bhārgave'sya striyāmapi \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / bacatuṣkam / fragment 1284

rājajambūstu jambūbhitpiṇḍakharjūrayoḥ smṛtā \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / bapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / bapañcakam / 14 \[unit 1285\]

dhūlīkadambo nīpe syāttiniśe varuṇadrume \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / bapañcakam / 15 \[unit 1286\]

gorakṣajambūrgodhūme tathā gorakṣataṇḍule \|

śṛgālajambūrgoḍumbe kvāpi ghoṇṭāphale'pi ca \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bāntavargaḥ / bapañcakam / fragment 1287

iti bāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhaikakam / fragment 1288

bhaḥ syānmayūkhe śukre bhaṃ nakṣatre ca prakīrttitam \|

bhūḥ pṛthivyāṃ sthānamātre-

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 16 \[unit 1289\]

svabhūrvedhasi śārṅgiṇi \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 17 \[unit 1290\]

śubho yoge śubhe kṣeme nibho vyājasadṛkṣayoḥ \|

vibhuḥ śive prabhau nitye śambhurbrahmārhatorhare \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 18 \[unit 1291\]

dambhastu kaitave kalke ḍimbho bāliśapotayoḥ \|

jambho daityāntare dambhe jambīre bhakṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 19 \[unit 1292\]

kumbhaḥ syāt kumbhakarṇasya sute veśyāsute ghaṭe \|

rāśibhede dvipāṅge ca kumbhaṃ trivṛti guggulau \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 20 \[unit 1293\]

garbho bhrūṇe'rbhake kukṣau sandhau panasakaṇṭake \|

jṛmbho mukhavikāśe ca stambhaḥ sthūṇājaḍatvayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 21 \[unit 1294\]

rambhā kadalyapsaraso rambho vaiṇavadaṇḍake \|

sabhā sāmājike dyūte goṣṭhīmandirayorapi \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 22 \[unit 1295\]

śobhā kāntīcchayoruktā dṛmbhūḥ pannagavajrayoḥ \|

nābhiḥ prāṇyaṅgaje kṣatre cakrāntacakravarttinoḥ \|

nābhiḥ pradhāne kastūrīmade ca kvacidīritaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 23 \[unit 1296\]

karabhā maṇibandhādikaniṣṭhānte tathoṣṭrake \|

karabhaḥ śarabhastvaṣṭāpade prokto mṛgāntare \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 24 \[unit 1297\]

ṛṣabhaḥ svarabhede syādaṣṭavargauṣadhe vṛṣe \|

śreṣṭhārthe ca varāhasya pucche randhre ca karṇayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 25 \[unit 1298\]

ṛṣabhī śūkaśimbyāṃ syānnarākārastriyāmapi \|

vidhavāyāṃ śirālāyāṃ vṛṣabhaḥ pujñave vṛṣe \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 26 \[unit 1299\]

vallabho dayite'yakṣe kulīnāśve ca vallabhaḥ \|

durlabhaḥ karcūre nyāye duṣprāpe vallabhe'pi ca \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 27 \[unit 1300\]

nikumbhaḥ kathito daitye kumbhakarṇasute'pi ca \|

kusumbho hemani mahārajane ca kamaṇḍalau \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 28 \[unit 1301\]

viṣṭambhaḥ pratibandhe ca vaidarbhe ca prayujyate \|

viśrambhaḥ kelikalahe viśvāse praṇaye vadhe \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 29 \[unit 1302\]

viṣkambho yogabhede syādvistārapratibandhayoḥ \|

rūpakāṅgaprabhede ca bandhabhede ca yoginām \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 30 \[unit 1303\]

kakubho rāgabhede'pi vīṇāṅge'rjjunapādape \|

ārambhastu turāyāṃ syādudyame vadhadarpayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 31 \[unit 1304\]

surabhiścampake svarṇe jātīphalavasantayoḥ \|

gandhotpale saurabheyyāṃ śallakīmātṛbhedayoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 32 \[unit 1305\]

sugandhe ca manojñe ca vācyavat surabhiḥ smṛtaḥ \|

sanābhiḥ sadṛśe jñātāvātmabhūrvedhasi smare \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 33 \[unit 1306\]

varṣābhūḥ punarnavāyāṃ plave kicaluke'pi ca \|

dundubhirditije bheryyāmakṣabindutrikadvaye \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 34 \[unit 1307\]

vaidarbhaṃ vākyavakratve vidarbhaśca nṛpāntare \|

garddabho gandhabhede syādgarddabhaṃ kairave khare \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 35 \[unit 1308\]

gardabho gardabhī kṣudrarogajantuviśeṣayoḥ \|

kakuba śobhādiśoḥ śāstre praveṇyāṃ campakasraji \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhacatuṣkam / 36 \[unit 1309\]

avaṣṭambhaḥ suvarṇe ca stambhaprārambhayorapi \|

śātakumbhaṃ suvarṇe syācchātakumbho'śvamārake \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / bhāntavargaḥ / bhacatuṣkam / fragment 1310

iti bhāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / maikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / maikakam / fragment 1311

maḥ śive mā ramāyāñca mā niṣedhe'vyayaṃ matam \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 1 \[unit 1312\]

kiṃ syādvitarke praśne ca kṣepe nindāprakārayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 2 \[unit 1313\]

damastu damathe daṇḍe karddame damane'pi ca \|

gamo dyūtaprabhede syādaparyālocite'dhvani \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 3 \[unit 1314\]

drumastarau pārijāte drumaḥ kimpuruṣeśvare \|

bhramo'mbunirgame bhrāntau kundākhye śilpiyantrake \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 4 \[unit 1315\]

himaṃ śīte tuṣāre ca candane ca himaṃ viduḥ \|

ramaḥ kānte ramā lakṣmyāṃ raktāśokadrume smare \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 5 \[unit 1316\]

yamo daṇḍadhare dhvāṅkṣe saṃyame yamaje'pi ca \|

śarīrasādhanāpekṣanityakarmaṇi cocyate \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 6 \[unit 1317\]

kramaḥ śaktau parīpāṭyāṃ kramaścalanakampayoḥ \|

kṣaumamaṭṭe dukūle syādatasīvasane'pi ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 7 \[unit 1318\]

kṣemā syānmaṅgale labdharakṣaṇe caurake'pi ca \|

kṣemā dhanaharīgauryoḥ kṣamaḥ khyātaḥ kṣamānvite \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 8 \[unit 1319\]

kṣitiḥ kṣāntau kṣamākhyātā hite śakte ca vācyavat \|

āmo roge tadviśeṣe āmo'pakve ca vācyavat \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 9 \[unit 1320\]

kāmaḥ smare'bhilāṣe ca kāmaṃ retonikāmayoḥ \|

avyayantvabhyanujñāyāṃ yāmastu praharaṃ vrate \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 10 \[unit 1321\]

śyāmaḥ syānmecake vṛddhadārake harite ghane \|

vaṭadrume prayāgasya śyāmaḥ śyāmā tu balgulau \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 11 \[unit 1322\]

aprasūtāṅganāyāñca tathā somalatauṣadhau \|
