trivṛtā śāribāgundrāniśākṛṣṇāpriyaṅguṣu \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 12 \[unit 1323\]

śyāmā nīlyāmpike śyāmaṃ marīce lavaṇāntare \|

śyāmo damanake gandhatṛṇe śyāme'bhidheyavat \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 13 \[unit 1324\]

gulmaḥ stambe plīhni ghaṭṭasainyayoḥ sainyarakṣaṇe \|

gulmī syādāmalakyelāparṇikāvastraveśmasu \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 14 \[unit 1325\]

jālmaḥ syāt pāmare krūre jālmo'samīkṣyakāriṇi \|

grāmaḥ svare saṃvasathe vṛnde śabdādipūrvakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 15 \[unit 1326\]

bhīmo'lpavetase śambhau ghore cāpi vṛkodare \|

yudhmaḥ saṅgāmadhanuṣo riṣmaḥ kāmavasantayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 16 \[unit 1327\]

tokmaṃ karṇamale tokmo harite ca haridyave \|

rukmañca kāñcane lohe kharmmaṃ kṣaume ca pauruṣe \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 17 \[unit 1328\]

dharmmaḥ puṇye yame nyāye svabhāvācārayoḥ kratau \|

upamāyāmahiṃsāyāṃ cāpe copanigadyate \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 18 \[unit 1329\]

gharmmaḥ syādātape grīṣme uṣṇasvedāmbhasorapi \|

jihmastu kuṭile mande jihmaṃ tagarapādape \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 19 \[unit 1330\]

sūkṣmaṃ syāt kaitave'dhyātme'pyaṇau sūkṣmolpake'nyavat \|

bhīṣmastu bhīṣaṇe rudre gāṅgeye ca niśācare \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 20 \[unit 1331\]

śuṣmaṃ tejo'rkayoruktaṃ sūmaṃ kṣīre nabhasya'pi \|

dasmastu yajamāne syādapi caure hutāśane \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 21 \[unit 1332\]

rāmaḥ paśuviśeṣe syājjāmadagnye halāyudhe \|

rāghave cā'sitaśvetamanojñeṣu ca vācyavat \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 22 \[unit 1333\]

rāmā'ṅganāhiṅgulinyo rāmaṃ vāstukakuṣṭhayoḥ \|

vāmaṃ savye pratīpe ca draviṇe cā'tisundare \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 23 \[unit 1334\]

payodhare hare kāme vidyādvāmāpi ca striyām \|

vāmī śagālīvaḍavārāsabhīkarabhīṣu ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 24 \[unit 1335\]

payaḥ syāt pannage vyūhe nidhau saṅkhyāntare'mbuje \|

padmake bindujāle'pi padmā bhāṅgīśriyorapi \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 25 \[unit 1336\]

somaḥ kubere pitṛdevatāyāṃ

vasuprabhede ca sudhākare ca \|

divyauṣadhīsomalatāsamīra-

karpūranīreṣu ca vānare ca \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 26 \[unit 1337\]

bhūmiḥ kṣitau sthānamātre vamirvāntau hutāśane \|

raśmiraṃśau pragrahe ca jāmiḥ svasṛkulastriyoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 27 \[unit 1338\]

nemistrikāyāṃ kūpasya cakrānte tiniśadrume \|

nemaḥ kāle'vadhau gartte prākāre kaitave'pi ca \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 28 \[unit 1339\]

kṛmiḥ syāttṛṇakīṭe ca lākṣāyāṃ kṛmile svare \|

kāmiḥ syāt kāmuke ratyāṃ homiḥ sarpiṣi pāvake \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 29 \[unit 1340\]

ūrmmistaraṅge pīḍāyāṃ vegabhaṅgaprakāśayoḥ \|

utkaṇṭhāvastrasaṅkocalekhayorapi kīrtitā \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 30 \[unit 1341\]

lakṣmīḥ śrīśivasampattipadmāśobhāpriyaṅguṣu \|

kirmī palāśe śālāyāṃ hemaputryāmudīritā \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 31 \[unit 1342\]

śamī śaktuphalāyāñca śimbāyāmapi valgulau \|

brāhmī tu bhāratīsomavallarībrahmaśaktiṣu \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 32 \[unit 1343\]

phañjikāpaṅkajaṭikāśākabhedeṣu ca smṛtā \|

samā varṣe samaṃ tulye sādhau ca sadṛśe'nyavat \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 33 \[unit 1344\]

sīmā ghoṭe sthitau kṣetre maryādāvelayorapi \|

kṣumā'tasīnālikayornṛnāmni paramadyutau \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 34 \[unit 1345\]

umā'tasīhaimavatīhastikīrttikāntiṣu \|

rumā sugrīvadāreṣu viśiṣṭe lavaṇākare \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 35 \[unit 1346\]

uttamā dugdhikāyāṃ syādutkṛṣṭe cottame'nyavat \|

madhyamaḥ syāt svare madhye madhyadeśe ca bhedaje \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 36 \[unit 1347\]

vācyavanmadhyamātūktā rākādṛṣṭarajaḥstriyoḥ \|

karṇikā tryakṣaracchandaḥkaramadhyāṅgulīṣu ca \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 37 \[unit 1348\]

adhamaḥ kutsite nyūne prakramo'vasare krame \|

vikramaḥ krāntimātre syādvikramaḥ śaktisampadi \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 38 \[unit 1349\]

saṅkramaḥ kramaṇe sampadvārisañcārayantrake \|

niṣkramo buddhisampattau nirgame duṣkule'pi ca \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 39 \[unit 1350\]

āgamaḥ śāstra āyāte vibhramo bhrāntihāvayoḥ \|

sambhramaḥ sādhvase'pi syāt saṃvegādarayorapi \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 40 \[unit 1351\]

vidrumo ratnadṛkṣe'pi pravāle pallave'pi ca \|

āśramo brahmacaryādicatuṣke'pi maṭhe'pi ca \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 41 \[unit 1352\]

sattamaścānyavat pūjye śreṣṭhasādhīyasorapi \|

niyamo mantraṇāyāṃ syāta pratijñāyāñca saṃyame \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 42 \[unit 1353\]

nigamo vāṇije puryāṃ kaṭe vede vaṇikpathe \|

naigamaḥ syādupaniṣadvaṇijornāgare'pi ca \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 43 \[unit 1354\]

kalamo leravanīcauraśālikākṣarakeṣu ca \|

talimaṃ kuṭṭime talpe candrahāse vitānake \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 44 \[unit 1355\]

paramaṃ syādanujñāte'pyavyayaṃ paramaḥ pare \|

paramaṃ syāt pradhānādyoroṅkāre'pi tathocyate \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 45 \[unit 1356\]

kusumaṃ puṣpaphalayoḥ strīrajonetrarogayoḥ \|

kṛtrimaṃ racite proktaṃ sihrake lavaṇāntare \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 46 \[unit 1357\]

suṣamaṃ cārusamayoḥ suṣamā paramadyutau \|

suṣīmaḥ śiśire cārau suṣīmaḥ pannagāntare \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 47 \[unit 1358\]

pañcamo rāgabhede syāt pañcānāmapi pūraṇe \|

pañcamaścature hṛdye pañcamī pāṇḍavastriyāma \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 48 \[unit 1359\]

gautamaḥ śākyasiṃhe ca munibhede'tha gautamī \|

rocanyāmambikāyāñca dāḍimaḥ karakailayoḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 49 \[unit 1360\]

godhūmo nāgaraṅge syādbheṣajavrīhibhedayoḥ \|

vyāyāmo durgasañcāre vyāyāmaḥ pauruṣe'pi ca \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 50 \[unit 1361\]

vilomastu pratīpe syād bhujaṅge varuṇe śuni \|

āmalakyāṃ vilomī ca vilomaṃ cārughaṭṭake \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 51 \[unit 1362\]

golomī śvetadūrvāyāṃ ṣaḍhgranthāvārayopitoḥ \|

pratimā dantabandhe syādgajasyānukṛtāvapi \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 52 \[unit 1363\]

plavaṅgamaḥ kapau bheke'nupamaḥ sundare'nyavat \|

supratīkasya yoṣāyāṃ bhavedanupamā'pi ca \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 53 \[unit 1364\]

abhyāgamo'ntike ghāte virodhābhyudgamādiṣu \|

yātayāmastu jīrṇe syāt paribhuktojjhiteṣu ca \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 54 \[unit 1365\]

daṇḍayāmastu kīnāśe divase kumbhasambhave \|

sārvabhaumastu diṅnāge sārvapṛthvīpatāvapi \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 1366\]

parākramo vikrame syāt sāmarthyodyogayorapi \|

upakramaḥ syādupadhācikitsārambhavikrame \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 1367\]

jalagulmo jalāvartte kacchape jalacatvare \|

mahāpadmaḥ smṛto nāganidhisaṅkhyāntareṣu ca \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / mapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / mapañcakam / 57 \[unit 1368\]

abhyupagamaḥ svīkāre samīpāgamane'pi ca \|

nakṣatranemiḥ śītāṃśau revatyāñca dhruve kvacit \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / māntavargaḥ / mapañcakam / fragment 1369

iti māntavargaḥ

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yaikakam / 1 \[unit 1370\]

yaḥ sarvanāmānilayorjyā maurvīmātṛbhūmiṣu \|

dyuragnau divase'pi syād dyauḥ svargasuravartmanoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 2 \[unit 1371\]

jayo jayantau vijaye jayā durgāgnimanthayoḥ \|

jayantī tithibhedomāsakhīpathyāsu ca smṛtā \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 3 \[unit 1372\]

cayaḥ samūhe prākāre mūlabandhe samāhṛtau \|

nayo nītau dyūtabhede śayaḥ śayyāhipāṇiṣu \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 4 \[unit 1373\]

bhayaṃ pratibhaye ghore prasūne kubjakasya ca \|

mayaḥ śilpini daityānāṃ karabhe'śvatare'pi ca \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 5 \[unit 1374\]

layo vināśe saṃśleṣe sāmye tauryyatrikasya ca \|

smayo garve'dbhute geyo gātavye gāyane'pi ca \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 6 \[unit 1375\]

kṣayo rogāntare veśmakalpāntāpacayeṣvapi \|

priyo dhave sauhṛdye ca vṛddhināmauṣadhe'pi ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 7 \[unit 1376\]

kāyaḥ kadaivate mūrttau saṅghe lakṣyasvabhāvayoḥ \|

kāyo manuṣyatīrthe'pi sāyaḥ kāṇḍāparāhṇayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 8 \[unit 1377\]

dāyo dāne yautakādighane solluṇṭhabhāṣaṇe \|

vibhaktapitṛdravyañca dāyamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 9 \[unit 1378\]

prāyaścā'naśane mṛtyau tulyabāhulyayorapi \|

peyaṃ pātavyapayasoḥ peyā śrāṇotthamaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 10 \[unit 1379\]

stheyo vivādasya padanirṇetari purohite \|

pīyuḥ kāle ravau ghūke yayuḥ kratuhaye haye \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 11 \[unit 1380\]

mayusturaṅgavadane mṛge'pi mayurucyate \|

manyuḥ krodhe kratau dainye mṛtyurmaraṇadevayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 12 \[unit 1381\]

dasyuḥ stene ca śatrau ca janyuḥ prāṇyagnidhātṛṣu \|

vanyaṃ vanabhave vanyā vanavārisamūhayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 13 \[unit 1382\]

janyaṃ haṭṭe parīvāde saṃyuje janake punaḥ \|

janyaḥ syācchayanīye ca janyā mātṛsakhīmudoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 14 \[unit 1383\]

janyo varadhūjñātipriyabhṛtyahite'pi ca \|

śūnyākhyā nirjjane nalyāmanyo bhinnāsamānayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 15 \[unit 1384\]

paśyaṃ pānīyake'linde pūjyaḥ śvasuravandyayoḥ \|

hāryo bibhītakatarau hartavye hāryyamanyavat \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 16 \[unit 1385\]

vīryaṃ śukre prabhāve ca tejaḥsāmarthyayorapi \|

āryaḥ sādhau sauvidalle'pyāryomāvṛttabhedayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 17 \[unit 1386\]

aryaḥ svāmini vaiśye ca kārya hetau prayojane \|

sūryaḥ sūre tatpriyāyāṃ sūryā syādoṣadhāvapi \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 18 \[unit 1387\]

śauryyaṃ cārabhaṭīśaktyorvaryaḥ smaravareṇyayoḥ \|

guhyaṃ rahasyupasthe ca guhyaḥ kamaṭhadambhayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 19 \[unit 1388\]

sahyaḥ śailāntarārogyasoḍhavyeṣu pracakṣyate \|

guhyaṃ purīṣamārge'pi bhavedasvairapakṣayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 20 \[unit 1389\]

gṛhyā ca śākhānagare gṛhyaścheke'pyudīritaḥ \|

yogyaḥ pravīṇe yogyārhopāyaśakteṣu cānyavat \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 21 \[unit 1390\]

yogyamudvyākhyabhaiṣajye yogyābhyāsārkayoṣitoḥ \|

bhāgyaṃ śubhātmakavidhau syācchubhāśubhakarmaṇi \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 22 \[unit 1391\]

pāṭhyaṃ piṇḍākhyalavaṇe yavakṣāre'pi dṛśyate \|

pathyaṃ hite harītakyāṃ pathyāpathyorathodvahe \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 23 \[unit 1392\]

rathyā rathaughe viśikhā varttanīcatvareṣu ca \|

arthyaṃ śilājatunyarthe budhe nyāyye tu vācyavat \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 24 \[unit 1393\]

grāmyo jane'nyavadgrāmyamaślīlarathabandhayoḥ \|

saumyo budhe manojñe syādanugre somadaivate \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 25 \[unit 1394\]

ilvalāsu ca saumyāḥ syurnīce bodhye ca vācyavat \|

ramyaṃ manorame ramyā rātrau ramyaśca campake \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 26 \[unit 1395\]

saṅkhyaṃ samiti saṅkhyā syādekatvādivicārayoḥ \|

madhyaṃ nyāyye'vakāśe cāvalagne lagnake'dhame \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 27 \[unit 1396\]

medhyaṃ śucau medure ca vācyavanmedhyamāśrame \|

sādhyā'bhikhyā sādhanīye yoge gaṇadaivatayoḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 28 \[unit 1397\]

vandhyo'phaladrume vandhyā tvaprajātastriyāmapi \|

hṛdyaṃ dhavalajīre ca hṛtpriye hṛdbhave'pi ca \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 29 \[unit 1398\]

vaśakṛdvedamantre ca hṛdyaṃ vṛddhākhyabheṣaje \|

codyaṃ syādadbhute praśne codanārhe tu vācyavat \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 30 \[unit 1399\]

padyaṃ śloke sṛtau padyā padyuḥ śūdre nigadyate \|

puṇyaṃ manojñe'bhihitaṃ tayā sukṛtadharmayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 31 \[unit 1400\]

dhiṣṇyaṃ padmani nakṣatre sthāne śaktau ca pāvake \|

dhānyaṃ vrīhisudhānyāke dhanyo dhanavati smṛtaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 32 \[unit 1401\]

dhanyā dhātryāmalakyośca kuḍyaṃ bhittau vilepane \|

kāvyaṃ granthe gṛhe kāvyaḥ kāvyā syāt pūtanādhiyoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 33 \[unit 1402\]

cavyantu cavike cavyā śataparvogragandhayoḥ \|

gavyañca gohite gavyaṃ tathā kṣīrādikaṃ gavām \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 34 \[unit 1403\]

rāgadravye ca gavyā tu gokule kathitā budhaiḥ \|

dravyaṃ syād draviṇe bhavye pṛthivyādai ca pittale \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 35 \[unit 1404\]

bheṣaje ca nivedye ca jatudrumavikārayoḥ \|

bhavyaṃ satye śubhe cā'tha bhedyavadyogyabhāvinoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 36 \[unit 1405\]

karmaraṅgatarau bhavyo bhavyā karikaṇomayoḥ \|

divyaṃ lavaṅgake divyaṃ valgau divibhave'nyavat \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 37 \[unit 1406\]

āmalakyāṃ smṛtā divyā savyaṃ vāmapratīkayoḥ \|

sevyaṃ proktamuśīre ca sevārhe punaranyavata \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 38 \[unit 1407\]

gopyo dāsīsute gopyo rakṣaṇīye'bhidheyavat \|

rūpyaṃ syādāhate svarṇarajate rajate'pi ca \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 39 \[unit 1408\]

rūpyaṃ praśastarūpe tu vācyavat samudīritam \|

ibhya āvye kareṇvāntu bhavedibhyā tu śallakau \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 40 \[unit 1409\]

labhyaṃ yukte ca labdhavye cārdhyamarghārthayogyayoḥ \|

cityaṃ mṛtakacaitye syāccityā mṛtacitāvapi \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 41 \[unit 1410\]

caityamāyatane buddhabimbe coddeśapādape \|

daityo'sure surāyāntu daityā caṇḍauṣadhāvapi \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 42 \[unit 1411\]

bhṛtyo dāse bhṛtau bhṛtyā dantyo dantabhave'dhame \|

satyañca śapathe tathye kṛte tadvati vācyavat \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 43 \[unit 1412\]

tapolokāt pare satyo nityantu satate dhruve \|

mūlyantu vetane vastre mālyaṃ mālāprasūnayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 44 \[unit 1413\]

balyaṃ praṣāne dhātau syād balyaṃ balakare'pi ca \|

kalpaṃ sajje prabhāte ca kalpo nīrogadakṣayoḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 45 \[unit 1414\]

kalyā kalyāṇavāci syāt kādambaryyāmapi smṛtā \|

śalyaṃ śaṅkau śare vaṃśakambikāyāñca tomare \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 46 \[unit 1415\]

śalyastu kathitaḥ śvāvinmadanadrumayorapi \|

kulyaḥ kulodbhave'mātye kulasyātihite'pi ca \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 47 \[unit 1416\]

kulyaṃ syāt kīkase'pyaṣṭadroṇīśūrpāmiṣeṣu ca \|

payaḥ praṇālīsaritoḥ kulyājīvantikauṣaṣau \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 48 \[unit 1417\]

veśyaṃ veśyāgṛhe veśyā gaṇikāyāmudīritā \|

āsyaṃ vaktre vaktramadhye sthitāvāsyā ca viśrutā \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 49 \[unit 1418\]

lāsyaṃ tauryatrike nāṭye śasye śaste phale guṇe \|

kaśyaṃ madhye turaṅgāṇāṃ kaśyaṃ madhyakaśārhayoḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 50 \[unit 1419\]

kāṃsyantu taijasadravye vādyabhitpānapātrayoḥ \|

matsyo mīnāntare mīne virāṭābhikhyayādave \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 51 \[unit 1420\]

tiṣyaḥ puṣye kalau dhātryāṃ tiṣyā puṣyavadiṣyate \|

dūṣyantu dūṣaṇīye syād dūṣyaṃ vastre ca tadgrahe \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 52 \[unit 1421\]

vīkṣyantu vismaye dṛśye vīkṣyo lāsakavājinoḥ \|

tārkṣyaḥ syādaśvakarṇākhyavṛkṣe rathaturaṅgayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 53 \[unit 1422\]

tārkṣyaṃ rasāñjane tārkṣyo garuḍe garuḍāgraje \|

lakṣyaṃ śaravye saṅkhyāyāṃ lakṣyaṃ chadmani sammatam \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 54 \[unit 1423\]

yāmyā'vācyāṃ bharaṇyāñca yāmyo'gastye ca candane \|

ijyā dāne'dhvare'rccāyāṃ saṅge cejyo gurau mataḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 55 \[unit 1424\]

vrajyā paryaṭane proktā vargaprasthānayorapi \|

śayyā talpe śabdatalpe syānmāyā śāmbarīdhiyoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 56 \[unit 1425\]

kanyā kumārikānāryoroṣadhīrāśibhedayoḥ \|

kakṣyā bṛhantikāyāṃ syāt kakṣyā madhyebhabandhane \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 57 \[unit 1426\]

harmyādīnāṃ prakoṣṭhe ca kṛtyaṃ vidviṣṭakāryayoḥ \|

kṛtyā'pi devatābhede kṛtyā stavyādiṣu smṛtā \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 58 \[unit 1427\]

vindhyā tu śailavalyāñca vindhyo rukśailabhedayoḥ \|

sandhyā nadīkālabhidościntāmaryādayorapi \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 59 \[unit 1428\]

pratijñāyāñca sandhāne sandhyā tu kusumāntare \|

chāyā syādātapābhāve pratibimbārkayoṣitoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 60 \[unit 1429\]

pālanotkocayoḥ kāntisacchobhāpaṅktiṣu smṛtā \|

kriyā karmaṇi ceṣṭāyāṃ karaṇe sampradhāraṇe \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 61 \[unit 1430\]

ārambhopāyaśikṣāryacikitsāniṣkṛtiṣvapi \|

māyā dambhe kṛpāyāñca māyaḥ pītāmbare'mbare \|

trayī trivedyāṃ tritaye purandhryāṃ sumatāvapi \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 62 \[unit 1431\]

vijayastu jaye pārthe gauryāntu vijayā tithau \|

vinayaṃ praṇatau prāhuḥ śikṣāyāṃ vinayā matā \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 63 \[unit 1432\]

viṣayaḥ syādindriyārthe deśe janapade'pi ca \|

gocare ca prabandhādye yasya jñātastu tatra ca \|\| 63 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 64 \[unit 1433\]

anayo vyasane daive aśubhe cāpadi smṛtaḥ \|

sannayaḥ samavāye'pi pṛṣṭhasthāyibale'pi ca \|\| 64 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 65 \[unit 1434\]

praṇayaḥ premṇi viśrambhe yācñāprasarayorapi \|

valayaḥ kaṇṭharoge syādvalayaṃ kaṅkaṇe'pi ca \|\| 65 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 66 \[unit 1435\]

malayo deśa ārāme śailāṃśe parvatāntare \|

malayā trivṛtāyāṃ syādvismayo'dbhutagarvayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 67 \[unit 1436\]

pralayo mṛtyukalpāntamūrcchārtheṣu prayujyate \|

abhayaṃ syāduśīre ca pathyāyāmabhayaṃ striyām \|\| 67 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 68 \[unit 1437\]

nirbhaye vācyavat proktaṃ hṛdayaṃ mānasorasoḥ \|

sṛkkāyāmudayaḥ pūrvaparvate connatāvapi \|\| 68 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 69 \[unit 1438\]

pratyayaḥ śapathe randhre viśvāsācārahetuṣu \|

prathitatve sanādau cā'pyadhīnajñānayorapi \|\| 69 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 70 \[unit 1439\]

atikrame ca daṇḍe ca vināśe doṣakṛcchrayoḥ \|

āśayaḥ syādabhiprāye mānasādhārayorapi \|\| 70 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 71 \[unit 1440\]

nikāyo nilaye lakṣye saṃhatānāṃ samuccaye \|

ekārthabhāji nivahe paramātmani ceṣyate \|\| 71 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 72 \[unit 1441\]

kṣetriyaṃ kṣetrajatṛṇe paradārarate'pi ca \|

anyadehacikitsye cā'sādhyaroge ca jānate \|\| 72 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 73 \[unit 1442\]

kaṣāyo rasabhede syādaṅgarāge vilepane \|

niryāse'pi kaṣāyo'tha surabhau lohiteśvavat \|\| 73 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 74 \[unit 1443\]

kulāyaḥ pakṣinilayasthānayornīḍavanmataḥ \|

upāyaḥ sāmabhedādādupāyaḥ syādupāgatau \|\| 74 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 75 \[unit 1444\]

paryāyastu prakāre syānnirmāṇe'vasare krame \|

saṃstyāyaḥ sanniveśe ca saṃsthāne viśrutāvapi \|\| 75 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 76 \[unit 1445\]

vyavāyaḥ surate'ntarddhau vyavāyaṃ tejasi smṛtam \|

śolayaṃ śatapuṣpāyāmāhuḥ śālyudbhavocite \|\| 76 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 77 \[unit 1446\]

śaileyaṃ sindhulavaṇe tālaparṇyāñca sindhuje \|

cañcarīke tu śaileyī gāṅgeyo jāhnavīsute \|\| 77 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 78 \[unit 1447\]

kaśerusvarṇamusteṣu gāṅgeyamiti kathyate \|

cāmpeyaścampake svarṇe kiñjalke nāgakeśare \|\| 78 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 79 \[unit 1448\]

kāleyo daityabhede syāt kāleyaṃ kālakhaṇḍake \|

bāleyo'ṅgāravallaryyāṃ svare bālahite mṛdau \|\| 79 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 80 \[unit 1449\]

ātreyo munibhede syādātreyī saridantare \|

ātreyī puṣpavatyāñca pānīyaṃ peyavāriṇoḥ \|\| 80 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 81 \[unit 1450\]

aiṇeyameṇacarmādau ratabandhāntare striyāḥ \|

aśvīyamaśvasaṅghāte'śvīyamaśvahite'nyavat \|\| 81 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 82 \[unit 1451\]

indriyañca hṛṣīke syādindriyaṃ retasi smṛtam \|

jaghanyaṃ carame śiśne jaghanyaṃ garhite'nyavat \|\| 82 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 83 \[unit 1452\]

vadānyo dānaśauṇḍe syādvadānyaścārubhāṣiṇi \|

parjanyo meghaśabde syāddhanadambudaśakrayoḥ \|\| 83 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 84 \[unit 1453\]

brahmaṇyo brahmasādhau syādbrahmaṇyaśca śanaiścare \|

brāhmaṇyaṃ brāhmaṇatve syāt samūhe'pi dvijanmanām \|\| 84 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 85 \[unit 1454\]

śīrṣaṇyamāhurviśadakeśaśīrṣakayorapi \|

hiraṇyamakṣayadravye varāṭe svarṇaretasoḥ \|\| 85 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 86 \[unit 1455\]

saukaryyaṃ syādanāyāse kriyāyāṃ sūkarasya ca \|

śvaśuryyau devare śyāle'pyahāryyaḥ sthiraśailayoḥ \|\| 86 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 87 \[unit 1456\]

prakīryyaḥ pūtikaraje vinikīrṇe tu vācyavat \|

saurabhyamāhuḥ saugandhye cārutve guṇagaurave \|\| 87 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 88 \[unit 1457\]

nepathyaṃ raṅgabhūmau syānnepathyañca prasādhane \|

ātithyamātitheye syādātithyaścātithāvapi \|\| 88 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 89 \[unit 1458\]

sāmarthyaṃ yogyatāśaktyorapatyaṃ putrayormatam \|

kaukṛtyamanutāpe syādayuktakaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 89 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 90 \[unit 1459\]

lauhityaḥ sāgare vrīhāvādityastridaśe ravau \|

paulastyo rāvaṇe śrīde'pyaucityaṃ satyayogyayoḥ \|\| 90 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 91 \[unit 1460\]

prasavyamanukūle syāt pratikūle ca vācyavat \|

avadhyamavadhārarye syādanarthakavacasyapi \|\| 91 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 92 \[unit 1461\]

praṇāyyo'sammate prokto'pyabhilāṣavivarjjite \|

śāṇḍilyo munibhede syānmālūre pāvakāntare \|\| 92 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 93 \[unit 1462\]

maṅgalyastrāyamāṇe syādbilve'śvatthe masūrake \|

maṅgalyaṃ dadhni maṅgalyo manojñe tvabhidheyavad \|\| 93 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 94 \[unit 1463\]

maṅgalyā rocanāyāñca priyaṅguśatapuṣpayoḥ \|

adhaḥ puṣpīśaṅkhapuṣpīśamīśuklavacāsu ca \|\| 94 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 95 \[unit 1464\]

adhṛṣyastu pragalbhe syādadhṛṣyā nimnagābhidi \|

pāruṣyaṃ paruṣatve syādvane śakrasya gīṣpatau \|\| 95 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 96 \[unit 1465\]

pāruṣyo'tha bhujiṣyaḥ syāt sahāye hastasūtrake \|

svatantre ca bhujiṣyā tu dāsīgaṇikayormatā \|\| 96 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 97 \[unit 1466\]

cakṣuṣyaḥ ketake puṇḍarīkavṛkṣe rasāñjane \|

kulatthikāsubhagayoścakṣuṣyo'kṣihite'nyavata \|\| 97 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 98 \[unit 1467\]

jaṭāyurguggulutarau jaṭāyurvihagāntare \|

ūrṇāyuḥ kṣaṇabhaṅge syānmeṣakambalameṣayoḥ \|\| 98 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 99 \[unit 1468\]

devayurdhārmike khyāto devayurlokayātrike \|\| '

mṛgayurbrahmaṇi prokto gomāyuvyādhayorapi \|\| 99 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 100 \[unit 1469\]

bhuvanyurjvalane bhānau bhuvanyuḥ śaśalāñchane \|

śaraṇyurvāride vāte kṣipanyuḥ surabhau tanau \|\| 100 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 101 \[unit 1470\]

tapasyā vratacaryāyāṃ tapasyo māsi phālgune \|

rahasyā nimnagābhede gopanīye tu vācyavat \|\| 101 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 102 \[unit 1471\]

payasyā kṣīrakākolyāṃ payohitabhave'nyavat \|

payasyā dugdhikāyāñca svarṇakṣīryāñca kathyate \|\| 102 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 103 \[unit 1472\]

syādahalyā'psarobhede gautamasya ca yoṣiti \|

viśalyā lāṅgalīdantīgudūcītripuṭāsu ca \|\| 103 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 104 \[unit 1473\]

abhikhyā kīrttiyaśasorabhikhyānāmaśobhayoḥ \|

dvitīyā tithibhitpatnyoḥ pūraṇyāmapi ca dvayoḥ \|\| 104 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 105 \[unit 1474\]

nādeyī nāgaraṅge syājjayāyāṃ jalavetase \|

bhūmijambvāṃ javāyāṃ ca kāṅguṣṭhe ca samīkṣyate \|\| 105 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 106 \[unit 1475\]

bhavedanuśayo dveṣe paścāttāpānubandhayoḥ \|

smṛtaḥ samudayo vṛnde saṃyuge samupakrame \|\| 106 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 107 \[unit 1476\]

pratiśrayaḥ sabhāyāṃ syādāśraye'pi pratiśrayaḥ \|

samucchrayaḥ samutsedhe virodhe'pi samucchrayaḥ \|\| 107 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 108 \[unit 1477\]

hiraṇmayo lokadhātau syāta suvarṇamaye'nyavat \|

avaśyāyo hime garve samudāyo gaṇe yudhi \|\| 108 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 109 \[unit 1478\]

paridhāyo jalasthāne paricchadanitambayoḥ \|

samparāyaḥ samīke syādāpaduttarakālayoḥ \|\| 109 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 110 \[unit 1479\]

nirāmayo'nyavat kalye syādiḍikke nirāmayaḥ \|

samāhvayaḥ syāt saṅgrāme dyūte ca paśupakṣibhiḥ \|\| 110 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 111 \[unit 1480\]

mahālayo vihāre syāttīrthe ca paramātmani \|

rauhiṇeyo bhavedvatse revatīramaṇe budhe \|\| 111 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 112 \[unit 1481\]

pauruṣeyo vikāre syāt puruṣasya padāntare \|

puṃsaḥ samūhavadhayoḥ puruṣeṇa kṛte'pi ca \|\| 112 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 113 \[unit 1482\]

bhāgadheyaṃ smṛtaṃ bhāgye bhāgapratyayayorapi \|

bileśayo mūṣike syād bhujaṅge'pi bileśayaḥ \|\| 113 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 114 \[unit 1483\]

jalāśayamuśīre ca jalādhāre jalāśayaḥ \|

candrodayo vitāne syāttathā candrodayoṣadhau \|\| 114 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 115 \[unit 1484\]

phalodayaḥ syāt tridive lābhe'pi ca phalodayaḥ \|

mahodayaḥ kānyakubje'pyādhipatyāpavargayoḥ \|\| 115 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 116 \[unit 1485\]

sthūloccayastva'sākalye gaṇḍopalakaraṇḍayoḥ \|

gajānāṃ madhyamagatau sthūloccaya udāhṛtaḥ \|\| 116 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 117 \[unit 1486\]

dhanañjayo'rjjune vahnau kakubhe dehamārute \|

nāgāntare cā'pasavyaṃ dakṣiṇapratikūlayoḥ \|\| 117 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 118 \[unit 1487\]

mārjjārīyaḥ smṛtaḥ śūdre biḍāle kāyaśodhane \|

taṇḍulīyaḥ śākabhede viḍaṅgatarutāpyayoḥ \|\| 118 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 119 \[unit 1488\]

tṛṇaśūnyaṃ mallikāyāṃ ketakyāśca phale matam \|

mahāmūlyaṃ mahārghe syāt padmarāgamaṇāvapi \|\| 119 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 120 \[unit 1489\]

adhaḥśayyā mṛtau bhūmiśayyāyāmpitṛkānane \|

upakāryyā vastrasadmanyupakārocite'nyavat \|\| 120 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yapañcakam / 121 \[unit 1490\]

dugdhatālīyamityetad dugdhāmre dugdhapheṇake \|

bhavetpravacanīyākhyā pravācye ca pravaktari \|\| 121 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yapañcakam / 122 \[unit 1491\]

kālānusāryyaṃ kālīye śaileye śiṃśapādrume \|

vṛṣākapāyī śrīgauryyorjīvantyāñca śatāvarī \|\| 122 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yaṣaṭkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yaṣaṭkam / 123 \[unit 1492\]

pratyudgamanīyamupastheye syācca dhautāṃśukadvaye \|

viśvaksenapriyā lakṣmyāṃ trāyamāṇauṣaghāvapi \|\| 123 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / yāntavargaḥ / yaṣaṭkam / fragment 1493

iti yāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / raikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / raikakam / 1 \[unit 1494\]

raḥ pāvake ca tīkṣṇe ca rāḥ smṛtaḥ svarṇavittayoḥ \|

sruḥ srave nirjjhare cā'pi drūḥ svarṇe kāmarūpiṇi \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / raikakam / 2 \[unit 1495\]

śrīrveśaracanāśobhābhāratīsaraladrave \|

lakṣmyāṃ trivargasampattau veśopakaraṇe matā \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 3 \[unit 1496\]

araṃ śīghre ca ca cakrāṅge śīghrage punaranyavata \|

karo varṣopale pāṇau śuṇḍāpratyayaraśmiṣu \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 4 \[unit 1497\]

kharaḥ syādgardabhe devatāḍe tīkṣṇāgrake'pi ca \|

garo vyādhāvupaviṣe viṣe ca karaṇe garam \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 5 \[unit 1498\]

varo dyūtaprabhede syāccārajaṅgamayoścale \|

naraṃ tu rāmakarpūre naro'je mānave'rjjune \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 6 \[unit 1499\]

paraḥ syāduttamānātmavairidūreṣu kevale \|

paramavyayamicchanti bharo'tiśayabhārayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 7 \[unit 1500\]

varo'bhīṣṭe devatādervaro jāmātṛṣiṅgayoḥ \|

śreṣṭhe'nyavat parivṛtau varaṃ kāśmīraje matam \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 8 \[unit 1501\]

triphalāyāṃ varā proktā śatāvaryyāṃ varī varam \|

avyayantu manāgiṣṭe saro dadhyagrabāṇayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 9 \[unit 1502\]

svaro'kārādimātrāsu madhyamādiṣu ca dhvanau \|

udāttādiṣvapi proktaḥ svaro nāsāsamīraṇe \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 10 \[unit 1503\]

śaraṃ nīre śaraḥ kāṇḍe śarastejanake'pi ca \|

dharastu śaile kārpāsatūlake kamaṭhādhipe \|

dharā medhasi bhūmyāñca strīṇāṃ garbhāśaye'pi ca \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 11 \[unit 1504\]

kṣaraṃ nīre kṣaro meghe daraḥ sādhvasagarttayoḥ \|

kandare tu darīmāhurīṣarthe darāvyayam \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 12 \[unit 1505\]

kṣuraḥ syācchedanadravye kokilākṣe ca gokṣure \|

puraṃ pāṭaliputre syāgṛhoparigṛhe puram \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 13 \[unit 1506\]

puraṃ puri śarīre ca guggulau kathitaḥ puraḥ \|

purā'vyayaṃ pūrvakāle khuraḥ koladale śaphe \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 14 \[unit 1507\]

suro deve surā madye caṣake'pi surā kvacit \|

kāro vadhe niścaye ca balau yatne yatāvapi \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 15 \[unit 1508\]

kārastuṣāraśaile ca kārā dūtyāṃ prasevake \|

bandhane bandhanāgāre kemakārikayorapi \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 16 \[unit 1509\]

cāraḥ priyālavṛkṣe syādgatau bandhāpasarpayoḥ \|

pāramparataṭe prānte pārī pātrītaḍāgayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 17 \[unit 1510\]

gargarīpūrayoḥ pārī pādarajjvāñca dantinaḥ \|

vāraḥ sūryādidivase vāro'vasaravṛndayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 18 \[unit 1511\]

kubjavṛkṣe harervāraṃ dvāre madyasya bhājane \|

tāro muktādisaṃśuddhau taraṇe śuddhamauktike \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 19 \[unit 1512\]

tārañca rajate'pyuccasvare'pyanyavadīritam \|

ṛkṣākṣimadhyayostārā sugrīvaguruyoṣitoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 20 \[unit 1513\]

buddhadevyāṃ matā tārā śāraḥ śabalavātayoḥ \|

bhārastu vīvadhe svarṇapalānāmayutadvaye \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 21 \[unit 1514\]

māro mṛtyau vṛṣe'naṅge mārī caṇḍyāṃ janakṣaye \|

sphāraḥ syādvikaṭe sphāraḥ kanakādeśca budbude \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 22 \[unit 1515\]

kṣāro rasāntare dhūrtte lavaṇe kācabhasmanoḥ \|

hāro muktāralau yuddhe'pyāraḥ kṣitisute'rkaje \|

ārā carmmaprabhedinyāṃ nārastarṇakanīrayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 23 \[unit 1516\]

sāro bale majjani ca sthirāṃśe

nyāyye ca nīre ca dhane ca sāram \|

vare'nyavat sāramudāharanti

dvāraṃ punarnirgamane'pyupāye \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 24 \[unit 1517\]

pūro jalapravāhe syād vraṇasaṃśuddhikhādyayoḥ \|

cirantu gostane haste cūḍāyāṃ sīsake'pi ca \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 25 \[unit 1518\]

krūrastu kaṭhine ghore nṛśaṃse tva'bhidheyavat \|

sūraścārabhaṭe sūryye coraścaurasugandhayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 26 \[unit 1519\]

tīraṃ taṭe trapau tīraḥ sīrastigmakare hale \|

cīrī kacchūrikājhilyoḥ svairaḥ svacchandamandayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 27 \[unit 1520\]

jīraḥ khaḍge vaṇigdravye mīrastoyadaśailayoḥ \|

kṣīraṃ nīre ca dugdhe ca kīro janapade śuke \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 28 \[unit 1521\]

gauraḥ pīte'ruṇe śvete viśuddhe cā'bhidheyavat \|

gaurantu viśade padmakeśare sitasarṣape \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 29 \[unit 1522\]

gauraḥ śaśini gaurī tu nagnakanyomayoḥ smṛtā \|

rocanī rajanīpiṅgāpriyaṅguvasudhāsu ca \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 30 \[unit 1523\]

nadībhede'pi gaurī syādvaruṇasya ca yoṣiti \|

paurantu kattṛṇe pauraḥ smṛtaḥ purabhave'nyavat \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 31 \[unit 1524\]

veraṃ śarīre kāśmīre veraṃ vātiṅgaṇe'pi ca \|

ghoraṃ bhīme hare ghoraṣṭāro laṅgaturaṅgayoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 32 \[unit 1525\]

horā lagne'pi rāśyarddhe śāstrarekhābhidorapi \|

hīrā pipīlikālakṣmyorhīraḥ śaṅkaravajrayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 33 \[unit 1526\]

gotraṃ nāmni kule kṣetre kānane cittavartmanoḥ \|

sambhāvanīyabodhe'pi gotraḥ kṣoṇīdhare mataḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 34 \[unit 1527\]

gosamūhabhuvorgotrā gātramaṅge kalevare \|

stamberamāgrajaṅghāyāṃ vibhāge'pi samīritam \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 35 \[unit 1528\]

patraṃ syādvāhane parṇe pakṣe ca śarapakṣiṇām \|

pātrañca bhājane yogye pātraṃ tīradvayāntare \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 36 \[unit 1529\]

pātraṃ sruvādau parṇe'pi rājamantriṇi ceṣyate \|

potraṃ vastre mukhāgre ca śūkarasya halasya ca \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 37 \[unit 1530\]

citraṃ syādadbhutālekhyatilakeṣu vihāyasi \|

citrā''khuparṇīgodugdhīsubhadrādantikāsu ca \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 38 \[unit 1531\]

ṛkṣāpsaro'hibhedeṣu karbure cābhidheyavat \|

caitraṃ mṛtakacitye syāccaitro māsādribhedayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 39 \[unit 1532\]

vṛtro ripau ghane dhvānte śaile śakre ca dānave \|

satramācchādane yajñe sadādāne ca kaitave \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 40 \[unit 1533\]

sūtrantu sūcanāyāṃ syāt mūtraṃ tantuvyavasthayoḥ \|

mitraṃ suhṛdi mitro'rke śāstraṃ granthanideṣayoḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 41 \[unit 1534\]

mātraṃ cā'vadhṛtau kārtsnye mātrā karṇavibhūpaṇe \|

akṣarāvayave vṛtte māne'lpe ca paricchade \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 42 \[unit 1535\]

śastraṃ lohāstrayoḥ śastrī churikāyāñca viśrutā \|

astraṃ praharaṇe cāpe vaktraṃ chando'ntare mukhe \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 43 \[unit 1536\]

netraṃ manthiguṇe vastre tarumūle vilocane \|

netraṃ rathe ca nāḍyāñca netro netari vācyavat \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 44 \[unit 1537\]

kṣetraṃ śarīre kedāre siddhasthānakalatrayoḥ \|

retraṃ retasi pīyūṣe paṭavāse'pi sūtake \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 45 \[unit 1538\]

mantro vedaprabhede syāddevādīnāñca sādhane \|

guptavāde ca totrantu prājane vallave'pi ca \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 46 \[unit 1539\]

tantraṃ kuṭumbakṛtye syāt kāraṇe ca paricchade \|

śāstre pradhāne siddhānte tantuvāye gadottame \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 47 \[unit 1540\]

tattvādisādhanopāye śrutiśākhāntare'pi ca \|

itikarttavyatāyāñca tantrī vīṇāguṇe tanoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 48 \[unit 1541\]

śirāyāmamṛtāyāñca chatramātapavāraṇe \|

chatrā madhurikāyāṃ syāt kustumburuśilīndrayoḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 49 \[unit 1542\]

cakro gaṇe cakravāke cakraṃ sainyarathāṅgayoḥ \|

grāmajāle kulālasya bhāṇḍe rāṣṭāsrayorapi \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 50 \[unit 1543\]

ambhasāmapi cā'vartte cukrastvamle'mlavetase \|

cukrī cāṅgerikāyāṃ syādvṛkṣāmle cukramiṣyate \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 51 \[unit 1544\]

śukraḥ kāvye'nale jyeṣṭhe śukro reto'kṣirogayoḥ \|

śukraṃ śakro mahendre syāt kuṭajārjjunabhūruhoḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 52 \[unit 1545\]

nakraḥ kumbhīrake nakraṃ nāsāyāmagradāruṇi \|

vakraḥ syāt kuṭile krūre puṭabhede śanaiścare \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 53 \[unit 1546\]

agramālambane vrāte parimāṇe palasya ca \|

prānte purastādadhike pradhāne prathamorddhvayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 54 \[unit 1547\]

ugraḥ śūdrāsute kṣattrācchrīkaṇṭhe cotkaṭe'nyavat \|

ugrā vacāchikkikayorvyagro vyāsakta ākule \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 55 \[unit 1548\]

bhadraṃ syānmaṅgale hemni mustake karaṇāntare \|

bhadro rudre vṛṣe rāmacandre merukadambayoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 56 \[unit 1549\]

hastijātyantare bhadro vācyavacchreṣṭhasādhunoḥ \|

bhadrā mandākinīrāsnākṛtsnānantāsu kaṭphale \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 57 \[unit 1550\]

kṣudraḥ syādadhame krūre kṛpaṇe'lpe ca vācyavat \|

kṣudrā veśyānaṭīkaṇṭakārikāsaraghāsu ca \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 58 \[unit 1551\]

cāṅgerī bṛhatīhiṃsrā makṣikāmātrakeṣu ca \|\| .

kṣaudraṃ madhuni pānīye vadhraṃ trapuvaratrayoḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 59 \[unit 1552\]

gṛdhraḥ khagāntare prokto vācyavaccārthalubdhake \|

rodhraḥ sāvarake rodhramaparādhe'pi kilviṣe \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 60 \[unit 1553\]

nīdhraṃ nemau valīkendvo revatībhe ca kānane \|

rāṣṭramuddiṣṭamutpāte tathā syādupavarttane \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 61 \[unit 1554\]

kṛcchramākhyātamābhīle tapaḥsāntapanādinoḥ \|

candraḥ sudhāṃśukarpūrakāmpillasvarṇavāriṣu \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 62 \[unit 1555\]

indraḥ śacīpatāvantarātmanyādityayogayoḥ \|

indrā phaṇijjhake sāndro ghanakānanayormṛdau \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 63 \[unit 1556\]

gundrastejanake gundrā priyaṅgau bhadramustake \|

kaivarttīmustake cā'pi raudro nāṭyarasāntare \|\| 63 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 64 \[unit 1557\]

raudro'tibhīṣaṇe tīvre raudrā skandasya mātari \|

uḍro jane japāvṛkṣe uḍrā janapadāntare \|\| 64 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 65 \[unit 1558\]

pitṛkedārayorvapro vapraḥ prākārarodhasoḥ \|

puṇḍro daityaviśeṣekṣubhedayoratimuktake \|\| 65 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 66 \[unit 1559\]

citre kṛmau puṇḍarīke puṇḍrāḥ syurnīvṛdantare \|

vajraṃ hīrakadambholibālakāmalakeṣu ca \|\| 66 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 67 \[unit 1560\]

vajrā guḍūcikāyāṃ syāt tāmraṃ śulve'ruṇe'nyavat \|

tīvramatyuṣṇakaṭukanitānteṣvanyavanmatam \|\| 67 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 68 \[unit 1561\]

tīvrā ca kaṭurohiṇyāmāsurīgaṇḍadūrvayoḥ \|

asraḥ koṇe śirasije cā'sramaśruṇi śoṇite \|\| 68 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 69 \[unit 1562\]

dasraḥ khare cā'śvinayorghasro divasahiṃsrayoḥ \|

vāśro'pi divase vāśraṃ mandire ca catuṣpathe \|\| 69 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 70 \[unit 1563\]

śīghraṃ drutagatau śīghraṃ cakrāṅgośīrayorapi \|

vyāghro dīpini vikhyātaḥ śuklairaṇḍakarañjayoḥ \|

vyāghrī nidigdhikāyāñca śreṣṭhe syāduttarasthitaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 71 \[unit 1564\]

abhraṃ nabhaḥ svargabalāhakeṣu

śubhraṃ pradīpte dhavale'bhrake ca \|

babhrurviśāle nakule kṛśānau

vajre munau śūlini piṅgale ca \|\| 71 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 72 \[unit 1565\]

śigruḥ śobhāñjane śāke śaruḥ kuliśakopayoḥ \|

svarurvajre'dhvare vāṇe yūpakhaṇḍe'pi ca svaruḥ \|\| 72 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 73 \[unit 1566\]

kharurdante hare darpe haye śvete tu vācyavat \|

carurbhāṇḍe ca havyānne bharurbharttari kāñcane \|\| 73 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 74 \[unit 1567\]

gururnipekādikare pitrādau suramantriṇi \|

durjjarāladhunoḥ prokto gururmahati vācyavat \|\| 74 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 75 \[unit 1568\]

rururmṛge daityabhede kāruḥ kārakaśilpinoḥ \|

viśvakarmaṇi śilpe ca kuruḥ śrīkaṇṭhajāṅgale \|\| 75 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 76 \[unit 1569\]

undane nṛpabhede ca puruḥ prājyaparāgayoḥ \|

puruḥ svarlokanṛpayormarurbhūdharadhanvanoḥ \|\| 76 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 77 \[unit 1570\]

ārustaruviśeṣe syādāruḥ karkaṭadaṃṣṭriṇoḥ \|

kadrūrmātari nāgānāṃ kadruḥ kanakapiṅgale \|\| 77 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 78 \[unit 1571\]

harirvātārkacandrendrayamopendramarīciṣu \|

siṃhāśvakapibhekāhiśukalokāntareṣu ca \|\| 78 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 79 \[unit 1572\]

harirvācyavadākhyāto haritkapilavarṇayoḥ \|

bhūrirbrahmācyuteśeṣu bhūriprājyasuvarṇayoḥ \|\| 79 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 80 \[unit 1573\]

girirgīrṇau giriyake krīḍākandukaśailayoḥ \|

netrāmayaviśeṣe'pi matsye tu giriranyavat \|\| 80 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 81 \[unit 1574\]

vāriḥ smṛtyā sarasvatyāṃ vāri hrīveranīrayoḥ \|

vārī ghaṭībhabandhanyo raṅghriḥ syāt pādabudhnayoḥ \|\| 81 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 82 \[unit 1575\]

śāristvakṣopakaraṇe tathā śakunikāntare \|

yuddhārthagajaparyāṇe vyavahāre'pi ca kvacit \|\| 82 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 83 \[unit 1576\]

kāriḥ kriyāyāmākhyātā nāpitādikaśilpini \|

adriḥ śailadrumārkeṣu aindriḥ kākajayantayoḥ \|\| 83 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 84 \[unit 1577\]

aindrirambhodhare jiṣṇau tandrī nidrāpramīlayoḥ \|

dhātrī jananyāmalakīvasumatyupamātṛṣu \|\| 84 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 85 \[unit 1578\]

uṣṭrī golakikāyāṃ syāt karabhasya ca yoṣiti \|

kroṣṭrī śṛgālikākṣīravidārīlāṅgalīṣu ca \|\| 85 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 86 \[unit 1579\]

tarirnāvi daśāyāñca vastrādīnāñca peṭake \|

jārī syādoṣadhībhede jārastūpapatāvapi \|\| 86 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 87 \[unit 1580\]

irā vārisurābhūmibhāratīṣu mayujyate \|

sthirā bhūmau śālaparṇyāṃ sthiro niścalamokṣayoḥ \|\| 87 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 88 \[unit 1581\]

dhārā sainyāgrimaskandhasantatyoḥ pattanāntare \|

dravadravyaprapāte'pi turaṅgagatipañcake \|\| 88 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 89 \[unit 1582\]

khaḍgādīnāñca niśitamukhe dhārā'pi kīrtyate \|

dhāraḥ kvacidghanāsāravarṣaṇe syādṛṇe'pi ca \|\| 89 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 90 \[unit 1583\]

vīrā syāt kṣīrakākolitāmalakyelavāluṣu \|

patiputravatīrambhāgambhīrāmadirāsu ca \|\| 90 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 91 \[unit 1584\]

goṣṭhodumbarikākṣīravidārīdugdhikāsu ca \|

vīrastu subhaṭe śreṣṭhe vīraṃ śṛṅgyāṃ nate'pi ca \|\| 91 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 92 \[unit 1585\]

ārdrā nakṣatrabhede syādārdraṃ klinne'bhidheyavat \|

yātrā tu yāpanopāye gatau devārccanotsave \|\| 92 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 93 \[unit 1586\]

usrā gavyupacitrāyāmusrastu kiraṇe smṛtaḥ \|

hiṃsrā kākādane matsyo hiṃsraḥ syādghātake'nyavat \|\| 93 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 94 \[unit 1587\]

amarastridaśe'pyasthisaṃhāre kulijñadrume \|

dūrvā'marāvatīsthūṇā guḍūcīṣva'marā matā \|\| 94 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 95 \[unit 1588\]

aparantvadhunārthe syāt paścādgātre ca dantinām \|

arvācīne paraṃ prāhurjarāyau cāparāmapi \|\| 95 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 96 \[unit 1589\]

adharo dantavasane'nūrddhve hīne'dharo'nyavata \|

adhvaro vasubhede syāt sāvadhāne kṛtāvapi \|\| 96 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 97 \[unit 1590\]

antarantu parīdhāne bhede randhrāvakāśayoḥ \|

ātmāntarddhivinātmīyabahirmadhyāvadhiṣvapi \|\| 97 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 98 \[unit 1591\]

tādarthye'vasare coktamavaraṃ carame'nyavat \|

gajāntyajaṅghādeśe ca bhavānyāmavarā matā \|\| 98 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 99 \[unit 1592\]

uttaraṃ prativākye syādūrddhvodīcyottame'nyavat \|

uttarastu virāṭasya tanaye diśi co'ttarā \|\| 99 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 100 \[unit 1593\]

itaraḥ pāmare'nyasminnakṣaraṃ brahmavarṇayoḥ \|

ṛkṣaraṃ vāridhārāyāmṛkṣaraścartviji smṛtaḥ \|\| 100 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 101 \[unit 1594\]

pravaraṃ santatau gotre śreṣṭhe tu pravaro'nyavat \|

śabaro vāricaṇḍālabhedayoḥ śaṅkare'pi ca \|\| 101 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 102 \[unit 1595\]

ambaraṃ vāsasi vyomni kārpāse ca sugandhake \|

śambaraṃ salile cittabauddhavrataviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 102 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 103 \[unit 1596\]

śambaro daityahariṇamatsyaśailajināntare \|

oṣadhau śambarīmāhuḥ prakharo'śvatare śuni \|\| 103 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 104 \[unit 1597\]

turaṅgānāñca sannāhe prakharo'tibhṛśasvare \|

śikharaṃ śailavṛkṣāgrakakṣāpulakakoṭiṣu \|\| 104 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 105 \[unit 1598\]

pakvadāḍimabījābhamāṇikyaśakale'pi ca \|

khiṅkhirastu śivābhede khaṭvāṅge vārivālake \|\| 105 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 106 \[unit 1599\]

khikhīrā tadbahutve ca viṣṭarastu mahīruhe \|

āsane kuśamuṣṭau ca dviradaskandhapīṭhayoḥ \|\| 106 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 107 \[unit 1600\]

kavaṭe cā'tha piṭharo mustamanthānadaṇḍayoḥ \|

piṭharaḥ syādukhāyāñca jaṭharaḥ kaṭhine'nyavat \|\| 107 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 108 \[unit 1601\]

kukṣau vṛddhe ca jaṭharaṃ vaṇṭharastālapallave \|

karīrakośe'pyararaṃ kapāṭe chadane'pi ca \|\| 108 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 109 \[unit 1602\]

krakaraḥ krakace dīne karīre ca khagāntare \|

kadaraḥ śvetakhadire kṣudraroge'pi bhāṣitaḥ \|\| 109 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 110 \[unit 1603\]

badaraṃ kolakārpāsyoḥ phale syādbadarī tayoḥ \|

elāparṇyāntu badarā viṣṇukrāntauṣadhāvapi \|\| 110 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 111 \[unit 1604\]

pradaro rogabhede syāt pradaraḥ śarabhaṅgayoḥ \|

makaro yādasi jñeyo nidhirāśiprabhedayoḥ \|\| 111 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 112 \[unit 1605\]

nikaro nivahe sāre nyāyadeyadhanāntare \|

śīkaraṃ śabale vātasṛtāmbukaṇayorviduḥ \|\| 112 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 113 \[unit 1606\]

prakaraścopakāraśca vikīrṇakusumādiṣu \|

saṃhatau copakṛtyāyāṃ yathāsaṅkhyamavasthitau \|\| 113 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 114 \[unit 1607\]

joṅgake prakaraṃ proktaṃ prakarī catvarāvanau \|

vistarastu prapañce syādvistāre praṇaye'pi ca \|\| 114 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 115 \[unit 1608\]

saṃstaraprastarāvetau saṃstarādhvarayorapi \|

maṇipāṣāṇayoścā'pi yathākramamudīritau \|\| 115 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 116 \[unit 1609\]

ṭagaraṣṭaṅkaṇakṣāre helāvibhramagocare \|

vācyavat kekarākṣe tu mudgaraṃ mallikābhidi \|\| 116 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 117 \[unit 1610\]

loṣṭrādibhedane'pi syādudaraṃ jaṭhare yudhi \|

prasaraḥ praṇaye vege saṅgare cā'tha matsaraḥ \|\| 117 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 118 \[unit 1611\]

asahyaparasampattau mātsaryyaṃ kṛpaṇe yudhi \|

matsarā makṣikāyāñca visaraḥ prasare vraje \|\| 118 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 119 \[unit 1612\]

kuharaḥ koṭhare chidre nāgarājaviśeṣayoḥ \|

daharo mūṣikāyāñca svalpabhrātari bālake \|\| 119 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 120 \[unit 1613\]

camaraṃ cāmare prāhurmañjarīmṛgabhedayoḥ \|

camarī bhramare bhṛṅge kāmuke'pi madhūtthavat \|\| 120 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 121 \[unit 1614\]

rudhiro'ṅgārake prokto rudhiraṃ kuṅkumāsṛjoḥ \|

candiro'nekape candre mudiraḥ kāmuke'mbude \|\| 121 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 122 \[unit 1615\]

mihiro bhāskare vṛddhe madiraḥ kāmamūrkhayoḥ \|

vivaraṃ dūṣaṇe gartte kathitaṃ chidrarandhravat \|\| 122 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 123 \[unit 1616\]

khapuraḥ kramuke bhadre mustake'lasake'pi ca \|

gopurantu puradvāri dvāramātre ca mustake \|\| 123 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 124 \[unit 1617\]

cikuraścañcale keśe gṛhababhrubhujaṅgayoḥ \|

pakṣidrubhedayoḥ śaile caṅkuro rathavṛkṣayoḥ \|\| 124 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 125 \[unit 1618\]

aṅkuro rudhire romṇi pānīye'bhinavodbhidi \|

kukkuro vakrapucche syādgranthiparṇe'pi kukkure \|\| 125 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 126 \[unit 1619\]

mukuro mallikāpuṣpe darpaṇe ca kalidrume \|

kulāladaṇḍe vakule makuro'pyeṣu viśrutaḥ \|\| 126 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 127 \[unit 1620\]

ajiraṃ prāṅgaṇe vāte viṣaye dardure tanau \|

aśite rākṣase vahnāvaśirastapane'pi ca \|\| 127 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 128 \[unit 1621\]

śiśiraḥ syādṛtorbhede tuṣāre śītale'nyavat \|

vaśiraṃ kiṇihīsindhulavaṇe kumbhakeṣu ca \|\| 128 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 129 \[unit 1622\]

suṣiraṃ vivare vādye sarandhre suṣiro'nyavat \|

suṣiro'gnau ca timiraṃ dhvānte netrāmaye'pi ca \|\| 129 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 130 \[unit 1623\]

chidiraḥ pāvake rajjau karavāle paraśvadhe \|

danturaṃ vācyavadvidyādviṣamonnatadantayoḥ \|\| 130 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 131 \[unit 1624\]

viduro nāgare vīre kauravāṇāñca mantriṇi \|

vidhuraṃ syāt praviśleṣe vidhuro vikale'nyavat \|\| 131 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 132 \[unit 1625\]

vidhurā'pi rasālāyāṃ madhurastu rase viṣe \|

madhuraṃ rasavat svādupriyeṣu madhuro'nyavat \|\| 132 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 133 \[unit 1626\]

madhurā śatapuṣpāyāṃ miśreyānagarībhidoḥ \|

madhukarkaṭikābhedāmadhūlīyaṣṭikāsu ca \|\| 133 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 134 \[unit 1627\]

bandhūrabandhurau ramye namre haṃse tu bandhuraḥ \|

bandhuke ca viduṅge ca bandhūrāpaṇyayoṣiti \|\| 134 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 135 \[unit 1628\]

barbaraḥ pāmare keśavinyāse nīvṛdantare \|

barbarā phañjikāyāntu barbarāśākapuṣpayoḥ \|\| 135 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 136 \[unit 1629\]

karburaṃ salile hemni karburaṃ pāparakṣasoḥ \|

karburā puṣpavṛntāyāṃ kirmīre karburā'nyavat \|\| 136 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 137 \[unit 1630\]

karparaḥ syāt kapāle ca śastrabhedakaṭāhayoḥ \|

kūrparo jānuni proktaḥ kaphoṇāvapi kūrparaḥ \|\| 137 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 138 \[unit 1631\]

kharparaḥ taskare pūrve bhikṣāpātrakapālayoḥ \|

gargaro mīnabhede syānmanthanyāmapi gargarī \|\| 138 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 139 \[unit 1632\]

ghargharastu caladvāridhvānolūkanadāntare \|

nirjharastu sahasrāṃśu turaṅge tuṣapāvake \|\| 139 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 140 \[unit 1633\]

murmmurastuṣavahnau syānmanmathe ravivājini \|

jarjaro vācyavajjīrṇe jarjaraṃ vāsavadhvaje \|\| 140 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 141 \[unit 1634\]

nirjjarastu jarātyakte vācyavannirjjaraḥ sure \|

nirjarā tu guḍūcyāṃ syāttālapatryāmapi kvacit \|\| 141 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 142 \[unit 1635\]

jarjharaḥ syāt kaliyuge vādye bhāṇḍe nadāntare \|

dardurastu girāvīṣadbhagnavastuni vācyavat \|\| 142 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 143 \[unit 1636\]

dardurastoyade bheke vādyabhāṇḍādribhedayoḥ \|

dardurā caṇḍikāyāñca grāmajāle tu darduram \|\| 143 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 144 \[unit 1637\]

vārddaraṃ dakṣiṇāvarttaśṛṅge kṛmijanīrayoḥ \|

kākacintyāśvabīje'pi vāci vārddaramiṣyate \|\| 144 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 145 \[unit 1638\]

durdharaṃ vācyavad duḥkhadhāryye syādvṛṣabhauṣadhe \|

durdharo nirbhare sāre nirbhaye nirapatrape \|\| 145 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 146 \[unit 1639\]

kuñjaro'nekape keśe ghātakyāmapi kuñjarā \|

piñjaraṃ kanake vājibhede pīte ca piñjaraḥ \|\| 146 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 147 \[unit 1640\]

mandaro manthare śaile svargamandārayorapi \|

mandaro bahale mande kandaro vivare kuśe \|\| 147 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 148 \[unit 1641\]

mandiraṃ nagare'gāre mandiro makarālaye \|

varkarastaruṇe meṣe karkkaro darpaṇe dṛḍhe \|\| 148 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 149 \[unit 1642\]

kandharā vārivāhe syādgrīvāyāmapi kandharā \|

vallaraṃ śādvale proktaṃ nirjalasthānakuñjayoḥ \|\| 149 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 150 \[unit 1643\]

vallakṣetre ca mañjaryyāṃ vallarī tu prakīrttitā \|

mantharaḥ sūcake koṭhe manthāne cā'tha mantharam \|\| 150 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 151 \[unit 1644\]

kusumbhyāṃ mantharo mande pṛthau vakre ca vācyavat \|

kaccaraṃ kutsite takre catvaraṃ sthaṇḍile'ṅgane \|\| 151 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 152 \[unit 1645\]

chitvaraṃ chedanadravye chitvaro dhūrttavairiṇoḥ \|

itvaro durvidhe nīce pathikakūrakarmpaṇoḥ \|\| 152 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 153 \[unit 1646\]

puṣkaraṃ paṅkaje vyomni payaḥkarikarāgrayoḥ \|

auṣadhadvīpavihagatīrtharājoragāntare \|\| 153 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 154 \[unit 1647\]

puṣkaraṃ tūryavaktre ca kāṇḍe khaḍgaphale'pi ca \|

ḍiṅgaro ḍaṅgare kṣepe saṅgaro'ṅgīkṛtau yudhi \|\| 154 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 155 \[unit 1648\]

viṣāpadoḥ kriyākāre saṅgarantu śamīphale \|

gahvarastu gṛhādambhanikuñjagahaneṣvapi \|\| 155 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 156 \[unit 1649\]

karvaraḥ kathito vyāghre śivāyāmapi karvarī \|

caturaścāturakavaccakragaṇḍau niyantari \|\| 156 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 157 \[unit 1650\]

etau syātāmubhau netragocare cāṭukāriṇi \|

īśvaro vibhavairāḍhye śambhau svāmini manmathe \|\| 157 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 158 \[unit 1651\]

pārvatyāmīśvarāmāhurdvāparaḥ saṃśaye yuge \|

ākaro nivahotpattisthānaśreṣṭheṣu kathyate \|\| 158 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 159 \[unit 1652\]

bhāskarastapane vahnau prabhākarasamaḥ smṛtaḥ \|

pārparo bhaktasikthe syāt kīnāśe rājayakṣmaṇi \|\| 159 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 160 \[unit 1653\]

jarāṭe'pi kadambasya keśare'pi ca bhasmani \|

śārvarantvandhatamase śārvarī dhātuke'nyavat \|\| 160 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 161 \[unit 1654\]

prāntaraṃ vipine dūraśūnyavartmani koṭare \|

śārkaro dugdhapheṇe'pi śarkarānvitadeśavat \|\| 161 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 162 \[unit 1655\]

śārkaro vṛṣabhe chandoviśeṣe śārkaraṃ matam \|

nāgaraṃ mustake śuṇṭhyāṃ vidagdhe nāgaro'nyavat \|\| 162 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 163 \[unit 1656\]

śaṃsanti nagarodbhūte ratabandhe ca nāgaram \|

vāgaro vārake śāṇe nirjhare vāḍave vṛke \|\| 163 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 164 \[unit 1657\]

viśārade mumukṣau ca gavāmaṅke ca vāgaram \|

cāmaraṃ grāvamadhunoḥ pāmaraḥ svasthanīcayoḥ \|

vāsaro divase rāgaprabhede'pi ca vāsaraḥ \|\| 164 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 165 \[unit 1658\]

kāntāramupasargādau vane durgamavartmani \|

ikṣuprabhede kāntāraḥ pādāraḥ pādadhūliṣu \|\| 165 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 166 \[unit 1659\]

pādaliṅge ca mandāraḥ pāribhadrārkaparṇayoḥ \|

suradrume'pi gāndhāraḥ sindūre rāgadeśayoḥ \|\| 166 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 167 \[unit 1660\]

ādhāraścā'dhikaraṇe'pyālavāle'mbudhāraṇe \|

āsāraḥ syāt prasaraṇe vegavarṣe suhṛdbale \|\| 167 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 168 \[unit 1661\]

kedāraḥ parvate śambhau kṣetrabhedālavālayoḥ \|

udāro dātṛmahatordakṣiṇe'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 168 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 169 \[unit 1662\]

madāro dvirade dhūrtte vidāro dāraṇe raṇe \|

vidārī śālaparṇyāñca rogabhede bhagandare \|\| 169 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 170 \[unit 1663\]

vikāro vikṛtau roge'pyākāraḥ saṃsthitāṅgayoḥ \|

nikāraḥ syāt paribhave dhānyasyotkiraṇe'pi ca \|\| 170 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 171 \[unit 1664\]

prakāraḥ sadṛśe bhede ṭaṅkāraḥ ṣiñjinīdhvanau \|

vismaye ca prasiddhau ca daṇḍāraḥ śarayantrake \|\| 171 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 172 \[unit 1665\]

kulālacakre vahane daṇḍāro mattavāraṇe \|

piṇḍāraḥ kṣapaṇakṣepe mahiṣīrakṣake drume \|\| 172 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 173 \[unit 1666\]

tuṣāro himabhede ca makare'pi hime'pi ca \|

śunārastu śunīstanye sarpāṇḍakalaviṅkayoḥ \|\| 173 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 174 \[unit 1667\]

kumāro bālake skande yuvarāje'śvacārake \|

śuke ca varuṇadrau ca kumāraṃ jātyakāñcane \|\| 174 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 175 \[unit 1668\]

kumārī rāmataraṇīnavamālyornadībhidi \|

kanyā'parājitāgaurījambūdvīpeṣu ca smṛtā \|\| 175 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 176 \[unit 1669\]

āhāro bhojane hāre bhavedāharaṇe'pi ca \|

vihāro bhramaṇe skandhe līlāyāṃ sugatālaye \|\| 176 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 177 \[unit 1670\]

kuṭāraṃ maithune vidyāt kuṭāraṃ kevale'pi ca \|

koṭṭāro nāgare kūpe puṣkariṇyāñca pāṭake \|\| 177 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 178 \[unit 1671\]

kaḍāraḥ piṅgale dāse sambhāraḥ sambhṛtau gaṇe \|

kenāraḥ kumbhinarake śiraḥkapālasandhiṣu \|\| 178 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 179 \[unit 1672\]

śaṅkaro'gnicaṭatkāre sammārjjanyavapuñjite \|

naradūṣitakanyāyāṃ śaṅkarā kvā'pi dṛśyate \|\| 179 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 180 \[unit 1673\]

paṅkāraḥ śaivale setau sopāne jalakubjake \|

saṃskāraḥ pratiyatne syāt saṅkalpe'nubhave'pi ca \|\| 180 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 181 \[unit 1674\]

śālāraṃ syāddhastinakhe sopāne pakṣipañjare \|

kañjāro jaṭhare sūrye viriñcau vāraṇe munau \|\| 181 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 182 \[unit 1675\]

mārjāra otau dhaṭvāṃśe bhṛṅgāraḥ kanakāluke \|

bhṛṅgārī jhillikāyāṃ syādaṅgāro'lātabhaumayoḥ \|\| 182 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 183 \[unit 1676\]

śṛṅgāraḥ surate nāṭyarase'pi gajamaṇḍane \|

śṛṅgāraṃ cūrṇasindūralavaṅgakusumeṣu ca \|\| 183 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 184 \[unit 1677\]

vistāro vistṛtau stamba uddhāraścoddhṛtāvṛṇe \|

māṭharo dvijabhede syādvyāse'rkapāripārśvake \|\| 184 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 185 \[unit 1678\]

śāvaraḥ paṭhyate rodhre tathā pāpāparādhayoḥ \|

śāvarī śūkaśimbyāñca śārīraṃ dehaje vṛṣe \|\| 185 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 186 \[unit 1679\]

karīraḥ kalatre vṛkṣabhede vaṃśāṅkure'pi ca \|

karīrī cīrikāyāñca dantamūle ca dantinām \|\| 186 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 187 \[unit 1680\]

śaṇīraṃ śoṇamadhyasthapuline darddarītaṭe \|

jambīraṃ prasthapuṣpākhyaśāke dantaśaṭhe'pi \|\| 187 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 188 \[unit 1681\]

auśīramāhuścamaradaṇḍe ca śayanāsane \|

sauvīraṃ badare stotrāñjane deśe ca kāñjike \|\| 188 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 189 \[unit 1682\]

kirmīraḥ karbure daityarākṣasāntarayorapi \|

kāśmīraṃ kuṅkume proktaṃ ṭaṅkapuṣkaramūlayoḥ \|\| 189 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 190 \[unit 1683\]

pāṭīro vārtike raṅge mūlake titaunyapi \|

dhārādhare'pi kedāre veṇusāre'pi ceṣyate \|\| 190 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 191 \[unit 1684\]

aṇḍīraḥ puruṣe śakte hiṇḍīraḥ puruṣe smṛtaḥ \|

pheṇe vātiṅgaṇe cāpi dhūsaraḥ pāṇḍure khare \|\| 191 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 192 \[unit 1685\]

kūbaraḥ kubjake cārau kūbaraśca yugandhare \|

rūbaro'śmaśrupuruṣe prauḍhāśṛṅgavṛṣe'pi ca \|\| 192 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 193 \[unit 1686\]

syānnīvaro vāṇijake vāstavye'pi ca nīvaraḥ \|

pīvaraḥ kacchape sthūle dhīvaro lubdhake budhe \|\| 193 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 194 \[unit 1687\]

keśaraṃ hiṅguni proktaṃ keśaro nāgakeśare \|

siṃhacchaṭāyāṃ punnāgakiñjalkavakuleṣvapi \|\| 194 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 195 \[unit 1688\]

kiśorastaruṇāvasthe hayaśāvakasūryayoḥ \|

tailāyanyāṃ kuberastu nandīvṛkṣe dhanādhipe \|\| 195 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 196 \[unit 1689\]

kaṇeruḥ syādvaśāveśyākarṇikāre gaṇeruvat \|

kaiṭīruḥ śakragope ca śakre ca nakule'pi ca \|\| 196 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 197 \[unit 1690\]

aguruḥ syācchiṃśapāyāṃ joṅgake'pi laghunyapi \|

kiṃśāruḥ sasyaśūke'pi viśikhe kaṅkapakṣiṇi \|\| 197 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 198 \[unit 1691\]

bhāryāruḥ śailabhede syānmṛgabhede ca tatra ca \|

krīḍāyāṃ parabhāryāyāṃ putro yenopapāditaḥ \|\| 198 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 199 \[unit 1692\]

kuṭhāruḥ pādape kīśe kaṭaprūḥ syānmahānaṭe \|

vidyādhare rakṣasi ca kaṭaprūrakṣadevane \|\| 199 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 200 \[unit 1693\]

dāsero dāsikāputre dāseraḥ syāt kramelake \|

mayūro barhicūḍāyāmapāmārge śikhaṇḍini \|\| 200 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 201 \[unit 1694\]

kharjjūraṃ rajate vidyāvṛścikadrumabhedayoḥ \|

kharjjūraścā'tha karccūraṃ śaṭīkāñcanayormatam \|\| 201 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 202 \[unit 1695\]

sindūrastarubhede syāt sindūraṃ raktacūrṇake \|

sindūrī rocanīraktacelikādhātakīṣu ca \|\| 202 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 203 \[unit 1696\]

vallūraṃ syādvanakṣetre vāhanoṣarayorapi \|

vallūramāhuḥ saṃśuṣkamāṃsaśūkaramāṃsayoḥ \|\| 203 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 204 \[unit 1697\]

ekāgramekatāne syādekāgrañcāpya'nākule \|

narendro vārttike rājñi viṣavaidye'pi kathyate \|\| 204 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 205 \[unit 1698\]

mahendraḥ parvate śakre jalendro varuṇe'mbudhau \|

jambhale'pi jalendraḥ syāt sāmudraṃ dehalakṣaṇe \|\| 205 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 206 \[unit 1699\]

sāmudrañca samudrīyalavaṇādiṣu bhedyavat \|

khaṇḍābhramabhraleśe syāt strīṇāṃ dantakṣatāntare \|\| 206 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 207 \[unit 1700\]

kaṭitraṃ rasanāyāṃ syāccarmāṅge kaṭivāsasi \|

śilīndhraṃ kadalīpuṣpe karakatripuṭākhyayoḥ \|\| 207 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 208 \[unit 1701\]

śilīndhraḥ kathito dhīraistaṅgamīnaprabhedayoḥ \|

mṛdā gaṇḍūpadī proktā śilīndhrī vihagībhidi \|\| 208 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 209 \[unit 1702\]

tamisraṃ timire kope tamisrā ca tamastatau \|

kṛṣṇapakṣaniśāyāñca tvakpatrantu varāṅgake \|\| 209 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 210 \[unit 1703\]

tvakpatrīti ca kāravyāṃ kalatraṃ śroṇibhāryayoḥ \|

durgasthāne nṛpādīnāṃ pavitraṃ varṣaṇe kuśe \|\| 210 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 211 \[unit 1704\]

pavitraṃ tāmrapayasormedhye cā'pi tadanyavat \|

sāvitraḥ śaṅkare proktaḥ sāvitrī devatāntare \|\| 211 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 212 \[unit 1705\]

daityāristridaśe viṣṇau tittirirvihage munau \|

śāṅkarirvighnahe skande jaimuriḥ pāvake pavau \|\| 212 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 213 \[unit 1706\]

mandurā vājiśālāyāṃ samīpārthe ca vastuni \|

urvarā sarvasasyāḍhyabhūmau syād mūmimātrake \|\| 213 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 214 \[unit 1707\]

asurā rajanīvāsyorasuro dānave ravau \|

vāsurā vāsitāyāṃ syādvāsiteyyāṃ matā bhuvi \|\| 214 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 215 \[unit 1708\]

masūrā masurā vrīhiprabhede paṇyayoṣiti \|

masūramasurau ca dvāvetāvapyetayormatau \|\| 215 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 216 \[unit 1709\]

śarkarā khaṇḍavikṛtāvupalākarparāṃśayoḥ \|

śarkarānvitadeśe ca rugbhede sakale'pi ca \|\| 216 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 217 \[unit 1710\]

kacchurā śūkaśimbyāñca śaṭīduḥsparśayorapi \|

kacchuraṃ vācyavat prāhuḥ pāmare puṃścale'pi ca \|\| 217 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 218 \[unit 1711\]

marmarā tu darībhāṇḍamurarānikulāsu ca \|

gāyatrī kathitā chandaḥprabhede khadire'pi ca \|\| 218 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 219 \[unit 1712\]

sairindhrī paraveśmasthaśilpakṛtsvavaśastriyām \|

varṇasaṅkarasambhūtastrīmahallikayorapi \|\| 219 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 220 \[unit 1713\]

khadirī śākabhede syāt khadiro dantadhāvane \|

sundarī strītarubhidoḥ śarvarī rātriyoṣitoḥ \|\| 220 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 221 \[unit 1714\]

haridrāyāñca kavarī keśaśākaviśeṣayoḥ \|

kavaraṃ lavaṇe'mle ca tumbarī śaramarddayoḥ \|\| 221 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 222 \[unit 1715\]

mañjarī vallarīsthūlamuktātilakaśākhiṣu \|

marmmarī pītadārau syādvastrabhede ca marmaraḥ \|\| 222 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 223 \[unit 1716\]

ṭaṭṭarī syānmṛṣāvāde lampāpaṭahavādyayoḥ \|

śakvarā mekhalānadyośchandobhede'pi śakvarī \|\| 223 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 224 \[unit 1717\]

sallarī sillarī ca dve huḍuke cāracakrake \|

kāverī syāt saridbhede paṇyanārīharidrayoḥ \|\| 224 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 225 \[unit 1718\]

bhavet parikaro vrāte paryaṅkaparivārayoḥ \|

pragāḍhagātrikābandhe vivekārambhayorapi \|\| 225 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 226 \[unit 1719\]

vyatikaraḥ samākhyāto vyasanavyatiṣaṅgayoḥ \|

vibhākaro'gnau tapane'vaskaro gūthaguhyayoḥ \|\| 226 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 227 \[unit 1720\]

karmmakarastu kīnāśe bhṛtye vetanajīvini \|

karmakarī tu mūrvāyāṃ bimbikāyamapi kvacit \|\| 227 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 228 \[unit 1721\]

aruṣkāro vraṇakṛti bhallāte cāpyaruṣkaram \|

bhavet sahacaro jhiṇṭyāṃ vayasyaṃ pratibimbake \|\| 228 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 229 \[unit 1722\]

niśācaraḥ pecakāhiyātudhāneṣu pherave \|

niśācarī paṃsulāyāṃ bhavet pakṣacaro vidhau \|\| 229 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 230 \[unit 1723\]

yūthabhraṣṭapṛthakcārī vāraṇe'pi prakīrttitaḥ \|

carācaraṃ syājjagati vācyavajjaṅgameṅgayoḥ \|\| 230 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 231 \[unit 1724\]

vidurdaśapuraṃ deśe mustāyāṃ pattane'pi ca \|

abhimaro vadhe yuddhe tathā svabalasādhvase \|\| 231 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 232 \[unit 1725\]

āhuḥ pratisaraṃ hastasūtre mālye'pi maṇḍane \|

vraṇaśuddhau camūpṛṣṭe niyojyārakṣakaṅkaṇe \|\| 232 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 233 \[unit 1726\]

mantrabhede'pyavasaro varṣaprastāvayorapi \|

smṛtaḥ parisaro mṛtyau daivopāntyapradeśayoḥ \|\| 233 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 234 \[unit 1727\]

digambaraḥ syāt kṣapaṇe nagne tapasi śaṅkare \|

pītāmbaraḥ padmanābhe bhavet pītāmbaro naṭe \|\| 234 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 235 \[unit 1728\]

kādambarantu dadhyagre madyabhede ca śīdhuni \|

kādambarī parabhṛtābhāratīśārikāsu ca \|\| 235 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 236 \[unit 1729\]

udumbarastu dehalyāṃ vṛkṣajātau ca paṇḍake \|

udumbaraṃ smṛtaṃ tāmre kuṣṭabhede'pyudumbaraḥ \|\| 236 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 237 \[unit 1730\]

audumbaraḥ śrāddhadeve rogabhede ca kathyate \|

nīlāmbaraḥ pralambaghne kauṇape krūralocane \|\| 237 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 238 \[unit 1731\]

āḍambaraḥ samārambhe gajagajjitatūryayoḥ \|

niṣadvarastu jambāle niśāyāñca niṣadvarī \|\| 238 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 239 \[unit 1732\]

upahvaraṃ samīpe syādekānte cā'pyupahvaram \|

indīvaraṃ kuvalaye varyyāmindīvarī matā \|\| 239 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 240 \[unit 1733\]

kārañjaro yogicakre melake bhairave girau \|

kārañjaro'cyute śakre bhavedviśvambharā bhuvi \|\| 240 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 241 \[unit 1734\]

durodase dyūtakare paṇe dyūte durodaram \|

lambodaraḥ syāduddhmāte gaṇānāmadhipe'pi ca \|\| 241 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 242 \[unit 1735\]

anuttaraḥ smṛtaḥ śreṣṭhe pratijalpavivarjjite \|

tulādharastulārāśau bhavedvāṇijake'pi ca \|\| 242 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 243 \[unit 1736\]

bhavettoyadharo meghe suniṣaṇṇauṣadhe'pi ca \|

dhurandharo dhavatarau dhūrvahe'pi ghurandharaḥ \|\| 243 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 244 \[unit 1737\]

cakragharo harau sarpe vācyavadgrāmajālini \|

aśvataro vesare ca nāgarājāntare'pi ca \|\| 244 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 245 \[unit 1738\]

vidurvīrataraṃ śreṣṭhe śaravīraṇayorapi \|

nabhaścaro ghane vāte vidyāgharavihaṅgayoḥ \|\| 245 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 246 \[unit 1739\]

uddanturaḥ syāduttuṅge karālotkaṭadantayoḥ \|

dvaimāturo jarāsandhavāraṇānanayormataḥ \|\| 246 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 247 \[unit 1740\]

paramparaṃ prapautrādau mṛgabhede paramparaḥ \|

paramparā parīpāṭyāṃ santāne'pi vadhe kvacit \|\| 247 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 248 \[unit 1741\]

dharādharaḥ kṣmābhṛti cakrapāṇau

dhārādharastoyadhare kṛpāṇe \|

payodharaḥ syāt kucakoṣakāra-

kaserukāmbhodharanārikele \|\| 248 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 249 \[unit 1742\]

kalikāraḥ karañje syād dhūmyāṭe pītamastake \|

alaṅkārastūpamādau hārakeyūrakādiṣu \|\| 249 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 250 \[unit 1743\]

karṇikāraśca sampāke karṇikāro drumotpale \|

rathakārastu māhiṣye karaṇīje ca takṣaṇi \|\| 250 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 251 \[unit 1744\]

carmakāraḥ smṛtaḥ pādukṛti carmmakaṣauṣadhe \|

carmakārī ca kathitā pratikaraḥ same bhaṭe \|\| 251 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 252 \[unit 1745\]

ghanasārastu karpūre dakṣiṇāvarttapārade \|

kṛṣṇasāraḥ snuhīvṛkṣe śiṃśapāmṛgabhedayoḥ \|\| 252 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 253 \[unit 1746\]

girisāro bhavellohe liṅge malayaparvate \|

pītasāro malayaje gomedakamaṇāvapi \|\| 253 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 254 \[unit 1747\]

śālasāro drume hiṅgau sūtradhāraḥ śacīpatau \|

nāṭyānantarasañcāripātraśilpiprabhedayoḥ \|\| 254 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 255 \[unit 1748\]

daṇḍadhāro yame bhūpe samprahāro raṇe gatau \|

pratīhārapratīhāryau dvāḥsthe dvāre'pyanantare \|\| 255 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 256 \[unit 1749\]

abhihāro'bhiyoge ca caurye sannahane'pi ca \|

vyavahāro drubhede syādvyavahāraḥ paṇe sthitau \|\| 256 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 257 \[unit 1750\]

avahāraḥ smṛtaścaure dyūtayuddhādiviśrame \|

nimantraṇopanetavyadravye grāhākhyayādasi \|\| 257 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 258 \[unit 1751\]

parivāraḥ parijane khaḍgakoṣe paricchade \|

pārāvāraḥ samudre ca pārāvāraṃ taṭadvaye \|\| 258 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 259 \[unit 1752\]

avatāro'vataraṇapuṣkariṇyāditīrthayoḥ \|

akūpāraḥ samudre'pi kūrmarāje'pi kīrttitaḥ \|\| 259 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 260 \[unit 1753\]

śiśumāro'mbusambhūtajantau tārātmakācyute \|

sukumāraśca puṇḍrekṣau komale tva'bhidheyavat \|\| 260 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 261 \[unit 1754\]

dhundhumāraḥ śakragope gṛhadhūme padālike \|

karṇapūro'vataṃse syānnīlotpalaśirīṣayoḥ \|\| 261 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 262 \[unit 1755\]

karavīraḥ kṛpāṇe syāddaityabhedāśvamārayoḥ \|

śreṣṭhagavyāṃ putravatyāmaditau karavīrya'pi \|\| 262 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 263 \[unit 1756\]

mahāvīraḥ pavau tārśve śūre siṃhe'pi kokile \|

makhānale ca karke ca mahāvīro jarāṭake \|\| 263 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 264 \[unit 1757\]

ātmavīraḥ prāṇavati śyālaputre vidūṣake \|

cārvaṭīrastrapāvāmrāsthnyaṅkure gaṇikāsute \|\| 264 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 265 \[unit 1758\]

citraṭīro vidhau bhālamaṅkitaṃ yena tat raca \|

ghaṇṭākarṇopahārāya hatacchāgāsrabindubhiḥ \|\| 265 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 266 \[unit 1759\]

pātraṭīro budhairukto muktavyāpāramantriṇi \|

lohakāṃsye jaratpātre pramāṇe pāvake'pi ca \|\| 266 \|\|
