## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 267 \[unit 1760\]

dhārāṅkuraḥ sunāsīre śīkare'pi ghanopale \|

samudrāruḥ smṛto grāhe setubandhe timiṅgile \|\| 267 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 268 \[unit 1761\]

viśvakadruḥ khale dhvāne syādākheṭikakukkure \|

asipatro bhavet koṣakāre ca narakāntare \|\| 268 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 269 \[unit 1762\]

śatapatro rājakīre dārvāghāṭamayūrayoḥ \|

rājīve śatapatrantu vātaputro hanūmati \|\| 269 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 270 \[unit 1763\]

mahādhūrtte ca bhīme ca lakṣmīputraḥ smare haye \|

pūrṇapātraṃ vastupūrṇapātre varddhāpake'pi ca \|\| 270 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 271 \[unit 1764\]

mahāmātraḥ samṛddhe'gramātye hastipakādhipe \|

bindutantraṃ viduḥ śāriphalake caturaṅgake \|\| 271 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 272 \[unit 1765\]

rāgasūtraṃ tulāsūtre paṭṭasūtre'pi dṛśyate \|

tālapatrantu tāṭaṅke raṇḍāyāṃ tālapatryapi \|\| 272 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 273 \[unit 1766\]

agnihotro'gnihaviṣorvītihotro'nalārkkayoḥ \|

vīrabhadro'śvamedhāśve vīraśreṣṭhe ca vīraṇe \|\| 273 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 274 \[unit 1767\]

pāribhadrastu mandāranimbapādapayormataḥ \|

balabhadro rauhiṇeye trāyamāṇauṣadhāvapi \|\| 274 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 275 \[unit 1768\]

balabhadrā kumāryāṃ syādarddhacandrastu candrake \|

galahaste bāṇabhede'pyarddhacandrā trivṛdbhidi \|\| 275 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 276 \[unit 1769\]

dhārttarāṣṭro'sitāsyāṅghrihaṃsakauravabhogiṣu \|

dhṛtarāṣṭraḥ surājñi syāt pakṣikṣatriyabhedayoḥ \|\| 276 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 277 \[unit 1770\]

dhṛtarāṣṭrī haṃsapantyāṃ sthiradaṃṣṭro bhujaṅgame \|

varāhākṛtiviṣṇau ca vaktranakraḥ śuke khale \|\| 277 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 278 \[unit 1771\]

kaṭambharā prasāraṇyāṃ rohiṇyāṃ gajayopaṣiti \|

kalambikāyāṃ golāyāṃ varṣābhūmūrvayorapi \|\| 278 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 279 \[unit 1772\]

maṇicchidrā tu medāyāmṛṣabhākhyauṣadhe'pi ca \|

tuṅgabhadrā nadībhede tuṅgabhadro madotkaṭe \|\| 279 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 280 \[unit 1773\]

daṇḍayātrā digvijaye sandānavarayātrayoḥ \|

dehayātrā bhojane syāt kṛtāntāgamane'pi ca \|\| 280 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 281 \[unit 1774\]

vibhāvarī haridrāyāṃ rajanyāñca vibhāvarī \|

vivāde vastrakuṭyāñca kuṭṭanyāṃ cakrayoṣiti \|\| 281 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 282 \[unit 1775\]

kumbhakārī kulālyāṃ syāt kumbhakāraḥ kulālake \|

tuṇḍikerī tu kārpāsyāṃ bimbikāyāmapīṣyate \|\| 282 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 283 \[unit 1776\]

pāṃśusañcāra ityeṣa dūrvāñcitataṭībhuvi \|

varddhāpake praśaṃsāyāṃ puroṭau dhūligucchake \|\| 283 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 284 \[unit 1777\]

syāt pādacatvaraśchāge saikate pippale'pi ca \|

vipratīsāramicchanti kaukṛtye'nuśaye ruṣi \|\| 284 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 285 \[unit 1778\]

samabhihāro bhṛśatve paunaḥpunye'pi kathyate \|

karake paradoṣaikabhāji pūruṣake'pi ca \|\| 285 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 286 \[unit 1779\]

gītakāveramicchanti pittale kuṅkume'pi ca \|

tālīśapatraṃ tālīśe tāmalakyāmapi smṛtam \|\| 286 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 287 \[unit 1780\]

bhavedutpalapatrantu taddale strīnakhakṣate \|

tamālapatraṃ tāpiñje tilake patrake'pi ca \|\| 287 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 288 \[unit 1781\]

sarvatobhadra ityuktaḥ kāvyacitre gṛhāntare \|

nimbe'pi sarvatobhadrā gambhārīnaṭayoṣitoḥ \|\| 288 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 289 \[unit 1782\]

vasvaukasārā śrīdasya śakrasya nalinīpuroḥ \|

bhavet kapiladhārākhyā svarnadītīrthabhedayoḥ \|\| 289 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / fragment 1783

iti rāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 1 \[unit 1784\]

balaṃ gandharase rūpe sthāmani sthaulpasainyayoḥ \|

balo halāyudhe daityabhede balini vāyase \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 2 \[unit 1785\]

vāṭyālake balā tūktā palañconmānamāṃsayoḥ \|

phalaṃ hetusamutthe syāt phalake vyuṣṭilābhayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 3 \[unit 1786\]

jātīphale'pi kakkole sasyavāṇāgrayorapi \|

phalinyāntu phalaṃ prāhustriphalāyāṃ phalaṃ kvacit \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 4 \[unit 1787\]

jalaṃ gokavale nīre hrīvere ca jaḍe'nyavat \|

khalaṃ bhūsthānakalkeṣu krūre nīce'dhame khalaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 5 \[unit 1788\]

galaḥ sarjjarase kaṇṭhe calaḥ kampe calāśraye \|

dalaṃ śastrī chadotsedhataddravye khaṇḍaparṇayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 6 \[unit 1789\]

nalaḥ poṭagale rājñi pitṛdeve kapīśvare \|

nalī manaḥśilāyāntu maline tu nalaṃ matam \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 7 \[unit 1790\]

talaṃ svarūpādharayoḥ khaḍgamuṣṭicapeṭayoḥ \|

jyāghātavāraṇe'pi syāttalastālatarāvapi \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 8 \[unit 1791\]

malaṃ kiṭṭe purīṣe ca pāpe ca kṛpaṇe malaḥ \|

śalantu śallakīlomni śalo bhṛṅge gaṇe vidhau \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 9 \[unit 1792\]

khilaṃ bhavedaprahate sārasaṃkṣiptavedhasoḥ \|

bilaṃ chidre guhāyāñca bila uccaiḥśravohaye \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 10 \[unit 1793\]

balantu skhalite vyāje pulantu puline'nyavat \|

pulaḥ syāt pulake cā'pi kulaṃ janapade gṛhe \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 11 \[unit 1794\]

sajātīyagaṇe gotre dehe'pi kathitaṃ kulam \|

kālo mṛtyau mahākāle samaye yamakṛṣṇayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 12 \[unit 1795\]

kṛṣṇatrivṛttau kālā tu nīlīmañjiṣṭhayorapi \|

kālī gauryyāṃ kṣīrakīṭe kālikāmātṛbhedayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 13 \[unit 1796\]

navāmbuvāhavṛnde'pi parivāde'pi ca kvacit \|

bhālaṃ lalāṭamahasorgālaḥ skandamahīruhoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 14 \[unit 1797\]

śālo hālanṛpe matsyaprabhede sarjjapādape \|

śālā gṛhe taruskandhaśākhāgāraikadeśayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 15 \[unit 1798\]

mālaṃ kṣetre gṛhe mālī mālā puṣpādidāmani \|

sālaḥ pādapamātraṃ syāt prākāre śaṅkukadrume \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 16 \[unit 1799\]

tālaḥ karatale'ṅguṣṭhamadhyamābhyāñca sammite \|

gīrākālakriyāmāne tālaḥ khaḍgādimuṣṭiṣu \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 17 \[unit 1800\]

drumabhede karāsphāle tālantu haritālake \|

vādyabhāṇḍe ca kāṃsyasya tālaśchadiṣi kampane \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 18 \[unit 1801\]

jālaṃ gavākṣa ānāye kṣārake dantavṛndayoḥ \|

jālo .nīpadrume jālī koṣātakyāmudāhṛtā \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 19 \[unit 1802\]

vyālo bhujaṅgame krūre śvāpade duṣṭadantini \|

nālaṃ daṇḍe sarojasya nālī śākakalambake \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 20 \[unit 1803\]

sthālaṃ bhājanabhede'pi sthālī syāt pāṭalokhayoḥ \|

phālaṃ śiropakaraṇe kārpāsādikavāsasi \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 21 \[unit 1804\]

bālaḥ kace śiśau mūrkhe hrīvere śvebhapucchayoḥ \|

alaṅkārāntare medhye vālī vālā truṭistriyoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 22 \[unit 1805\]

celo nīce'nyavaccelamaṃśuke garhite'nyavat \|

bhelaḥ plave bhīruke ca nirbuddhimunibhedayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 23 \[unit 1806\]

kaulo'ṅkapālāvṛtsaṅge bhelake potricitrayoḥ \|

kolā kaṇāyāṃ cavye ca kolantu badarīphale \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 24 \[unit 1807\]

śailo bhūbhṛti śailantu śaileye tārkṣyaśailake \|

śīlaṃ svabhāve sadvṛtte nīlaḥ kṛṣṇe nagāntare \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 25 \[unit 1808\]

nīlaḥ kapīśvare nīlī vṛkṣalāñchanabhedayoḥ \|

kūlaṃ stūpe taḍāge ca sainyapṛṣṭhapratīrayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 26 \[unit 1809\]

kūlaḥ picau bhavet kūlaṃ brahmadāruvihāyasoḥ \|

mūlamādye śiphāyāṃ syādbhanikuñjāntikeṣu ca \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 27 \[unit 1810\]

śūlaṃ praharaṇe mṛtyau ketane rogayogayoḥ \|

śūlā duṣṭavadhārthāya kīlake paṇyayoṣiti \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 28 \[unit 1811\]

sthūlaṃ syāt pīvare kūṭe niṣprajñe punaranyavat \|

amlo rasaprabhede syādāmlī cāṅgerikauṣadhau \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 29 \[unit 1812\]

śuklo yogāntare śvete śuklañca rajate matam \|

mallaḥ pātre kapāle ca matsyabhede balīyasi \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 30 \[unit 1813\]

bhallo bhallī ca bāṇe syādbhallo bhalluka iṣyate \|

bhallātakyāntu bhallī syādollaścārdre ca śūraṇe \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 31 \[unit 1814\]

cullacillau matau klinne netre klinnākṣṇi pillavat \|

ātāpini bhaveccillaścillī syādgauḍavāstuke \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 32 \[unit 1815\]

cullī citāyāmudvāne kvacidvādye'pyudīritā \|

balī vīrudhi santāne valkale kumumāmbare \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 33 \[unit 1816\]

khallī tu hastapādāvamarddanākhyaruji smṛtā \|

khallo vastraprabhede syānnimne carmaṇi cātake \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 34 \[unit 1817\]

ālurgalantikāyāṃ syāt kandabhede ca bhelake \|

pīlurgaje drume kāṇḍe paramāṇuprasūnayoḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 35 \[unit 1818\]

pīlustālāsthikhaṇḍe ca māluḥ patralatāstriyoḥ \|

śāluḥ kaṣāyadravye syāccaurakākhyauṣadhe'pi ca \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 36 \[unit 1819\]

valiścāmaradaśve ca jarayā ślathacarmaṇi \|

udarāvayave daitye karapūjopahārayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 37 \[unit 1820\]

gṛhadāruviśeṣe ca gandhake'pi kvacinmataḥ \|

dolā yānāntare nīlyāṃ melā syānmelake munau \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 38 \[unit 1821\]

ilā kalatre saumyasya dharitryāṃ gavi vāci ca \|

kīlaḥ kaphoṇau nirghāte śaṅkājvāle tu kīlavat \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 39 \[unit 1822\]

śilā kunadyāṃ dṛṣadi dvārādhaḥsthitadāruṇi \|

śilaṃ śilī bhaveduñche gaṇḍūpadyāṃ yathākramam \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 40 \[unit 1823\]

tulā palaśate rāśau bhāṇḍe syād dṛśyamānayoḥ \|

gṛhāṇāṃ dārubaddhāyāṃ pīṭhikāyāmapīṣyate \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 41 \[unit 1824\]

velā kāle ca jaladhestīranīravikārayoḥ \|

akliṣṭamaraṇe rāge sīmni vāci budhastriyām \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 42 \[unit 1825\]

bhojane'pīśvarāṇāṃ syājjhalā duhutari smṛtā \|

ātapormīṣu lukāyāṃ jhālī kvāpi vilokyate \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 43 \[unit 1826\]

kalā syānmūlavivṛddhau śilpādāvaṃśamātrake \|

ṣoḍaśāṃśe ca candrasya kalanākālayoḥ kalā \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 44 \[unit 1827\]

kalaṃ śukre kalo jīrṇe'pyavyaktamadhuradhvanau \|

lolā jihvākamalayorlolasvhcalasatṛṣṇayoḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 45 \[unit 1828\]

līlāṃ viduḥ kelivilāsakhelā-

śṛṅgārabhāvaprabhavakriyāsu \|

helāmavajñānavilāsayośca

hālā surāyāṃ nṛpatau ca hālaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 46 \[unit 1829\]

golā sakhīvarttulayorgolaṃ lakṣyānusārataḥ \|

patrāñjane kunadyāñca godāvaryāñca maṇḍale \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 47 \[unit 1830\]

aliḥ surāpuṣpalihorālistu viśadāśaye \|

āliḥ paṅktau ca sakhyāñca setau ca parikīrtitā \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 48 \[unit 1831\]

kaliḥ syāt kalahe śūre kalirantyayuge yudhi \|

dhūliḥ saṅkhyāntare reṇau ḍulistu kamaṭhe munau \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 49 \[unit 1832\]

mauliḥ kirīṭe dhammile kaṅkelidrumacūḍayoḥ \|

śālistu gandhamārjjāre kalamādiṣu ca smṛtaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 50 \[unit 1833\]

pāliḥ karṇalatāgre'śrau paṅktāvaṅkapradeśayoḥ \|

pāliḥ praste ca yūkāyāṃ jātaśmaśrustriyāmapi \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 51 \[unit 1834\]

jhillī syādātaparuṣi varttyāmudvarttanāṃśuke \|

vallī syādajamodāyāṃ puṣpabhede'pi vīrudhi \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 52 \[unit 1835\]

pallī kuṭīgrāmakayoḥ pallaḥ sthūlakuśūlake \|

tallī taruṇyāṃ tallastu jalādhārāntare'pi ca \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 53 \[unit 1836\]

kamalaṃ salile tāmre jalaje lomni bheṣaje \|

mṛgabhede ca kamalaḥ kamalā śrīvarastriyoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 54 \[unit 1837\]

capalaḥ pārade mīne caurake prastarāntare \|

capalā kamalāvidyutpuṃścalīpippalīṣu ca \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 55 \[unit 1838\]

capalaṃ kṣaṇike śīghre capalaścikure cale \|

upalaḥ prastare raje śarkarāyāṃ smṛtopalā \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 56 \[unit 1839\]

utpalaṃ māṃsaśūlye syāt kuṣṭhendīvarayorapi \|

analo vāsudeve'gnāvanalo vasuvātayoḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 57 \[unit 1840\]

śakalaṃ tvaci khaṇḍe syādrāgavastuni valkale \|

garalantu viṣe māne garalaṃ tṛṇapūlake \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 58 \[unit 1841\]

taralaścañcale ṣiṅge hāramadhyamaṇāva'pi \|

bhāsure cā'tha taralā yavāgusurayormatā \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 59 \[unit 1842\]

saralaḥ pūtikāṣṭhe syādudārāvakrayorapi \|

kapilo munibhede syād vāsudeve ca pāvake \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 60 \[unit 1843\]

kapilā śiṃśapāyāñca reṇukāgoviśeṣayoḥ \|

puṇḍarīkakariṇyāñca piṅge tu kapilo'nyavat \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 61 \[unit 1844\]

palalaṃ tilacūrṇe syāt palalaṃ paṅkamāṃsayoḥ \|

palalo yātudhāne syāt paṭalaṃ chadiṣi vraje \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 62 \[unit 1845\]

piṭake netraroge ca tilake paricchade \|

jagalaḥ piṣṭamadye ca medake madanadrume \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 63 \[unit 1846\]

kitave chagalaśchāge chagalī chāgamātari \|

vṛddhadārakabheṣajye chagalaṃ nīlavāsasi \|\| 63 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 64 \[unit 1847\]

pippalo bodhivṛkṣe syāt patibhede niraṅgule \|

pippalaṃ vasanacchede bhede'pi salile matam \|\| 64 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 65 \[unit 1848\]

kaṇāyāṃ pippalī coktā dhavalaḥ sundare site \|

mahokṣe cā'tha dhavalī saurabheyyāmudīritā \|\| 65 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 66 \[unit 1849\]

kuvalañcotpale muktāphale'pi badarīphale \|

kuśalaḥ śikṣite kṣemaparyāptisukṛteṣu ca \|\| 66 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 67 \[unit 1850\]

kuśalaṃ muṣalaṃ tu syādayo'gre muṣalī matā \|

tālamūlyāmāsuparṇī gṛhagodhikayorapi \|\| 67 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 68 \[unit 1851\]

niculo hijjalatarau niculaśca nicolake \|

piculo jhāvuke'pi syād hijjale jalavāyase \|\| 68 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 69 \[unit 1852\]

vipulaḥ pṛthule'gādhe merupaścimabhūdhare \|

tumulaḥ kalivṛkṣe syāttumulaṃ raṇasaṅkule \|\| 69 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 70 \[unit 1853\]

bahulaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe'gnau śitau ca bahulā gavi \|

elāyāṃ nīlikāyāñca bahulā kṛttikāsu ca \|\| 70 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 71 \[unit 1854\]

bahulaṃ prājyanabhasormātulo madanadrume \|

mātṛbhrātari dhuttūre vrīhibhede'pi mātulaḥ \|\| 71 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 72 \[unit 1855\]

kuṭilaṃ vācyavad bhugne kuṭilā saridantare \|

jaṭilastu jaṭāyukte jaṭilā māṃsikauṣaghau \|\| 72 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 73 \[unit 1856\]

sutalapratalau ceti pātālabhuvanadvaye \|

syātāmaṭṭālikābandhe tatāṅgulikare kramāt \|\| 73 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 74 \[unit 1857\]

pittalaṃ taijasadravye pittalaḥ pittasaṅgate \|

pittalā toyapippalpāṃ nistalaṃ vartule cale \|

kuntalaḥ syādyave deśe keśalāṅgalayogapi \|\| 74 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 75 \[unit 1858\]

kambalo nāgarāje syāta sāsnāprāvārayoḥ kṛmau \|

kambalaścottarāsaṅge kambalaṃ salile matam \|\| 75 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 76 \[unit 1859\]

śambalaṃ kulapātheyamatsareṣu samīritam \|

kandalastu kapāle syāduparāge navāṅkure \|\| 76 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 77 \[unit 1860\]

kaladhvanau kandalī tu mṛgapakṣiviśeṣayoḥ \|

piṅgalaḥ syānmunau rudre caṇḍāṃśoḥ pāripāśvake \|\| 77 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 78 \[unit 1861\]

nidhibhede kapāvagnau piṅgalaḥ kapile'nyavata \|

karāyikāyāṃ veśyāyāṃ piṅgalā kumudastriyām \|\| 78 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 79 \[unit 1862\]

jaṅgalo jalanirmuktadeśe māṃse ca jaṅgalam \|

maṅgalaṃ śreyasi śvetadūrvāyāmapi maṅgalā \|\| 79 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 80 \[unit 1863\]

sarvārthalakṣaṇe cāpi maṅgalaṃ samudīritam \|

kuṇḍalaṃ valaye pāśe kuṇḍalaṃ karṇapūraṇe \|

guḍūcyāṃ kāñcanādau ca kuṇḍalī samudīritā \|\| 80 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 81 \[unit 1864\]

syānmaṇḍalaṃ dvādaśarājake ca

deśe ca bimbe ca kadambake ca \|

kuṣṭhaprabhede'pyupasūryake'pi

bhujaṅgabhede śuni maṇḍalaḥ syāt \|\| 81 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 82 \[unit 1865\]

syādargalantu kallole daṇḍe cāntaḥkapāṭayoḥ \|

nirmalaṃ vimale vidyānnirmmālyābhrakayorapi \|\| 82 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 83 \[unit 1866\]

śauṣkalaḥ śuṣkamāṃsasya paṇake piśitāśini \|

pāṃśulaḥ puṃścale śambhoḥ khaṭvāṅge pāṃśulā bhuvi \|\| 83 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 84 \[unit 1867\]

saṅkulaṃ vācyavadvyāpte'vispaṣṭārthavacasyapi \|

ujjvalo dīptaśṛṅgāraviśadeṣu vikāśini \|\| 84 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 85 \[unit 1868\]

vañjulo vetasāśokatiniśeṣva'tha mañjulam \|

nikuñje śaivale mañjau jalaraṅkau tu mañjulā \|\| 85 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 86 \[unit 1869\]

piñjalantu haridrābhe kuśapatre'pi piñjalam \|

pudgalaḥ sundarākāre pudgalaścātmadehayoḥ \|

jambhalo devatābhede jambīre'pi ca jambhalaḥ \|\| 86 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 87 \[unit 1870\]

kumbhilaḥ śyālake caure ślokacchāyāhare jhaṣe \|

candrilo nāpite khyāto haravāstukaśākayoḥ \|\| 87 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 88 \[unit 1871\]

granthilo granthisahite karīradrau vikaṅkate \|

vāmilo dāmbhike yāme rāmilo ramaṇe smare \|\| 88 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 89 \[unit 1872\]

vārddala durdine melānandāyāmapi vārddalaḥ \|

taṇḍulākhyā viḍaṅge syāddhānyādinikare'pi ca \|\| 89 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 90 \[unit 1873\]

vṛṣalaḥ kathitaḥ śūdre candragupte ca vājini \|

niṣkalastu kalāśūnye naṣṭabīje tu vācyavat \|\| 90 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 91 \[unit 1874\]

pākalaṃ kuṣṭhabhaiṣajye pākalaḥ kuñjarajvare \|

pācalo rādhanadravye'pyanilānalayorapi \|\| 91 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 92 \[unit 1875\]

mācalo vandicaure syādgrāhe rakṣasi cā'maye \|

pāṭalaḥ śvetarakte syādāśuvrīhau ca pāṭalaḥ \|\| 92 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 93 \[unit 1876\]

pāṭalī pāṭalā'pi syādasyāḥ puṣpe'pi pāṭalam \|

lohalaḥ śṛṅkhalādhārye bhavedavyaktavāci ca \|\| 93 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 94 \[unit 1877\]

kohalo madyabhede syāt kohalaḥ syānmunāva'pi \|

taitilo gaṇḍake proktastaitilaṃ karaṇāntare \|\| 94 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 95 \[unit 1878\]

hemalaḥ svarṇakāre syāt kṛkalāse śilāntare \|

śyāmalo meceke'śvatye komalo mṛdule dale \|\| 95 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 96 \[unit 1879\]

kāmalaḥ kāmuke roge marudeśavasantayoḥ \|

peśalo rucire dakṣe gokilo muṣale hale \|\| 96 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 97 \[unit 1880\]

gaurilo lohacūrṇe syādgaurilo gaurasarṣape \|

phenilaḥ syādariṣṭe ca phenilaṃ badarīphale \|\| 97 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 98 \[unit 1881\]

madanasya phale cā'tha vācyavata phenavanyapi \|

śāvalākhyā tu śaivāle śaileye'pi ca śāvalam \|\| 98 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 99 \[unit 1882\]

śaivalaṃ padmakāṣṭhe ca śaivalañca jalāñcale \|

śītalaṃ puṣpakāsīse śailaje malayodbhave \|\| 99 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 100 \[unit 1883\]

bhavedāsanaparṇyāñca śītalaḥ śiśire'nyavat \|

kallolo harṣa ullole kallolaḥ paripanthini \|\| 100 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 101 \[unit 1884\]

paṭolamoṣadhīvastrabhidorjyotsnyāṃ paṭolya'pi \|

kākolī viṣabhede syāddroṇakākakulālayoḥ \|\| 101 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 102 \[unit 1885\]

tamālastilake khaḍgere tāpiñche varuṇadrume \|

suvelaḥ praṇate śānte suvelaḥ parvatāntare \|\| 102 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 103 \[unit 1886\]

avelastvapalāpe syādavelā pūgacūrṇake \|

kapālaṃ śiraso'sthni syād ghaṭādiśakale vraje \|\| 103 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 104 \[unit 1887\]

kuṣṭharogaviśeṣe ca gopālo gopabhūpayoḥ \|

kiṭṭālastāmrakalaśe kiṭṭālo lohagūthake \|\| 104 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 105 \[unit 1888\]

pātālaṃ nāgaloke syāt pātālaṃ vaḍavānale \|

pravālo vallakīdaṇḍe vidrume navapallave \|\| 105 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 106 \[unit 1889\]

arāla kuṭile sarjjarase samadadantini \|

karālo danture tuṅge karālañca kuṭherake \|\| 106 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 107 \[unit 1890\]

karāloddhūnataile'pi karālo bhīṣaṇe'nyavat \|

śṛgālo jambuke daitye śṛgālī ḍamare viduḥ \|\| 107 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 108 \[unit 1891\]

kulālaḥ kumbhakāre syāt kukkubhe kauśike'pi ca \|

kuddālo yugapatre syāt kuddālo bhūmidāraṇe \|\| 108 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 109 \[unit 1892\]

viḍālo netrapiṇḍe syādviḍālo'pi khagāntare \|

viḍālī pūtanābhede latāghūke'pi śasyate \|

jambālaṃ śaivale paṅke kīlālaṃ rudhire jale \|\| 109 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 110 \[unit 1893\]

vaṇṭhīlaṃ śūrayoryuddhe naukāyāśca khanitrake \|

ābhīlañcā'pi kṛcchre syādābhīlaśca bhayānake \|\| 110 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 111 \[unit 1894\]

nābhīlaṃ nābhigarbhāṇḍe vaṅkṣaṇe proktamastriyāḥ \|

utphullaḥ karaṇe strīṇāmuttāle ca vikasvare \|\| 111 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 112 \[unit 1895\]

śayāluḥ syādajagare nidrāśīle'pi kukkure \|

śraddhālurdohidinyāñca śraddhāvati tu vācyavat \|\| 112 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 113 \[unit 1896\]

lāṅgulaṃ mehane pucche śārdūlo rākṣasāntare \|

vyāghre ca paśubhede ca sattame tūttarasthitaḥ \|\| 113 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 114 \[unit 1897\]

vātūlo vātasaṅghāte vātule mārutāsahe \|

kukūlaṃ śaṅkusaṅkīrṇe śvabhre cā'pi tuṣānale \|\| 114 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 115 \[unit 1898\]

kukūlaṃ syād dukūlantukṣaume sūkṣmāṃśuke'pi ca \|

jambūlo jambuviṭape jambūlaḥ krakacacchade \|\| 115 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 116 \[unit 1899\]

caṣālo homakuṇḍe syādgartte cottāla utkaṭe \|

śreṣṭhe'pi vikarāle'pi syāduttālaḥ plavaṅgame \|\| 116 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 117 \[unit 1900\]

cūḍālī tūccaṭāyāṃ syāccūḍāvati tu vācyavat \|

viśālā tvindravāruṇyāṃ viśālo'jjayinīpuri \|\| 117 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 118 \[unit 1901\]

viśālo vṛkṣabhede'pi viśālaḥ pṛthule'nyavat \|

mekhalā khaḍgabandhe syāt kāñcīśailanitambayoḥ \|\| 118 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 119 \[unit 1902\]

śṛṅkhalā puṃskaṭīvastrabandhe'pi nigaḍe'pi ca \|

kucelā viddhaparṇyāṃ syāt kucelastu kuvāsasi \|\| 119 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 120 \[unit 1903\]

acalā vasudhāyāṃ syādacalaḥ śailakīlayoḥ \|

cañcalā tu taḍillakṣmyoścañcalaḥ kāmuke'nile \|\| 120 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 121 \[unit 1904\]

ilvalā tārakābhede ilvalo daityamatsyayoḥ \|

picchilā śālmalīsindhubhedapotakikāmu ca \|\| 121 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 122 \[unit 1905\]

picchilā śiṃśapāyāñca picchilo vijjale'nyavat \|

saptalā pāṭalau guñjānavamālikayorapi \|\| 122 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 123 \[unit 1906\]

bhaveccarmmakaśāyāñca mahilā bhīrugundrayoḥ \|

sidhmalā matsyacūrṇe syāta sidhmalaśca kilāsini \|\| 123 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 124 \[unit 1907\]

sandhilā tu suruṅgāyāṃ nadīmandirayorapi \|

rasālā mārjjitāyāṃ syājjihvādūrvāvidāriṣu \|

rasālaṃ sihlake vole rasālaścekṣucūtayoḥ \|\| 124 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 125 \[unit 1908\]

vimalā śītalāyāṃ syād bhuvo bhede ca nirmmale \|

vimale cā'malā lakṣmyāma'malaṃ viśade'bhrake \|\| 125 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 126 \[unit 1909\]

śrīphalī nīlikādhātryormālūre śrīphalaḥ smṛtaḥ \|

khaṇḍālī tailamāne syāt sarasīkāmukastriyoḥ \|\| 126 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 127 \[unit 1910\]

pātalī vāgurāyāṃ syānnārīmṛtpātrabhedayoḥ \|

jāṅgalī śūkaśimbyāṃ syājjāṅgalaśca kapiñjale \|\| 127 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 128 \[unit 1911\]

lāṅgalī gajapippalyāṃ lāṅgalaṃ kusumāntare \|

sīre'pi lāṅgalaṃ tālagṛhadāruviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 128 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 129 \[unit 1912\]

aṅgulī karaśākhāyāṃ karṇikāyāṃ gajasya ca \|

jāṅgulī viṣavidyāyāṃ jāṅgulaṃ jālinīphale \|\| 129 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 130 \[unit 1913\]

kadalī vaijayantyāñca rambhāyāṃ hariṇāntare \|

kadalā śālmalībrāhmīḍimbikāsu ca kathyate \|\| 130 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 131 \[unit 1914\]

nakulī kukkuṭīmāṃsyornakulaḥ pāṇḍave paśau \|

nākulī kukkuṭīkande nākulī cavyarāsnayoḥ \|\| 131 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 132 \[unit 1915\]

naipālī navamālīmanaḥśilāsuvahāsu ca \|

tāmbūlī nāgavallyāñca tāmbūlaṃ kramuke'pi ca \|\| 132 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 133 \[unit 1916\]

pañcālī putrikāgītyoḥ pañcālo janadeśayoḥ \|

śālmalī tarubhede syāddīpabhede ca śālmalī \|\| 133 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 134 \[unit 1917\]

kevalo jñānabhede syāt kevalaścaikakṛtsnayoḥ \|

nirṇīte kevalañcoktaṃ kevalaḥ kuhane kvacit \|\| 134 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 135 \[unit 1918\]

kāhalī tu taruṇyāṃ syāt kāhalaṃ bhṛśaśuṣkayoḥ \|

vādyabhāṇḍaviśeṣe tu kāhalā kāhale khale \|\| 135 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 136 \[unit 1919\]

gandholī varaṭābhadrāśaṭīṣu kathitā budhaiḥ \|

añjalī kuḍave vidyādañjaliḥ karasampuṭe \|\| 136 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 137 \[unit 1920\]

madakalaḥ syānmattebhe madenāvyaktavāci ca \|

bhavet kalakalaḥ sarjjarase kolāhale'pi ca \|\| 137 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 138 \[unit 1921\]

smṛto vicikilo mallīprabhede madane'pi ca \|

bṛhannalo guḍākeśe mahāpoṭagale mataḥ \|\| 138 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 139 \[unit 1922\]

parimalo vimarśe ca syānmanoharagandhavat \|

ratopamarddavikasaddeharāgādisaurabhe \|\| 139 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 140 \[unit 1923\]

yavaphalaḥ smṛto veṇau māṃsīkuṭajayorapi \|

mucāphalantu karpūre mauktike lavalīphale \|\| 140 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 141 \[unit 1924\]

sadāphalaḥ skandaphale nārikele'pyudumbare \|

viduḥ karmaphalaṃ karmaraṅgakarmavipākayoḥ \|\| 141 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 142 \[unit 1925\]

mṛtyuphalo mahākāle kadalyāṃ mṛtyuphalya'pi \|

bhavedvāyuphalaṃ śakrakārmuke karake'pi ca \|\| 142 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 143 \[unit 1926\]

halāhalo viṣe jyeṣṭhyāṃ hayalālāhvayorage \|

kutūhalaṃ kautuke syāt praśaste'pi kutūhalam \|\| 143 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 144 \[unit 1927\]

dalāmalaṃ damanake tathā maruvake'pi ca \|

mahābalaṃ sīsake syād balāhye'pi mahābalā \|\| 144 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 145 \[unit 1928\]

vedatibalāyāñca khatamālo balāhake \|

dhūme'pi kandarālaḥ syāt parkaṭīgarddabhāṇḍayoḥ \|\| 145 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 146 \[unit 1929\]

mahākālastrinayane kimpāke pramathāntare \|

mahānīlo bhṛṅgarāje maṇināgaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 146 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 147 \[unit 1930\]

bhaved bahuphalābhikhyā''malakīnīpavṛkṣayoḥ \|

jalāñcalaṃ svatovārinirgame śaivale'pi ca \|\| 147 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 148 \[unit 1931\]

gaṇḍaśailo lalāṭe syāccyute sthūlopale gireḥ \|

ulūkhalaṃ guggule syāt kaṇḍanyarthe ulūkhalam \|\| 148 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 149 \[unit 1932\]

cakravālo'dribhede syāccakravālantu maṇḍale \|

smṛtaḥ poṭagalaḥ kāśe nale poṭagalo jhaṣe \|\| 149 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 150 \[unit 1933\]

hastimallo'bhramātaṅge hastimallo vināyake \|

bhasmatūlaṃ grāmakūṭe pāṃśuvarṣe hime'pi ca \|\| 150 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 151 \[unit 1934\]

vātakeliḥ kalālāpe piṅgānāṃ dantalekhane \|

kamaṇḍaluḥ syāt karake parkaṭīpādape'pi ca \|\| 151 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 152 \[unit 1935\]

akṣamālā'kṣasūtre syādarundhatyāmapi smṛtā \|

maṇimālā smṛtā hāre strīṇāṃ dantakṣatāntare \|\| 152 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 153 \[unit 1936\]

dhvanilīlā smṛtā veṇau kāhalāvīṇayorapi \|

rajasvalā puṣpavatyāṃ sairibhe'pi rajasvalaḥ \|\| 153 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 154 \[unit 1937\]

ekāṣṭīlāmapi prāhuḥ śivamallauṣadhībhidoḥ \|

atibalā balābhede pravāle'tibalo mataḥ \|\| 154 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 155 \[unit 1938\]

bhadrakālī tu gandholyāmumāyāmoṣadhībhidi \|

haritālī nabhorarevādūrvākhaḍgalatāsu ca \|\| 155 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 156 \[unit 1939\]

andhapālī smṛtā koṭyāṃ dhātrikāparirambhayoḥ \|

gandhaphalī priyaṅgau syāt korake campakasya ca \|\| 156 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam / 157 \[unit 1940\]

āsutībala ityākhyā kanyāpālakayajvanoḥ \|

syāt paṇḍukamvalaḥ śvetakambale ca śilāntare \|\| 157 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam / 158 \[unit 1941\]

ekakuṇḍala ityeṣa saunandini dhanādhipe \|

bhaveduddaṇḍapālastu sarpamatsyaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 158 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam / 159 \[unit 1942\]

kṛpīṭapālamicchanti kenipātasamudrayoḥ \|

bhavet suratatālī tu dūtikāyāṃ śiraḥsraji \|\| 159 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam / fragment 1943

iti lāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vaikakam / 1 \[unit 1944\]

vaṃ pracetasi jānīyādivārthe ca tadavyayam \|

svaṃ jñātāvātmadhanayorātmīye ca pracakṣyate \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 2 \[unit 1945\]

bhavaḥ saṃsārasampāptiśreyaḥśaṅkarajanmasu \|

davo dāva iva khyāto vanāgnivanayorapi \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 3 \[unit 1946\]

dravaḥ pradrāvarasayorgatau narmaṇi vidrave \|

ghavaḥ patyau nare dhūrtte vṛkṣabhede'pi kīrttitaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 4 \[unit 1947\]

javo vegini vege syādoṇḍrapuṣpe javā matā \|

lavo leśe vināśe ca chedane rāmanandane \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 5 \[unit 1948\]

plavaḥ kāraṇḍave bheke kulake bhelake kapau \|

śabde plutagatau plakṣe caṇḍālajalakākayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 6 \[unit 1949\]

plavaṃ gandhatṛṇe proktaṃ kaivarttīmustake'pi ca \|

havo yajñe tathāhvāne nideśe'pi havo mataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 7 \[unit 1950\]

śavo mṛte śavaṃ nīre savaḥ sandhānayajñayoḥ \|

kṣavaḥ kṣute rājikāyāṃ navaṃ navye stave viduḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 8 \[unit 1951\]

dhruvaḥ kīle śive śaṅkau vasau yoge vaṭe munau \|

dhruvā mūrvāśālaparṇyorgītisrugbhedayorapi \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 9 \[unit 1952\]

dhruvantu niścite tarke niścale śaśvate'nyavat \|

stuvākhyā sallakīmūrvāsrugbhedeṣu ca viśrutā \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 10 \[unit 1953\]

śivaṃ mokṣe sukhe bhadre salile'tha śivo hare \|

vede yogāntare kīle vāluke guggule'pi ca \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 11 \[unit 1954\]

puṇḍarīkadrume cā'pi śivā jhaṇṭāmalauṣadhau \|

abhayā''malakīgaurīkroṣṭrīsaktuphalāsu ca \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 12 \[unit 1955\]

divaṃ svarge'ntarīkṣe ca klībaṃ śaṇḍe'pyapauruṣe \|

jīvo vṛkṣaprabhede syājjīvaḥ prāṇini gīṣpatau \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 13 \[unit 1956\]

jīvā jīvantikābhūmyormaurvīśiñjitavṛttiṣu \|

vacāyāmapi jīvā syājjīvaṃ jīvyañca jīvite \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 14 \[unit 1957\]

bhāvaḥ svabhāve'bhiprāye ceṣṭāsattātmajanmasu \|

kriyālīlāpadārtheṣu budhajantuvibhūtiṣu \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 15 \[unit 1958\]

devaḥ sure ghane rājñi devamākhyātamindviye \|

devī bhaṭṭārikāyāñca tejanīspṛkkayorapi \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 16 \[unit 1959\]

sattvaṃ drume piśācādau bale dravyasvabhāvayoḥ \|

ātmatve vyavasāye ca citte prāṇeṣu jantuṣu \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 17 \[unit 1960\]

sāntaṃ sāmani dākṣiṇye kaṇvaṃ pāpe munau viduḥ \|

kiṇvaṃ pāpe surābīje bilvantu śrīphale phale \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 18 \[unit 1961\]

tattvaṃ vādyaprabhede snmayāt svarūpe paramātmani \|

hrasvakharvāvimau śabdāvekārthau vāmanārthayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 19 \[unit 1962\]

viśvaṃ samaste jagati viśvadeve'pi nāgare \|

viśvā cā'tiviṣāyāṃ syādaśvaḥ pumbhedavājinoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 20 \[unit 1963\]

pārśvaṃ kakṣādhare cakropānte parśugaṇe'pi ca \|

prādhvaṃ supraṇate cātidūravartmani bandhane \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 21 \[unit 1964\]

pakvaṃ pariṇatārthe syādvināśābhimukhe'pi ca \|

dvandvo rahasye kalahe dvandvaṃ mithunayugmayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 22 \[unit 1965\]

ūrddhvaṃ syāducchrite tuṅge copariṣṭādapi smṛtam \|

divaṃ dyauḥ svargaloke ca gagane ca prayujyate \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 23 \[unit 1966\]

aviḥ śaile ravau meṣe bhavenmūṣikakambale \|

ṛtumatyāmaviḥ proktā śivirbhūrjje nṛpāntare \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 24 \[unit 1967\]

kaviḥ kāvyakare sūrī kavirvālmīkiśukrayoḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 25 \[unit 1968\]

grīvā'pi kandharāyāṃ syāttacchirāyāmapīṣyate \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 26 \[unit 1969\]

laṭvā karañjabhede'sya phale vādye khagāntare \|

pṛthvī bhūmau pṛthau hiṅgupatrikākṛṣṇajīrayoḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 28 \[unit 1971\]

prabhavo jalamūle syājjanmabhūmau parākrame \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 29 \[unit 1972\]

prasavastu phale puṣpe vṛkṣāṇāṃ garbhamocane \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 30 \[unit 1973\]

kitavo dhūrttavanmatte vañcake kanakāhvaye \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 31 \[unit 1974\]

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 32 \[unit 1975\]

śṛṅgāre raktarāge ca vidravastu palāyane \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 33 \[unit 1976\]

uddhavo mātule viṣṇorutsave kratupāvake \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 34 \[unit 1977\]

keśavo vāsudeve syāt punnāge keśavatyapi \|

kaitavantu chale dyūte pheravo jamūke'srape \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 35 \[unit 1978\]

rauravo narake ghore kairavaḥ kitave ripau \|

kairavaṃ kumude coktaṃ candrikāyāñca kairavī \|\| 35 \|\|

bhairavaṃ bhīṣaṇe garbhe bhairavaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 37 \[unit 1980\]

vāḍavaṃ karaṇe strīṇāmaurve vipre'pi vāḍavaḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 39 \[unit 1982\]

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 40 \[unit 1983\]

madhunaḥ śarkarāyāñca gālavo munilodhrayoḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 41 \[unit 1984\]

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 42 \[unit 1985\]

āhavaḥ saṅgare yāge rāghavo raghuvaṃśaje \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 44 \[unit 1987\]

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 45 \[unit 1988\]

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 46 \[unit 1989\]

abhāvaḥ syādasattāyāyabhāvo nidhane'pi ca \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 47 \[unit 1990\]

niṣpāvaḥ sūryapavane rājamāṣe kaḍiṅgare \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 48 \[unit 1991\]

gāṇḍīvaṃ gāṇḍivaṃ jiṣṇoḥ kodaṇḍe kārmuke'pi ca \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 50 \[unit 1993\]

pārthivo nṛpatau bhūmivikāre pārthivo'nyavat \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 51 \[unit 1994\]

prasevaḥ kathito dhīrairvīṇāṅgasūtayorapi \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 52 \[unit 1995\]

kāravī madhurādīpyatvakpatrīkṛṣṇajīrake \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 54 \[unit 1997\]

pariplavaścañcale syādākule'pi pariplavaḥ \|

kuśīlavastu vālmīkau naṭayācakayorapi \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 1998\]
