parābharastiraskāre vināśe ca parābhavaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 1999\]

bhavet pāraśavaḥ pārastraiṇe śūdrāsute dvijāt \|

śastre'pyādīnavo doṣe parikliṣṭadurantayoḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 57 \[unit 2000\]

viduḥ śītaśivaṃ śamyāṃ śaileyaśatapuṣpayoḥ \|

saindhave'pi puṭagrīvo gargarītāmrakumbhayoḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 58 \[unit 2001\]

dhāmārgavastvapāmārge devadālyāmapi smṛtaḥ \|

anubhāvaḥ prabhāve syānniścaye bhāvasūyane \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 59 \[unit 2002\]

baladevaḥ smṛto vāte kālindībhedane'pi ca \|

baladevāmapi prāhustrāyamāṇauṣadhe budhāḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 60 \[unit 2003\]

jalavilvastu pañcāṅge karkaṭe jalacatvare \|

rohitāśvo bṛhadbhānau hariścandre nṛpātmaje \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 61 \[unit 2004\]

sahadevā balādaṇḍotpalaśārivabheṣaje \|

sahadevī tu sarpākṣyāṃ sahadevaśca pāṇḍave \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / fragment 2005

jīvañjīvaścakore syād drumapakṣiviśeṣayoḥ \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vapañcakam / 62 \[unit 2006\]

āśitambhavamannādye tṛptāvāpyāśitambhavaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / vāntavargaḥ / vapañcakam / fragment 2007

iti vāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śaikakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śaikakam / fragment 2008

śaṃ sukhe śarmaṇi proktaṃ śreyasi śaśca śastrake \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 1 \[unit 2009\]

vaśamāyattatāyāṃ syādvaśamicchāprabhutvayoḥ \|

vaśā vandhyāsutāyoṣāstrīgavīkariṇīṣu ca \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 2 \[unit 2010\]

kuśo rāmasute darbhe yoktre dvīpe kuśaṃ jale \|

kuśī phāle'pi valgāyāṃ kuśā pāpiṣṭhamattayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 3 \[unit 2011\]

kuśo vācyavadākhyātaḥ spaśaḥ praṇidhiyuddhayoḥ \|

śaśo gandharase lodhre syāt paśau puruṣāntare \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 4 \[unit 2012\]

darśastu saṅgame sūryacandrayoravalokane \|

pakṣānte vaidikavidhau darśaśca samudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 5 \[unit 2013\]

sparśo rujāyāṃ dāne ca sparśane sparśake'pi ca \|

viṭ smṛto vaiśyamanujapraveśe tu manīṣibhiḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 6 \[unit 2014\]

pāśaḥ kacānte saṅghārthaḥ karṇānte śobhanārthakaḥ \|

chātrādyante ca nindārthaḥ pāśaḥ pakṣyādibandhane \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 7 \[unit 2015\]

nāśaḥ palāyane prokto nidhanānupalambhayoḥ \|

kāśastṛṇe syāt kṣavathau vārāṇasyāṃ tu kāśyapi \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 8 \[unit 2016\]

keśo daityāntare bāle hrīvere ca pracetasi \|

kīśo digambare proktaḥ kīśaḥ śākhāmṛge'pi ca \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 9 \[unit 2017\]

īśaḥ prabhau śaṅkare syādīśā lāṅgaladaṇḍake \|

veśo veśyāgṛhe prokto nepathye gṛhamātrake \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 10 \[unit 2018\]

kleśo duḥkhe'pi rāgādau vyavasāye'pi ca kvacit \|

vaṃśo veṇau kule varge pṛṣṭhasyāvayave'pi ca \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 11 \[unit 2019\]

daṃśaḥ syāt khaṇḍane doṣe daṃśo marmaṇi kīrttitaḥ \|

sarpakṣate'pi sannāhavanamakṣikayorapi \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 12 \[unit 2020\]

aṃśurleśe ravau raśmāvāśuvrīhau ca satvare \|

pāṃśurdhūliṣu śasyārthacirasañcitagomaye \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 13 \[unit 2021\]

dṛgdarśane locane ca dṛgbuddhau vīkṣake'pi ca \|

daśā varttāvavasthāyāṃ vastrāṃśe syurdaśā api \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 14 \[unit 2022\]

niśā dāruharidrāyāṃ syāt triyāmāharidrayoḥ \|

āśā tṛṣṇādiśoḥ proktā rāśirmeṣādipuñjayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 15 \[unit 2023\]

peśī palalapiṇḍyāṃ syānmāṃsyāṃ khaḍgapidhānake \|

aṇḍabhede'pi peśī syāt supakvakaṇike'pi ca \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 16 \[unit 2024\]

kapiśaḥ sihlake śyāve mādhavyāṃ kapiśī matā \|

kuliśo matsyacaṇḍe syāddambholau kuliśaṃ smṛtam \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 17 \[unit 2025\]

vivaśaḥ syādavaśyātyā kliṣṭaduṣṭadhiyorapi \|

nistriṃśo nirddaye khaḍge sadṛśantūcite same \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 18 \[unit 2026\]

bāliśaḥ śāvake mūrkhe lomaśo lomasaṃyute \|

meṇḍake cātha kāsīsaśūgālījaṭilāsu ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 19 \[unit 2027\]

lomaśā śūkaśimbyāñca markaṭīkākajaṅghayoḥ \|

mahāmedātibalayoḥ śākinībhidi ceṣyate \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 20 \[unit 2028\]

vikāśo vijane vyaktau sakāśaḥ sadṛśe'ntike \|

prakāśo'tiprasiddhe syāt prahāsātapayoḥ sphuṭe \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 21 \[unit 2029\]

nīkāśo niścaye tulye hatāśo nirdaye khale \|

kīnāśaḥ karkaśe kṣudre kṛtāntapaśughātinoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 22 \[unit 2030\]

sukhāśo rājatiniśe śobhanā''śapracetasoḥ \|

piṅgāśo matsyabhede syāttathā pallīpatāvapi \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 23 \[unit 2031\]

piṅgāśī nīlikāyāñca piṅgāśaṃ jātyakāñcane \|

palāśaḥ kiṃśuke śaṭyāṃ harite rākṣase'pi ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 24 \[unit 2032\]

patre palāśaṃ saṃveśaḥ śayane cāsane'pi ca \|

niveśaḥ śibirodvāhavinyāseṣu prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 25 \[unit 2033\]

nirveśo mūrcchane bhoge nirveśo vetane'pi ca \|

pradeśo deśamātre syāttarjjanyaṅguṣṭhasammite \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 26 \[unit 2034\]

bhittāvapi pradeśaḥ syāt sadeśo'bhyarṇadeśayoḥ \|

nideśaḥ śāsanopāntabhāṣaṇe'pi prayujyate \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 27 \[unit 2035\]

ādarśo darpaṇe ṭīkāpratipustakayorapi \|

karkaśaḥ paruṣe krūre kṛpaṇe nirddaye dṛḍhe \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 28 \[unit 2036\]

ikṣau sāhasike kāśamarddakampillayorapi \|

girīśo vākpatau rudre girīśo'dripatāvapi \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 29 \[unit 2037\]

uṭṭīśo granyabhede syāduḍḍīśaścaṇḍikāpatau \|

tuṅgīśaḥ śaṅkare candre pakṣīśastārkṣyakṛṣṇayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 30 \[unit 2038\]

upāṃśurjapabhede syādupāṃśu vijane'vyayam \|

duḥsparśākhyā kharasparśe kaṇṭakāryāñca vāsake \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 31 \[unit 2039\]

vipāśākhyā saridbhedapāśavarjjitayormatā \|

vikeśī paṭuvarttau syādvikeśī niṣkacastriyām \|

bhūkeśī valvajeṣu syād bhūkeśaḥ śaivale vaṭe \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 32 \[unit 2040\]

apadeśaḥ smṛto lakṣye nimittavyājayorapi \|

jīviteśo yame kānte jīvātau jīvitādhipe \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 33 \[unit 2041\]

āśrayāśo hutavahe bhavedāśrayanāśake \|

nāgapāśaḥ smṛtaḥ strīṇāṃ karaṇe varuṇāyudhe \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 34 \[unit 2042\]

puroḍāśo havirbhede camasyāṃ piṣṭakasya ca \|

rame somalatāyāśca hutaśeṣe'pi kīrttitaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 35 \[unit 2043\]

pariveśaḥ parivṛttau bhānoḥ savidhamaṇḍale \|

pratiṣkaśaḥ sahāye syādvārttāharapurogayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 36 \[unit 2044\]

upasparśaḥ sparśamātre snānācamanayorapi \|

bhūmispṛgvaiśyanarayoḥ pūradṛka syāt khale śanau \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 37 \[unit 2045\]

apabhraṃśo'paśabde syādbhāṣābhedāvapātayoḥ \|

upadaṃśo'vadaṃśe syānmeṇḍhre roge'pi varttate \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 38 \[unit 2046\]

khaṇḍaparśuḥ parśurāme śaṅkare cūrṇalepini \|

khaṇḍāmalakabhaiṣajye siṃhikātanaye'pi ca \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 39 \[unit 2047\]

pādapāśī khaṇḍakāyāṃ śṛṅkhalākaṭake'pi ca \|

smṛtā pañcadaśī paurṇamāsyamāvāsyayorapi \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śapañcakam / 40 \[unit 2048\]

dhūrjaṭau khaṇḍaparaśurjāmadagnye vidhuntude \|

khaṇḍāmalakabhaiṣajye cūḍāle'pi smṛto budhaiḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / śāntavargaḥ / śapañcakam / fragment 2049

iti śāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 1 \[unit 2050\]

vṛṣaḥ syādvāsake dharme saurabheye ca śukrale \|

puṃrāśibhedayoḥ śṛṅgyāṃ mūṣikaśreṣṭhayorapi \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 2 \[unit 2051\]

krapikacchvāṃ vṛṣā proktā vratināmāsane vṛṣī \|

viṣantu garale toye'tiviṣāyāṃ viṣā bhavet \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 3 \[unit 2052\]

tuṣo dhānyatvaci khyāto vibhītakatarāvapi \|

niṣaśca sparśane vyāje tarṣo lipsāpipāsayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 4 \[unit 2053\]

varṣaṃ saṃvatsare vṛṣṭau jambūdvīpe ghane viduḥ \|

prāvṛṭkāle tu varṣāḥ tu karṣaḥ karṣaṇamānayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 5 \[unit 2054\]

māṣo vrīhyantare mūrkhe mānatvagdoṣabhedayoḥ \|

meṣo rāśiviṣeṣe syāduraṇe bheṣajāntare \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 6 \[unit 2055\]

śeṣaḥ saṃkarṣaṇe'nanta upayuktatare vadhe \|

prasādānnijanirmālyadāne śeṣe'pi kīrttitā \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 7 \[unit 2056\]

śoṣastu śoṣaṇe rājayakṣmaṇyapi nigadyate \|

doṣaḥ syād dūṣaṇe pāpe doṣā rātrau bhuje'pi ca \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 8 \[unit 2057\]

koṣastu kuḍmale pātre divye khaḍgapidhānake \|

jātikoṣe'rthasaṅghāte peśyāṃ śabādisaṅgrahe \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 9 \[unit 2058\]

ghoṣastu ghoṣake dhvāne gopālābhīrapalliṣu \|

ghoṣā tu śatapuṣpāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ kāṃsye'mbudadhvanau \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 10 \[unit 2059\]

praiṣaḥ syāt preṣaṇe kleśe marddanonmānayorapi \|

pauṣo māsaviśeṣe syāt pauṣantu mahayuddhayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 11 \[unit 2060\]

tviṭa kāntau vyavasāye ca jigīṣāyāṃ rucau giri \|

viṭ vyāpane ca viṣṭhāyāṃ śuṣiḥ śoṣe vile'pi \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 12 \[unit 2061\]

ṛṣirvede vaśiṣṭhādau dīdhitāvapi paṭhyate \|

uṣā vāṇasutārātryoruṣaḥ kāmini guggulau \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 13 \[unit 2062\]

jhaṣā nāgabalāyāṃ syādvaisāriṇi jhaṣo mataḥ \|

karṣūḥ karīpadahane kulyāyāmapi ceṣyate \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 14 \[unit 2063\]

nikaṣaḥ śāṇaphalake nikaṣā yātumātari \|

nahuṣo rājñi bhujage kaluṣañcāvilainasoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 15 \[unit 2064\]

kilviṣe vṛjine roge'pyaparādhe'pi kilviṣam \|

nimeṣanimiṣau kālaprabhede'kṣinimīlane \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 16 \[unit 2065\]

paruṣaṃ karbure rūkṣe syānniṣṭhuravacasyapi \|

puruṣaḥ pūruṣe sāṅkhyajñe ca punnāgapādape \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 17 \[unit 2066\]

pauruṣaṃ puruṣasyoktaṃ bhāve karmaṇi tejasi \|

ūrddhvavistṛtadoḥpāṇinṛmāne'pi ca pauruṣaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 18 \[unit 2067\]

kalmāṣo rākṣase kṛṣṇe kalmāṣaṃ kṛṣṇapāṇḍure \|

kulmāṣo yāvake proktaḥ kulmāṣaṃ kāñjike'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 19 \[unit 2068\]

uṣṇīṣantu śiroveṣṭe kirīṭe lakṣaṇāntare \|

tarīṣaḥ śobhanākāre bhele'bdhivyavasāyayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 20 \[unit 2069\]

rohiṣaṃ kattṛṇe jñeyaṃ rohiṣo hariṇāntare \|

viśleṣaḥ syādvighaṭane viśleṣo vidhure'pi ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 21 \[unit 2070\]

pradoṣaḥ kālabhede syāt pradoṣo doṣa iṣyate \|

abhīṣuḥ pragrahe raśmau pratyūṣo'harmukhe vasau \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 22 \[unit 2071\]

gaṇḍūṣo mukhapūrttau syāt karihastāṅgulāvapi \|

prasṛtonmite śailūṣaḥ kathito naṭabilvayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 23 \[unit 2072\]

māriṣaḥ śākabhede syānmāriṣā dakṣamātari \|

nāṭyoktyāṃ māriṣaścārye tārīṣaḥ kanake'mbudhau \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 24 \[unit 2073\]

āmiṣaṃ palale lobhe sambhogotkocayorapi \|

āmiṣaṃ sundarākārapūpādiviṣaye'pi ca \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 25 \[unit 2074\]

ākarṣaḥ śāriphalake pāśake dūta indriye \|

ākarṣaṇe'pi cā'karṣaḥ kodaṇḍābhyāsavastuni \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 26 \[unit 2075\]

saṃharṣamāhuḥ sparddhāyāṃ pramode'pi prabhañjane \|

śuśrūṣā śrotumicchāyāṃ paricaryākathānayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 27 \[unit 2076\]

jigīṣā jetumicchāyāṃ vyavasāyaprakarṣayoḥ \|

mahiṣī nṛpapatnyāñca sairibhyāmoṣaghībhidi \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 28 \[unit 2077\]

animiṣaḥ sure matsye'pyanukarṣo'nukarṣaṇe \|

anukarṣo rathasyā'dhaḥsthite dāruṇi ceṣyate \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 29 \[unit 2078\]

anutarṣaḥ surāpānapātre tṛṣṇābhilāṣayoḥ \|

vātarūṣastu vātūlotkocayoḥ śakrakārmmuke \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 30 \[unit 2079\]

ambarīṣaṃ raṇe bhrāṣṭre'pyambarīṣo nṛpāntare \|

mārttaṇḍe khaṇḍaparaśāvāmrātakakiśorayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 31 \[unit 2080\]

parighoṣo nināde syādavācye jaladadhvanau \|

nandighoṣo'rjjunarathe ghoṣe vandijanasya ca \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 32 \[unit 2081\]

mahāghoṣo mataṅge ca mahāghoṣo'tighoṣaṇe \|

smṛtā karkkaṭaśṛṅgyāñca mahāghoṣā manīṣibhiḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 33 \[unit 2082\]

kimpuruṣaḥ kinnare syāllokabhede'pyalambuṣaḥ \|

charddane'lambuṣā proktā muṇḍīrīsvargaveśyayoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 34 \[unit 2083\]

palaṅkaṣā gokṣurakarāsnāguggulukiṃśuke \|

tuṇḍīrīlākṣayoścā'pi rākṣase'pi palaṅkaṣaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / ṣāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / fragment 2084

iti pāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 1 \[unit 2085\]

raso gandharase svāde cittādo viṣarāgayoḥ \|

śṛṅgārādau drave vīrye dehadhātau ca pārade \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 2 \[unit 2086\]

rasā tu sallakīpāvājihvādharaṇikaṅguṣu \|

bhāso bhāsi samākhyāto ghoṣṭakukkuṭagṛdhrayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 3 \[unit 2087\]

dāsaḥ śūdre dānapātre bhṛtyadhīvarayorapi \|

dāsī ceṭyāñca jhiṇṭyāñca rāsaḥ kolāhale dhvanau \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 4 \[unit 2088\]

bhāṣāśṛṅkhalake rāsaḥ krīḍāyāmapi goduhām \|

vāsaḥ suvarṇacele syāt prabhede kiṣkuparvaṇaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 5 \[unit 2089\]

bhāḥ prabhāve mayūkhe ca māḥ smṛtaścandramāsayoḥ \|

adaḥ parasminnatrā'pi goso gandharasoṣayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 6 \[unit 2090\]

vatsastarṇakaputrādivarṣe vatsantu vakṣasi \|

butsaḥ stambe hārabhede stabake granthiparṇake \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 7 \[unit 2091\]

vāso gehe'pyavasthāne vāsā syādaṭarūṣake \|

vyāso munau syādvistāre trāso bhīmaṇidoṣayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 8 \[unit 2092\]

haṃso vihaṅgabhede syādarke viṣṇau hayāntare \|

yogimantrādibhedeṣu paramātmani matsare \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 9 \[unit 2093\]

nirlobhanṛpatau haṃsaḥ śārīramarudantare \|

aṃsaḥ skandhe vibhāge ca kaṃso daityāntare smṛtaḥ \|

kāṃsye ca kāṃsyapātre ca mānabhede'pi kīrttitaḥ \|

māṃsaḥ syādāmiṣe māṃsī kakkolījaṭayorapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 10 \[unit 2094\]

vasurmayūkhāgnijanādhipeṣu

yoktre vake smādvasuhaṭṭake ca \|

vṛddhyauṣadhaśyāmadhaneṣu ratne

vasu smṛtaṃ syānmaghure'nyavacca \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 11 \[unit 2095\]

nāsā tu nāsikāyāṃ syānnāsā dvārordhvadāruṇi \|

mṛtsā tu kathitā vāsiśreṣṭhamṛttikayorbudhaiḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 12 \[unit 2096\]

hiṃsā cauryādivadhayoḥ śaṃsā vacanavāñchayoḥ \|

misistu madhurāmāṃsyorgajapuṣpājamodayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 13 \[unit 2097\]

kāsūrvikalavāci syāt lāsūḥ śaktyāyudhe'pi ca \|

prasūraśvājananyośca kandalīvīrudhorapi \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 14 \[unit 2098\]

alasaḥ pādaroge syāt kriyāmande drumāntare \|

alasā haṃsapadyāñca rabhaso vegaharṣayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 15 \[unit 2099\]

panasaḥ kaṇṭakiphale kaṇṭake kapirugbhidoḥ \|

surasaḥ syāt sumadhure parṇāse surasā'pi ca \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 16 \[unit 2100\]

sārasaḥ pakṣibhede syāt sārasaṃ sarasīruhe \|

śrīvāso yakṣadhūpe syāt paṅkaje pītavāsasi \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 17 \[unit 2101\]

sāhasantu balātkārakṛtakārye dame'pi ca \|

vāhaso jalaniryāse'jagare suniṣaṇṇake \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 18 \[unit 2102\]

lālaso lālasā'pi syādyācñātṛṣṇātirekayoḥ \|

autsukye ca samāsastu saṃkṣepe ca samarthane \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 19 \[unit 2103\]

kīkasaḥ kṛmijātau syādasthni kīkasamiṣyate \|

pāyasaṃ paramānne syācchrīvāse'pi ca pāyasaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 20 \[unit 2104\]

vāyaso nāḍijaṅghe syācchrīvāse cāgurudrume \|

kākodumbarikāyāñca kākamācyāñca vāyasī \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 21 \[unit 2105\]

mānasaṃ sarasi svānte'pyāśvāso nirvṛtau mataḥ \|\| '

ākhyāyikāparicchede'pyabhyāso vyasane'ntike \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 22 \[unit 2106\]

ucchvāsaḥ prāṇanāśvāsagadyabandhaguṇāntare \|

iṣvāso dhanvidhanuṣorvilāso bhāvalīlayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 23 \[unit 2107\]

vītaso bandhanopāye mṛgāṇāṃ pakṣiṇāmapi \|

teṣāmapi ca viśvāsahetoḥ prāvaraṇe smṛtam \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 24 \[unit 2108\]

uttaṃsaḥ karṇapūre syācchekare ca vataṃsavat \|

bībhatso vikṛte krūre'pyarjune ca ghṛṇātmani \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 25 \[unit 2109\]

vidvānātmavidi prājñe paṇḍite cābhidheyavat \|

bhūyān bahutarārthe syāt punararthe tvado'vyayam \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 26 \[unit 2110\]

jyāyān vṛddhe ca śaste ca havirhotavyasarpiṣoḥ \|

reṣāḥ syādadhame krūre kṛpaṇe'pi ca kathyate \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 27 \[unit 2111\]

vedhā vidhau budhe viṣṇāvāgaḥ pāpāparādhayoḥ \|

varcco dīptau purīṣe ca varcco rūpe'pi ca kvacit \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 28 \[unit 2112\]

chandaḥ padye ca vede ca svairācārābhilāṣayoḥ \|

eno'parādhe kaluṣe manaścittamanīṣayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 29 \[unit 2113\]

rodaśca rodasī cā'pi divi bhūmau pṛthaka pṛthak \|

saha prayoge'pyanayo rodaḥ syādapi rodasī \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 30 \[unit 2114\]

ojastejasi dhātūnāmavastambhaprakāśayoḥ \|

bhujo vale ca dīptau ca tejo dhāmni parākrame \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 31 \[unit 2115\]

prabhāvaretasoścātha rajaḥ syādārttave guṇe \|

rajaḥparāge reṇau tu rajavat parikīrttitam \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 32 \[unit 2116\]

vapurbhavyākṛtau dehe payaḥ syāt kṣīranīrayoḥ \|

vayaḥ pakṣiṇi bālyādau vayo yauvanamātrake \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 33 \[unit 2117\]

śreyastu maṅgale dharme śreyān śaste tu vācyavat \|

śreyasī karipippalyāmabhayārāsnayorapi \|\| 33 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 34 \[unit 2118\]

sroto'mbuvegendriyayorokaḥ sadmani cāśraye \|

śiraḥ pradhāne senāgrabhāgamastakayorapi \|\| 34 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 35 \[unit 2119\]

tapaścāndrāyaṇādau syāddharme lokāntare'pi ca \|

tapā māghe ca śiśire sarastoyataḍāgayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 36 \[unit 2120\]

uṣaḥ prabhāte sandhyāyāmuraḥ śreṣṭhe ca vakṣasi \|

tamo'ndhakāre svarbhānau tamaḥśoke guṇāntare \|\| 36 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 37 \[unit 2121\]

nabho vyomni nabhā meghe śrāvaṇe ca patadgrahe \|

ghrāṇe mṛṇālasūtre ca varṣāsu ca nabhāḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 38 \[unit 2122\]

taro bale ca vege ca retaḥ śukre ca pārade \|

arccirmayūkhaśikhayorbarhiḥ kuśahutāśayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 39 \[unit 2123\]

saho vale jyotiṣi mārgaśīrṣa-

hemantayoścā'pi sahaḥ pradiṣṭaḥ \|

raho'pi guhye surate ca tattve

maho bhavedutsavatejasośca \|\| 39 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 40 \[unit 2124\]

jyotiḥ prakāśe dṛśi tārakāsu

jyotirdineśānalayorapi syāt \|

ghanuḥ priyāladrumarāśibhedayoḥ

śarāsane cāpi dhanurdhanurdhare \|\| 40 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 41 \[unit 2125\]

āśīruktā hitāśaṃsāpavanāśanadaṃṣṭrayoḥ \|

rākṣasīti ca daṃṣṭrāyāṃ rākṣasī rakṣasaḥ striyām \|\| 41 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 42 \[unit 2126\]

tāmasīti ca durgāyāṃ tāmaso bhujage khale \|

pukkasī kālikānīlyoḥ pukkasaḥ śvapace'dhame \|\| 42 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 43 \[unit 2127\]

bhavedghanarasaḥ sāndraniryāsamoraṭāpsu ca \|

karpūre pīluparṇyāñca sasye siddharase'pi ca \|\| 43 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 44 \[unit 2128\]

sarvarasaḥ smṛto vādyabhāṇḍabhede ca dhūnake \|

smṛtaṃ tāmarasaṃ padme tāmrakāñcanayorapi \|\| 44 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 45 \[unit 2129\]

siddharaso rase vyādhiprabhṛtīṣu ca dṛśyate \|

mahārasaḥ syāt kharjjūre kośakārakaseruṇoḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 46 \[unit 2130\]

rāserasastu rāse syādrasasiddhibalāvapi \|

ṣaṣṭhījāgarike goṣṭhyāṃ śṛṅgāraparihāsayoḥ \|\| 46 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 47 \[unit 2131\]

śvaḥśreyasañca bhadre syāt parānande ca śarmaṇi \|

niḥśreyasañca kalyāṇe candracūḍāpavargayoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 48 \[unit 2132\]

kumbhīnaso viṣajvālākuladṛṣṭibhujaṅgame \|

kumbhīnasī jananyāñca lavaṇākhyasya rakṣasaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 49 \[unit 2133\]

malīmasantu maline puṣpakāsīsalohayoḥ \|

pauṇemāsapaurṇamāsyau yajñapūrṇimayoḥ kramāt \|\| 49 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 50 \[unit 2134\]

adhivāso nivāse ca saṃskāre dhūpakādibhiḥ \|

candrahāso daśagrīvakaravāle'simātrake \|\| 50 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 51 \[unit 2135\]

viśvāvasuḥ syādgandharvabhede viśvāvasurniśi \|

vibhāvasurdinamaṇau hārabhede ca pāvake \|\| 51 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 52 \[unit 2136\]

punarvasurbhavedṛkṣe kātyāyanamunāvapi \|

rājahaṃso nṛpaśreṣṭhe kādambakalahaṃsayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 53 \[unit 2137\]

kalahaṃsastu kādambe rājahaṃse nṛpottame \|

avadhvaṃsaḥ parityāge nindane'pyavacūrṇane \|\| 53 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 54 \[unit 2138\]

smṛtā madhurasā drākṣāmūrvikādugdhikāsu ca \|

nīlañjasā'psarobhede saridbhede'pi vidyuti \|\| 54 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 2139\]

sumedhāstu subuddhau syājjyotiṣmatyāmapīṣpate \|

sumanāḥ puṣpamālatyostridaśe kovide'pi ca \|\| 55 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 2140\]

pracetāstu munau hṛṣṭe syāt pratīcīpatāvapi \|

vihāyāstu śakunte syādvihāyaḥ suravartmani \|\| 56 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 57 \[unit 2141\]

trisrotā jahnukanyāyāṃ trisrotāḥ saridantare \|

agaukāśca nagaukāśca śarabhe siṃhapakṣiṇoḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 58 \[unit 2142\]

udarccirutprabhe vahnau divaukāścātake sure \|

dīrghāyuḥ śālmalau kāke mārkaṇḍeye'tijīvini \|\| 58 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 59 \[unit 2143\]

saptārcciḥ pāvake proktaḥ saptārcciḥ krūralocane \|

kanīyānanuje'tyalpe kanīyānatiyūni ca \|\| 59 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 60 \[unit 2144\]

varīyānatiyūni syādvariṣṭhe śreṣṭhayogayoḥ \|

sādhīyānatibāḍhe syādatisādhau ca vācyavat \|\| 60 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sapañcakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sapañcakam / 61 \[unit 2145\]

syānnabhaścamasaścandre citrāpūpendrajālayoḥ \|

hiṅguniryāsa ityeṣa nimbe hiṅgurase'pi ca \|\| 61 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / sapañcakam / fragment 2146

divyacakṣuḥ sugandhasya bhede'ndhe ca sulocane \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / saṣaṭkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / saṣaṭkam / 62 \[unit 2147\]

hiraṇyaretasamprāhurdivākarahavirbhujoḥ \|\| 62 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / sāntavargaḥ / saṣaṭkam / fragment 2148

iti sāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 1 \[unit 2149\]

saho bale sahā mudgaparṇyāñca nakhabheṣaje \|

sahā devīkumāryośca sahaḥ kānte sahā bhuvi \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 2 \[unit 2150\]

graho'nugrahanirbandhagrahaṇeṣu raṇodyame \|

sūryādau pūtanādau ca saiṃhikeyoparāgayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 3 \[unit 2151\]

vahaḥ syādvṛṣabhaskandhadeśe gandhavahe'pi ca \|

barhaṃ dale kekipicche gṛhā patnyāṃ gṛhe smṛtāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 4 \[unit 2152\]

grāho grahe'vahāre ca nāse bandhanakūṭayoḥ \|

snehastailādikarasadravye syāt sauhṛde'pi ca \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 5 \[unit 2153\]

proho nipuṇatarke syāt proho hastyaṇghriparvaṇoḥ \|

mohamicchanti mūrcchāyāmavidyāyāñca sūrayaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 6 \[unit 2154\]

lohastu śastrakaṃ lohaṃ joṅgake sarvataijase \|

vyūhaḥ syād balavinyāse nirmāṇe vṛndatarkayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 7 \[unit 2155\]

siṃhaḥ kaṇṭhīrave rāśau sattame cottarasthitaḥ \|

siṃhī kṣudrābṛhatyoḥ syādvāsake rāhumātari \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 8 \[unit 2156\]

bahuḥ syādvipule tryādi saṅkhyāsu tvabhidheyavat \|

ahirvṛtrāsure sarpe'pīhā tūdyamavāñchayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 9 \[unit 2157\]

guhā tu gahvare siṃhapucchyāṃ skande guho mataḥ \|

vāho bhujāyāṃ vāhastu mānabhede vṛṣe haye \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 10 \[unit 2158\]

mahī bhūmau mahī nadyāṃ maha utsavatejasoḥ \|

kuhūḥ syāt kokilārāvanaṣṭendukaradarśayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 11 \[unit 2159\]

paṭahastu samārambha ānakaṃ paṭahaṃ matam \|

kalahaḥ khaḍgakośe syādbhaṇḍane yuddharāḍhayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 12 \[unit 2160\]

pragrāhastu tulāsūtre bandyāṃ niyamane bhuje \|

hayādiraśmau raśmau ca suvarṇahalipādape \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 13 \[unit 2161\]

nigraho bhartsane prokto maryādāyāñca bandhake \|

vigrahaḥ samare kāye vistārapravibhāgayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 14 \[unit 2162\]

saṅgrāho bṛhaduttuṅge muṣṭisaṃkṣepayorapi \|

āgraho'nugrahāśaktyorākrame grahaṇe'pi ca \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 15 \[unit 2163\]

navāhaḥ syānnavadine navāhaḥ pratipattithau \|

pravāho vyavahāre syādapi srotasi vāriṇaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 16 \[unit 2164\]

kaṭāho ghṛtatailādipākapātre ca kharpare \|

kaṭāhaḥ kūrmapṛṣṭhe ca stūpe ca mahiṣīśiśau \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 17 \[unit 2165\]

utsāhastūdyame sūtre'pyārohe dairghyamānayoḥ \|

ārohaṇe nitambe ca samucchrayaniṣādinoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 18 \[unit 2166\]

niryūhaḥ śekhare dvāre niryāse nāgadantake \|

videho dehaśūnye syādvideho mithile smṛtaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 19 \[unit 2167\]

nirūho vastibhede syāt tarkaniścitayorapi \|

dātyūhaḥ kālakaṇṭhe syāddātyūścātake'pi ca \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 20 \[unit 2168\]

atyūhā nīlikāyāṃ syādatyūhaścitramekhale \|

suvahā sallakīgodhāpadīśephālikāsu ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 21 \[unit 2169\]

elāparṇyāñca suvahā vallakīrāsnayorapi \|

samyagvāhe tu suvaho vācyavat samudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 22 \[unit 2170\]

vaidehī rocanāsītāvaṇikstrīpipyalīṣvapi \|

vārāhī mātṛbhede syād gṛṣṭināmauṣadhe'pi ca \|\| 22 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 23 \[unit 2171\]

avagrahā iti khyāto dṛṣṭirome gajālike \|

svatantratāniṣedhe'pi pratibandhe'pyavagrahaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 24 \[unit 2172\]

pratigrahaḥ svīkaraṇe sainyapṛṣṭhe patadgrahe \|

dvijebhyo vidhivaddeye tadgrahe ca grahāntare \|\| 24 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 25 \[unit 2173\]

parigrahaḥ parijane patnyāṃ svīkāramūlayoḥ \|

śāpe'pyupagraho vandyāmupayoge'nukūlane \|\| 25 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 26 \[unit 2174\]

abhigraho'bhiyoge'bhigrahaṇe gaurave'pi ca \|

parivarhantu rājārhavastunya'pi paricchade \|\| 26 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 27 \[unit 2175\]

pitāmaho viriñcau syāttātasya janake'pi ca \|

tamo'pahaḥ sahasrāṃśumṛgāṅkajinavahniṣu \|\| 27 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 28 \[unit 2176\]

upanāho vraṇālepapiṇḍe vīṇānibandhane \|

parīvāho jalocchvāse mahībhṛdbhogyavastuni \|\| 28 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 29 \[unit 2177\]

tanūruhaṃ garullomnorvarārohākaṭāvapi \|

varārohastu kathito hastyārohāvarohayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 30 \[unit 2178\]

avaroho'vataraṇe taroraṅge latodgame \|

aśvārohā'śvagandyāyāmaśvāroho'śvavārake \|\| 30 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / 31 \[unit 2179\]

mahāsahā māṣaparṇyāmamlānakusume'pi ca \|

ganyavahā tu nāsāyāṃ vāyau gandhavaho mataḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hacatuṣkam / fragment 2180

bhavedekasahābhikhyā sūte daṇḍotpalauṣadhau \|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hapatrakam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hapatrakam / 32 \[unit 2181\]

prapitāmaha ityeṣa vidhau pitṛpitāmahe \|\| 32 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / hāntavargaḥ / hapatrakam / fragment 2182

iti hāntavargaḥ \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 2183\]

ṣāntāḥ syuryadyapi kṣāntā varṇānāmanurodhataḥ \|

pṛthakkrameṇa kathyante tathā'pyete samanvayāt \|\| 1 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 2 \[unit 2184\]

akṣaḥ karṣe tuṣe cakre śakaṭe vyavahārayoḥ \|

ātmajñe pāśake cākṣaṃ tutthasauvarccalendriye \|\| 2 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 3 \[unit 2185\]

ṛkṣo'dribhede bhallūke śoṇake kṛtavedhane \|

ṛkṣamuktañca nakṣatre yakṣaḥ śrīde ca guhyake \|\| 3 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 4 \[unit 2186\]

dakṣaḥ paṭau haravṛṣe tāmracūḍe prajāpatau \|

munibhede drume vahnau dakṣo dakṣā bhuvi smṛtā \|\| 4 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 5 \[unit 2187\]

pakṣo māsārddhake pārśve grahe sādhyavirodhayoḥ \|

pakṣaśca parato vṛnde bale sakhisahāyayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 6 \[unit 2188\]

patatre cullirandhre ca dehāṅge rājakuñjake \|

lakṣaṃ śaravye saṅkhyāyāṃ lakṣaṃ chadmani kathyate \|\| 6 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 7 \[unit 2189\]

plakṣo dvīpaviśeṣe syāt parkkaṭīgarddabhāṇḍayoḥ \|

pippale dvārapārśve ca gṛhasya parikīrttitaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 8 \[unit 2190\]

nyakṣaḥ parakṣurāme syānnyakṣaḥ kārtsnye nikṛṣṭayoḥ \|

cokṣo gīte dhruvau dakṣe tayā tīkṣṇamanojñayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 9 \[unit 2191\]

dhvāṅkṣastu kākabakayostarkuke bhikṣuke gṛhe \|

dhvāṅkṣī kakkolikāyāṃ syāt kakṣo dormūlakacchayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 10 \[unit 2192\]

sairibhe ca vṛpe kakṣaḥ śuṣkakānanavīrudhoḥ \|

kakṣā grāhaṇikākāñcīprakoṣṭhagajarajjuṣu \|\| 10 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 11 \[unit 2193\]

sārddhe pade parīdhānapaścādañcalapallave \|

rathabhāge'pi kakṣā syādvīkṣā vismayaraśyayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 12 \[unit 2194\]

mokṣo muṣkakavṛkṣe syādapavarge ca mocane \|

rūkṣaḥ pādapajātau syād rūkṣo'premaṇyacikkaṇe \|\| 12 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣadvikam / 13 \[unit 2195\]

bhikṣā bhṛtau ca yācñāyāṃ sevābhikṣitavastunoḥ \|

prekṣā'nutprekṣaṇe buddhau rakṣā rakṣaṇarakṣayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatrikam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatrikam / 14 \[unit 2196\]

adhyakṣo'dhikṛte proktaḥ pratyakṣe'dhyakṣamiṣyate \|

gorakṣo nāgaraṅge syād gavāñca parirakṣake \|\| 14 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatrikam / 15 \[unit 2197\]

ārakṣo rakṣaṇīye syācchīrṣamarmaṇi dantināma \|

raktākṣaḥ kāsare krūre pārāvatacakorayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatrikam / 16 \[unit 2198\]

samīkṣā granthabhede syāt tattvabuddhau nibhālane \|

utprekṣā'navadhāne syāt kāvyālaṅkaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 16 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣatrikam / 17 \[unit 2199\]

mṛgākṣī cendravāruṇyāṃ mṛganetrātriyāmayoḥ \|

gavākṣī śakravāruṇyāṃ gavākṣo jālake kapau \|\| 17 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacatuṣkam

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacatuṣkam / 184 \[unit 2200\]

vīravṛkṣastu bhallātakakubhadrumayormataḥ \|\| 184 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacatuṣkam / 18 \[unit 2201\]

rājavṛkṣaḥ priyāle syāt suvarṇālutarāvapi \|\| 18 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacatuṣkam / 19 \[unit 2202\]

devavṛkṣaḥ saptaparṇe mandarādiṣu guggulau \|

bhūtavṛkṣastu śākoṭe tathā śyonākapādape \|\| 19 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacatuṣkam / 20 \[unit 2203\]

viśālākṣo hare tārkṣye viśālākṣī varastriyām \|

sakaṭākṣo ghavadrau syāt kaṭakṣasahite'pi ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ / kṣāntavargaḥ / kṣacatuṣkam / fragment 2204

iti kṣāntavargaḥ \|\|

iti śrīsakalavaidyarājacakramuktāśekharasya gadyapadyavidyānidheḥ śrīmaheśvarasya kṛtau viśvaprakāśe anekārthaśabdaparicchedaḥ prathamaḥ \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / 1 \[unit 2205\]

athāvyayāni vakṣyante vyaktaṃ pūrvākṣarakramāt \|

akārādikamapyasti nādhikaṃ pūrvato'pi ca \|\| 1 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / a

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / a / fragment 2206

a syādabhāve svalpārthe viṣṇāvīśe tvanavyayam \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / āṅa

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / āṅa / 2 \[unit 2207\]

āṅ sīmāyāṃ parivyāptau kiyāyogeṣadarthayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ā

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ā / fragment 2208

ā pragṛjñaḥ smṛtau vākye āḥ syāt santāpakopayoḥ \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / i

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / i / 3 \[unit 2209\]

i khede ca ruṣoktau ca kāmadeve tvanavyayam \|\| 3 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ī

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ī / fragment 2210

ī duḥsabhāvane kope lakṣmyāmīḥ syādanavyayam \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ca

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ca / 4 \[unit 2211\]

u sambuddhau ruṣoktau ca śambhuvācī tvanavyayam \|\| 4 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ū

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ū / fragment 2212

ū vākyārambhe'nukampārakṣāhūtiṣvanavyayam \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṛ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṛ / 5 \[unit 2213\]

ṛ vākye cā'pi kutsāyāṃ devāmbāyāntvanavyayam \|\| 5 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / e ai

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / e ai / fragment 2214

e-ai-śabdau tu he-haivat smṛtyāmantraṇahūtiṣu \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / o au

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / o au / 6 \[unit 2215\]

oauśabdau ca hohauvata sambuddhyāhvānayormatau \|\| 6 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / kāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / kāntāḥ / 7 \[unit 2216\]

ku pāpe ceṣadarthe ca kutsāyāñca nivāraṇe \|

manāgalpe ca mande ca dhig nirbhartsananindayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / gāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / gāntāḥ / fragment 2217

aṅga sambodhane harṣe punararthe ca kīrttitam \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / cāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / cāntāḥ / 8 \[unit 2218\]

tiryak tiro'rthe vakre ca vihaṅgādau tvanavyayam \|\| 8 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / cāntāḥ / 9 \[unit 2219\]

cānvācaye samāhāre'pyanyonyārthe samuccaye \|

pakṣāntare tathā pādapūraṇe'pyavadhāraṇe \|\| 9 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / cāntāḥ / 10 \[unit 2220\]

prāka prabhāte'pyatīte ca digdeśakālataḥ smṛtam \|

prāga'gre'pi ca pūrvasmin bhave prāgapyanantare \|\| 10 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / cāntāḥ / 11 \[unit 2221\]

nanuca praśnadṛṣṭyoktyoḥ samyaggāḍhapraśaṃsayoḥ \|

kiñcārambhe ca sākalye hirug madhyavinārthayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 12 \[unit 2222\]

naña'bhāve niṣedhe ca svarūpārthe vyatikrame \|

īṣadarthe ca sādṛśye tadviruddhatadanyayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 42 \[unit 2254\]

sma pādapūraṇe'tīte sāmi nindārddhayormatam \|\| 42 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 43 \[unit 2255\]

amā sahārthāntikayornūnaṃ niścitatarkayoḥ \|

kāmam prakāme'numatāvasūyānugame'pi ca \|\| 43 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 44 \[unit 2256\]

nāma prākāśyasambhāvyakutsābhyupagameṣu ca \|

sāmprataṃ cādhunārthe syād yuktārthe'pi ca sāmpratam \|\| 44 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 45 \[unit 2257\]

huṃ vitarke paripraśne huṃ ruṣoktyanunītiṣu \|

tum praśne'ṅgīkṛtau roṣe āsmṛtau cāvadhāraṇe \|\| 45 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 46 \[unit 2258\]

kuṃ praśne'pi ruṣoktau ca prādhvaṃ namrānukūlayoḥ \|

kiṃ praśne'pi ca kutsāyāṃ kaṃ śiraḥmukhavāriṣu \|\| 46 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 47 \[unit 2259\]

numinyanumatau proktaṃ praṇave cā'pyupakrame \|

evaṃ prakāropamayoraṅgīkāre'vadhāraṇe \|\| 47 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 48 \[unit 2260\]

alaṃ bhūṣaṇaparyāptivāraṇeṣu nirarthake \|

alaṃ śaktau ca nirddiṣṭaṃ śaṃ kalyāṇe sukhe'pi ca \|\| 48 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 49 \[unit 2261\]

saṃ saṅgārthe prakṛtyarthe śobhanārthasamarthayoḥ \|

joṣaṃ sukhe praśaṃsāyāṃ tūṣṇīṃlaṅghanayorapi \|\| 49 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 50 \[unit 2262\]

tūṣṇīkaṃ tu smṛtaṃ maune maunaśīle tvanavyayam \|

taddinaṃ dinamadhye syāt taddinaṃ prativāsare \|\| 50 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 51 \[unit 2263\]

abhīkṣṇaṃ muhuraśrānte śīghraprakarṣayorapi \|

abhīkṣṇaṃ tu tathā paunaḥpunye syāt santate'pi ca \|\| 51 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 52 \[unit 2264\]

idānīṃ vākyabhūṣāyāṃ sampratyarthe ca varttate \|

kathaṃ praśne sambhrame ca prakārārthe ca sambhave \|\| 52 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / māntāḥ / 53 \[unit 2265\]

kimu sambhāvanāyāṃ syādvimarśe ca kimu smṛtam \|

bhṛśaṃ prakarṣe cātyarthe'vaśyaṃ nityaprayatnayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṭhāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṭhāntāḥ / 13 \[unit 2223\]

suṣṭu praśaṃsane'pi syādatyarthe'pi ca kathyate \|

apaṣṭhu niravadye syācchobhamāne'pi ca smṛtam \|\| 13 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṇāntaḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṇāntaḥ / fragment 2224

antareṇa-padaṃ vidyādvināmadhyārthayorapi \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 14 \[unit 2225\]

tu pādapūraṇe bhede samuccaye'vadhāraṇe \|\| 14 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 15 \[unit 2226\]

astu syādabhyanujñāne'pyasūyānīhayorapi \|

kiṃsvit praśne vitarke ca śaśvat punaḥsadārthayoḥ \|

paścāt pratīcyāṃ carame sākṣāt pratyakṣatulyayoḥ \|

sakṛt sahaikavāre syādārād dūrasamīpayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 16 \[unit 2227\]

yāvat tāvat paricchede kārtsnye māne'vadhāraṇe \|

purastāccaturṣu prācyāṃ purārthe prathame'grataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 17 \[unit 2228\]

uta praśne vitarke syādutā'pyarthavikalpayoḥ \|

kimutātiśaye praśne vikalpe ca prayujyate \|\| 17 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 18 \[unit 2229\]

hanta harṣe viṣāde ca vākyārambhavivādayoḥ \|

svastyāśīḥkṣemapuṇyādau tadvata praśnavikalpayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 19 \[unit 2230\]

batā'mantraṇasantoṣakhedānukropavismaye \|

ahobatā'nukampāyāṃ khede sambodhane'pi ca \|\| 19 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 20 \[unit 2231\]

tata ādau paripraśne pañcamyarthe kathāntare \|

ānantarye kutaḥ praśne pañcamyarthe ca nihnave \|\| 20 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 21 \[unit 2232\]

ato bhavet kāraṇāpadeśanirddeśayorapi \|

pañcamyarthe tataḥ proktaṃ pañcamyarthe ca śāsane \|\| 21 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 22 \[unit 2233\]

sambhāvanāvayavayorantataḥ-padamiṣyate \|

ito yataśca niyame pañcamyarthavibhāgayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 23 \[unit 2234\]

iti prakaraṇe hetau prakāśādisamāptiṣu \|

nidarśanaprakāre syādanukarṣe ca sammatam \|\| 23 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 24 \[unit 2235\]

atiśabdaḥ praśaṃsāyāṃ prakarṣe laṅghane'pyati \|

nitāntāsampratikṣepavācako'pyeṣa darśitaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / tāntāḥ / 25 \[unit 2236\]

prati pratividhau vīpsā lakṣaṇeṣu prayogataḥ \|

mātrārthe cābhimukhye ca pratidānādiṣu prati \|\| 25 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / thāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / thāntāḥ / 26 \[unit 2237\]

atho atha ca sampraśne maṅgalārambhayorapi \|

anantare ca kārtsnye ca saṃśaye ca prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / thāntāḥ / 27 \[unit 2238\]

yathāśabdastu nirdiṣṭastulyayogānumānayoḥ \|

tathā syānniścaye pṛṣṭaprativākye samuccaye \|\| 27 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / thāntāḥ / 28 \[unit 2239\]

ubhau coddeśanirddeśasādṛśyeṣu prakāśitau \|

kārakasyo'papattau ca sarvathā hetubāḍhayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / thāntāḥ / 29 \[unit 2240\]

anyathā vitathārthe syādanyathā cā'parārthake \|

vṛthā niṣkāraṇe bandhe vṛthā syādvidhivarjjite \|\| 29 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / dāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / dāntāḥ / 30 \[unit 2241\]

ut prakāśe vibhāge ca prābalyāsvāsthyaśaktiṣu \|

prādhānye bandhane bhāve mokṣe lābhorddhakarmaṇoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / nāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / nāntāḥ / 31 \[unit 2242\]

nu syāt praśne vikalpārthe'pyatītānunayārthayoḥ \|

nanu praśne'pyanunaye'nujñāne'pyavadhāraṇe \|\| 31 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / nāntāḥ / 32 \[unit 2243\]

āmantraṇe cā'pi nanu kinnu praśnavitarkayoḥ \|

nānā vinārthe'pi bhavennānā'nekobhayārthayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / nāntāḥ / 33 \[unit 2244\]

sthāne tu kāraṇārthe ca yuktasādṛśyayorapi \|

anu hīne sahārthe ca paścāt sādṛśyayoranu \|\| 33 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / nāntāḥ / 34 \[unit 2245\]

āyāme ca niyāme ca lakṣaṇādāvanukrame \|

ni niveśabhṛśārthādhobhāvavinyāsarāśiṣu \|\| 34 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / nāntāḥ / 35 \[unit 2246\]

āśraye bandhane mokṣe dārakarmaṇi darśane \|

antarbhāve ca sāmīpyakauśaloparameṣu ca \|

nityārthe saṃyame cā'pi kṣepārthe'pi ca viśrutam \|\| 35 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / pāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / pāntāḥ / 36 \[unit 2247\]

apa syādapakṛṣṭārthe varjjanārthaviyogayoḥ \|

viparyaye'pi vikṛtau caurye nirddeśaharṣayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / pāntāḥ / 37 \[unit 2248\]

upa sāmarthyadākṣiṇyadoṣākhyānātyayeṣu ca \|

ācāryakaraṇe dāne vyāptāvārambhapūjayoḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / pāntāḥ / 38 \[unit 2249\]

tadyoge'pi ca lipsāyāṃ bharaṇārthopamārthayoḥ \|

upa hīne'dhike prokto'pyāsanne'pyupa kīrttitaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / pāntāḥ / 39 \[unit 2250\]

api sambhāvanāpraśnaśaṅkāgarhāsamuccaye \|

tathā yuktapadārtheṣu kāmacārakriyāsu ca \|\| 39 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / bāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / bāntāḥ / 40 \[unit 2251\]

baśabda upamāyāṃ syādvaruṇe vānunaye'pi ca \|

vā syādvikalpopamayorevārthe'pi samuccaye \|\| 40 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / bāntāḥ / 41 \[unit 2252\]

vi śreṣṭhātītanānārthe viḥ syāt pakṣiṣvanavyayam \|

vai syāt sambodhane pādapūraṇe'nunaye'pi ca \|\| 41 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / bhāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / bhāntāḥ / fragment 2253

abhī'tthambhūtakathane'pyabhi vīpsābhimukhyayoḥ \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / yāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / yāntāḥ / 54 \[unit 2266\]

ayi praśnānunayayoḥ samayāntikamadhyayoḥ \|

aye kroṣe viṣāde ca sambhrame smaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 54 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / rāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / rāntāḥ / 55 \[unit 2267\]

pra prakarṣe gatādyarthe duḥ syāt kaṣṭaniṣedhayoḥ \|

punaraprathame bhede svaḥ svargaparalokayoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / rāntāḥ / 56 \[unit 2268\]

parā vimokṣaprādhānyapratilomeṣu varttate \|

ābhimukhye bhṛṣārthe ca vikrame ca gatau vadhe \|\| 56 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / rāntāḥ / 57 \[unit 2269\]

purā purāṇe nikaṭaprabandhātītabhāviṣu \|

pari syāt sarvatobhāve varjjane lakṣaṇādiṣu \|\| 57 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / rāntāḥ / 58 \[unit 2270\]

pūjāliṅganavīpsāsu bhūṣaṇe vyādhiśokayoḥ \|

doṣākhyāne'pyuparame vyāptau nivasane'pi ca \|\| 58 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / rāntāḥ / 59 \[unit 2271\]

prāduḥ prākāśyavṛttau syāt sambhāvye ca prayujyate \|

nirniścaye krāntādyarthe nirniḥśeṣaniṣedhayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / rāntāḥ / 60 \[unit 2272\]

antarmadhye tathā prānte svīkārārthe'pi dṛśyate \|

urarī corarī corī vistāre'ṅgīkṛtau trayam \|\| 60 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / rāntāḥ / fragment 2273

antarāpi vinārthe syānmadhyārthanikaṭārthayoḥ \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / lāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / lāntāḥ / 61 \[unit 2274\]

kilaśabdastu vārttāyāṃ sambhāvyānunayārthayoḥ \|\| 61 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / lāntāḥ / 62 \[unit 2275\]

khalu syādvākyabhūṣāyāṃ jijñāsādau ca sāntane \|

niṣedhane'pi vīpsāyāṃ samu māne ca kīrttitam \|\| 62 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / vāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / vāntāḥ / 63 \[unit 2276\]

avā'lambanavijñānaviyogavyāptiśuddhiṣu \|

iṣadarthe paribhave'pyevaupamye'vadhāraṇe \|\| 63 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṣāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṣāntāḥ / 64 \[unit 2277\]

uṣā rātrau tadante syādatrānavyayamapyuṣā \|

doṣā rātrimukhe rātrāvatrānavyayamapyasau \|\| 64 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṣāntāḥ / 65 \[unit 2278\]

nikaṣā tvantike madhye rakṣomātaryanavyayam \|

su pūjāyāṃ bhṛṣārthānumatikṛcchrasamṛddhiṣu \|\| 65 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṣāntāḥ / 66 \[unit 2279\]

purataḥ prathame cā'gre'pyagrataḥ prathamāgrayoḥ \|

puro'gre prathame ca syādañjasā tattvatūrṇayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṣāntāḥ / 67 \[unit 2280\]

amitaḥ śīghrasākalyasammukhobhayato'ntike \|

bhūyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ khyātaṃ prabhūtārthe tvanavyayam \|\| 67 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṣāntāḥ / 68 \[unit 2281\]

nirniścaye niṣedhe ca sākalyātītayorapi \|

tiro'ntarddhau tiryagarthe mitho'nyonyaṃ mitho rahaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / ṣāntāḥ / 69 \[unit 2282\]

pūrvedyuriṣyate prātaḥ pūrvedyuḥ pūrvavāsare \|

śanaiḥ śanaiścare svaire nīcaiḥ svairālpayormatam \|\| 69 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / hāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / hāntāḥ / 70 \[unit 2283\]

ha syāt sambodhane pādapūraṇe'navyayaṃ śive \|

hā viṣāde ca śoke ca duḥkhārthe'pi ca kathyate \|\| 70 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / hāntāḥ / 71 \[unit 2284\]

hi pādapūraṇe hetau viśeṣe'pyavaghāraṇe \|

hī duḥkhahetāvuddiṣṭo hī vismayaviṣādayoḥ \|\| 71 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / hāntāḥ / 72 \[unit 2285\]

hāhā duḥkhaṃ huhū harṣe gandharve'mū tvanavyaye \|

he hai vyastau samastau ca hūtisambodhanārthayoḥ \|\| 72 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / hāntāḥ / 73 \[unit 2286\]

ho hau caivaṃvidhau jñeyau sambuddhāhvānayorapi \|

saha sākalyasādṛśyayaugapadyasamṛddhiṣu \|\| 73 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / hāntāḥ / 74 \[unit 2287\]

vidyamāne ca sambandhe sahaśabdaḥ prakīrttitaḥ \|

aho dhigarthe śoke ca karuṇārthaviṣādayoḥ \|\| 74 \|\|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / hāntāḥ / fragment 2288

āho utāho dvāvetau paripraśnavicārayoḥ \|

ahaśabdo niyogārthe kṣepārthe'pi nigadyate \|

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / kṣāntāḥ

## ayavyayānekārthavargaḥ / avyayāni / kṣāntāḥ / 75 \[unit 2289\]

maṅkṣu śīghre bhṛṣārthe ca tattvārthe'pi kvacinmatam \|\| 75 \|\|

## Colophon

## Colophon / 1 \[unit 2290\]

ityavyayānekārthavargaḥ \|\|

yadyapūrvatayā kiñcinnāmātra pratibhāti ca \|

tattadanviṣyatāṃ sadbhirnāmapārāyaṇādiṣu \|\| 1 \|\|

## Colophon / 2 \[unit 2291\]

tattaktavīndrairatha paṇḍitendraiḥ

prayogasambodhaphaladvayāptyai \|

yairnāmakaṇṇṭhābharaṇaṃ kṛtaṃ taiḥ

sarvajñatā svapraṇayīkṛtaiva \|\| 2 \|\|

## Colophon / 3 \[unit 2292\]

svairapracāraiḥ parikalpitābhiḥ

śabdārthasambodhakathāprathābhiḥ \|

vyākhyābhiraprāptamudāṃ pramoda-

mādhātumatraiṣa pariśramo naḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## Colophon / 4 \[unit 2293\]

etāṃ kṛtiṃ kṛtadhiyaḥ kṛtakṛtyabhāva-

māpādayantu sadayaṃ, sadayaṃ tu cetaḥ \|

nityaṃ maheśvarakaveḥ paribhāvayantaḥ

santaḥ paronnatiratā hi bhavanti loke \|\| 4 \|\|

## Colophon / 5 \[unit 2294\]

rāmānalavyomarūpaiḥ śakakāle'bhilakṣite \|

koṣaṃ viśvaprakāśākhyaṃ niramācchrīmaheśvaraḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## Colophon / closing

iti śrīsakalavaidyarājacakramuktāśekharasya gadyapadyavidyānidheḥ śrīmaheśvarasya kṛtau viśvaprakāśe anekārthāvyayapari cchedo dvitīyaḥ \|

samāpto'yaṃ viśvaprakāśaḥ \|

śubhamastu \|
